We argue that a more effective path toward UHR understanding lies not in accessing more, but in organizing better. To this end, we propose WeaveEarth, a training-free framework that reformulates UHR understanding as a problem of structured evidence construction and reasoning under global context constraints. Specifically, WeaveEarth first employs Global-Aware Evidence Construction to select a compact, low-redundancy, and spatially complementary Minimal Support Evidence Set. It then introduces Structured Evidence Reasoning, which weaves local evidence, spatial metadata, and relative topology into a unified reasoning interface, thereby enhancing the VLM's ability to perform global-local joint reasoning. Extensive experiments show that WeaveEarth consistently outperforms strong baselines and existing UHR methods across multiple UHR remote sensing benchmarks and multiple frozen VLM backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/XianZhi-Ma/WeaveEarth.

</details>


### [36] [TextGaze: Prompting Gaze Target Estimation with Textual Scene Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10130)
*Junhui She,Fei Wang,Kun Li,Yiqi Nie,Yuxin Liu,Zhangling Duan,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TextGaze，一种利用大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）提供语义引导的跨模态注视目标估计架构，以统一多分支与流线型两种设计范式，在无需额外标注的情况下实现有竞争力的性能与跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前注视目标估计存在两类设计范式的困境：多分支方法依赖额外的监督信号与标注，标注负担重且难以跨域迁移；流线型设计则倾向于低层视觉显著性而非真实注视意图，导致预测注意力与实际注视目标之间出现错位。作者旨在寻找一种能兼顾两者优势、避免各自缺陷的解决方案。

Method: 提出TextGaze架构：(1) 使用冻结的视觉编码器提取视觉特征；(2) 利用大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）获取与注视对齐的文本语义线索；(3) 设计基于Transformer的融合模块，并引入层级文本监督以保留任务语义；(4) 采用轻量化解码头联合预测注视热图与框内/框外状态。

Result: 在四个主流数据集上评估，TextGaze在关键指标上取得了有竞争力的性能，且在无需额外微调的情况下展现出鲁棒的跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: TextGaze为传统注视估计设计提供了一种流线型替代方案，证明了LVLM作为可及的辅助语义引导在注视估计中的潜力，有效平衡了多分支与流线型两种范式的优劣。

Abstract: Gaze target estimation aims to infer the position of a person's gaze within a scene. Within mainstream design logic, multi-branch methods require extra supervision and annotations, while streamlined designs prioritize low-level visual saliency over true gaze intent. The former leads to a high annotation burden and hinders domain transfer, whereas the latter causes misalignment between predicted attention and actual gaze targets. To address this issue, we propose TextGaze, a unified cross-modal architecture that leverages a Large Vision-Language Model (LVLM) as scalable semantic guidance to balance the two design paradigms. The model extracts visual features from a frozen encoder and utilizes an LVLM to obtain gaze-aligned textual cues. We design a transformer-based fusion module with hierarchical text supervision to preserve task semantics. Lightweight decoding heads enable the joint prediction of gaze heatmaps and in-/out-of-frame status. We evaluate our method on four mainstream datasets, and the results show competitive performance across key metrics with robust cross-dataset generalisation without extra fine-tuning. Overall, we provide a streamlined alternative to traditional designs and highlight the potential of LVLMs as accessible auxiliary guidance for gaze estimation.

</details>


### [37] [FlowPainter: Inpainting Optical Flow via Confidence-Guided Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10140)
*Yuang Meng,Chenyang Wu,Xianshun Liu,Chun-Le Guo,Zichen Liang,Lina Lei,Jie Liang,Hui Zeng,Chongyi Li,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowPainter 提出了一种基于扩散的光流估计框架，将稠密光流生成重新定义为置信度引导的软修复（soft inpainting）。它利用轻量置信度感知网络预测粗糙光流和像素级置信度掩码，以此区分简单区域与困难区域，并将简单流先验通过置信度门控残差引导注入迭代去噪过程，从而降低去噪负担、加速收敛并提升训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光流方法中，迭代优化法（如RAFT）在大位移和复杂运动场景下仍有困难；扩散模型虽然在这些模糊区域表现有潜力，但通常对整个稠密光流场从高斯噪声开始全局去噪，包括那些轻量网络已能可靠估计的简单区域。这增加了去噪负担，导致收敛缓慢和训练不稳定。因此，作者希望利用简单区域的可靠判别先验来减轻扩散模型的去噪压力。

Method: FlowPainter 框架包含两个核心设计：(1) 轻量置信度感知网络，预测粗糙光流和像素级置信度掩码，区分可靠简单区域与不确定困难区域；(2) 置信度门控残差引导机制，将简单流先验用于基于置信度的初始化，并在迭代去噪过程中注入残差引导，且引导强度动态衰减——早期稳定去噪、后期保留扩散模型的细节细化灵活性。

Result: 在 Sintel、KITTI 和 Spring 等公开基准上，FlowPainter 在可比训练设置下取得了高准确率，收敛速度显著优于现有基于扩散的光流方法，在挑战性基准划分上增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: FlowPainter 成功将可靠的判别先验与扩散模型细化相结合，为光流估计提供了一种实用方案：既利用轻量网络处理简单区域以降低扩散模型负担，又保留扩散模型在困难区域的生成灵活性，实现了更高效、更稳定的训练与推理。

Abstract: Existing optical flow methods broadly follow two paradigms: iterative optimization and diffusion-based estimation. Iterative methods, exemplified by RAFT, achieve high accuracy through recurrent refinement, but remain challenged by large displacements and complex motion. Diffusion-based methods introduce generative modeling and show promise in such ambiguous regions. However, existing diffusion models usually denoise the entire dense flow field from Gaussian noise, including simple regions where reliable motion can already be estimated by a lightweight network. This increases the denoising burden and may cause slow convergence and unstable training. To address this issue, we introduce FlowPainter, a diffusion-based optical flow framework that reformulates dense-flow generation as confidence-guided soft inpainting. FlowPainter employs a lightweight confidence-aware network to predict a rough flow and a pixel-wise confidence mask, distinguishing reliable simple regions from uncertain hard regions. The resulting simple-flow prior is used for confidence-based initialization and further injected into iterative denoising through confidence-gated residual guidance. With dynamically decaying guidance strength, FlowPainter stabilizes early denoising while preserving the flexibility of the diffusion model for late-stage detail refinement. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks, including Sintel, KITTI, and Spring, show that FlowPainter achieves strong accuracy under comparable training settings and converges more efficiently than existing diffusion-based optical flow methods, with notable gains on challenging benchmark splits. Our approach offers a practical way to integrate reliable discriminative priors with diffusion-based refinement for optical flow estimation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/mya012/FlowPainter.

</details>


### [38] [REVA-PO: Stabilizing Reinforcement Learning for Chest X-ray Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10147)
*Li Guo,Anas M. Tahir,Z. Jane Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REVA-PO 是一个用于自动胸部X光报告生成的强化学习框架，通过响应加权正则化（RER）和验证锚定策略重置（VAPR）解决RL训练中KL正则化固定和探索受限的问题，在MIMIC-CXR和IU-Xray数据集上取得了新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练存在两个核心问题：(1) 固定的KL散度正则化和静态参考策略导致训练不稳定或探索受限；(2) KL惩罚随时间累积，压缩了可探索空间。需要一种能够动态调整约束、定期释放累积压力的方法。

Method: 提出REVA-PO框架，包含两大创新：RER根据优势值和参考策略熵动态调整每条响应的KL权重，对高质量响应放松约束、对低质量响应收紧约束；VAPR周期性将参考策略和当前策略同步到最佳验证检查点，重置累积的正则化压力。训练采用三阶段流程：预热训练→分类器引导的监督微调→RL训练。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR上BLEU-4提升5.1%，CheXpert F1提升4.5%，RadGraph F1提升12.8%；在IU-Xray上BLEU-4提升3.6%，各项指标均达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: REVA-PO通过动态KL正则化和周期性策略重置有效稳定了长期RL训练并拓展了探索空间，在报告生成的语言质量和临床准确性两个维度上均显著超越先前方法。

Abstract: Automated chest X-ray report generation has recently benefited from reinforcement learning (RL) and large language models. However, RL training often suffers from instability or limited exploration due to fixed Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization and a static reference policy that accumulates KL pressure over time. We propose Response-Weighted and Validation-Anchored Policy Optimization (REVA-PO), a RL framework that stabilizes long-term training via Response-Weighted Regularization (RER) and Validation-Anchored Policy Reset (VAPR). RER dynamically adjusts per-response KL weights based on advantage and reference-policy entropy, relaxing constraints for high-quality responses while tightening them for low-quality ones. Complementarily, VAPR periodically synchronizes the reference and current policies to the best validation checkpoint, resetting accumulated regularization pressure to expand the viable exploration space. To ensure a robust starting point, we employ a three-stage pipeline consisting of warm-up training, classifier-guided supervised fine-tuning, and RL. Extensive evaluations on MIMIC-CXR and IU-Xray demonstrate that REVA-PO sets new state-of-the-art benchmarks in both linguistic quality and clinical accuracy. Notably, BLEU-4 improves by 5.1% on MIMIC-CXR and 3.6% on IU-Xray, while CheXpert F1 and RadGraph F1 scores increase by 4.5% and 12.8%, respectively, over prior leading methods. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/LiGuo12/REVA_PO/.

</details>


### [39] [EmoStyle: Affective Conditioning of Style-Specialist Experts for Emotional Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10165)
*Dexiang Hong,Yijie Guo,Weidong Chen,Xinyan Liu,Zixuan Zou,Zhendong Mao,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmoStyle 是一个基于 Z-Image 的情感感知艺术图像生成框架，通过 LLM 推理情感线索、AdaLN 调制注入情感条件、按风格分桶训练 LoRA 适配器，以及 VLM 引导的候选筛选，在 AffectiveArt Challenge 2026 Track 1 中获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感感知艺术图像生成的核心难点在于，测试时仅提供文本提示，而训练期间的视觉和情感属性不可见，导致生成器无法精确控制颜色、光照、笔触、构图等细粒度条件，形成控制缺口（control gap）。现有方法仅将情感标签作为附加文本使用，缺乏对中间特征的直接引导。

Method: 提出 EmoStyle 框架：(1) LLM 推理器从输入提示中预测情感线索（效价-唤醒度、主导情绪、治疗效果标签）和宽高比；(2) 将情感字段编码为情感条件向量，通过 AdaLN 风格调制注入去噪块，直接引导中间特征生成；(3) 为每种艺术风格分桶训练专用 LoRA 适配器，推理时选择对应专家，使相同情感线索以分桶特定的颜色、纹理、笔触和构图先验进行渲染；(4) 轻量级 VLM 引导的候选筛选，按提示对齐、风格一致性、情感表达和视觉质量对生成图像进行排序。

Result: 在 AffectiveArt Challenge 2026 Track 1 中，USTC_PI_LAB_TEAM 提交的方案获得第一名。

Conclusion: EmoStyle 通过将情感推理、条件调制、风格分桶专家适配和 VLM 筛选相结合，有效弥合了文本提示与细粒度情感-风格条件之间的控制缺口，实现了高质量的情感感知艺术图像生成。

Abstract: Emotion-aware artistic image generation requires an image to match the input prompt, follow the specified artistic style, and convey the target emotion. In this challenge, the main difficulty is that the visual and affective attributes available in the training data are not explicitly provided at test time. Without these attributes, the generator has to decide not only what to depict, but also how the target emotion should be expressed through color, lighting, brushwork, composition, line, and layout. This creates a control gap between the available test prompt and the fine-grained conditions needed for emotion-aware artistic generation. To bridge this gap, we propose EmoStyle, a Z-Image-based framework that converts the input prompt into a structured generation state. An LLM reasoner first predicts affective cues (valence-arousal, dominant emotion, and therapeutic-effect labels) and an aspect-ratio decision. Instead of using these predictions only as additional prompt text, we encode the affective fields into an affective condition vector and inject it into the denoising blocks through AdaLN-style modulation. This allows the inferred control variables to directly guide the generation of intermediate features. Since emotional expression is also style-dependent, we further train a dedicated LoRA adapter for each artistic style bucket and select the corresponding expert during inference, enabling the same affective cues to be rendered with bucket-specific priors for color, texture, brushwork, and composition. Finally, a lightweight VLM-guided candidate selection step ranks the generated images based on prompt alignment, style consistency, emotional expression, and visual quality. In Track 1 of the AffectiveArt Challenge 2026, our USTC\_PI\_LAB\_TEAM submission achieved first place.

</details>


### [40] [BiLoG-Net: A Bi-Context Location-Guided Network for Breast Mass Segmentation and Malignancy Classification in Mammography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10188)
*Abu Fatema Mohammad Abdun Noor,Md Imam Ahasan,Md Samiul Ahasan,Kah Ong Michael Goh,S M Hasan Mahmud,Raihana Zannat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BiLoG-Net，一种端到端的多任务深度学习框架，联合执行乳腺X线图像中肿块分割与恶性分类，通过双向上下文位置感知建模和分割引导注意力机制，在CBIS-DDSM和INBreast数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺X线摄影中肿块的精准检测与表征仍面临巨大挑战，原因包括：细微的强度变化、异质性组织密度以及模糊的病变边界，这些因素严重干扰放射科医生的判读。现有方法多为多阶段流水线，存在误差传播问题，亟需一种能同时实现像素级定位与图像级诊断的联合框架。

Method: 提出BiLoG-Net框架：(1) 采用编码器-解码器范式，集成Fire模块进行特征提取；(2) 设计轻量级全局与局部特征增强模块；(3) 引入自适应位置感知门控机制，同时捕获长程上下文依赖和细粒度边界敏感细节；(4) 构建紧耦合的多任务设计，使分割与分类任务相互增强，分割引导的注意力机制实现空间定位的恶性预测。

Result: 在CBIS-DDSM上：Dice 94.20%，分类准确率95.20%，AUC 97.10%；在INBreast上：Dice 93.10%，分类准确率93.60%，AUC 96.00%，显著优于现有CNN和Transformer基线模型。

Conclusion: BiLoG-Net通过单一端到端模型同时实现精确的边界勾画和可靠的恶性评估，在临床计算机辅助检测系统中具有强大潜力，可帮助放射科医生优先处理可疑病例，提升繁忙临床环境中的筛查效率。

Abstract: Breast cancer remains the most commonly diagnosed malignancy among women worldwide, yet accurate detection and characterization of breast masses in mammography remain challenging due to subtle intensity variations, heterogeneous tissue densities, and indistinct lesion boundaries that complicate radiological interpretation. To address these limitations, we propose BiLoG-Net, a deep learning framework that jointly performs breast mass segmentation and malignancy classification through bi-context location-aware feature modeling and segmentation-guided attention mechanisms. Our architecture integrates a novel encoder-decoder paradigm with Fire-based feature extraction, lightweight global and local feature enhancement modules, and adaptive location-aware gating to simultaneously capture long-range contextual dependencies and fine-grained boundary-sensitive details. Unlike conventional multi-stage pipelines, our tightly coupled multi-task design enables mutual reinforcement between pixel-level localization and image-level diagnosis, reducing error propagation while producing spatially grounded malignancy predictions. Evaluated on CBIS-DDSM and INBreast benchmarks, BiLoG-Net achieves state-of-the-art performance with Dice scores of 94.20% and 93.10%, classification accuracies of 95.20% and 93.60%, and AUC values of 97.10% and 96.00%, respectively, substantially outperforming existing CNN and transformer-based baselines. By combining precise boundary delineation with reliable malignancy assessment in a single end-to-end model, this work holds strong potential for clinical computer-aided detection systems, helping radiologists prioritize suspicious cases and improve screening efficiency in busy clinical settings.

</details>


### [41] [ScratNet: A Swin-Based Multi-Scale Dilated Network with Precision Refinement for Semiconductor Scratch Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10214)
*Sachin Ranjan,Hoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对半导体制造中表面划痕缺陷检测难题，提出 ScratNet——一种融合 Swin Transformer 编码器与多阶段解码器的端到端分割框架，通过多尺度扩张聚合、空间细节恢复和边界锐化分支，实现对细薄不规则缺陷的高精度分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造中的表面划痕缺陷具有形状不规则、低对比度、尺度多变等特点，传统检测方法难以可靠检出；基于 CNN 的深度学习方法虽提升了精度，但在细粒度边缘细节捕捉上仍显不足，亟需一种能同时兼顾全局上下文与精细边缘的检测方案。

Method: 提出 ScratNet 端到端分割框架：(1) 采用改进的 Swin Transformer 作为骨干网络提取层次化特征；(2) 设计多尺度扩张聚合模块(MDA)，通过不同扩张率的卷积捕获局部与全局上下文；(3) 引入 Stem 集成模块(SIM)恢复空间细节信息；(4) 构建精度优化分支(PR)，利用各向异性卷积增强边界锐度。整体实现阶段自适应特征聚合与边界感知优化。

Result: 大量实验表明，ScratNet 在细薄和不规则缺陷检测上持续优于现有方法，验证了其在分割精度和鲁棒性方面的优势。

Conclusion: ScratNet 为高精度制造中的自动化划痕检测提供了一种可扩展且鲁棒的解决方案，有效解决了不规则、低对比度划痕的分割难题。

Abstract: Surface scratch defects in semiconductor manufacturing pose significant challenges due to their irregular shapes, low contrast, and varying scales. Traditional inspection methods often struggle to detect such defects reliably, especially in complex imaging scenarios. While deep learning approaches based on Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) have improved accuracy, they often fail to capture fine-grained edge details. To address these limitations, we propose ScratNet, a novel end-to-end scratch segmentation framework that integrates a modified Swin Transformer backbone with a tailored decoder. The decoder incorporates a Multi-Scale Dilated Aggregation (MDA) module to capture both local and global context, a Stem Integration Module (SIM) to restore spatial detail, and a Precision Refinement (PR) branch that enhances boundary sharpness using anisotropic convolutions. Through this stage-adaptive feature aggregation and boundary-aware refinement, ScratNet achieves superior accuracy on thin and irregular defects. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ScratNet consistently outperforms existing methods, providing a scalable and robust solution for automated scratch inspection in high-precision manufacturing.

</details>


### [42] [PhenoEmbed: Self-Supervised Multispectral UAV Time-Series Embeddings for Individual Tree Crown Phenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10231)
*Taimur Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhenoEmbed 是一个自监督的树冠时序嵌入模型，在18期多光谱无人机基准上学习将整季树冠物候变化压缩为256维向量，检索相似度中位数达0.946，为季节性变化下的森林冠层表征学习提供了可复用的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树冠因其光谱响应、内部纹理、半透明性和表观边界在生长季中持续变化，使其成为鲁棒AI的挑战性目标。现有方法缺乏能有效捕捉整季物候动态的树冠级紧凑表征。

Method: 在 HeideBench（18期无人机多光谱时序基准，覆盖 Dölauer Heide 森林）上，以分割树冠多边形为锚点提取时序对齐的冠心裁剪块，使用对比学习与掩码重建双目标训练 PhenoEmbed 模型，将每棵树的季节性冠层外观压缩为256维嵌入向量。

Result: 在5,885棵安全树冠上，嵌入呈现结构化的低维组织，前两个主成分解释25.1%方差，最近邻检索的top-1余弦相似度中位数达0.946。相比手工时序特征和均值池化基线，PhenoEmbed 的最近邻结构显著更紧凑。消融实验表明对比损失、掩码重建损失和显式季节时间特征各自影响嵌入空间结构。

Conclusion: PhenoEmbed 可作为可复用的森林冠层表征学习器，结果支持其有效性，并激励未来在下游任务中测试此类特征能否改善季节性变化下的树级模型性能。

Abstract: Tree crowns are a challenging target for resilient AI because they are not static objects: their spectral response, internal texture, translucency, and apparent boundaries change substantially across the growing season. We develop PhenoEmbed, a self-supervised crown-centric temporal embedding model trained with contrastive and masked reconstruction objectives on HeideBench, an 18-date UAV multispectral time-series benchmark for forest crown phenology in D{ö}lauer Heide. The model treats seasonal crown dynamics as phenological appearance change driven by leaf emergence, canopy closure, senescence, and leaf-off conditions. Segmented tree crown polygons are retained as object anchors to extract aligned crown-centered crops through time, allowing one 256-dimensional vector summarizing seasonal crown appearance to be learned per tree. On 5,885 crop-safe crowns, the exported embeddings show structured low-dimensional organization, with the first two principal components explaining 25.1\% of variance and nearest-neighbor retrieval producing a median top-1 cosine similarity of 0.946. Compared with handcrafted temporal features and a learned mean-pooling baseline, PhenoEmbed yields substantially more compact nearest-neighbor structure, while ablations show that the contrastive loss, masked reconstruction loss, and explicit seasonal time features each affect the structure of the learned embedding space. These results support PhenoEmbed as a reusable forest crown representation learner and motivate future downstream tests of whether such features improve tree-level models under seasonal change.

</details>


### [43] [CoSAG: Compact Semantic Anchor Gaussians via Training-Free Rate-Distortion Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10237)
*Yuang Jia,Jinlong Wang,Junhong Lin,Ruiting Dai,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoSAG 提出将开放词汇 3D 场景理解中的语义场构建与存储解耦，通过闭式传递率加权提升、空间锚点绑定和空间预测熵编码，在无逐场景训练的情况下实现亚兆字节存储，精度匹敌或超越现有方法，体积相比 LangSplatV2 缩小 37–76 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放词汇 3D 场景理解方法（如将 CLIP 嵌入附加到 3D 高斯上）导致单场景存储膨胀至 GB 级别，而现有压缩方案（逐场景编解码器、自编码器、量化码本等）都将场景构建与存储耦合，且从未压缩占总成本大头的逐高斯特征分配。作者认为构建与存储应当解耦，存储本质上是一个针对逐高斯与锚点表绑定的率失真问题。

Method: CoSAG 包含两个解耦阶段：(1) 无训练场景构建：通过闭式传递率加权提升（transmittance-weighted lift）将 2D 多视图特征聚合到 3D，建立空间锚点（spatially grounded semantic anchors），并结合多视图去噪获得干净、视图一致的语义分配；(2) 高效存储：利用空间预测熵编码器（spatially predictive entropy coder）对高斯-锚点绑定进行压缩，无需传输解码器，仅编码绑定相对于空间预测的残差，实现高度可压缩性。

Result: CoSAG 在 2D 渲染、3D 选择和密集 LSeg 等多个协议上达到或超越现有最优方法，同时将存储压缩至亚兆字节级别；相对 LangSplatV2，存储体积缩小 37 到 76 倍，且精度更高。

Conclusion: 将场景构建与存储解耦是解决开放词汇 3D 语义场存储瓶颈的关键，结合空间锚点绑定和预测熵编码可在不牺牲精度的前提下实现超高压缩率，为开放词汇 3D 场景理解的实际部署扫清了存储障碍。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding is commonly achieved by embedding 2D vision-language features such as CLIP into a 3D Gaussian Splatting scene, turning it into a text-queryable semantic field. However, attaching a high-dimensional feature to each of millions of Gaussians inflates a single scene to gigabytes, which makes storage and deployment the real bottleneck of these fields. Existing compact methods each learn and ship a per-scene codec, an autoencoder, a quantized codebook, or a distilled feature field, entangling field construction with field storage and never compressing the per-Gaussian assignment that holds the bulk of the cost. We argue that construction and storage should be decoupled, and that storage is a rate-distortion problem over the per-Gaussian binding to a small anchor table, a structure no prior open-vocabulary method compresses. We present CoSAG, which constructs the field without any per-scene training through a closed-form transmittance-weighted lift, spatially grounded semantic anchors, and multi-view denoising, and stores it with a spatially predictive entropy coder that ships no decoder. Because the anchors are spatially grounded, the binding is predictable and therefore highly compressible. The transmittance-weighted lift and multi-view denoising yield a clean, view-consistent assignment, so the entropy coder spends almost no rate on correcting noise and instead codes only the residual against its spatial prediction. CoSAG reaches sub-megabyte storage while matching or exceeding the state of the art across the 2D-rendered, 3D-selection, and dense-LSeg protocols, reducing field size by 37 to 76x relative to LangSplatV2 at higher accuracy.

</details>


### [44] [Benchmarking Dynamic Affective Reasoning: A Viewer-Centric Video Emotion Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10238)
*Zhiyan Zhang,Peipei Song,Jinpeng Hu,Jingyang Jia,Xun Yang,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DAR（Dynamic Affective Reasoning）基准数据集，从观众视角标注连续视频事件中的情感转换与因果推理链，并设计DAR-R1两阶段框架（SFT+GRPO），在情感定位与情感推理任务上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频情感分析将每个片段视为独立的静态分类单元，忽视了情感随连续因果事件累积而动态变化这一心理学事实，缺乏从观众视角对情感转换和因果推理的建模。

Method: 构建DAR数据集（15,087视频，36,908事件对齐的情感片段，27类情感），定义情感分割、细粒度情感分类和情感推理三项任务；提出DAR-R1框架，结合监督微调（SFT）与Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行两阶段训练。

Result: 在10+多模态大语言模型上的实验表明，DAR-R1在情感定位和情感推理两方面均达到新的最优性能。

Conclusion: DAR填补了动态情感推理的基准空白，DAR-R1为视频情感理解从静态分类走向动态因果推理提供了有效的基线方案。

Abstract: Video emotion analysis is typically framed as a static classification problem, treating each clip as an independent labeled unit. However, such a formulation overlooks a key psychological fact: emotions change as a result of cumulative reactions to consecutive causal events. To bridge this gap, we introduce Dynamic Affective Reasoning, the first large-scale benchmark for viewer-centric affect transitions and causal reasoning over consecutive video events. DAR contains 15,087 videos and 36,908 event-aligned affective segments annotated with 27 emotion categories. Unlike existing video-based emotion datasets, DAR presents a new viewer-centric perspective on fine-grained emotional expressions and transitions, and provides dense, temporally grounded, and causally explicit reasoning chains. Based on DAR, we formally define three challenging tasks: affective segmentation, fine-grained emotion classification, and affective reasoning. Complementing this benchmark, we propose DAR-R1, a two-stage framework that combines supervised fine-tuning with Group Relative Policy Optimization. Experiments across 10+ MLLMs show that DAR-R1 sets a new state-of-the-art for dynamic affective reasoning, in terms of both emotional localization and affective reasoning. Project page: https://github.com/Zhang-Zhiyan/DAR.

</details>


### [45] [What Does Your Short-Answer VQA Score Actually Measure? Evaluator-Dependent Instability in Multimodal Short-Answer Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10240)
*Guanhua Ye,Niu Jingbin,Yan Li,Meiyu Liang,Zhe Xue,Yingxia Shao,Yawen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 短答案VQA基准测试将语义正确性与表面形式匹配混为一谈，导致大量语义正确的答案被错误判错。通过对六种模型和六种基准的3.7万余个官方错误进行人工验证审计，发现在文本密集型基准中高达半数的"错误"实际上是语义可接受的答案，且这一不稳定性受答案类型（抽取式、多段式答案远比标量答案更敏感）的结构性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前短答案视觉问答（VQA）基准测试的自动评估器将"语义正确"与"表面形式匹配"两个不同概念混为一谈，导致模型真实能力被系统性低估。作者旨在系统量化这一混淆的严重程度，揭示其对基准排名可解释性的威胁。

Method: 采用双重审计策略：（1）设计人工验证的语义裁判模型（精确率97.6%），对六种视觉语言模型在六个VQA基准上的37,000余个官方判定错误进行逐一审计；（2）引入纯文本第二裁判模型以排除单一模型偏差；（3）通过良性的提示词改写和上下文改写检验评估稳定性的边界；（4）实施确定性CPU-only合约修复以验证错误判定的可恢复性。同时按答案类型（抽取式、多段式、标量等）分层分析评估器敏感度的结构性差异。

Result: （1）在文本密集型基准上，高达50%的官方错误实际为语义正确的答案，仅因表面形式不匹配而被误判；（2）纯文本裁判复现了相同的基准级假阴性模式，排除单一审计模型偏差；（3）抽取式和多段式答案的评估敏感度远高于标量答案；（4）良性的提示词和上下文改写即可显著翻转题目级正确性判定，在不改变底层任务的情况下改变官方结果；（5）合约修复能部分恢复被低估的正确率。

Conclusion: 官方短答案VQA评分存在系统性偏差，应辅以语义审计和答案类型诊断才能保持可解释性；研究者在使用这些基准排名时应警惕评估器表面形式偏好带来的误导，基准构建方应考虑引入语义级评估机制。

Abstract: Short-answer VQA benchmarks conflate two distinct quantities: whether a model's answer is semantically correct, and whether that answer matches the surface form expected by the automatic evaluator. We study this conflation across six vision--language models and six benchmarks, using a human-validated semantic judge (97.6% precision) to audit over 37k official errors. A second text-only judge reproduces the same benchmark-level false-negative pattern, showing that the effect is not an artifact of a single audit model. On text-rich benchmarks, up to half of these errors are semantically acceptable answers penalized purely for surface-form mismatch. This instability is structured by answer type: extractive and multi-span answers are far more evaluator-sensitive than scalar answers. Benign prompt and context rewrites further destabilize official outcomes, flipping item-level correctness at substantial rates without changing the underlying task. A deterministic CPU-only contract repair confirms that the undercount is partially recoverable. These findings imply that official short-answer VQA scores should be accompanied by semantic audits and answer-type diagnostics to remain interpretable.

</details>


### [46] [Geometry-aware Gaussian Prior and Axial Attention for Cervical Cytology Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10278)
*Yating Li,Cheng Ye,Nenan Lyu,Weidong Chen,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种几何感知的宫颈细胞学图像分类框架，通过从预训练视觉-语言特征中学习语义抽象和结构先验，利用高斯专家模块生成轴向先验并嵌入轴向自注意力机制，在Mendeley和SIPaKMeD数据集上分别达到99.48%和96.08%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫颈细胞学图像分类面临细胞形态复杂、类内差异细微、类间相似性强的挑战。现有卷积模型擅长局部纹理但缺乏长程关系建模能力，注意力模型提供更广上下文但缺乏显式结构引导。需要一种兼顾全局语义与结构先验的方法来提升分类性能。

Method: 提出几何感知分类框架：(1) 从预训练视觉-语言特征中提取语义抽象和结构先验；(2) 使用高斯专家模块从全局语义信息中生成轴向先验，捕获细胞核排列和细胞空间组织等结构规律；(3) 将这些先验嵌入轴向自注意力模块，沿水平和垂直方向调制相似度计算，增强长程依赖建模和结构敏感的特征交互。

Result: 在Mendeley液基细胞学数据集上达到99.48%准确率，在SIPaKMeD数据集上达到96.08%准确率，召回率和精确率均有均衡提升。可视化分析表明学习的先验能够突出诊断相关的细胞区域。

Conclusion: 该几何感知框架通过融合结构先验与自注意力机制，有效提升了宫颈细胞学图像分类性能，具备作为宫颈癌筛查决策支持工具的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate cervical cytology image classification is a key component of automated cervical cancer screening, where reliable recognition of normal, precancerous, and cancer-associated cellular patterns from Pap smear images can improve screening efficiency and diagnostic consistency. However, this task remains challenging because cervical cells exhibit complex morphology, subtle intra-class variations, and strong inter-class similarities. Existing convolution-based models capture local texture well but have limited ability to model long-range relationships, whereas attention-based models provide broader context but often lack explicit structural guidance. To address these limitations, we propose a geometry-aware classification framework for cervical cancer screening-oriented cytology image analysis, incorporating semantic abstraction and structural priors learned from pre-trained vision-language features. The method uses Gaussian expert modules to generate axis-wise priors from global semantic information, capturing structural regularities such as nuclear alignment and cellular spatial organization. These priors are embedded into an axial self-attention module to modulate similarity computation along horizontal and vertical directions, improving long-range dependency modeling and structure-sensitive feature interaction. Experiments on the Mendeley liquid-based cytology and SIPaKMeD datasets show that the proposed method achieves 99.48% accuracy on the former and 96.08% on the latter, with balanced gains in recall, precision, and overall classification performance. Visual analysis further shows that the learned priors highlight diagnostically relevant cellular regions, demonstrating the potential of the proposed framework as a screening-oriented decision-support tool for cervical cytology.

</details>


### [47] [InterPet4D: A Multimodal 4D Human-Pet Interaction Dataset for Pet Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10287)
*Yichen Peng,Jyun-Ting Song,Chen-Chieh Liao,Kris Kitani,Hideki Koike,Erwin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了首个大规模人宠交互多模态数据集InterPet4D（680万帧，13只狗、23位人类参与者），涵盖多视图/第一人称视频、分割、2D/3D关键点、网格和音频等标注；同时提出InterPetMoGen人宠交互动作生成框架，FID达11.21，显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人宠交互估计与生成领域长期缺乏高质量大规模数据集，制约了相关研究发展。现有数据集规模小、模态单一，无法支持逼真的人宠交互建模。

Method: （1）使用同步多视图采集系统录制人-狗服从性任务交互，构建InterPet4D数据集，提供多视图/第一人称视频、分割掩码、2D/3D关键点、三维网格及音频等多模态标注。（2）提出InterPetMoGen框架用于人宠交互动作生成。

Result: InterPetMoGen模型在生成任务上取得FID=11.21，大幅超越Seq2Seq和DiT基线模型，验证了InterPet4D数据集对建模真实人宠交互的有效性。

Conclusion: InterPet4D填补了人宠交互大规模多模态数据集的空白，InterPetMoGen框架证明了该数据集在下游任务中的实用价值，为人宠交互建模研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Human-pet interaction estimation and generation remain underexplored due to the absence of a high-quality large-scale dataset. We present InterPet4D, the first multimodal dataset capturing natural interactions between humans and dogs. Using a synchronized multi-view capture system, we record human-dog obedience tasks and provide annotations for both humans and dogs, including multi-view and egocentric videos, segmentations, 2D and 3D keypoints, meshes, and audio tracks. InterPet4D consists of 6.8 million frames collected from 13 dogs of 11 breeds interacting with 23 human participants. We further introduce the InterPetMoGen framework for human-pet interaction motion generation. Our proposed model achieves an FID score of 11.21 and substantially outperforms the Seq2Seq and DiT baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of InterPet4D for modeling realistic human-pet interactions.

</details>


### [48] [Structured Evidence Selection for Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10298)
*Chenglizhao Chen,Tianxiang Nan,Wen Li,Xinyu Liu,Guisheng Zhang,Mengke Song,Xiaomin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SESAD框架，将弱监督视频异常检测重新定义为对片段级视觉证据的结构化推理过程，通过场景与动作约束下的上下文条件选择机制抑制场景干扰，并引入轻量几何判别模块实现基于嵌入空间相对几何关系的异常判定，在三个基准数据集上取得优异且稳定的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督视频异常检测中，异常行为在表观和时间跨度上变化大，且场景外观与动作动态高度纠缠，导致现有模型倾向于依赖场景相关统计线索而非真正的行为偏差，检测性能不稳定。

Method: 提出结构化证据选择框架SESAD：(1) 将片段表征重组为语义结构化的候选证据，在场景和动作约束下进行上下文条件选择，自适应强调异常相关语义、抑制场景干扰；(2) 引入轻量几何判别模块，在嵌入空间构建双原型结构，通过相对几何关系进行异常决策。

Result: 在UBnormal、ShanghaiTech和UCF-Crime数据集上分别达到67.92、97.99和88.46 AUC，同时保持高计算效率和整体持续稳定的异常判别能力。

Conclusion: SESAD通过结构化证据选择缓解语义纠缠，结合几何判别模块的双原型结构，在弱监督下实现了高精度且稳定的视频异常检测，兼顾了性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Weakly supervised video anomaly detection relies solely on video-level labels for training, making it difficult to accurately localize anomalous events in complex scenes. In real-world videos, anomalous behaviors exhibit large variations in appearance and temporal duration, while scene appearance and action dynamics are often tightly entangled. Consequently, existing models tend to rely on scene-related statistical cues rather than true behavioral deviations, resulting in unstable detection performance. To address this challenge, we propose a Structured Evidence Selection framework (SESAD) that reformulates anomaly detection as a structured reasoning process over clip-level visual evidence. Instead of directly mapping aggregated features to anomaly scores, SESAD reorganizes clip representations into semantically structured candidate evidence and performs context-conditioned selection under scene and action constraints. This mechanism adaptively emphasizes anomaly-relevant semantics while suppressing scene interference, thereby alleviating semantic entanglement under weak supervision. Furthermore, we introduce a lightweight geometric discrimination module that constructs a dual-prototype structure in the embedding space, enabling anomaly decisions through relative geometric relations. Extensive experiments on UBnormal, ShanghaiTech, and UCF-Crime show that SESAD achieves 67.92, 97.99, and 88.46 AUC, respectively, while maintaining high computational efficiency and overall consistently stable anomaly discrimination.

</details>


### [49] [ChartSync: A Benchmark for Visuo-Logical Cascading Chart Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10301)
*Jiakang Yu,Yixuan Chai,Tianci Wang,Rihui Jin,Guangkai Xu,Hongtao Deng,Xun Zhu,Wang Gao,Xinrun Guo,Haipang Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ChartSync基准，系统评估图像编辑模型在统计图表中执行"视觉-逻辑级联编辑"(VLCE)的能力，即修改图表数据时需同步更新几何结构。评估14个模型后发现大多数开源模型在几何同步上严重退化，仅两个前沿闭源模型展现初步VLCE能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式图像编辑模型在处理结构化统计图表时，无法在数据修改时保持几何同步。现有方法局限于局部文本替换，缺乏对依赖感知的级联更新能力，且缺乏系统性的评估基准来量化这一能力差距。

Method: (1) 将图表中的级联编辑任务形式化为Visuo-Logical Cascading Editing (VLCE)；(2) 通过程序化渲染管线构建ChartSync基准，包含870个三元组、9种图表类别、4种任务类型，其中235个为几何耦合的VLCE实例，保证真值的确定性视觉-逻辑耦合；(3) 采用两层评估框架：客观视觉指标 + 视觉语言模型评判范式，同时评估低保真度与多模态理解推理能力。

Result: 评估14个图像编辑模型和1个代码介导管线后发现：大多数开源模型在几何同步上性能严重下降；仅两个前沿闭源模型展现出初步的VLCE能力，其残差错误主要集中在语义隔离和背景损坏；错误分析解构了这些失败范式，识别出核心元能力。

Conclusion: 当前图像编辑模型在需要几何同步的级联图表编辑任务上存在显著能力差距，未来多模态架构需加强依赖感知的级联更新能力。ChartSync数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Generative image editing models struggle with structured statistical charts when data modifications require geometric synchronization. We formalize this task as Visuo-Logical Cascading Editing (VLCE). However, existing methods remain confined to localized text substitutions and struggle with dependency-aware cascading updates. To systematically evaluate this capability, we introduce ChartSync, an expert-validated benchmark constructed via a programmatic rendering pipeline that guarantees deterministic visuo-logical coupling for the ground truth. ChartSync comprises 870 triplets across 9 chart categories and 4 task types, including 235 geometry-coupled VLCE instances that specifically test cascading text-to-geometry synchronization. We further evaluate these instances via a two-tier framework combining objective visual metrics with a vision-language model judge paradigm to assess low-level fidelity alongside multimodal comprehension and reasoning. Evaluating 14 image editing models and one code-mediated pipeline reveals a nuanced capability gap: most open-source models suffer severe drops in geometric synchronization, while only two frontier proprietary models show emerging VLCE capability, with their residual errors mainly involving semantic isolation and background corruption. Our detailed error analysis deconstructs these failure paradigms to identify core meta-abilities for guiding future multimodal architectures. The ChartSync dataset and code are publicly released at https://github.com/kaka-yjk/ChartSyncCodebase.

</details>


### [50] [Generalize LMMs to Versatile Visual Modalities via Fabricated Modality Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10308)
*Shihao Yuan,Yuanze Li,Ruyi Zhang,Ming Liu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VVM-Tuning训练框架，通过模态合成与模态上下文，使大语言多模态模型具备模态无关的语义感知能力和对未见视觉模态的零样本泛化能力，并构建VVM-Bench基准进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言多模态模型(LMMs)在RGB视觉上取得了显著进展，但将其泛化到训练中未见过的视觉模态（如深度图、红外、X光等）仍是一个未被充分探索的挑战。作者认为不同视觉模态本质上是对同一物理世界的不同采样，因此模型需要同时具备模态无关的场景语义感知能力和对模态特定特征的适应能力。

Method: 提出VVM-Tuning训练框架，包含两个核心部分：(1) 模态合成：从RGB场景合成多样化的外观变化图像，训练模型将不变语义与变化的视觉外观解耦，并将这些外观与语言对齐，形成解耦于模态的视觉概念；(2) 模态上下文：在提示中引入模态上下文，通过指令微调辅助模型将这些外观变化映射回模态相关属性，从而在推理时实现对未见模态的零样本适应。同时构建了VVM-Bench基准，包含6种真实和合成模态，用于评估语义感知和模态理解能力。

Result: 通过仅在合成模态上训练，5个被测模型在真实世界模态和新颖合成模态上均表现出了一致的性能提升，且无需进行模态内训练。

Conclusion: VVM-Tuning框架有效赋予LMMs对未见视觉模态的零样本泛化能力，验证了通过模态合成训练实现跨模态语义感知的可行性，为多模态模型的跨模态泛化研究提供了新的思路和基准。

Abstract: Despite the advancements of Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) in RGB vision, their ability to generalize to unseen visual modalities remains a largely unexplored challenge. We argue that different visual modalities are merely distinct samplings of the same physical world. Therefore, effective generalization requires models to possess both modality-agnostic perception of scene semantics and the adaptability to modality-specific characteristics. To achieve this, we propose a training framework, VVM-Tuning, to equip LMMs with these capabilities through modality synthesis and modality contexts. Specifically, we synthesize diverse appearance-varied images from RGB scenes, training the model to disentangle invariant semantics from varying visual appearances, and align these appearances with language for visual concepts decoupled from modalities. We then introduce modality contexts in the prompt and use instruction tuning to assist the model in mapping these appearance variations back to modality-related attributes, enabling zero-shot adaptation to unseen modalities during inference. To facilitate research in this direction, we introduce VVM-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark featuring 6 real and synthetic modalities to evaluate semantic perception and modality understanding. Experiments demonstrate that, via our training on synthetic modalities, 5 tested models exhibit consistent improvements on both real-world and novel synthetic modalities without in-modality training. Source code and data will be publicly available at https://github.com/Hunter-Will/VVM-Tuning.

</details>


### [51] [Imperceptible and Reversible Adversarial Examples against Vision-Language Models for Privacy Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10329)
*Qi Lu,Ziqi Zhou,Yufei Song,Zijing Li,Lulu Xue,Minghui Li,Shengshan Hu,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CloakDiff 提出首个针对 VLM 文本查询攻击的可逆高保真隐私保护框架，结合扩散对抗编辑与可逆网络，在保证图像可无损恢复的同时，实现跨模型、跨提示的强迁移性对抗保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLMs 面临文本查询隐私攻击（攻击者通过在线图片结合自然语言查询提取敏感属性），而现有的可逆对抗样本方法仅适用于纯视觉任务，无法应对多模态场景；同时，当前 VLM 对抗样本依赖高频噪声，严重损害图像视觉质量。因此亟需一种兼顾可逆性、高保真度和多模态隐私保护的新方法。

Method: CloakDiff 包含三大核心组件：(1) 基于扩散模型的对抗编辑，在像素空间嵌入扰动的同时操纵潜在交叉注意力图，确保跨模型和跨提示的迁移性并保留全局视觉结构；(2) 可逆网络模块，将原始图像嵌入对抗样本中以实现无损恢复；(3) EDM 启发式采样策略，设计有原则的扩散调度来引导对抗扰动，进一步提升保真度。

Result: 在多个数据集和 VLM 上的实验表明，CloakDiff 在实现多模态隐私保护的同时，保持了高视觉质量和可逆性，验证了其在对抗文本查询攻击中的有效性。

Conclusion: CloakDiff 首次在 VLM 多模态场景下统一了可逆性、高保真度和强迁移性，为视觉语言模型的隐私保护提供了新的技术范式，具有重要的实用价值和推广前景。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) offer powerful multimodal ability but also expose users to text-based privacy attacks where adversaries crawl online photos and query VLMs to extract sensitive attributes. Existing reversible adversarial example (RAE) methods protect images in purely visual tasks but fail in multimodal settings, and current adversarial examples on VLMs rely on high frequency noise that severely degrades visual quality. We propose CloakDiff, the first framework for reversible, high fidelity privacy protection against text-based query attacks in VLMs. CloakDiff produces imperceptible adversarial examples by combining diffusion based adversarial editing with an invertible network that embeds the original image for lossless recovery. It perturbs both pixel space embeddings and manipulates latent cross attention maps to ensure strong cross-model and cross-prompt transferability while preserving global visual structure. To further enhance fidelity, we design EDM Heuristic Sampling, a principled diffusion schedule for adversarial guidance. Experiments on multiple datasets and VLMs demonstrate that CloakDiff delivers multimodal privacy preservation with high visual quality and reversibility.

</details>


### [52] [GRC-ProbNet: Uncertainty-aware Feature Extraction for Cardiovascular Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10357)
*Yash Shah,Omar Todd,Philipp Seeböck,Georg Langs,Ben Glocker,Raghav Mehta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRC-ProbNet，在GRC-Net基础上引入深度集成生成多个分割掩码，提取不确定性特征用于心血管疾病CT分类，在MM-WHS和ASOCA数据集上AUROC从91.25%提升至92.92%，同时揭示了最佳反映分割质量的不确定性度量未必对下游分类任务最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRC-Net依赖单一确定性分割掩码提取放射组学和几何特征，忽略了心脏解剖结构固有的模糊性（如边界不清、标注不一致），这种不确定性可能在级联流水线中传播并影响下游CVD分类性能。

Method: 提出GRC-ProbNet，采用深度集成（deep ensemble）为同一输入生成多个分割掩码，从中提取多种不确定性特征；系统分析这些不确定性特征与分割误差的相关性，并研究其向下游CVD分类任务的传播效应。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 最能反映分割质量的不确定性度量并非必然为下游CVD分类提供最强信号；(2) GRC-ProbNet利用不确定性特征将CVD分类AUROC提升至92.92%，优于基线GRC-Net的91.25%。

Conclusion: 在混合流水线中引入概率分割和不确定性建模可显著改善下游CVD分类性能，但不确定性度量的选择需针对最终任务而非仅依赖分割精度指标。代码已开源。

Abstract: The automatic detection and classification of cardiovascular disease (CVD) from computed tomography (CT) images plays an important role in clinical practice. Recently, a hybrid pipeline (GRC-Net) for CVD classification was proposed, which leverages a deep-learning-based segmentation and registration method to extract radiomic and geometric features. However, GRC-Net relies on a deterministic segmentation mask, without considering the inherent ambiguity associated with cardiac anatomy. In this paper, we propose GRC-ProbNet, which takes advantage of a deep ensemble to produce multiple segmentation masks for a given input. From these masks, we extract multiple uncertainty features. We analyze these uncertainty features for both their correlation with segmentation error and their propagation effects on downstream CVD classification performance. Our experiments on the publicly available MM-WHS and ASOCA datasets show that the uncertainty measure that best reflects segmentation quality is not necessarily the one that provides the strongest signal for downstream CVD classification. Overall, our results demonstrate that GRC-ProbNet utilizing uncertainty features substantially improves CVD classification AUROC (92.92\) compared to the baseline GRC-Net model (91.25%). Our code is publicly available: https://github.com/biomedia-mira/GRC-ProbNet.

</details>


### [53] [Benchmarking the Robustness of Foundation Models for Mammography under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10358)
*Giang Nguyen,Raghav Mehta,Emma A. M. Stanley,Tian Xia,Thi Hao Nguyen,Hieu Pham,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对15个基础模型在乳腺X线影像上的分布外(OOD)泛化能力进行基准测试，发现乳腺影像专用视觉语言模型(Mammo-FM、MaMA)平均OOD性能最强，但泛化能力不能仅由领域数据暴露量解释；DINOv3作为纯视觉基线仍具竞争力；数据集层面的异质性分析揭示了OOD评估的核心重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型越来越多地被用作乳腺X线影像的特征提取器，但其在外部领域迁移（external domain shift）下的鲁棒性尚不明确。现有研究缺乏对多种backbone在多样化的OOD数据集上系统性的公平比较。

Method: 采用统一的"冻结backbone + 线性探针"协议，在15个基础模型backbone上进行基准测试。涵盖乳腺密度、BI-RADS严重程度和癌症状态三个临床维度；在3个源数据集上训练线性分类器，在12个任务兼容的OOD数据集上进行评估，且评估前进行了标签协调（label harmonization）。还对特征空间进行了可视化分析。

Result: 1）乳腺影像专用视觉语言模型(Mammo-FM、MaMA)平均OOD性能最强；2）但鲁棒性不能仅由"是否接触过乳腺影像数据"来解释，乳腺影像适配的预训练并未一致地提升泛化能力；3）DINOv3作为纯视觉基线模型仍具竞争力；4）即使是领先模型，在不同数据集上也表现出异质性的性能；5）特征空间检查显示，有用的表征可以在保留临床信号的同时保留数据集和采集来源的结构。

Conclusion: 数据集级别的OOD评估应作为评估乳腺X线影像表征质量的核心标准。单纯依赖领域内微调或领域数据暴露不足以确保泛化鲁棒性，需要在模型选择和评估策略上进行更审慎的设计。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly used as image feature extractors for mammography, but their robustness under external domain shift remains unclear. We benchmark 15 foundation-model backbones across breast density, BI-RADS severity, and cancer status using a unified frozen-backbone linear-probe protocol, training on 3 source datasets and evaluating on 12 task-compatible out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets after label harmonization. Mammography-specific vision-language models (Mammo-FM and MaMA) provide the strongest mean OOD performance, but robustness is not explained by mammography exposure alone. DINOv3 remains a competitive vision-only baseline, and mammography-adapted pretraining does not consistently improve generalization. Dataset-level analysis further shows that even leading models show heterogeneous performance across datasets. Feature-space inspection reveals that useful representations can preserve clinical signal while retaining dataset and acquisition structure. These findings highlight dataset-level OOD evaluation as a central criterion for assessing mammography representations. Our code is publicly available: https://github.com/biomedia-mira/mammo-ood.

</details>


### [54] [Gradient-Skipping Relevance Propagation for Efficient Explainability of Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10365)
*Christopher Buratti,Michele Marchetti,Federica Parlapiano,Davide Traini,Domenico Ursino,Luca Virgili*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GradSkip 是一种面向 Vision Transformer 的新型相关性传播方法，通过自适应注意力头加权和跳跃连接感知的传播机制，解决了现有方法忽略注意力头重要性差异及残差连接建模不准确的问题，在保真度和计算效率上均达 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ViT 可解释性方法存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 假设所有注意力头重要性均等，忽略了不同头对输出的贡献差异；(2) 将残差连接简单建模为恒等路径，导致相关性归因不准确。这促使作者提出更精细的传播机制。

Method: 提出 GradSkip 方法，包含两大核心设计：(1) 自适应头加权——根据梯度信息动态评估每个注意力头的重要性，差异化分配相关性；(2) 跳跃连接感知传播——显式建模残差路径与注意力路径之间的相关性分配，避免恒等假设带来的归因偏差。

Result: 在 ImageNet1K 和 BloodMNIST 上，GradSkip 实现了 SOTA 的保真度（faithfulness），同时计算量（GFLOPs）比最优现有方法减少 14 倍以上。在基于 Transformer 的分割任务中，进一步验证了其定位精度和与真值区域对齐能力的提升。

Conclusion: GradSkip 通过自适应头加权和跳跃连接感知传播，显著提升了 ViT 相关性归因的准确性与计算效率，为 ViT 可解释性提供了更可靠且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are difficult to interpret because current methods of relevance propagation and attention flow do not fully consider some key architectural features, such as the uneven importance of attention heads and residual connections. Prior approaches typically assume uniform importance across attention heads; furthermore, they model skip connections as identity paths, leading to inaccurate relevance attribution. To address these issues, we introduce GradSkip, a novel relevance propagation method for ViTs based on adaptive head weighting and skip-aware propagation. GradSkip models the different importance of the attention heads and dynamically distributes relevance between the attention and residual paths. Experiments on ImageNet1K and BloodMNIST demonstrate a state-of-the-art faithfulness of GradSkip while requiring over 14 times fewer GFLOPs than the best-performing existing approaches. Additional evaluations using transformer-based segmentation confirm improved localization and alignment with ground-truth regions.

</details>


### [55] [Neural Motion Blending Across Arbitrary Character Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10370)
*Luca Cazzola,Giulia Martinelli,Nicola Conci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于语义编码器和扩散解码器的框架，通过在高维潜空间中对异构骨骼的运动状态进行插值，实现跨不同骨骼拓扑结构的运动混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动混合方法通常局限于固定或高度相似的骨骼拓扑结构，无法处理不同骨骼结构间的混合，限制了其在多样化角色动画中的应用。

Method: 框架由两部分组成：(1) 语义编码器，从每帧运动中提取与骨骼无关的潜空间表征；(2) 基于扩散模型的解码器，以该潜码为条件重建特定角色的运动。推理时，通过对两个输入运动的潜表征进行插值获得混合运动。

Result: 在 Truebones Zoo 数据集上，针对相同和不同骨骼拓扑的运动进行了训练和评估，在多种场景下均实现了平滑且合理的运动混合效果。

Conclusion: 该方法成功突破了骨骼拓扑结构的限制，实现了跨异构骨骼的运动混合，为角色动画中的运动合成提供了更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Motion blending in character animation enables the synthesis of new motions by interpolating between existing examples. Current methods are typically restricted to fixed skeleton topologies, requiring identical or near-identical skeletal structures across characters. We present a novel framework for motion blending across heterogeneous skeletons. The proposed architecture combines a semantic encoder, which extracts per-frame latent representations of the motion state, with a diffusion-based decoder, which reconstructs character-specific motion conditioned on this latent code. At inference, blended motions are obtained by interpolating the latent representations of two input motions. We train and evaluate the method on the Truebones Zoo dataset using motions defined on both same and distinct skeleton topologies, demonstrating the ability to achieve smooth and plausible blending in a variety of scenarios.

</details>


### [56] [ABot-N1: Toward a General Visual Language Navigation Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10383)
*Ruiyan Gong,Yingnan Guo,Junjun Hu,Jintao Kong,Xiaoxu Leng,Tianlun Li,Weize Li,Fei Liu,Zhicheng Liu,Jia Lu,Minghua Luo,Chenlin Ming,Yanfen Shen,Jiyue Tao,Zhengbo Wang,Mingyang Yin,Minqi Gu,Zihao Guan,Wei Guo,Guoqing Liu,Huachong Pang,Menglin Yang,Zeqian Ye,Xiaoxiao Geng,Zhining Gu,Honglin Han,Di Jing,Hongyu Pan,Mingchao Sun,Kuan Yang,Jianfang Zhang,Yanghong Chen,Ye He,Wei Mei,Jiahao Shi,Xiangpo Yang,Yanqing Zhu,Zedong Chu,Xiaolong Wu,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ABot-N1 提出了一种慢-快解耦架构的视觉语言导航基础模型，通过将认知推理与运动控制分离，并利用像素级锚点作为通用接口，在多种导航任务上取得了SOTA性能，尤其在城市级导航中实现了大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航模型通常采用从观测直接映射到动作的单一策略，存在坐标漂移、长尾语义处理不佳、缺乏可解释性等问题，难以同时兼顾通用性、鲁棒性和透明度。

Method: 提出ABot-N1，采用慢-快解耦架构：慢通道的视觉语言推理器执行显式思维链推理并生成像素目标（图像空间锚点）作为通用任务接口；快通道的动作专家利用文本线索和像素引导，以原生控制频率生成连续航路点。该架构通过像素锚点与显式语言轨迹桥接高层意图与低层控制。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界基准上均取得SOTA：城市级POI到达率提升35.0%至77.3%；复杂室内外场景SR达95.4%/92.9%；在物体到达、行人跟随、指令跟随等任务上保持优越鲁棒性。同时开源了新的Point-Goal/POI-Goal基准。

Conclusion: 通过认知-控制解耦和像素锚点+语言轨迹的双重引导，ABot-N1实现了鲁棒、可泛化且可解释的导航能力，为通用视觉语言导航基础模型迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Visual Language Navigation foundation models aim to unify deep reasoning for grounded spatial decisions with broad versatility for diverse embodied tasks. Current approaches typically achieve this integration via monolithic policies that map observations directly to actions, yet they often suffer from coordinate drift and poor handling of long-tail semantics. Furthermore, these black-box mappings lack interpretability, hindering the simultaneous achievement of generality, robustness, and transparency. We present ABot-N1, a step toward a general Visual Language Navigation foundation model, that addresses these challenges by decoupling cognition from control via a slow-fast architecture guided by dual visual-language signals. More specifically, a slow vision-language reasoner performs explicit Chain-of-Thought reasoning while producing a pixel goal. This compact set of image-space anchor points serves as a universal interface for diverse tasks, including point-goal, object-goal, poi-goal, instruction-following, and person-following. Subsequently, a fast action expert leverages both the textual cues and the pixel guidance to generate continuous waypoints at the native control frequency. By bridging high-level intents and low-level control through pixel-grounded anchors paired with explicit linguistic traces, our approach ensures robust, generalizable, and interpretable navigation across simulation and real-world benchmarks. ABot-N1 establishes new state-of-the-art records, delivering massive gains specifically in urban-scale navigation: boosting POI arrival by 35.0% (to 77.3%) and achieving 95.4%/92.9% SR in complex indoor and outdoor scenes. It also maintains superior robustness across object-reaching, person-following, and instruction-following tasks. New Point-Goal/POI-Goal benchmarks are released as open source to advance the field of urban-scale navigation.

</details>


### [57] [Vertical Fusion: Condensing Internal Representations for Robust ViT Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10391)
*Francesco Di Salvo,Shyam Nandan Rai,Hamed Damirchi,Ignacio Meza De la Jara,Sebastian Doerrich,Marco Lents,Christian Ledig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"可恢复性"概念，发现ViT中间层可修正最后一层的分类错误（18%-76%），并提出VFusion——一种纵向聚合策略，通过可学习的低维潜在空间融合ViT内部层级特征，显著超越传统横向集成方法，在ID和OOD设置中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ViT几乎仅被用作黑盒特征提取器，只使用最后一层表征，完全忽略了中间层蕴含的丰富信息。作者质疑这一惯例，试图挖掘中间层表征的"可恢复性"——即中间层纠正最后一层分类错误的能力。

Method: 1. 在16个数据集上对ViT各深度层训练独立分类探针，量化中间层对最后一层误分类样本的纠正能力；2. 提出VFusion：一种纵向特征聚合策略，通过可学习映射将所有中间层特征投影到低维潜在空间进行融合；3. 与横向集成（多模型）方法及多种聚合基线进行全面对比。

Result: 1. 中间层探针可正确分类18%-76%的最后一层误分类样本；2. 这种增益主要来自"冗余-正确性对应"而非预测多样性——内部层级充当共享判别信号的稳定冗余探针系列；3. VFusion显著超越既有聚合基线，弥补了最佳单层与理论oracle性能差距的45%；4. 该增益在不同模型规模和预训练方案上一致泛化。

Conclusion: ViT中间层包含未被充分利用的判别信息，其纵向聚合可以高效提升性能，VFusion为横向集成方法提供了鲁棒且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Despite exposing rich intermediate representations, Vision Transformers (ViTs) are almost exclusively utilized as black-box feature extractors, where only the last layer is considered for downstream tasks. We challenge this convention by introducing the notion of recoverability: the capacity of intermediate representations to correct last-layer failures. By evaluating independent classification probes at every model depth across 16 datasets, we observe that intermediate probes correctly classify 18% to 76% of samples that the last-layer probe misclassifies. We show that these gains are not primarily driven by predictive diversity, but by a redundancy-correctness correspondence, where the internal hierarchy acts as a series of stable, redundant probes of a shared discriminative signal. While established horizontal ensemble strategies (i.e., across multiple models) can improve performance, they incur high computational cost and ignore this vertical signal within a single model. To bridge this gap, we propose VFusion, a principled vertical aggregation strategy employing a learnable mapping into a low-dimensional latent space that synthesizes features across the internal ViT hierarchy. VFusion substantially outperforms established aggregation baselines in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, notably closing 45% of the accuracy gap between the best individual layer and a theoretical oracle performance. Our gains consistently generalize across model sizes and pre-training regimes, confirming that VFusion offers a robust and efficient alternative to horizontal ensemble methods. The code is available at https://github.com/francescodisalvo05/vit-vertical-fusion.

</details>


### [58] [Self-supervised Automatic Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10395)
*Xiaonan Hu,Zhiyuan Lu,Jingdong Zhao,Hao Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SSMatte 是一个自监督图像抠图框架，首次仅使用 RGB 图像（无需任何人工标注）即达到了与全监督自动抠图相当的性能。其核心思路是将问题分解为语义锚定和细节抠图两个阶段，通过语义锚定损失和不动点损失联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量 alpha 遮罩标注成本极高，现有方法即便使用 trimap 或 mask 等粗粒度标签，仍依赖逐像素监督，限制了可扩展性和泛化能力。本文旨在探索完全无需人工标注、仅用裸 RGB 图像训练自动抠图模型的可能性。

Method: SSMatte 将抠图分解为两步：(1) 语义锚定：利用冻结的自监督 ViT 特征，通过基于广义 Rayleigh 商的语义锚定损失传播 class-token 种子，生成语义抠图提示（prompt）；(2) 细节抠图：以该提示为引导，通过不动点损失（fixed-point-based loss）强制 alpha 与 RGB 一致性来优化细节抠图网络。

Result: 在肖像基准上，SSMatte 超越以往弱监督方法，匹配全监督模型的性能，并在额外数据上展现出良好的缩放与泛化行为。

Conclusion: SSMatte 将自动抠图推向了一个全新的、完全无需标注的范式，首次证明纯自监督学习可在抠图任务上达到全监督水平。

Abstract: High-quality alpha mattes are notoriously expensive to annotate, creating a fundamental data bottleneck for deep image matting. While prior work attempts to reduce annotation cost using coarser labels like trimaps or masks, they remain reliant on costly per-pixel supervision, limiting scalability and generalization. In this work, we push the boundary further and ask: can we train an automatic matting model using only RGB images, with no manual annotation at all? We answer this by presenting SSMatte, a self-supervised framework that for the first time achieves performance on par with fully-supervised automatic matting. Our key insight is to decompose the problem into semantic anchoring and detail matting. SSMatte first generates a semantic matting prompt from frozen self-supervised ViT features by propagating class-token seeds via a novel, training-efficient semantic anchoring loss based on a generalized Rayleigh quotient. This prompt then anchors a detail matting network, which is optimized via a fixed-point-based loss that enforces alpha-RGB consistency. Extensive experiments show SSMatte outperforms prior weakly-supervised methods, matches the performance of fully-supervised models on portrait benchmarks, and demonstrates favorable scaling and generalization behaviors with additional data. Our work pushes automatic matting to an fresh, fully annotation-free paradigm. Code will be available.

</details>


### [59] [SynthDocBench: Controlled Benchmark for Long-Context Visual Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10400)
*Abhigya Verma,Khyati Mahajan,Amit Kumar Saha,Shruthan Radhakrishna,Sagar Davasam,Vikas Yadav,Sai Rajeswar Mudumba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SynthDocBench，一个完全合成的长上下文视觉文档理解基准，通过组合设计系统控制文档长度、布局、模态和问题类型，揭示了七个前沿 VLM 在长文档上的三种新失败模式：长度退化、中间段落位置敏感性、长文档中图表理解崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档理解基准（如 DocVQA、ChartQA）中的真实文档混杂了长度、布局、模态和问题难度等多种因素，导致难以将模型失败归因于具体原因。需要一种能系统性控制各因素、进行消融分析的基准。

Method: (1) 采用组合设计，独立变化文档长度、布局结构、模态组成和问题类型四个因素；(2) 使用 LLM 流水线端到端生成六种布局原型的文档；(3) 引入 40% 随机覆盖（random override）防止模型利用虚假相关性；(4) 文档长度和结构多样性远超现有基准。

Result: 评估七个前沿 VLM 后发现三种现有基准无法揭示的失败模式：(a) 性能随文档长度急剧退化；(b) 系统性位置敏感性——六款模型中有五款在文档中间三分之一段落表现最差，五款模型呈负向 Early-to-Late 趋势（最大降幅 8.3 个百分点）；(c) 长文档场景下图表理解能力崩溃。

Conclusion: 当前模型可能过度拟合了现有基准的 artifacts，而非真正具备鲁棒的长上下文视觉文档理解能力。SynthDocBench 为诊断和改进 VLM 提供了更精细的受控分析工具。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on visual document understanding benchmarks such as DocVQA, ChartQA, and MMLongBench-Doc. However, real-world documents combine multiple factors such as length, layout complexity, modality, and question difficulty, which makes it difficult to attribute model failures to specific causes. We introduce SynthDocBench, a fully synthetic benchmark for long-context visual document understanding that systematically controls factors including document length, layout structure, modality composition, and question type. The benchmark is constructed using a combinatorial design, each factor is varied independently across generated documents, enabling controlled analysis of model behavior. Documents are generated end to end using an LLM pipeline across six layout archetypes, with a 40 percent random override to prevent models from exploiting spurious correlations. Additionally, SynthDocBench spans long-context documents with substantially greater length and structural diversity than existing benchmarks. Evaluating seven frontier VLMs, we uncover three failure modes that existing benchmarks cannot surface: sharp degradation with document length, a systematic positional sensitivity in which the middle third of a document is hardest for five of six models and five of six models show a negative Early-to-Late trend (steepest decline: 8.3 percentage points), and breakdown of chart comprehension in long-document settings. These results suggest that current models may be overfitting to benchmark artifacts rather than achieving robust long-context visual document understanding.

</details>


### [60] [TVT-PAPD: Pathology-Aware Prototype Distillation for Self-Supervised Whole Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10406)
*Ramesh Naidu Laveti,Jaya Sreevalsan-Nair,T K Srikanth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督病理表示学习框架 TVT-PAPD，将微型视觉Transformer与病理感知原型蒸馏模块相结合，通过可学习的病理原型库捕捉组织形态模式，在脑胶质瘤分类任务中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法主要学习通用视觉特征，未能显式捕获对疾病表征至关重要的病理特异性形态模式。为此，需要一种能发现并保留代表性组织形态模式、使语义相似的病理区域学习到一致且具有判别性表示的方法。

Method: 提出 TVT-PAPD 框架：(1) 采用 Tiny Vision Transformer (TVT) 作为骨干网络，参数量仅 90M，保持计算效率；(2) 引入 Pathology-Aware Prototype Distillation (PAPD) 模块，构建可学习的病理原型库（prototype bank），用于发现并保留代表性组织形态模式，引导语义相似的病理区域学习一致且具有判别性的表示。

Result: 在 TCGA LGG/GBM 数据集和 IPD-Brain 数据集上，TVT-PAPD 分别取得了 93.02% 和 90.23% 的加权 F1-score，并在跨队列的独立胶质瘤数据集上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: TVT-PAPD 能够有效增强病理感知的特征学习，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升胶质瘤分类性能，且具备良好的跨队列泛化能力，证明了病理特异性形态模式建模对于自监督病理图像分析的重要性。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as an effective paradigm for learning transferable representations from large-scale unlabeled whole slide images (WSIs). However, existing SSL methods primarily learn generic visual features and often fail to explicitly capture pathology-specific morphological patterns that are critical for disease characterization. To address this limitation, we propose Tiny Vision Transformer with Pathology-Aware Prototype Distillation (TVT-PAPD). This self-supervised pathology representation learning framework integrates a Tiny Vision Transformer (TVT) with a novel Pathology-Aware Prototype Distillation (PAPD) module. PAPD employs a learnable pathology prototype bank to discover and preserve representative tissue morphology patterns, encouraging semantically similar pathological regions to learn consistent and discriminative representations. The proposed framework enhances pathology-aware feature learning while maintaining computational efficiency with 90M parameters. Experiments on the Cancer Genome Atlas (TCGA) low-grade glioma (LGG)/glioblastoma (GBM) dataset and the Indian Pathology Brain (IPD-Brain) dataset demonstrate that TVT-PAPD achieves weighted F1-scores of 93.02% and 90.23%, respectively, for LGG-GBM classification, while exhibiting strong cross-cohort generalization across independent glioma datasets.

</details>


### [61] [GNOCHI: Generative Neural mOdel for Close Human-Human Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10408)
*Gonzalo Gómez-Nogales,Marc Comino-Trinidad,Andrés Casado-Elvira,Dan Casas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件变分自编码器(cVAE)的生成模型，用于合成逼真的近距离3D人体交互姿态，并通过自动数据增强和基于碰撞解决的自监督损失，解决了数据稀缺和碰撞感知两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在生成近距离3D人体交互方面存在显著不足：传统方法（运动捕捉、3D重建）缺乏生成能力，而最近的生成方法（文本/图像驱动的运动合成）难以精确建模近距离交互中的物理约束，尤其是避免身体碰撞。

Method: 提出基于cVAE的生成模型，以一人姿态为条件生成另一人的交互姿态。针对数据稀缺问题，设计自动化有监督数据增强策略来合成逼真的交互姿态；针对碰撞感知问题，提出基于体积代理（volumetric proxies）碰撞解决技术的自监督损失函数，确保物理正确的交互。

Result: 模型能够生成当前最先进方法无法实现的、多样化且物理正确的近距离交互姿态，在合理性和物理正确性方面均表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 该模型成功解决了近距离3D人体交互生成中的数据稀缺和碰撞感知两大核心挑战，为虚拟环境中逼真的人与人交互合成提供了有效的生成方案。

Abstract: Creating realistic 3D human-human interactions in virtual environments is challenging due to the high degrees of freedom in the human body and the need for physically accurate poses that do not collide with each other. Traditional methods for human-human interaction are based on motion tracking or 3D body reconstruction, but lack generative capabilities. Recent generative methods enable the synthesis of individual or interacting motions via text or image input, but generally fall short in modeling close interactions. This paper introduces a novel generative model for close 3D human-human interactions using a conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE), which generates poses for one human conditioned on the pose of another, allowing for controlled and diverse interaction synthesis. To train our model, we address two underlying long-standing challenges in the field of human-human interaction: data scarcity, for which we propose an automated supervised data augmentation strategy that generates synthetic yet realistic interaction poses; and collision awareness in generative approaches, for which we propose a self-supervised loss based on a collision resolution technique using volumetric proxies to ensure physically correct interactions. We extensively evaluate the capabilities of our model, and demonstrate a wide variety of plausible and physically correct interactions, not possible to generate with current state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [62] [SPORT: Structure-Aware Prototype Disentanglement for Incomplete Multi-View Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10413)
*Yaoyuan Guo,Zhibin Gu,Songhe Feng,Yuhui Zheng,Bing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPORT 提出了一种结构感知的原型解耦框架，通过将原型显式分离为共享和视图特定分量，并引入结构感知对比学习和混合填补策略，解决了不完整多视图聚类中跨视图一致性与视图特异性保留、聚类级结构建模以及缺失视图准确恢复三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于原型的不完整多视图聚类方法存在三个核心局限：(1) 仅强制跨视图原型一致性，忽略原型中的视图特定信息，限制了多视图表达能力；(2) 依赖实例级对比学习，只对齐成对样本，无法保留聚类级别的关系结构；(3) 缺失视图填补仅使用全局原型，忽略局部几何邻域结构，导致缺失表示恢复不准确。

Method: SPORT 框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 原型解耦——将原型正交分解为共享分量和视图特定分量，仅对齐共享分量以捕获共识语义，同时去相关视图特定分量以保留互补信息；(2) 结构感知对比学习——在跨视图表示学习中显式建模聚类级关系；(3) 混合填补策略——融合全局原型匹配与局部邻域匹配，联合利用语义原型和流形结构进行缺失视图恢复。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SPORT 在不同缺失率下均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SPORT 通过结构感知的原型解耦机制，有效解决了不完整多视图聚类的核心挑战，在多个基准上取得了优越性能。

Abstract: Prototype-based Incomplete Multi-view Clustering has recently attracted increasing attention by exploiting prototypes as semantic anchors for missing-view imputation. However, existing approaches are still limited in three aspects. First, they typically focus on enforcing cross-view prototype consistency, while ignoring view-specific information embedded in prototypes, thus limiting multi-view expressiveness. Second, most methods rely on instance-level contrastive learning that only aligns paired samples across views, failing to preserve cluster-level relational structures. Third, missing-view imputation is usually performed using global prototypes alone, without considering local geometric neighborhood structures, leading to inaccurate recovery of missing representations. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework termed Structure-aware PrOtotype disentanglement foR incomplete multi-view clusTering (SPORT), which explicitly disentangles shared and view-specific components of prototypes while preserving cluster-level relational structures. Specifically, we decouple prototypes into orthogonal shared and view-specific components, aligning only shared components to capture consensus semantics while de-correlating view-specific components to preserve complementary information. Meanwhile, a structure-aware contrastive learning mechanism is incorporated to explicitly model cluster-level relationships during cross-view representation learning. Furthermore, a hybrid imputation strategy integrates global prototype matching with local neighborhood matching, enabling joint exploitation of semantic prototypes and manifold structures for missing-view recovery. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets show that SPORT achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art methods under various missing rates.

</details>


### [63] [BOCCHI: A More Realistic and Challenging Benchmark for Local Motion Blur Detection with MSDCT-UNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10427)
*Kuan-Lin Chen,Yuan-Kang Lee,Cheng-Yuan Chiang,Jian-Jiun Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了BOCCHI真实模糊检测基准和MSDCT-UNet模型，前者通过模糊-清晰区域梯度分布重叠消除捷径依赖，后者利用多尺度DCT先验注入频域感知，在域内和跨域迁移上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部运动模糊检测基准存在梯度分布偏差，使得模型可依赖简单的梯度捷径（如清晰区域边缘梯度强、模糊区域弱）进行判别，导致模型泛化能力差、无法迁移。需要构建一个真实拍摄的、消除此类捷径的基准，并设计不依赖梯度捷径的频率感知模型。

Method: (1) 构建BOCCHI数据集：真实多相机拍摄、人工标注的模糊物体图像，确保模糊区域与清晰区域的梯度分布重叠，打消梯度捷径。(2) 提出MSDCT-UNet：基于UNet的编码器-解码器架构，通过DCT Attention模块和FiLM（特征线性调制）将多尺度离散余弦变换先验注入网络，使模型从频域角度感知模糊。

Result: MSDCT-UNet在BOCCHI数据集上的域内mIoU和边界定位精度排名第一。仅使用633张训练图像，在BOCCHI上训练的模型在跨数据集迁移任务中优于所有其他训练源。

Conclusion: 消除梯度捷径对泛化性至关重要，BOCCHI提供了更真实的评测平台；MSDCT-UNet通过频域先验注入有效捕获运动模糊特征，小样本即可实现优异的域内和跨域性能。

Abstract: Local motion blur detection requires pixel-level localization of blurred regions. Existing benchmarks let models rely on gradient shortcuts that fail to transfer. We introduce BOCCHI (Blurred Objects Captured across Cameras with Human-annotated Imagery), a real-captured benchmark whose sharp regions overlap the blur gradient distribution and defeat these shortcuts, and propose MSDCT-UNet (Multi-Scale Discrete Cosine Transform UNet), a frequency-aware encoder-decoder injecting multi-scale DCT priors through DCT Attention and FiLM. MSDCT-UNet ranks first in in-domain mIoU and boundary localization on BOCCHI, and BOCCHI-trained models outperform every other training source on cross-dataset transfer with only 633 training images.

</details>


### [64] [Annotation-Free Furniture Codes: What They Encode, and How Far They Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10461)
*Benjamin Friedman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究能否用单个自监督的几何token同时替代3D场景合成中使用的类别标签和规范姿态两个人工标注通道，并通过FSQ点云自编码器在无标注数据上验证了该表征的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统layout-based 3D场景合成方法依赖两个人工标注通道（类别标签和规范姿态），标注成本高且难以规模化。作者探索能否从物体几何本身提取一个统一的自监督token来替代两者，降低对人工标注的依赖。

Method: 采用有限标量量化（FSQ）点云自编码器，以Chamfer距离为训练目标，在3D-FUTURE家具数据集上进行无标签、无姿态标注的自监督训练。通过诊断探针（diagnostic probes）从学习到的编码中恢复细粒度类别、粗粒度类别和偏航角信息。进一步通过交换Chamfer目标（从旋转点云换成未旋转点云）来验证编码中旋转信息的可控性，并在ShapeNet上进行跨资产库的迁移实验。

Result: 诊断探针从编码中恢复细粒度类别准确率62.6±0.5%，粗粒度类别85.6±1.3%，偏航角误差52.7±0.5度。交换Chamfer目标后，偏航信号消失而类别恢复率上升，表明旋转内容可通过训练目标控制。ShapeNet跨库迁移显示：盒状家具可迁移，有机形状家具不可迁移，目标盲增广可部分缩小差距。

Conclusion: 单个自监督几何token能够同时编码类别和姿态信息，其旋转内容可通过训练目标显式控制。但跨资产库的迁移存在形状依赖性，盒状结构迁移良好而有机形状较差，暗示该表征的泛化能力仍有局限。

Abstract: Layout-based 3D scene synthesizers place each object using two human-annotated channels: a categorical class label and a canonical-pose convention. We ask whether a single self-supervised token derived from object geometry can replace both, and study such tokens directly as a representation, decoupled from any synthesizer. A Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ) point-cloud autoencoder is chamfer-trained on placed 3D-FUTURE furniture with no labels or pose annotations. Diagnostic probes recover fine-category (62.6 +/- 0.5%), super-category (85.6 +/- 1.3%), and yaw (52.7 +/- 0.5 deg) from the codes alone. Swapping the chamfer target from the rotated to the un-rotated point cloud collapses the yaw signal while raising class recovery, showing the codes' rotation content can be set by the training objective. Scaling across asset libraries needs codes that transfer; on an unseen dataset (ShapeNet), alignment is category-dependent: box-like furniture transfers, organically-shaped furniture does not, and a target-blind augmentation partly closes the gap.

</details>


### [65] [Not All Color Categories Are Equally Stable: A Multilingual Free Color Naming Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10465)
*Nuray Toganas,Adilet Yerkin,Elnara Kadyrgali,Muragul Muratbekova,Aron Karatayev,Pakizar Shamoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过自由颜色命名实验，考察了红、黄、绿三类颜色在不同饱和度与亮度下的命名一致性。92名参与者使用哈萨克语、俄语或英语为18个颜色样本自由命名。结果表明，绿色命名一致性最高，红色次之，黄色最易产生多样化命名（如金、棕等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颜色命名是人类颜色感知的重要环节，但颜色类别之间的边界往往模糊不清，同一颜色在不同饱和度和亮度下可能被赋予不同名称。目前尚不清楚哪些颜色类别在视觉变化下仍能保持命名一致性，哪些则容易发生偏移。该研究旨在系统量化红、黄、绿三类颜色的命名一致性，揭示不同颜色类别在感知空间中的稳健性差异。

Method: 从COLIBRI数据集中选取18个颜色样本，涵盖红、黄、绿三类颜色的不同饱和度与亮度变体。招募92名参与者，以自由命名范式（不限定预定义类别）分别使用哈萨克语、俄语或英语为每个样本赋予颜色名称。通过分析各颜色类别内部命名的一致性程度，比较三类颜色在视觉外观变化下的命名稳定性。

Result: （1）绿色：尽管外观变化，仍被高度一致地识别为"绿色"，命名一致性最高；（2）黄色：命名多样性最大，除"黄色"外还出现大量金色、棕色等相关描述；（3）红色：命名一致性居中。表明某些颜色类别在感知空间中占据更广区域，对视觉变化更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 不同颜色类别的命名一致性存在显著差异：绿色在感知上最为稳健，黄色最易受饱和度和亮度变化影响而产生命名漂移。该发现可用于开发更具感知意义的颜色模型和颜色命名系统。

Abstract: Color naming is an important part of human color perception. Its task is to allow people to describe continuous colors using discrete color categories. However, the boundaries between color categories are often unclear, and some colors may be perceived differently depending on their saturation and brightness. While certain color categories remain recognizable across a wide range of shades, others may be associated with different color names when their appearance changes. This study investigates the consistency of color naming for red, yellow, and green color categories using a free color-naming experiment. A set of 18 color samples was selected from the COLIBRI dataset to represent different shades of these colors. Participants (n = 92) were asked to freely assign color names to each sample in Kazakh, Russian, or English without being limited to predefined categories. The results show that color categories differ in their consistency. Green shades were consistently identified as green despite variations in appearance, whereas yellow shades received a wider variety of names, including gold- and brown-related descriptions. Red shades showed moderate naming consistency. Our findings suggest that some color categories occupy broader perceptual regions than others and may therefore be more robust to visual variations. The study results can be used to develop perceptually meaningful color models and color naming systems.

</details>


### [66] [On the Real-World Generalisability of Optical Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10470)
*Petter Reijalt,Sander Gielisse,Rickard Karlsson,Jan van Gemert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究指出现有光流模型在合成基准上的表现无法有效预测真实世界性能，为此构建了包含 FlowFactor 在内的真实世界评估基准，揭示了光照变化和大位移是影响泛化的关键因素，并发现单纯扩大训练数据规模无法弥合合成与真实场景之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光流估计领域长期依赖合成数据（如 Sintel、KITTI、Spring）进行训练和评估，但真实场景的真值获取困难，导致模型在真实世界视频上的泛化能力缺乏系统性检验。作者质疑合成基准的准确率是否真正能代理真实世界光流性能。

Method: 构建了一个包含 8,204 帧对的真实世界评估基准，由 TAP-Flow、Slow Flow 和自建的 FlowFactor 三部分组成。FlowFactor 包含 1,000 帧高清人工标注的帧对，按四个混淆因素（大位移、重复纹理、遮挡、光照变化）组织，每个设置仅控制单一变量，支持诊断性分析。使用标准检查点评估了大量近期光流模型。

Result: （1）光照变化和大位移场景下的表现与真实世界整体准确率相关性最强；（2）在大运动区域上的改进可能以牺牲小运动、静止场景的鲁棒性为代价；（3）Sintel、KITTI、Spring 上的进展仅能微弱预测真实世界精度；（4）扩大训练数据规模并不能必然弥合合成与真实之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: 当前光流研究过度依赖合成基准，亟需一个广泛的真实世界光流评估基准来引导研究。单纯扩展数据和算力并非解决之道，需要更具创新性的研究方向来提升模型对真实场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of vision models to broadly benefit society is arguably a main research objective. In optical flow, however, the difficulty to obtain the ground truth has focused research mainly on synthetic data and domain-specific benchmarks. Here, we investigate the severity of this mismatch. We study how well modern optical flow estimation models generalise to real-world video and question if accuracy on synthetic benchmark proxies actually predicts accuracy on real-world optical flow. To address this, we build a real-world evaluation benchmark and evaluate the real-world generalisability of a broad set of recent optical flow models using standard checkpoints. Our benchmark contains 8,204 frame pairs across TAP-Flow, Slow Flow, and our own dataset FlowFactor. FlowFactor is a manually annotated real-world benchmark of 1,000 HD frame pairs organised into four confounding factors: large displacements, repetitive textures, occlusions, and lighting variation. Each setting mainly varies only one factor, enabling diagnostic, confounder-specific analysis. Using FlowFactor, we reveal that performance on varying lighting and large displacements correlates most strongly with real-world accuracy, and that improvements on large-motion regimes can trade off against robustness in small-motion, stationary scenes. Our experiments show that progress on Sintel, KITTI and Spring only weakly predicts accuracy on real-world data, highlighting the need for a broad real-world optical flow benchmark. Interestingly, scaling up the amount of training data does not necessarily resolve the gap, calling for new innovative research instead of simply scaling data and compute.

</details>


### [67] [Grassmannian Splatting I: Moving rank-2 Spacetime Surfels for Dynamic Scene Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10489)
*Aaron Maurice Berman,Shantanu Dave*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Grassmannian Splatting，一种使用时空3-平面（Gr(3,4)流形）上高斯原语的动态场景表示。通过秩-3协方差参数化，每帧条件化后得到秩-2 surfel，实现闭式运动模型，无需学习形变场或定制CUDA。在MonoDyGauBench上训练速度最快，质量排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原生4D高斯泼溅方法使用满秩时空协方差，每帧原语为体积椭球；而要在每帧切片中得到秩-2 surfel，需要秩-3的时空协方差。作者旨在用Grassmann流形上的3-平面参数化来精确实现这一结构，获得闭式运动模型和更高效的动态场景表示。

Method: 将动态场景表示为Grassmann流形Gr(3,4)上的高斯原语，每个原语由单位法向量n∈S³/{±1}≅Gr(3,4)和自由矩阵L∈R^{4×3}参数化。协方差Σ₄D=(P_n L)(P_n L)^T，其中P_n=I-nn^T为投影算子。对时间条件化后得到每帧的秩-2 surfel，法向量和沿法线速度由n直接读出，圆盘形状和切向漂移由L决定。采用Schur分母中的软钳位正则化静态方向，连续桥接秩-3静态和秩-2动态行为。

Result: 在MonoDyGauBench的17个HyperNeRF场景上，训练速度在所有对比方法中最快（比最强质量基线快4.9至5.6倍），PSNR、MS-SSIM和LPIPS指标排名第二。

Conclusion: Grassmannian Splatting通过Grassmann流形上的低秩参数化，在保持高质量动态场景重建的同时大幅提升训练效率，证明了秩约束几何参数化在4D高斯泼溅中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Grassmannian splatting, a dynamic scene representation whose primitives are Gaussians supported on 3-planes in spacetime $\R^4$: generically, spatial 2-planes in uniform translation along their normals. Each primitive carries a unit normal $n \in \mathbb S^3/\{\pm 1\} \cong \mathrm{Gr}(3,4)$ and an unconstrained factor $L \in \mathbb R^{4 \times 3}$, with covariance \[
  Σ_{4\mathrm{D}} = (P_n L)(P_n L)^T, \qquad P_n = I - n n^T. \] For generic $L$ and $n \neq \pm e_0$, conditioning on time returns a rank-2 surfel at every frame. The normal of the disk and its velocity along that normal are read off from $n$; the disk shape and the tangential drift of its center are set by $L$. Existing native 4D Gaussian splatting methods [\it{Yang et. al. 2023,Duan et. al. 2024}] slice full-rank spacetime covariances, so their per-frame primitive is a volumetric ellipsoid; since conditioning lowers rank by exactly one, a rank-2 surfel in the slice requires a rank-3 spacetime covariance, and the parameterization above realizes exactly these. The motion model is closed form, i.e. no deformation field is learned, and no custom CUDA is required: the conditioned disk feeds a standard 3DGS rasterizer through its precomputed-covariance interface. A soft clamp in the Schur denominator regularizes the static orientation and continuously bridges rank-3 static and rank-2 dynamic behavior, so static and moving primitives form a single continuous family. On the 17 HyperNeRF scenes of MonoDyGauBench, training is fastest among all compared methods (4.9 to 5.6 times faster than the strongest quality baselines), while ranking second in PSNR, MS-SSIM, and LPIPS. Code: https://github.com/PaulCelanCoding/grassmannian-splatting

</details>


### [68] [NanoVSR: Towards Real-Time Video Super-Resolution on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10495)
*Filip Pawlicki,Marcel Kańduła,Marcin Pucek,Kamil Dobies*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NanoVSR 是一个专为边缘设备设计的全卷积视频超分辨率架构，通过结构重参数化在推理时折叠为标准卷积，实现硬件兼容；无需显式光流即可隐式学习时空对齐，在 REDS4 上以极低计算代价达到竞争性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VSR 方法依赖 Transformer 和显式光流，计算开销大且包含定制算子，难以在 TensorRT 等硬件加速器上高效部署。需要一种可在资源受限边缘设备上实时运行、且与标准推理框架完全兼容的架构。

Method: 提出 NanoVSR，一种可扩展的全卷积架构。核心思路是采用结构重参数化技术——训练时使用多分支卷积结构增强表示能力，推理时将其折叠为单一标准卷积，保证硬件兼容性和零额外开销；同时通过渐进式训练策略隐式学习时空对齐，无需显式运动补偿模块。

Result: 在 REDS4 基准上，NanoVSR-644k 版本达到 28.64 dB PSNR，在 NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 16GB（25W）上实现 27.2 FPS；NanoVSR-1.7M 版本达到 29.15 dB，吞吐量为 19.58 FPS，在精度与效率之间取得了优异的平衡。

Conclusion: NanoVSR 证明了全卷积架构结合结构重参数化可以在边缘设备上实现高质量、高效率的视频超分辨率，无需光流或 Transformer，为紧凑型 VSR 架构提供了更优的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Recent Video Super-Resolution (VSR) methods rely heavily on transformers and explicit optical flow, creating computational overhead and custom operations that hinder deployment on hardware accelerators like TensorRT. To address this, we introduce NanoVSR, a scalable, fully convolutional architecture designed for resource-constrained edge devices. Using structural reparameterization, NanoVSR collapses into standard convolutions during inference, ensuring seamless hardware compatibility and negligible runtime overhead. Furthermore, despite lacking explicit motion compensation, it maintains competitive restoration quality by implicitly learning spatio-temporal alignments through progressive training. Evaluated on the REDS4 benchmark, NanoVSR demonstrates an exceptional balance between accuracy and computational efficiency, significantly improving the trade-off for compact architectures. Our NanoVSR-644k baseline yields 28.64 dB PSNR while delivering 27.2 FPS on the NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 16GB (25W), offering massive speed gains over heavier models. The scaled NanoVSR-1.7M variant reaches 29.15 dB with a throughput of 19.58 FPS, providing superior, edge-optimized upscaling. Code is available at https://github.com/filippawlicki/nanovsr.

</details>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [69] [CLIR-Bench: Benchmarking Multimodal Question Answering over Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09880)
*Frank Nie,Ethan B. Liu,Yuan Zhu,Loe Yan,Wei Fan,Jindong Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了CLIR-Bench，一个面向不规则临床时间序列问答的基准，包含6,600个QA实例，覆盖11个临床变量和4个能力维度，用于评估模型在稀疏、不规则采样数据上的证据检索与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床问答基准主要关注规则采样时间序列或静态医学数据，无法评估模型在不规则、稀疏、异步的临床观测中定位时间证据的能力，因此需要一个针对性的评估框架。

Method: 基于去标识化的ICU记录，通过四阶段流水线构建CLIR-Bench：包含6,600个QA实例、11个临床变量、4个能力维度和11个任务。每个问题均关联明确的时间证据和任务特定的答案推导规则，支持答案准确性与证据使用的双重评估。

Result: 实验表明，现有通用模型难以在稀疏临床证据上进行有效检索和推理，暴露了其在处理不规则时间序列推理方面的不足。

Conclusion: 临床时间序列问答需要更强的不规则时序推理方法，CLIR-Bench为这一方向提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Clinical time series are central to patient monitoring, risk assessment, and clinical decision support. However, they are often sparse, irregularly sampled, and asynchronous, making it difficult for models to identify the temporal evidence required for clinical Question Answering (QA). Existing benchmarks primarily focus on regularly sampled time-series QA or medical QA over static data, and therefore rarely assess whether models can faithfully ground their answers in irregular temporal observations.
