To fill this gap, we introduce CLIR-Bench, a benchmark for irregular clinical time series QA constructed from de-identified ICU records through a principled four-stage pipeline. CLIR-Bench contains 6,600 QA instances spanning 11 clinical variables, organized into four capability dimensions and 11 tasks. Each question is linked to explicit temporal evidence and task-specific answer derivation rules, enabling evaluation of both answer accuracy and evidence use. Experiments show that existing generalist models struggle to retrieve and reason over sparse clinical evidence, highlighting the need for stronger irregular time-series reasoning methods. Our code and data are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/winall/CLIR-Bench.

</details>


### [70] [Index SLM Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09885)
*Lusheng Zhang,Shien He,Tianxing Yan,Mengran Yu,Ziang Cui,Kai Zhao,Xiaojing Liu,Tianjiao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Index-1.9B 是B站开源的一系列19亿参数小语言模型，包含Base/Pure/Chat/Character四个版本，采用Warmup-Stable-Decay学习率调度和Norm-Head输出层，在标准基准上达到64.92平均分，性能媲美数倍参数规模的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索小规模语言模型（1.9B参数）的极限性能，研究预训练阶段学习率调度、数据质量、指令数据混入等因素对模型能力的影响，并提供一个全面开源的小模型系列供社区使用。

Method: 1) 在2.8T中英文token上预训练1.9B非嵌入参数的基础模型；2) 采用Warmup-Stable-Decay三阶段学习率调度，在decay阶段大幅提升高质量数据浓度；3) 使用Norm-Head输出层在大学习率下稳定训练；4) 设置Pure对照组（严格过滤指令数据）；5) 通过SFT+DPO对齐得到Chat版本；6) 引入RAG实现Character版本的少样本角色扮演定制。

Result: 1) Index-1.9B-Base在考试、推理、数学、代码等基准上平均得分64.92，达到或超过数倍参数量的开源模型；2) 发现恒定学习率阶段中期出现了无法解释的基准性能突增现象；3) 通过对照实验揭示了模型深度、学习率大小与调度策略、decay阶段数据质量的作用，以及预训练中混入指令数据的影响。

Conclusion: 小语言模型通过精心设计的学习率调度（Warmup-Stable-Decay）、高质量数据编排和Norm-Head等训练技巧，能够以远小于大模型的参数量实现有竞争力的基准性能；同时揭示了训练过程中基准性能突增等值得进一步研究的现象。

Abstract: We present Index-1.9B, a series of open small language models developed at Bilibili. The series comprises four models: Index-1.9B-Base, a foundation model with 1.9 billion non-embedding parameters pre-trained on 2.8 trillion predominantly Chinese and English tokens; Index-1.9B-Pure, a control variant trained with an identical recipe but with all instruction-like data strictly filtered from the corpus; Index-1.9B-Chat, aligned from the base model with supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization; and Index-1.9B-Character, which augments the chat model with retrieval-augmented generation for few-shot role-playing customization. Pre-training employs a Warmup-Stable-Decay learning-rate schedule in which the concentration of curated data is raised substantially during the decay phase, together with a Norm-Head output layer that stabilizes training under large learning rates. On a suite of standard benchmarks covering examination, reasoning, mathematics, and code, Index-1.9B-Base attains an average score of 64.92, competitive with or exceeding open models of several times its size. We further report controlled studies on model depth, learning-rate magnitude and scheduling, the interaction between learning-rate decay and data quality, and the effect of including instruction data during pre-training, and we document an unexplained surge in benchmark performance midway through the constant-learning-rate phase. All models, together with evaluation code, are released at https://github.com/bilibili/Index-1.9B.

</details>


### [71] [RouteRec: Strict Evaluation of Recommender-Agent Selection and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09908)
*Kaiji Zhou,Vladimir Kalmykov,Yue Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出RouteRec框架，研究推荐系统中任务感知的智能体排序问题。在MovieLens-1M数据集上，实验表明请求级别的硬选择效果不佳（低于BM25基线），而基于门控的全智能体聚合方法可在控制LLM调用成本（70.2%）的同时达到HR@10=0.295，说明项目级聚合是更有前景的动作空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统面临多种异构智能体（协同过滤、序列模型、基于内容的检索器、LLM重排序器）的选择问题，但没有单一智能体在所有场景下表现最优。现有方法缺乏在成本约束下进行任务感知的智能体排序与选择的系统性研究。

Method: 提出RouteRec框架，对比两种策略：(1) 请求级别的硬选择（hard selection），即每个请求选择单一智能体的完整推荐列表；(2) 项目级别的学习聚合（learned aggregation），包括仅使用廉价智能体的变体和门控全智能体聚合变体。实验采用MovieLens-1M数据集，使用无泄漏的5折交叉验证协议，以BM25作为对比基线，评估指标为HR@10和NDCG。

Result: 全质量预言机（full quality oracle）HR@10=0.584，表明跨智能体信号确实存在。但在严格协议下：(1) 硬选择HR@10仅0.223，低于BM25的0.254，选择性LLM升级未能改善；(2) 廉价聚合变体HR匹配BM25但NDCG更高（0.123 vs 0.114）；(3) 门控全智能体聚合HR@10=0.295，且仅需70.2%的LLM调用。

Conclusion: 请求级别选择单一智能体完整列表的粒度过粗，不适合稀疏固定候选集场景；项目级别的聚合是更有前景的动作空间。路由问题远未解决，未来工作应聚焦于更细粒度的智能体协同机制。

Abstract: Recommender systems increasingly face a choice among heterogeneous agents -- collaborative filters, sequential models, content-based retrievers, and LLM-based rerankers -- yet no single agent is uniformly best. We study this choice as task-aware agent ranking under cost constraints using RouteRec, a framework that compares request-level hard selection with item-level learned aggregation over four traditional recommender agents and one LLM reranker agent. On MovieLens-1M, the full quality oracle has substantial headroom (HR@10 = 0.584), confirming that useful cross-agent signal exists. Under a leakage-free 5-fold out-of-fold protocol, however, hard selection remains below BM25 (0.223 vs. 0.254), and selective LLM escalation does not improve it. The same protocol yields a different outcome for learned aggregation: its cheap-only variant matches BM25 in HR and has a higher NDCG point estimate (0.123 vs. 0.114), while gated all-agent aggregation reaches HR@10 = 0.295 with 70.2\% LLM calls. The resulting lesson is not that routing is solved, but that request-level selection of one complete agent list is too coarse for this sparse fixed-candidate setting; item-level aggregation is the more promising action space.

</details>


### [72] [Global Merger-Arbitrage Forecasting with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09921)
*Hinal Jajal,Michal Mucha,Charles Sweat,Chris Pulman,Charlie Flanagan,Peter Anderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于并购套利预测的语言模型系统，结合专家引导的上下文工程与基于后见之明的推理轨迹微调，在400+跨国大型交易上实现最优预测性能，Brier得分较市场隐含概率降低24%，较前沿语言模型降低25-42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM判断性预测研究多关注宽泛混合主题基准和短文本片段，尚未探索需要长上下文技术文档推理的专业高 stakes 金融场景。并购套利正是一个需要处理数百页专业文件、进行长程推理的典型任务，亟需验证LLM在该场景下的可行性与有效方法。

Method: 系统设计包含两大核心：(1) 专家引导的上下文工程——针对并购交易构建高质量的长上下文输入；(2) 基于后见之明的推理轨迹微调——利用历史交易已知结果生成推理链，对语言模型进行监督微调。给定已公告的交易，模型输出三种互斥结果的概率分布：按原条款完成、更高出价、或交易终止。

Result: 在42个国家400+大型交易的样本外测试中，微调系统取得所有评估方法中的最佳性能，类别平衡Brier得分降至0.151。相比校准后的市场隐含概率降低24%，比XGBoost低19%，比前沿语言模型低25-42%。消融实验进一步验证了后见之明监督与专家上下文设计的关键作用。

Conclusion: LLM可以在专业化、长上下文的金融工作流中取得成功，其中基于后见之明的监督信号和专家设计的上下文策略至关重要。这为LLM在更多垂直领域的高风险预测任务中的应用开辟了道路。

Abstract: We present a language-model forecasting system for merger arbitrage, a specialized high-stakes financial setting in which the task is to predict the outcome of announced M\&A deals. Unlike prior work on judgmental forecasting with LLMs, which has focused on broad mixed-topic benchmarks and short context such as news snippets, we study a setting that requires long-context reasoning over hundreds of pages of technical documents. Our system combines expert-guided context engineering with finetuning on hindsight-guided reasoning traces derived from historical deals. Given an announced deal, it outputs a probability distribution over three mutually exclusive outcomes: closing at announced terms, a higher bid, or deal termination. On an out-of-sample set of more than 400 large deals spanning 42 countries, our finetuned system achieves the best performance of any method we evaluate, reducing class-balanced Brier score to 0.151. This is 24\% below calibrated market-implied probabilities, 19\% below XGBoost, and 25-42\% below frontier language models. These results, together with ablation studies, show that LLM-based forecasting can succeed in specialized, long-context financial workflows, with hindsight-based supervision and expert-designed context playing a critical role.

</details>


### [73] [Faithful by Design: Evaluating and Improving LLM-Generated Clinical Trial Summaries for Multi-Stakeholder Audiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09932)
*Robert Williams*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个基准评估框架，用于衡量LLM生成的临床试验摘要在三类利益相关者（医护人员、患者、支付方）中的忠实度，发现“无依据声明”是主要失败模式，并提出了一种知识图谱增强检索系统，能显著提升忠实度评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于总结临床试验结果，但在医疗这一高风险场景中，模型的“幻觉”（hallucination）倾向带来了重大风险。当前缺乏系统性的评估框架来衡量LLM生成的临床试验摘要在不同受众中的忠实度（faithfulness），因此需要建立一个标准化的基准测试和缓解策略。

Method: 1. 从Aggregate Analysis of ClinicalTrials.gov数据库中分层抽取200项临床试验作为评估素材；2. 设计面向三类利益相关者（医护人员、患者、支付方）的提示模板，生成共1,800篇摘要；3. 建立六维度忠实度标注模式（annotation schema），并使用跨编码器自然语言推理（NLI）模型进行自动评分；4. 对GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash进行基线测量；5. 开发知识图谱增强检索系统（knowledge-graph-augmented retrieval），与基线对比以评估改进效果。

Result: 1. “无依据声明”（Unsupported Claims）是三种模型最主要失败模式，平均标注得分仅1.55/3；2. 知识图谱增强检索系统带来了统计显著的忠实度提升：蕴含（entailment）+0.0125，忠实度（faithfulness）+0.0130，p < 0.0001；3. 改进路径因模型而异：GPT-4o主要通过减少矛盾（contradiction）实现提升，而Claude Sonnet 4.6和Gemini 2.5 Flash则通过增加蕴含关系改善。

Conclusion: LLM在临床摘要生成中普遍存在忠实度问题，尤以无依据声明最为突出；知识图谱增强检索是一种有效的缓解策略，能显著提升忠实度，但不同模型的最优改进路径存在差异，提示需要模型特异性的优化策略。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to summarize clinical trial results for healthcare providers, patients, and payers, but their tendency to hallucinate poses significant risks in this high-stakes context. This study introduces a benchmark evaluation framework for measuring the faithfulness of LLM-generated clinical trial summaries across three stakeholder audiences. The framework consists of 200 stratified trials drawn from the Aggregate Analysis of ClinicalTrials.gov database, evaluated using audience-specific prompt templates and a six-dimension faithfulness annotation schema. Baseline measurements were established for GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Gemini 2.5 Flash across 1,800 generated summaries scored using a cross-encoder natural language inference (NLI) model. Unsupported Claims was identified as the dominant failure mode across all three models, with a mean annotation score of 1.55 out of three. A knowledge-graph-augmented retrieval system was developed and evaluated against the baseline, producing statistically significant improvements in NLI-based faithfulness scores (entailment +0.0125, faithfulness +0.0130, p < 0.0001). Improvement pathways were model-dependent, with GPT-4o improving primarily through contradiction reduction while Claude Sonnet 4.6 and Gemini 2.5 Flash improved through increased entailment.

</details>


### [74] [Workload-Driven Optimization for On-Device Real-Time Subtitle Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09957)
*Tsz-To Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究面向台湾地区的设备端英译繁体中文实时字幕翻译，提出通过缩小词表（从151k降至64k）并适配嵌入空间来加速GGUF量化后的解码过程，在OpenSubtitles2024测试子集上取得了对Google Translate 59.2%的胜率，并在Apple M2 Metal上实现1.63倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型服务优化通常针对长上下文或高吞吐场景设计，而在短输入、短输出、batch-size=1、低延迟及隐私约束的设备端字幕翻译场景下，这些优化价值有限。初步分析发现，GGUF量化后Transformer块的相对计算成本降低，词汇投影（vocabulary projection）成为解码阶段的重要瓶颈。

Method: 以LMT-60-0.6B为基座模型，采用以下流程：(1) 将原始151k词表替换为64k字幕领域专用tokenizer；(2) 迁移嵌入空间（embedding space migration）；(3) 通过嵌入校准（embedding calibration）结合全量监督微调（full supervised fine-tuning）进行模型适配。

Result: 在OpenSubtitles2024测试集的固定500样本子集上，经GPT-4o成对评判，LocalSubs相比Google Translate获得59.2%的平局排除胜率（tie-excluded win rate）。短字幕翻译表现最佳，随字幕长度增加性能下降。在Apple M2 Metal上的初步测试显示，64k词表模型相比151k词表基线实现1.63倍加速（注：原始基准配置不完整，该延迟结果为初步测量）。

Conclusion: 面向特定领域的设备端翻译场景，通过缩小词表并结合嵌入校准与监督微调，可在保持翻译质量的同时显著降低解码延迟。该方法验证了在隐私敏感、低延迟约束下优化小模型词表是有效的工程路径，但完整基准测试仍需进一步完善。

Abstract: This report studies on-device English-to-Traditional-Chinese subtitle translation for Taiwan under short inputs, short outputs, batch-size-one inference, low latency, and privacy constraints. These conditions limit the value of optimizations designed for long-context or high-throughput language-model serving.
  Starting from LMT-60-0.6B, preliminary profiling suggests that vocabulary projection becomes a more important decode-time cost after GGUF quantization reduces the relative cost of Transformer blocks. We replace the original 151k-token vocabulary with a 64k-token subtitle-domain tokenizer, migrate the embedding space, and adapt the model through embedding calibration followed by full supervised fine-tuning.
  On a fixed 500-example subset of the OpenSubtitles2024 test set, the LocalSubs achieves a 59.2% tie-excluded win rate against Google Translate under GPT-4o pairwise judging. Performance is strongest on short cues and declines as cue length increases. Preliminary Apple M2 Metal measurements on a 64k-vocabulary model show a 1.63$\times$ speedup over a 151k-vocabulary profiling baseline. The raw benchmark configuration is incomplete, so the latency result is treated as preliminary.

</details>


### [75] [Silent Failures in Quantized LLM Reasoning: A Taxonomy-Based Analysis of Hollow Convergence and Failure Mode Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09999)
*Renuka Oladri,Mohan Vamsi Varadaraju Priya,Jerry Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 后训练量化会在不改变任务准确率的情况下，悄无声息地改变大语言模型的推理方式。研究发现NF4量化下，"空心收敛"（推理过程不完整却得出正确答案）在小型模型中显著下降，而大型模型保持稳定；同时错误模式也发生了质变，且这类失败无法通过表面文本特征检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM部署中广泛使用后训练量化来降低计算成本，评估通常仅关注任务准确率。然而，量化是否会在准确率不变的情况下改变模型的底层推理行为，这一问题尚未被系统研究。作者旨在揭示这种"沉默的推理退化"现象。

Method: 构建六类思维链失败分类体系（经两名独立标注者验证，Cohen's κ = 0.906），对5个指令微调LLM（3B–14B参数）在三种量化精度（FP32、FP16、NF4）下，跨四个推理基准（GSM8K、LogiQA、ARC-Challenge等）生成30,000条思维链输出进行分类标注和分析。

Result: (1) 各精度下准确率鲁棒，最大仅3.1个百分点下降；(2) NF4下，Hollow Convergence呈现显著的模型规模依赖效应：两个最小模型该指标急剧下降，12B及以上模型保持不变；(3) 该效应具有基准特异性：GSM8K完全免疫，LogiQA和ARC-Challenge变化最大；(4) NF4下LLaMA 3.2-3B的Shortcut Collapse从错误答案失败的44%升至78%，Confidence Snowballing从15.8%骤降至接近零；(5) 表面文本特征无法可靠检测Hollow Convergence（最佳F1=0.53）。

Conclusion: 量化引入的推理行为变化被标准准确率评估完全掩盖，Hollow Convergence成为一种部署相关的隐蔽失败模式，无法通过常规评估流程捕获。这一发现对依赖量化模型的安全关键部署具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: We show that post-training quantization can silently alter how large language models reason even when task accuracy is preserved. Using a six-category failure taxonomy validated by two independent human annotators (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.906), we classify 30,000 chain-of-thought outputs from five instruction-tuned LLMs (3B--14B parameters) across three quantization precisions (FP32, FP16, NF4) and four reasoning benchmarks. We find that while accuracy is robust across precisions (maximum 3.1 pp drop), Hollow Convergence (correct answers reached through incomplete or unverifiable reasoning) shows a significant size-dependent shift under NF4, dropping sharply for the two smallest models tested but remaining invariant for models at 12B parameters and above. This effect is also benchmark-specific: GSM8K is categorically immune while LogiQA and ARC-Challenge show the largest shifts. Furthermore, under NF4, Shortcut Collapse rises from 44% to 78% of wrong-answer failures in LLaMA 3.2-3B while Confidence Snowballing collapses from 15.8% to near zero, a qualitative shift invisible to accuracy metrics. Finally, we show Hollow Convergence cannot be reliably detected from surface-level text features (best F1 = 0.53), establishing it as a deployment-relevant failure mode that standard evaluation pipelines cannot catch.

</details>


### [76] [Robust, Scalable Detection of Text Containment in Large Web-Crawled Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10020)
*Lars Henry Berge Olsen,Pierre Lison,Martin Jullum,Mark Anderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FindMyText 是一个开源Python工具包，通过显式捕捉匹配指纹序列（指纹链）来检测文本是否部分或全部出现在语料库中。它在分布式磁盘索引框架上运行，在三个数据集上优于现有方法，特别适用于版权内容检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档指纹技术在检测文本包含关系时，往往只能发现文本相似性，难以可靠区分"近乎逐字复制"与"一般性文本相似"。作者旨在提供一个能明确识别连续匹配序列的工具，以更精准地验证受版权保护的材料是否出现在大型语料库中。

Method: 基于文档指纹技术，引入新颖的"指纹链"（chains of matching fingerprints）机制来显式捕获匹配指纹的连续序列。采用分布式、基于磁盘的索引框架以支持大规模网络爬取数据集的可扩展性。同时构建了新的文本包含评估基准。

Result: 在三个数据集（ArXiv论文、维基百科、通用网页内容）上，FindMyText 在文本包含检测任务中均优于其他替代方法。

Conclusion: FindMyText 通过指纹链机制有效提升了近乎逐字文本复制检测的可靠性，结合分布式架构实现了大规模语料库的可扩展处理，在学术论文、百科和网页等多个领域均表现出色，为版权内容验证提供了一种实用工具。

Abstract: We present FindMyText, an open-source Python package designed to efficiently assess whether a given text appears, in part or in full, within a text corpus. The tool builds on prior techniques for document fingerprinting, but extends them with a novel mechanism to explicitly capture sequences of matching fingerprints. By identifying such chains, the tool can more reliably detect near-verbatim copies of a given text rather than mere textual similarities. This makes FindMyText particularly suited for verifying the presence of copyrighted material in a corpus. Leveraging a distributed, disk-based indexing framework, the system scales to large web-crawled datasets. Using a new benchmark for evaluating text containment methods, we show that FindMyText outperforms alternative approaches across three datasets (ArXiv papers, Wikipedia, and generic web content).

</details>


### [77] [Efficiently Adapting Spoken Language Models for the Singaporean Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10092)
*Ng Jia Sheng Jason*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HT-Moonstone (5B)，通过LoRA微调、代理文本QA数据集及多任务目标，将开源SLM适配到新加坡内政团队的多语言语音场景，性能媲美或超越7倍规模模型，且灾难性遗忘低于2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语语言模型（SLM）在敏感领域（如执法、内政）的适配研究不足，尤其是原始训练数据不可访问、且需支持多语言口语查询的场景。需探索如何在保护隐私和避免灾难性遗忘的前提下，将开源SLM有效适配到特定垂直领域。

Method: 基于开源SLM，采用三管齐下策略：(1) LoRA低秩适配微调；(2) 构建代理文本QA数据集以抑制灾难性遗忘；(3) 将CoBa重加权方案适配至语音模态的多任务目标。同时构建了包含504,853条多语言文本与语音QA样本的HTD-multilingual-QA数据集。

Result: HT-Moonstone (5B) 在多数任务上匹配或超越达7倍规模的其他SLM；在口音和性别识别任务上取得所有评估模型中的最佳表现；原始语音QA能力损失低于2%。

Conclusion: 通过LoRA微调、代理文本数据与多任务重加权相结合，可在保护原始能力的同时，将开源SLM高效适配到特定敏感领域的多语言语音场景，为SLM在垂直行业的落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Spoken language models (SLMs) unify speech perception and reasoning, but adapting them to sensitive domains is underexplored, especially when the original training data is inaccessible and the use case demands multilingual, spoken-query interaction. We adapt an open-source SLM to the Singaporean Home Team context across five speech tasks in Singapore's four official languages, combining LoRA fine-tuning, a surrogate text-QA dataset that guards against catastrophic forgetting, and a multi-task objective that adapts the CoBa reweighting scheme to speech. We also build HTD-multilingual-QA, a 504,853 sample multilingual QA dataset in text and spoken form. The resulting HT-Moonstone (5B) matches or outperforms SLMs up to 7x its size on most tasks, attains the best accent and gender recognition among all models evaluated, and loses under 2\% of its original speech QA ability.

</details>


### [78] [Cost of Reasoning in non-English Languages: A Case Study on Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10114)
*Yuu Jinnai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了训练日语推理语言模型（RLM）的可行性，基于 Qwen-3-Swallow-8B 使用 GRPO 训练日语推理变体，在编码、数学和科学基准上评估。结果表明语言推理控制是可行的，但性能最多与强英语推理基线持平，且日语推理并不会自动提升文化相关任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型（RLMs）在英语推理时表现最强，因为面向推理的训练数据以英语最为丰富。然而，推理轨迹对模型可解释性和安全性至关重要，对用户和开发者都有实用价值。因此，研究者希望开发能以用户所选语言进行推理、同时保持较强推理性能的模型。

Method: 以 Qwen-3-Swallow-8B（由 Qwen-3-8B 持续预训练得到的日语 LLM）为基础，使用 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）训练一个日语推理变体，并在编码、数学和科学基准上进行评估，同时还在日本文化基准上测试。

Result: 通过 GRPO 训练日语持续预训练模型来实现推理语言控制是可行的，但在多个基准上其性能最多与强英语推理基线持平。在日本文化基准上，该模型表现甚至不如基线模型，表明日语推理并不能自动免费提升文化相关任务的表现。

Conclusion: 训练日语推理模型在技术上是可行的，但推理性能并未超越英语推理的强基线，且对文化相关任务并无自动加成效应。暗示语言与推理能力的解耦仍是一个需要深入研究的挑战。

Abstract: Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) achieve their strongest performance when they reason in English, the language for which reasoning-oriented training data is most abundant. However, reasoning trace is a clue for model interpretability and safety, and useful in practice for both the model users and for model developers. Thus, it is desirable to be able to develop a model that reasons in a language of the user's choice, while still maintaining strong reasoning performance. To this end, we study the feasibility of training a model that reasons in Japanese. We develop a Japanese-reasoning variant of Qwen-3-Swallow-8B, which is a Japanese LLM continually pretrained from Qwen-3-8B, with GRPO and evaluate it across coding, math, and science benchmarks. The study shows that reasoning-language control is feasible by training a Japanese continually pretrained model with GRPO. However, its performance is at best on par with strong English-reasoning baselines on several benchmarks. We also evaluate the trained model on Japanese cultural benchmarks and observe that the model's performance is worse than the baseline models, suggesting that the reasoning in Japanese does not immediately improve performance on culturally relevant tasks for free.

</details>


### [79] [Instruction Set and Language for Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10194)
*Mario Pascual-Gonzalez,Ezequiel Lopez-Rubio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出IsalHG，一种将有界超边元数的有限连通超图编码为紧凑字符串的方法，支持规范形式并猜想其为完全同构不变量，同时首次对原生超图同构与基于Levi图的基准进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图同构判定依赖将超图归约为Levi关联图再调用图同构引擎，缺乏原生、直接作用于超图结构的编码与判定方法。作者希望构建一种直接对超图进行字符串编码的框架，实现超图同构的原生判定。

Method: 设计了一个小型虚拟机（含稀疏超图、循环双向链表CDLL及k个遍历指针）和紧凑指令字母表Σ_HG。提出贪心h2s算法将连通超图编码为字符串；通过回溯变体在字典序最大结构元组的节点上播种，生成规范字符串w*（猜想为完全同构不变量）。同构判定即规范字符串相等性比较。

Result: 在150个随机均匀超图和命名组合设计上验证了往返性质s2h(h2s(H))≅H。在(n,c)网格上（每格10种子）与nauty、Traces、bliss三种基于2-着色Levi图的方法对比，600个同构判定全部一致，支持完备性猜想。但挂钟时间上Levi基线方法快3至5个数量级（几何平均比311×到117,672×）。

Conclusion: IsalHG提供了超图同构的原生表示框架与规范编码方法，规范字符串猜想为完全同构不变量需进一步证明。该方法在正确性上与成熟工具有竞争力，但在效率上差距显著，离实用尚有较大距离。

Abstract: We present IsalHG, a method for representing the structure of any finite, connected hypergraph of bounded hyperedge arity as a string over a compact instruction alphabet $Σ_{\mathrm{HG}}$. The encoding is executed by a small virtual machine comprising a sparse hypergraph, a circular doubly-linked list (CDLL) of node references, and $k$ traversal pointers, where $k$ bounds the hyperedge arity. Instructions either move a pointer through the CDLL or insert a hyperedge, optionally together with new nodes, into the hypergraph. Every string over $Σ_{\mathrm{HG}}$ decodes to a valid hypergraph; the alphabet is closed. A greedy \emph{HypergraphToString} (h2s) algorithm encodes any connected hypergraph into a string; a backtracking variant seeded at nodes of lexicographically maximal structural tuple produces a \emph{canonical string} $w^{*}$, which we conjecture to be a complete isomorphism invariant. Canonical-string equality then decides hypergraph isomorphism natively, without the standard reduction to the Levi incidence graph followed by a graph-isomorphism engine. We verify the round-trip property $s2h(h2s(H)) \cong H$ on 150 connected random uniform hypergraphs and on named combinatorial designs, and we benchmark the canonical algorithm against the three practically available exact baselines -- nauty, Traces, and bliss operating on the 2-coloured Levi graph -- across a $(n, c)$ grid with ten seeds per cell. All four methods agree on every one of 600 isomorphism verdicts, consistent with the completeness conjecture. On wall-clock time the Levi baselines dominate every tested cell by three to five orders of magnitude (geometric-mean ratio $311\times$ to $117{,}672\times$), which we report as measured. We contribute the representation framework, a conjecture of canonical completeness, and the first native-versus-Levi benchmark for hypergraph isomorphism.

</details>


### [80] [Equal Accuracy, Unequal Evidence: Search APIs as Decision Surfaces for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10198)
*Sriram Selvam,Anneswa Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出应将商业搜索API理解为"决策表面"（decision surface），而非仅评估其答案召回率。通过在固定Agent下比较Brave、Tavily、Firecrawl三个搜索提供商，发现它们在答案准确率上接近（25-26/100），但在"证据经济"（evidence economy）上差异显著：Brave的摘要富含金答案信息，Tavily的金URL集中在排名第一，Firecrawl则倾向于更广泛的页面探索。提供商选择本质上是检索预算与策略决策，而非纯粹的召回问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agent架构越来越多地采用渐进式披露（progressive disclosure）：Agent先看搜索摘要，再决定是否抓取完整页面。然而现有评估主要关注答案准确率，忽略了搜索API的排序摘要、URL和元数据如何构成一个影响Agent后续行为（立即回答、重新搜索或打开页面）的"决策表面"。研究者意在揭示：不同搜索提供商在相同准确率下，可能引导Agent走向截然不同的检索路径和Token消耗模式。

Method: 实验设计：(1) 使用一个冻结的GPT-5.4 Agent，配备search_web和fetch_page两个工具；(2) 从SEALQA-HARD中抽取100个问题，仅改变搜索提供商（Brave、Tavily、Firecrawl）；(3) 用Kimi-K2.6作为Oracle标注器，对Agent可见的每个内容元素（URL、标题、摘要、抓取页面）进行评判，共产生6,869条有效URL级标注；(4) 采用经审计的"语义匹配"（semantic match）作为正确答案标签，保留精确匹配的同时容忍无害的格式与命名变体；(5) 引入"表面矛盾-金URL比率"（surface contradiction-to-gold URL ratio）作为新指标。

Result: 三个提供商在100题上的答案准确率接近（Brave 25、Tavily 25、Firecrawl 26），但证据经济差异明显：(1) Brave的摘要中富含可直接导向金答案的信息；(2) Tavily将可支持金答案的URL高度集中在排名第一的位置；(3) Firecrawl在该固定Agent策略下触发了更广泛的页面探索行为。表面矛盾-金URL比率在提供商间从0.92到2.59不等。

Conclusion: 搜索API应被理解为一种"决策表面"——其排序后的摘要、URL和元数据共同决定了Agent是立即作答、重新搜索还是消耗Token打开页面。提供商选择因此是一个涉及检索预算和策略权衡的决策，而不仅仅是召回率的考量。这一视角对Agent系统的搜索层设计与评估具有重要启示。

Abstract: Search APIs are the fundamental retrieval layer for many agents and are often their most frequently used tool. Traditional search APIs provide URLs, titles, and snippets that preview website contents. Because full-page retrieval is token-intensive, agent retrieval architectures increasingly use progressive disclosure: the agent first sees snippets and then chooses whether to fetch full pages. In such systems, search API performance is often evaluated primarily by answer accuracy. We argue that a commercial search API is better understood as a decision surface: the ranked snippets, URLs, and metadata that determine whether an agent answers immediately, searches again, or spends tokens opening pages. We test this claim with one frozen GPT-5.4 agent, two tools (search_web and fetch_page), and 100 questions from SEALQA-HARD, varying only the search provider (Brave, Tavily, Firecrawl). A Kimi-K2.6 oracle labels every content element visible to the agent (URL, title, snippet, and fetched page, when fetched), producing 6,869 valid per-URL judgments. We use an audited correct-answer label, semantic match, which preserves exact matches while accepting harmless formatting and naming variants. Under this measure, the providers remain close (25, 25, 26 / 100), but their evidence economies differ sharply: Brave offers gold-answer-rich snippets, Tavily concentrates gold-supporting URLs at rank 1, and Firecrawl is associated with broader exploration under this fixed agent policy. We also introduce a surface contradiction-to-gold URL ratio, which varies from 0.92 to 2.59. Provider choice is therefore a retrieval-budget and policy decision, not merely a recall decision.

</details>


### [81] [Consensus vs. Dissent: Dynamic LLM Modeling of Subjective Preferences in Group Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10235)
*Cedric Waterschoot,Nava Tintarev,Francesco Barile*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型是否能够根据群体偏好分布，动态选择最优的偏好聚合策略，以最大化公平性、满意度和共识。通过微调LLM模拟人类判断，并在用户研究中验证了该方法在满意度和群体共识上得分最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体推荐系统中，偏好聚合策略的选择对群体内偏好分布高度敏感。现有工作已证明考虑群体配置（如少数派、联盟等）能提升推荐质量，但如何让系统自动感知并适配这些细微的人类主观判断（公平性、满意度、共识）仍是一个挑战。本文旨在探索LLM是否能模仿这种敏感性，从而动态选择最优聚合策略。

Method: （1）使用从DeepSeek-V3.1蒸馏的推理数据集和人类真实评估标注，分别微调得到Judgmental Llama和Judgmental OLMo两个模型，用于实时模拟群体评估；（2）基于社会选择理论生成多个推荐候选项，由微调后的LLM动态选择最大化人类感知评分（公平性、满意度、共识）的候选项；（3）通过用户研究（n=284）验证该方法，并分析LLM判断与群体配置（如少数派、联盟）的交互效应。

Result: 所提方法在满意度和群体共识两个维度上取得了最高评分；LLM判断与人类对公平性、满意度和共识的感知最为一致时，考虑了LLM方法与群体配置之间的交互效应。

Conclusion: 研究进一步支持了根据群体内部偏好分布动态调整聚合策略的必要性，并证明了使用LLM实现与主观人类判断对齐的策略自适应具有显著优势。

Abstract: Previous work in group recommender systems has demonstrated a sensitivity to the distribution of preferences within a group. Specifically, the selection of the preference aggregation strategy benefits from considering such group configurations. In this paper, we study whether LLMs are able to mimic this sensitivity and to select the ideal aggregation strategy (and corresponding recommendation) according to nuanced human perceptions of fairness, satisfaction, and consensus.
  We do this by fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) on human survey data to serve as real-time judgmental models within the recommendation pipeline. Using a reasoning dataset distilled from DeepSeek-V3.1 and human ground truth assessments, we develop Judgmental Llama and Judgmental OLMo to simulate group assessments. Our pipeline successfully generates multiple recommendation candidates based on social choice-based aggregation strategies and dynamically selects the one that maximizes these predicted human-like evaluations. We further validate these suggestions in a user study (n=284) and find that our methodology achieved the highest scores for satisfaction and group consensus. Furthermore, we find that LLM judgments are most aligned with human perceptions of fairness, satisfaction and consensus when we also consider interaction effects between our LLM-based method and group configuration (e.g., minority or coalition). These findings give further support for dynamically adapting aggregation strategies to specific within-group preference distributions, and highlight the advantage of using LLMs for an adaptation that is aligned with subjective human judgments.

</details>


### [82] [PTEI: Integrating Personality Traits to Enhance Emotional Intelligence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10245)
*Amir Reza Jafari,Praboda Rajapaksha,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PTEI 是一个将人格特质（MBTI 和 OCEAN）融入大语言模型情感推理的框架，通过人格感知提示和对比学习优化的检索系统，显著提升了模型在情感理解任务上的表现，结合思维链推理可额外提升 4% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在复杂情感推理上仍远逊于人类，核心原因之一在于模型未能充分融入个体差异信息，尤其是人格特质——而人格恰恰是人类情感推断的基础。因此需要一种方法将人格特质系统地整合到 LLM 的情感智能任务中。

Method: 提出 PTEI 框架，包含三个关键环节：(1) 从给定的情感场景中直接提取 MBTI 和 OCEAN 人格特质；(2) 将提取的人格特质作为情境知识融入人格感知提示（personality-aware prompts），引导 LLM 推断情感及其成因；(3) 采用对比学习构建优化的检索系统，检索情感和人格上对齐的场景，以确保最优的情境锚定。

Result: 在多个情感智能基准数据集上的实验表明，PTEI 增强了多种 LLM 的情感理解能力，GPT 系列模型提升最为显著。PTEI 与思维链推理结合后，准确率进一步提升 4 个百分点。

Conclusion: PTEI 有效弥补了 LLM 在情感推理中对人格特质建模的不足，为构建具有更深层社会与心理基础的 AI 系统做出了贡献。

Abstract: Despite advances in Emotional Intelligence (EI), Large Language Models (LLMs) still significantly underperform humans in complex emotional reasoning. This gap originates partly from the limited incorporation of individual differences, particularly personality traits, which are fundamental to human emotional inference. To address this, we propose PTEI, a novel framework for integrating Personality Traits into Emotional Intelligence tasks using LLMs. In PTEI, MBTI and OCEAN personality traits are first extracted directly from the given emotional scenarios and then utilized as contextual knowledge within personality-aware prompts, guiding LLMs to accurately infer emotions and their underlying causes. To ensure optimal contextual grounding, we employ Contrastive Learning to construct an optimized retrieval system that surfaces emotionally and personally aligned scenarios, enhancing reasoning quality. Extensive experiments on established EI benchmarks show that PTEI enhances the Emotional Understanding (EU) capabilities of various LLMs, with the strongest improvement observed in GPT models. Combining PTEI with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning yields an additional 4 percent increase in accuracy. These findings underscore PTEI's contribution toward advancing AI systems with more sophisticated social and psychological grounding.

</details>


### [83] [One mechanism for many mental spaces: a shared router over a value slot in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10248)
*Oliver Steele,Jiangtao Wen,Yuxing Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探究了Transformer语言模型如何表征"心理空间"（mental spaces），发现模型采用一种统一的路由器/槽位机制来跨类型处理反事实、信念、虚构和时间等不同话语语境，而非像形式语义学那样将它们分开处理。该机制具有低秩、可加性组合的特点，并能驱动推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言通过构建不同的"心理空间"（如现实、信念、虚构、反事实、时间等）来运作，同一实体在不同空间中可具有不同属性值。形式语义学认为这些空间的逻辑不同（模态、时间、信念、描绘等），应分开处理；而Fauconnier的心理空间理论将其统一为一种"空间构建"操作。本文旨在探究Transformer语言模型究竟采用了哪一方的方案，即模型内部是否实现了跨类型统一的表征机制。

Method: 研究者使用分布式对齐搜索（Distributed Alignment Search, DAS）训练子空间来操控模型对心理空间的表征。具体而言，在Transformer模型中定位一个可复用的"值槽位"（存储属性内容）和一个因果可操控的"路由器"（即空间索引，用于选择读取哪个空间）。通过对反事实、信念、虚构、时间四种空间类型分别训练子空间，检验其跨类型泛化能力。进一步实验验证该路由器是否低秩、是否与实体身份表征可加性组合、是否通过少数后期注意力头起作用，以及该机制是否驱动推理并支持空间构建算子的组合。

Result: 模型确实实现了类似Fauconnier理论的统一机制：一个可复用的值槽位存储被归因的内容，一个因果可操控的路由器选择读取哪个空间。针对一种空间类型（如反事实）训练的子空间，在三种模型家族上均能显著超过随机基线地控制其他空间类型（信念、虚构、时间）。值得注意的是，信念（被形式语义学视为特殊案例）在模型内部并未被特别分离。路由器具有低秩特性，与实体身份表征呈可加性组合，并通过少数几层后期的注意力头发挥作用。此外，在规则推导结论上训练的子空间可以翻转模型的推理结果，同时与模型所报告的内容解耦；组合两个空间构建算子可以生成一个新的路由器，覆盖在共享槽位之上。

Conclusion: Transformer语言模型在内部实现了Fauconnier心理空间理论的统一机制版本，而非形式语义学的分治方案。该统一的路由器/槽位机制跨越反事实、信念、虚构、时间等多种空间类型，具有低秩、可加性组合的特点，并能驱动推理过程。这确立了该机制的跨类型通用性。关于信念的深入分析将在配套论文中展开。

Abstract: Language builds discourse contexts other than the actual: a painting, a belief, a memory, a hypothetical. Each is a mental space in which the same entity can take a different value, as when a flower is red in reality but purple in a portrait. Formal semantics keeps these contexts apart because their logics differ (modal, temporal, doxastic, depictive); Fauconnier's mental-space theory treats them as one space-building operation. We ask which of these a transformer language model implements, and find a mechanistic version of Fauconnier's unification. The model uses one router/slot format across the inventory: a reusable value slot stores attributed content, and a causally manipulable router (the space index) selects which space is read. A subspace trained with Distributed Alignment Search to control one space type, counterfactual, belief, fictional, or temporal, also controls the others, well above a random floor, on three model families; belief, which formal semantics marks as a distinct case, is not specially separated. The router is low-rank, composes additively with entity identity, and acts through a few late-layer heads. Two further results show the mechanism drives inference and composes: a subspace trained on a rule-derived conclusion flips what the model infers while dissociating from what it reports, and composing space-builders mints a fresh router over the shared slot. This paper establishes the cross-type generality. A companion paper develops belief in depth, because of its special status in philosophy, psychology, and linguistics (epistemology, theory of mind, and propositional attitude reports).

</details>


### [84] [Which Languages Transfer Best to Warlpiri? A Similarity-Based Study for Low-Resource ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10256)
*Pravina Mylvaganam,Eliathamby Ambikairajah,Ting Dang,Vidhyasaharan Sethu,Tuende Szalay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合声学相似性与语言类型学相似性的框架，用于在极低资源场景下为Warlpiri语选择最优的高资源源语言进行跨语言ASR迁移，实验表明声学相似性是微调性能的最强预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Warlpiri作为澳大利亚原住民语言， transcribed speech数据极度匮乏，迁移学习是唯一可行方案。然而，如何系统性地选择最适合的源语言进行跨语言迁移缺乏有效方法，需要超越简单的语言家族相近假设，综合考虑声学与语言学多维度的相似性。

Method: 提出一个双维度框架：(1) 利用预训练语音模型（Whisper）提取声学嵌入，计算源语言与目标语言之间的声学相似度；(2) 基于类型学特征、音素库存、语法和句法特征计算语言学相似度。综合两个维度对高资源源语言进行排序，选出最优候选语言进行ASR微调迁移实验，并分别评估零样本与微调场景下的表现。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 声学和类型学上均相似的语言（如Assamese和Hindi）在WER和CER上显著优于单语和多语基线；(2) 相关性分析揭示，声学相似度是微调性能的最强预测因子，而音素库存和类型学相似度更能解释零样本迁移表现。

Conclusion: 语言相似性（尤其是声学相似性）是指导极低资源ASR跨语言迁移中源语言选择的有效准则。该框架可推广至其他低资源语言的ASR系统构建。

Abstract: This paper investigates how language similarity can improve cross-lingual transfer for automatic speech recognition (ASR) in extremely low-resource settings. Warlpiri, an Australian Aboriginal language, has very limited transcribed speech data, making transfer learning essential. We propose a framework combining acoustic similarity from pre-trained speech models with linguistic similarity based on typology, phoneme inventories, grammatical, and syntactic features to rank high-resource source languages and evaluate their effectiveness for ASR transfer to Warlpiri. Experiments with Whisper show that acoustically and typologically similar languages outperform monolingual and multilingual baselines. Assamese and Hindi achieve substantial reductions in word and character error rates. Correlation analysis further indicates that acoustic similarity is the strongest predictor of fine-tuning performance, while phoneme inventory and typological similarity better explain zero-shot transfer.

</details>


### [85] [Language Re-generation: An investigation into information locality effects on reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10268)
*Amirhossein Mohammadi,Laurence E. Frank,Albert Gatt,Robert A. Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过重构框架研究了GPT-2从"不可能语言"中恢复自然英语的能力。研究发现，恢复后的结构表现出更短的依存距离，反映了模型对信息局部性的归纳偏置；恢复难度随局部性破坏程度增加而上升，且结构恢复与表面恢复相互解离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人研究多关注语言模型能否习得"不可能语言"，但未探究模型是否能从此类输入中恢复自然语言，以及这能揭示模型的何种归纳偏置。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过重构框架揭示仅靠可学性实验无法发现的架构偏置。

Method: 采用重构框架：将在三种扰动类型（局部打乱、全局打乱等）构造的"不可能语言"上预训练的GPT-2模型进行微调，使其重建自然英语。通过依存长度、依存Triple F1、Exact Match等指标评估结构恢复与表面恢复，并考察句子长度的调节效应。

Result: 恢复结构比原始文本具有更短的依存长度，体现了局部性偏好。恢复难度随局部性破坏程度递增；结构恢复（Triple F1）与表面恢复（Exact Match）解离；全局打乱条件下流畅度与忠实重建解离；长句在局部结构保留时促进恢复，但在全局打乱时导致完全崩溃；恢复难度与可学性难度在不同扰动类型间一致。

Conclusion: 信息局部性是约束语言模型学习与重构的共同原则。重构框架能够揭示传统可学性方法无法捕获的架构性归纳偏置，为理解语言模型的内部工作机制提供了新的方法论视角。

Abstract: Information locality, the tendency for syntactically related words to appear close together, shapes both human language processing and language model learning. While prior work has examined whether language models can acquire impossible languages, it remains unclear whether they can recover natural language from such input and what this reveals about their inductive biases. We address this by complementing learnability-based approaches with a reconstruction framework: fine-tuning GPT-2 models pre-trained on impossible languages to reconstruct natural English from three perturbation types. Our findings show that the recovered structures exhibit shorter dependency lengths than the original text, mirroring the locality preference observed in unconstrained language model generation and providing a quantitative signature of an architectural bias that learnability experiments alone do not reveal. Recovery difficulty increases with the degree of locality disruption. Structural recovery (dependency Triple F1) dissociates from surface recovery (Exact Match), while fluency dissociates from faithful reconstruction under global shuffling. Sentence length further modulates performance: longer sentences facilitate recovery when local structure is preserved but lead to complete collapse under global shuffling. Finally, recovery difficulty tracks learnability difficulty across perturbation types, suggesting that information locality is the shared constraint governing both.

</details>


### [86] [PolyInterview: An LLM-based Platform for Immersive Mock Interview Practice with Comprehensive Multimodal Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10310)
*Zhiyuan Wen,Jiannong Cao,Zijian Wang,Chen Chen,Xiaoyun Liu,Jianing Yin,Zhuo Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PolyInterview 是一个基于大语言模型的沉浸式模拟面试平台，利用数字人面试官进行多轮语音面试，并从内容、语音和非语言行为三个维度提供全面的多模态评估反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求职面试练习需求大但现实途径匮乏：真实面试机会少、专家模拟辅导成本高、自主练习缺乏自适应对话和结构化评估。现有系统仅能部分满足需求，如固定问题序列、有限交互通道或缺乏证据支持的反馈。

Method: PolyInterview 结合目标职位描述和求职者简历生成定制化问题，通过唇形同步的数字人面试官进行多轮语音面试并提问基于回答的追问；四个并行评估器提取13个行为级特征，聚合为10个评估维度和两个能力轨道；基于KSA和STAR框架生成报告，将每项评分与行为证据和可操作建议关联。

Result: 平台目前拥有101个账户、1,564次面试会话、7,665个生成问题及1,422个五阶段问题集。生成的问题在93.7%的会话中与目标职位描述的匹配度高于跨岗位职位描述。十位专家的评估认为问题计划质量高且反馈具有可操作性。

Conclusion: PolyInterview 实现了端到端的自适应模拟面试，将问题生成、多模态交互与循证评估整合到一个统一平台中，并已公开上线，展现出良好的生成质量和专家认可度。

Abstract: Preparing for job interviews is important for securing desired positions, yet realistic practice remains difficult to access: real interviews are infrequent, expert mock coaching is costly, and self-practice offers neither adaptive dialogue nor structured assessment. Existing systems typically address only parts of this need through fixed question sequences, limited communication channels, or feedback with little supporting evidence. We present PolyInterview, an LLM-based platform for immersive mock interview practice with comprehensive multimodal assessment. PolyInterview uses the target job description and CV to generate questions tailored to the role and candidate, conducts multi-turn spoken interviews with a lip-synced digital human interviewer that asks answer-aware follow-up questions, and evaluates response content, vocal delivery, and non-verbal behavior. Four parallel evaluators produce 13 behavior-level features that are aggregated into 10 assessment aspects and two competency tracks. Guided by the KSA and STAR frameworks, the report links each score to behavioral evidence and actionable recommendations. PolyInterview is publicly accessible. Its current all-account snapshot contains 101 accounts, 1,564 interview sessions, 7,665 generated questions, and 1,422 five-stage question sets. Generated questions are more closely aligned with their matched job description than with cross-role job descriptions in 93.7% of sessions. An evaluation by ten experts found strong question plans and actionable feedback.

</details>


### [87] [Polarization Detection: A Hybrid Approach with AfroXLMR-Social and DeBERTa for Low- and High-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10312)
*Muhammad Abdullahi Said*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对POLAR 2026共享任务，提出了一种混合建模策略：英语二分类使用DeBERTa，豪萨语及所有细粒度子任务使用AfroXLMR-Social，结合LoRA微调和文本数据增强，在三个子任务上均取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线极化现象迅速蔓延，威胁社会凝聚力，迫切需要能在跨语言场景下自动检测极化话语的鲁棒系统，尤其是对英语和低资源语言（豪萨语）的支持。

Method: 提出混合建模策略：(1) 英语二分类检测采用单语DeBERTa模型；(2) 豪萨语及全部细粒度子任务（类型与表现形式分类）采用领域自适应的多语言模型AfroXLMR-Social；(3) 使用低秩适应（LoRA）降低计算开销，并通过nlpaug进行文本数据增强以缓解数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在POLAR 2026共享任务的三个子任务（二分类检测、极化类型分类、极化表现形式分类）上均取得了有竞争力的结果，验证了针对不同子任务需求进行模型定制化选择能够实现性能最优平衡。

Conclusion: 针对子任务特性进行差异化模型选择（单语强模型 vs. 领域自适应多语言模型），结合参数高效微调和数据增强，是应对跨语言极化检测中计算资源受限与数据稀缺双重挑战的有效方案。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of online polarization threatens social cohesion, necessitating robust automated detection systems that operate effectively across diverse linguistic contexts. This paper presents our system description for the POLAR Shared Task 2026, focusing on the detection and characterization of polarized discourse in English and Hausa. We propose a hybrid modeling strategy: for English binary detection, we leverage the monolingual strength of \textbf{DeBERTa}, while for Hausa and all fine-grained subtasks (Types and Manifestations), we utilize \textbf{AfroXLMR-Social}. This domain-adapted multilingual model proved critical for capturing the nuances of polarization in social media text. To further address computational constraints and data scarcity, we implement Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and textual data augmentation via \texttt{nlpaug}. We report competitive results across all three subtasks, demonstrating that model selection tailored to specific subtask requirements yields the best balance of performance.

</details>


### [88] [Neutralizing Structural Inequality in the Nigerian FinTech Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10317)
*Muhammad Abdullahi Said*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层人机协同分诊模型，用于尼日利亚金融科技领域的POS欺诈检测，通过三梯队路由策略与动态影子定价机制，在提升欺诈召回率24.79个百分点的同时，将地区间性能差距从19.43个百分点压缩至2.88个百分点，有效中和了结构性偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融算法决策系统中，数据代理变量常无意中编码结构性不平等，即"歧视漂白"现象——系统将基础设施相关的偶然噪声（如农村网络超时）误读为欺诈意图。现有自主系统对此缺乏辨别能力，且完全自动化方案忽视了有限的人类专家资源与技能维持问题。

Method: 采用"众生平等"世界观，构建三层路由策略：(1) 以校准后的集成模型作为初级过滤器；(2) 将认知不确定性高的交易（如冷启动新账户）路由至专业分析师；(3) 高风险案件交由高级主管处理。引入动态影子价格机制配给有限的人类注意力资源，并通过随机审计机制防止人类技能退化。

Result: 实验表明，相比纯自主基线，模型实现了统计显著的1.88%互补性差距，欺诈召回提升24.79个百分点，地区性能差距从19.43个百分点降至2.88个百分点。

Conclusion: 分层人机协同为实质性机会平等提供了稳健机制，确保农村账户不因"环境性原生运气"而被排斥在数字经济之外。

Abstract: Algorithmic decision systems in financial services often rely on data proxies that inadvertently encode structural inequalities. This paper introduces a hierarchical human-AI triage model for Point of Sale fraud detection in the Nigerian FinTech sector. Adopting a We Are All Equal worldview, we address the challenge of discrimination laundering, wherein the system misinterprets infrastructure related aleatoric noise such as rural network timeouts as fraudulent intent. We implement a three-tier routing policy utilizing a calibrated ensemble model as a primary filter. The policy routes transactions characterized by epistemic uncertainty such as cold start new accounts to specialist analysts while reserving high stakes cases for a senior supervisor. To manage finite human capacity, we utilize a dynamic shadow price to ration human attention and implement a random audit mechanism to prevent human skill atrophy. Our experimental results demonstrate a statistically significant 1.88\% complementarity gap and a 24.79\% percentage point gain in fraud recall over an autonomous baseline. Crucially, the model reduces the regional performance gap from 19.43 to 2.88 percentage points, neutralizing structural bias. Hierarchical collaboration provides a robust mechanism for substantive equality of opportunity, ensuring that rural accounts are not excluded from the digital economy due to environmental brute luck.

</details>


### [89] [CAFE: A Compound-AI Factorial Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10380)
*Fabian Lukassen,Christoph Weisser,Thomas Kneib,Alexander Silbersdorff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAFE 是一个开源平台，将实验设计方法引入复合AI系统的评估，通过因子实验分析各组件的质量贡献、效应大小及显著性，并提供最佳配置、成本延迟权衡与评判可靠性报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI系统包含多个可互换组件（检索器、模型、提示等），实践者通常不清楚哪个组件对回答质量影响最大。现有工具大多仅搜索最优配置或孤立评分，缺乏对质量变异来源的归因与统计显著性判断。

Method: 将管道中每个可替换组件注册为因子，构建因子设计；运行所有配置组合，用可配置LLM评判器与人工评分者按统一量规打分；采用混合效应模型将回答质量方差归因至各组件及其交互作用，并报告效应量、显著性、最佳配置、成本与延迟权衡以及评判者-人类可靠性。

Result: 在HotpotQA数据集上的检索增强问答管道验证中，CAFE成功恢复了预设的因子效应，并在排列零假设下保持校准。

Conclusion: CAFE不仅能够找到最佳配置，还能解释哪个组件驱动质量变化及差异是否显著，填补了现有评估工具的空白，以Python包和Web应用形式发布。

Abstract: We introduce CAFE (Compound-AI Factorial Evaluation), an open-source platform that brings design of experiments to the evaluation of compound AI systems (CAIS). Such systems expose many interchangeable choices - e.g. which retriever, model, or prompt - and practitioners rarely know which of them most affects answer quality. With CAFE, a practitioner registers each swappable component of a pipeline as a factor to build a factorial design over the chosen factors, run the resulting configurations, and score the answers on a shared rubric using a configurable LLM judge together with human raters. From these ratings it attributes answer-quality variance to the components and their interactions with mixed-effects models and reports effect sizes, significance, the best configuration, cost and latency trade-offs, and judge-human reliability. Whereas existing tools mostly either search for a good configuration or score outputs in isolation, CAFE also explains which component drives quality and whether an observed difference is significant. We validate CAFE on a retrieval-augmented question-answering (QA) pipeline over the HotpotQA benchmark dataset, where it recovers planted factor effects and stays calibrated under a permutation null. CAFE is released as a Python package and as a Web application.

</details>


### [90] [Structured Thoughts For Improved Reasoning And Context Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10386)
*Zain Sarwar,Supriyo Chakraborty,Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Chia-Hsuan Lee,Anirban Das,Stephen Rawls,Kartik Balasubramaniam,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Structured Thoughts 框架，将推理过程组织为交替的 <try>（探索性草稿）和 <outcome>（提炼结论）块，通过微调使模型采用此结构化推理风格，在推理基准上最高提升 8.08%；同时支持上下文剪枝，平均节省 85% 内存，性能仅下降 8.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的长链思维推理虽然有效，但推理轨迹冗长且内存效率低下，既占用大量上下文窗口，又包含许多冗余信息。现有方法缺乏对推理过程的结构化组织，难以在保留关键推理结论的同时丢弃中间草稿。

Method: 提出 Structured Thoughts 框架：(1) 将推理轨迹分割为 <try> 块（探索性草稿）和 <outcome> 块（提炼结论），形成交替结构；(2) 通过提示 LLM 对每个步骤进行摘要来构建结构化思维数据集；(3) 在该数据集上微调预训练基础模型，使其内化结构化推理风格；(4) 利用显式结构实现上下文剪枝：每对 <try>/<outcome> 完成后即可删除 <try> 块，仅保留结论。

Result: 相比标准 SFT，结构化推理微调在推理基准上性能提升最高达 8.08%；概念验证的剪枝实现在数学任务上平均节省 85% 内存/上下文，伴随 8.67% 的性能下降。

Conclusion: Structured Thoughts 框架通过显式的 <try>/<outcome> 块组织推理过程，在提升推理性能的同时实现高效的上下文压缩。该工作在推理质量与内存效率之间取得了良好的权衡，为长链推理的高效部署提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at generating long chains of thought, but long reasoning traces are often verbose and memory-inefficient. In this work, we introduce Structured Thoughts, a framework that organizes reasoning into alternating <try> and <outcome> blocks: <try> captures exploratory scratch work, while <outcome> contains the distilled conclusion of that step. We construct a dataset of structured thoughts by segmenting reasoning traces into <try> blocks and prompting an LLM to summarize each step into its corresponding <outcome>. Fine-tuning pretrained foundation models on this reformatted data produces models that adopt the structured reasoning style, leading to performance gains of up to 8.08\% on reasoning benchmarks compared to standard SFT. The explicit structure also enables context pruning: after each <try>/<outcome> pair, the <try> can be pruned, allowing the model to retain conclusions without keeping the full scratch work in the context. A proof-of-concept pruning implementation achieves an average of 85\% memory / context savings with an 8.67\% performance drop across mathematical tasks.

</details>


### [91] [A Stepwise Questioning Expert-Editor Multi-Agent Framework for Long-Document Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10390)
*Lingyun Shen,Xuejia Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"专家-编辑"分步提问的多智能体方法，用于解决大语言模型在长文档摘要任务中输入长度受限的问题，通过在两个长文档科学数据集上实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在长文档摘要任务中面临输入长度限制的挑战，而智能体（agent）技术为增强LLMs固有能力提供了可能。现有方法难以有效处理超出输入限制的长文档摘要。

Method: 提出"专家-编辑"分步提问多智能体方法（expert-editor stepwise questioning multi-agent method）。其中，专家智能体和编辑智能体分别从内容的不同方面提出问题，并提供针对性的修订线索，引导另一个智能体逐步优化摘要质量。

Result: 在两个代表性长文档科学数据集上开展实验，采用广泛认可的自动评估指标进行评价，实验结果证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的专家-编辑分步提问多智能体方法能够有效提升基于LLM的长文档摘要效果，为突破LLM输入长度限制提供了可行的多智能体协作方案。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promising potential in news summarization tasks, their performance on long-document summarization remains challenging as their length often exceeds the input limits. As the agent investment, which provide possibility to improve the inherent capabilities of LLMs. To enhance the effectiveness of long-document summarization based on LLMs, this paper proposes an expert-editor stepwise questioning multi-agent method, in which the expert and the editor guide another agent to refine the summary by posing questions on different aspects of the content and providing targeted clues for revision. We conducted experiments on two representative long-document scientific datasets and evaluated the results through widely recognized automatic metrics. The results demonstrated the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [92] [Enjoy Your Talk: A Human-Centered Benchmark for Multi-Turn Dialogue with Decoupled User Simulation, Target Modeling, and Judging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10428)
*Jinglan Gong,Jiefan Lu,Hewei Guo,Kehan Li,Zhiyuan Han,Jihang Jiang,Wenwen Tong,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了 EYT-Bench，一个面向多轮对话的人本评估基准，采用三方解耦设计（用户模拟器、目标模型、独立裁判），引入意图漂移和最终意图完成率两个轨迹级指标，在 17 个模型 × 200 条对话的评估中揭示了推理能力、角色格式对客观指标影响的四个关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单轮基准无法评估 LLM 作为多轮对话伙伴所需的关键能力：角色一致性、动态意图追踪、情感动态和目标完成。需要一个人本设计的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 提出 EYT-Bench，采用三方解耦架构：(1) 基于公开人工策划语料（Nemotron-Personas-USA 和 PersonaMem-v2）采样的角色扎根用户模拟器，避免 LLM 合成引入的角色偏差；(2) 目标模型将意图感知与回复生成分离；(3) 独立的第三方 LLM 裁判，支持多裁判集成。引入两个轨迹级指标：基于嵌入的意图漂移和最终意图完成率 (FICR)。在 17 个目标模型 × 200 条对话上评估。

Result: 四个发现：(i) 闭源与开源 SOTA 模型在主观维度（共情/角色一致性/拟人化）上差异 ≤0.3，但在客观意图追踪上差异可达 9 倍；(ii) 推理模式（“思考开启”）显著提升长上下文角色的潜在意图准确率（Gemma-4 上 +0.47–0.50），而主观评分几乎不变；(iii) 角色格式主导轨迹分布差异，FICR 在 Nemotron-USA 上饱和至 >0.95，而在 PersonaMem-v2 上从 0.53 到 0.88 分散；(iv) 16/17 模型存在稳健的热身效应（GPT-5.5 为异常值，效应反转），排名在 α ∈ [0.05, 0.15] 区间内稳定。跨裁判消融（使用 deepseek-v4-pro）确认目标排名和最终意图满意度在不同裁判间保持一致。

Conclusion: EYT-Bench 通过解耦设计和人本角色采样，提供了比合成角色基准更可靠的多轮对话评估。结果表明客观意图追踪指标比主观评分更能区分模型能力，推理模式对客观指标有显著增益但主观体验几乎不变，为后续对话系统评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) as multi-turn conversational partners requires probing capabilities that single-turn benchmarks miss: persona consistency, evolving intent tracking, emotional dynamics, and goal completion. We introduce EYT-Bench, a human-centered benchmark built around a three-party decoupled design: a persona-grounded user simulator, a target model that separates intent perception from response generation, and an independent third-party LLM judge with optional multi-judge ensembling. Personas are sampled from public human-curated corpora, Nemotron-Personas-USA and PersonaMem-v2, rather than synthesized, reducing LLM-induced persona bias. EYT-Bench also introduces two trajectory-level metrics: embedding-based intent drift and final-intent completion rate (FICR), inspired by tau-bench. In a 17-target x 200-dialogue evaluation, EYT-Bench reveals four findings: (i) state-of-the-art closed- and open-source models are statistically close on subjective dimensions (empathy / persona / anthropomorphism vary within <= 0.3), but differ by up to 9x on objective intent tracking; (ii) reasoning ("thinking on") sharply improves objective tracking on long-context personas (+0.47-0.50 latent-intent accuracy on Gemma-4) while leaving subjective scores nearly unchanged; (iii) persona format dominates trajectory spread, with FICR saturating above 0.95 on Nemotron-USA but spreading from 0.53 to 0.88 on PersonaMem-v2; and (iv) the warm-up effect is robust on 16/17 models (one outlier, GPT-5.5, reverses the effect), with stable rankings across alpha in [0.05, 0.15]. A cross-judge ablation using deepseek-v4-pro confirms that target rankings and final-intent satisfaction are preserved across judges.

</details>


### [93] [Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models Using Diversion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10476)
*Basel Abdeen,S M Tahmid Siddiqui,Meah Tahmeed Ahmed,Anoop Singhal,Latifur Khan,Punya Parag Modi,Ehab Al-Shaer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"分流解码"（diversion decoding）方法，通过在解码阶段主动挑战模型生成的响应来构建LLM不确定性启发式指标，用于检测幻觉，该方法相比现有方案计算复杂度更低且性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在幻觉问题，即生成事实错误或编造知识，严重影响其可靠性和可信度。现有的不确定性评估和幻觉检测方法多为概率性方法，且计算开销大，限制了实际部署。因此需要一种高效、鲁棒的替代方案。

Method: 提出"分流解码"方法：在解码阶段主动挑战模型的生成响应，提取能够刻画LLM对产生替代答案的"抗拒程度"的特征，然后利用这些特征训练一个机器学习模型，最终构建出衡量LLM不确定性的启发式指标。

Result: 实验结果表明，分流解码在幻觉检测任务上优于现有方法，且计算复杂度显著降低，验证了其作为高效、鲁棒解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 分流解码是一种兼顾效率与性能的LLM不确定性评估新范式，通过解码阶段的主动干预和轻量级机器学习模型，实现了低计算成本的幻觉检测，具有良好的实用前景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for retrieving knowledge through seamless, human-like interactions. Despite their advanced text generation capabilities, LLMs exhibit hallucination tendencies, where they generate factually incorrect statements and fabricate knowledge, undermining their reliability and trustworthiness. Multiple studies have explored methods to evaluate LLM uncertainty and detect hallucinations. However, existing approaches are often probabilistic and computationally expensive, limiting their practical applicability. In this paper, we introduce diversion decoding, a novel method for developing an LLM uncertainty heuristic by actively challenging model-generated responses during the decoding phase. Through diversion decoding, we extract features that capture the LLM's resistance to produce alternative answers and utilize these features to train a machine-learning model to develop a heuristic measure of the LLM's uncertainty. Our experimental results demonstrate that diversion decoding outperforms existing methods with significantly lower computational complexity, making it an efficient and robust solution for evaluating hallucination detection.

</details>


### [94] [When Reasoning Hurts Legal Drafting: The Verbalization Bottleneck in Patent Claim Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10480)
*Lekang Jiang,Wenjun Sun,Stephan Goetz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了思维链(CoT)提示在专利权利要求生成中的效果，发现隐式CoT（内部推理）始终优于显式CoT（口头化推理），并揭示了显式推理通过关键细节抽象化、生成模式中断和级联错误传播三种机制损害输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利权利要求起草是一项高度结构化、模式敏感的法律起草任务，需要进行精确的语言控制和复杂的逻辑关系维护。近期研究表明CoT在高度结构化任务中效果有限甚至负面，因此需要系统考察CoT提示是否真正有利于专利权利要求生成这一特定法律任务。

Method: 提出一种面向专利权利要求生成的特定任务CoT方法，通过自动指标评估与人类专家评估相结合的方式，系统对比显式CoT与隐式CoT的效果差异，并对显式CoT造成的质量损失进行机制分析。

Result: 推理增强提示可提升权利要求质量；隐式CoT（推理保持内部化）一致优于显式CoT（推理口头化）。显式CoT引入不必要的信息瓶颈，通过三种机制损害输出质量：关键细节抽象化、内部生成模式中断、级联错误传播。

Conclusion: 在法律起草等高度结构化任务中，显式CoT的口头化推理可能适得其反，隐式推理是更优策略。该发现为法律任务与CoT应用提供了新见解，对LLM在专业领域应用具有重要启示。

Abstract: Patent claim drafting is a challenging legal drafting task that requires technical expertise, precise linguistic control, strict adherence to formal conventions, and the preservation of complex logical relationships among claim elements. While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has been widely used to improve the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), recent evidence suggests that its benefits may be limited, or even negative, in highly structured or pattern-sensitive tasks. Therefore, this paper investigates whether CoT prompting benefits patent claim generation. We propose a task-specific CoT method for patent claim generation and evaluate its effectiveness through both automatic metrics and human expert assessment. Our results show that reasoning-enhanced prompting can improve claim quality. Moreover, we demonstrate a counter-intuitive but important empirical finding: implicit CoT, where reasoning is kept internal rather than explicitly verbalized, consistently outperforms explicit CoT. Through systematic analysis, we show that explicit CoT can introduce an unnecessary information bottleneck for claim generation. Verbalized reasoning may compromise the quality of final outputs through three specific mechanisms: abstraction of critical details, disruption of internalized generation patterns, and cascading error propagation. Our findings provide new insights into legal tasks and CoT applications.

</details>


### [95] [Articulate Intuition or Genuine Analysis? Benchmarking Epistemic Reliability in LLM-as-a-Judge Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10511)
*Nuo Chen,Qian Wang,Qingyun Zou,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Kahneman4Review基准，利用卡尼曼双过程理论构建同行评审评估框架。研究发现：决策层级与文本认识质量不相关、LLM代理评审在原始分数上虚高但主要由长度和发表场所解释、ICLR评审文本特征在2022-2023出现显著变化。核心论点是：LLM评审可信度基准必须区分分析形式与认识功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当LLM评审被评价为"分析性强"、人类评审被评为"高质量"时，二者衡量的是否为同一事物？作者认为并非如此，且这一差异具有哲学意义。现有评审质量评估混淆了表面的分析形式与深层的认识功能，需要建立能区分二者的可信度基准。

Method: 将卡尼曼双过程理论（系统1快速直觉/系统2慢速分析）操作化为结构化评审评分表，涵盖9个文本维度、8个偏差诊断指标和1个连续推理质量分数。构建Kahneman4Review基准（3563条评审）。通过匹配功能探针实验区分真实错误发现与表面流畅性。

Result: 三大发现：(1)决策层级与基于文本的认识质量代理指标无显著关联；(2)公开展示的LLM代理评审原始分数高于人类评审，但差距主要由长度和发表场所解释，且样本未按论文配对；(3)ICLR评审文本特征在2022-2023年出现时序性转变，与LLM广泛可用时间吻合但无法确定因果关系。功能探针实验显示评分表能区分真实错误发现与表面流畅性。

Conclusion: 构建LLM评审可信度基准必须将"分析形式"与"认识功能"分离，并提出具体设计建议。交互演示已上线。

Abstract: When an LLM judge calls a peer review analytical and a human committee calls another review high quality, are they tracking the same thing? We argue they are not, and that the difference matters philosophically. We operationalise Kahneman's dual-process theory into a structured rubric for peer review and release Kahneman4Review, a benchmark of 3,563 rated reviews scored along nine theoretically motivated textual dimensions, eight bias diagnostics, and a continuous reasoning-quality score. Three findings bear on trustworthiness: decision tier is not detectably aligned with the rubric's text-grounded epistemic-quality proxy; public-showcase agentic reviews receive higher raw scores than pooled human reviews, but length and venue explain most of the gap and the samples are not paper-paired; and ICLR review-text diagnostics shift at the 2022--2023 transition, temporally coincident with widespread LLM availability but without identifying its cause. A matched function-probe pilot further shows that the rubric distinguishes textual probes designed to contrast genuine fault-finding with surface fluency. We argue that a trustworthy reliability benchmark for LLM judges must separate analytical form from epistemic function, and propose concrete design choices toward that goal. An interactive demo is available at https://huggingface.co/spaces/nuojohnchen/Kahneman4Review.

</details>


### [96] [UNIBROWSE: A Data-to-Agent Framework for Multimodal BrowseComp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10557)
*Xiyu Wei,Qingwei Zong,Zhuocheng Yu,Sujian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UNIBROWSE 提出首个统一数据管线，同时覆盖文本、图像到文本、文本到图像三种信息流模式，结合实时网络检索增强的知识图谱和探索度指标筛选高信号数据。基于此管线训练 35B 规模智能体，在多模态 BrowseComp 基准上取得 54.4 平均准确率，超越 GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro 等闭源方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界多模态网页浏览存在三种信息流模式（纯文本、图像到文本、文本到图像），但现有数据构建方法仅覆盖前两种，文本到图像模式几乎未被涉及。这种覆盖缺口限制了智能体的泛化能力和鲁棒性，因此需要一种能同时覆盖全部三种模式的统一数据生成方法。

Method: 提出 UNIBROWSE 统一数据管线，包含三个核心组件：(1) 首次同时生成覆盖纯文本、图像到文本、文本到图像三种模式的训练数据；(2) 利用实时网络检索增强策划的知识图谱以提升数据保真度；(3) 引入"探索度"（exploration degree）这一新指标，用于过滤低信号实例，从而提高强化学习效率。基于该管线生成高质量冷启动工具使用轨迹和富含探索的 QA 对，随后通过监督微调（SFT）和探索感知的强化学习（exploration-aware RL）训练 35B 规模模型。

Result: UNIBROWSE 在五个多模态 BrowseComp 基准上平均准确率达 54.4，相较基座模型 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 提升 10.5 个百分点，超越 GPT-5（42.9）、Gemini-2.5 Pro（44.8）和 Gemini-2.5 Flash（41.3）等多个闭源智能体工作流。

Conclusion: UNIBROWSE 通过统一覆盖全部三种信息流模式、实时检索增强及探索度过滤，有效提升了多模态网页浏览智能体的性能与鲁棒性，在多个基准上取得最优结果，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal BrowseComp tasks require agents to combine perception, tool use, and long-horizon reasoning over dynamic web content, challenging their ability to handle compositional structure, open-world uncertainty, and multimodal integration across extended interactions. Crucially, real-world multimodal browsing involves three distinct information-flow patterns: text-only, image-to-text, and text-to-image, yet existing data construction methods cover only the text-only and image-to-text patterns, leaving text-to-image largely unaddressed and limiting agent generality and robustness. We introduce UNIBROWSE, a unified data pipeline that for the first time simultaneously generates training data covering all three patterns, augments curated knowledge graphs with live web retrieval for improved fidelity, and introduces a novel metric of exploration degree to filter low-signal instances for efficient reinforcement learning. Through this pipeline, we produce high-quality cold-start tool-use trajectories and exploration-rich QA pairs, and train a 35B-scale agent via supervised fine-tuning and exploration-aware RL.The resulting UNIBROWSE agent achieves state-of-the-art performance on multimodal BrowseComp benchmarks, attaining an average accuracy of 54.4 across five diverse benchmarks -- an improvement of 10.5 points over its base model Qwen3.5-35B-A3B -- and surpassing serveral closed-source agent workflows such as GPT-5 (42.9), Gemini-2.5 Pro (44.8), and Gemini-2.5 Flash (41.3).

</details>


### [97] [Demographic Prompting at Scale: When More Attributes Hurt LLM--Human Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10590)
*Mahammed Kamruzzaman,Shrabon Kumar Das,Gene Louis Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在提示中注入标注者人口统计属性如何影响LLM预测与人类标注的对齐。研究发现：对齐在1-3个高信号属性时达到峰值，全属性反而导致退化；人口统计影响力不能单独预测对齐改善，需同时考虑属性的可学习性和方向一致性；神经元探测显示仅在标注信号一致时，专门化激活才与对齐增益相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在主观标注任务中与人类判断存在偏差，而标注者的人口统计属性（如年龄、性别、种族等）已知会影响标注结果。现有研究对如何在提示中利用这些属性来改善LLM对齐缺乏系统性理解，特别是在属性数量、组合方式及作用机制方面存在空白。

Method: 采用五款开源LLM，在五个任务上系统性地变化提示中的人口统计属性数量和组合——从单属性到全属性配置的每一种组合。通过对比LLM预测与人类标注的对齐程度，分析属性的信号质量（可学习性与方向一致性），并结合神经元探针技术考察模型内部激活模式与对齐增益的关系。

Result: （1）对齐在1-3个高信号属性时达到峰值，全属性配置下对齐反而下降，揭示了明确的"过度指定阈值"；（2）人口统计属性对人类标注的整体影响幅度并不能预测该属性能否改善LLM对齐，需联合考虑属性信号的可学习性和方向一致性；（3）神经元探针表明，仅在标注信号方向一致时，专门化激活才与对齐增益相关，且激活量本身并不意味着可操控性。

Conclusion: 人口统计提示并非一种单一、普适的干预手段；其实用性高度依赖上下文，受属性信号质量、任务特征及模型架构的共同塑造。该发现为如何在LLM提示工程中审慎设计人口统计信息提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We investigate how annotator demographic attributes, supplied as prompt cues, shape the alignment between large language model (LLM) predictions and human annotations across five tasks. Using five open-source LLMs, we systematically vary the number and composition of demographic components in the prompt, spanning every combination from single-attribute through full-attribute configurations. Our experiments reveal three principal findings. First, alignment consistently peaks with one to three high-signal attributes and degrades under the full attribute set, establishing a clear over-specification threshold. Second, the overall magnitude of demographic influence on human annotations does not predict which attributes improve LLM alignment; instead, both the learnability and the directional coherence of each attribute's annotation signal need to be considered jointly. Third, neuron probing reveals that specialized activation correlates with alignment gains only under coherent annotation signals, and that activation volume alone does not imply steerability. Together, these results demonstrate that demographic prompting is not a monolithic intervention: its utility is highly context-dependent, shaped by attribute signal quality, task characteristics, and model architecture.

</details>


### [98] [Non-binary bottom-up constituency parsing without arity actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10591)
*Jungyeul Park,Eunkyul Leah Jo,Zihao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无歧义动作的 bottom-up 选区解析器，通过定界符（delimiter）和标签标记（label marker）来隐式确定归约的元数（arity），从而将显式的 arity 动作替换为栈配置的派生属性。该方法在 PTB 和 CTB 上取得与基线相当的性能，同时大幅缩减了动作表大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非二叉 bottom-up 选区解析通常需要带有元数参数的动作（如 Reduce-X#k），导致动作表膨胀。作者希望证明 arity 参数并非必要的转移基元，从而实现动作空间的因式分解（factorization），减小动作库存，同时不牺牲解析精度。

Method: 作者提出「定界符引导的解析器」（delimiter-guided parser）：在栈配置中引入定界符来标记短语边界，归约动作仅指定母标签而无需元数信息——归约跨度由当前活跃定界符和标签标记唯一确定，元数成为栈状态的派生属性而非动作标签的一部分。

Result: 在 PTB（宾州树库）和 CTB（中文树库）上的实验表明，该解析器在相同实现框架下与需要显式元数的 bottom-up 基线保持竞争力，动作库存显著缩小。分析还显示，预测的元数分布与金标树库接近，且去除元数动作并未导致高元数成分的塌缩。

Conclusion: 元数信息可以从解析器状态中派生，无需作为动作参数显式编码。这种因式分解策略有效缩减了动作空间，为非二叉 bottom-up 选区解析提供了一种更简洁的转移系统设计范式。

Abstract: Non-binary bottom-up constituency parsing is usually taken to require arity actions: reductions such as \(\textsc{Reduce-}X\#k\) specify both the mother label and the number of children to be composed. We show that this arity parameter is not a necessary transition primitive. Our parser introduces constituent labels separately and recovers reduction spans from delimiter-bounded stack configurations. In a well-formed reduction configuration, arity is uniquely determined by the active delimiter and the label marker, making it a derived property of parser state rather than an action label. This factorization removes label--arity-specific reduce actions while preserving direct construction of original non-binary trees. Experiments on PTB and CTB show that the delimiter-guided parser remains competitive with an arity-specific bottom-up baseline under the same implementation framework, with substantially smaller action inventories. Analyses further show that its predicted arity profile remains close to the gold treebanks and that high-arity constituents do not collapse when arity actions are removed.

</details>


### [99] [Eval-Pair Matrix: Answer-Paired Meta-Evaluation of LLM Judges for Grounded RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10626)
*Sriram Selvam,Anneswa Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Eval-Pair Matrix协议，通过在同一候选答案上配对评判者的方法，发现RAG评估中评判者自利偏差趋于零，真正的稳健差异在于匹配评判者对规避诱导声明的答案标记率更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-a-judge评估中，生成器和评判器使用同一模型族可能导致难以识别的自利偏差。需要一种受控元评估协议来系统检验这一问题。

Method: 提出Eval-Pair Matrix协议：从GaRAGe数据集中引入隐藏的答案-因果矛盾，用GPT、Grok、Gemini从扰动段落生成答案，再用相同模型作为盲评进行评判。在300条核心记录上进行3×3交叉矩阵实验，核心分析采用配对方法——将评判器配对到完全相同的候选答案上，而非比较对角线与非对角线单元格。

Result: 对角线与非对角线F1相似；配对同模型召回率效应接近零（-0.5 pp，95% CI [-2.7, +1.7]）。唯一稳健的配对差距是匹配评判器对规避诱导声明的答案的标记率更低（-4.3 pp）。人工评估发现报告的假阳性实际上属于替代源错误检测、标注错误或模糊案例，无真正误报。

Conclusion: 方法论层面的启示：RAG评判器研究应报告完整矩阵、答案配对效应、行为分层以及标签-任务对齐。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge evaluation is widely used for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), but reusing the same model family as both generator and judge makes self-leniency difficult to identify. We introduce Eval-Pair Matrix, a controlled meta evaluation protocol for source-grounded RAG. Starting from GaRAGe questions and grounding passages, we induce one hidden answer-causal contradiction per record, generate answers from perturbed passages with GPT, Grok, and Gemini models, and then use the same models as blind judges to evaluate each answer against the original passages. The experiment contains 300 core records, 897 labeled generator outputs, and 2,683 judge verdicts in a crossed 3 x 3 matrix; the primary analysis uses 275 fully validated records. Instead of comparing diagonal and off-diagonal cells across different answers, we estimate same-model effects by pairing judges on the exact same candidate answer. This changes the interpretation: diagonal and off diagonal F1 are similar, and the paired same-model recall effect is near zero (-0.5 pp; 95% cluster bootstrap CI [-2.7, +1.7]). The only robust paired gap is lower matching-judge flagging for answers that avoided the induced claim (-4.3 pp). A targeted human evaluation finds that reviewed apparent false positives are alternate source-error detections, mistakes in labeling whether the induced claim was adopted, or unclear cases; none were adjudicated as genuine false alarms. The lesson is methodological: RAG judge studies should report full matrices, answer-paired effects, behavior strata, and label-task alignment.

</details>


### [100] [Anamnesis: An Open-Source Platform for Large-Scale Backstory-Conditioned Survey Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10628)
*Song-Ze Yu,Joseph Suh,Serina Chang,David M. Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Anamnesis 是一个基于大语言模型的开源交互式调查模拟系统，通过结构化叙事背景条件模型响应，实现对虚拟人群的民主可控调查模拟，在政治民调和偏好模拟两项案例中比标准角色提示基线更贴合真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的调查模拟方法（如简单角色提示）生成的意见分布与真实世界调查数据存在偏差，且缺乏面向非技术用户的透明、可复现、开源的替代方案。作者旨在通过结构化叙事框架（Anthology/Alterity）来更真实地调控虚拟受访者的条件响应，从而实现对调查工具的原型设计与压力测试。

Method: 提出 Anamnesis 交互式系统，集成 Anthology 和 Alterity 框架：前者利用结构化叙事背景（backstories）为LLM赋予人口统计特征和生命历程；后者处理群体层面的异质性。系统支持开放式生成、概率性人口重采样以及多模态（图像和音频）调查，并通过统一Web界面面向非技术用户。评估采用两个案例研究：复制皮尤研究中心ATP面板的政治类型学和生物医学问题，模拟《纽约客》漫画标题竞赛中的人类偏好。

Result: 在两个案例研究中，Anamnesis 生成的意见分布比标准角色提示（persona-prompting）基线更贴近真实世界调查数据，验证了结构化叙事条件方法在提高模拟真实性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Anamnesis 为调查模拟提供了一种透明、可复现且开源的新范式，其结构化叙事方法能更准确地复现真实人群的意见分布，有望作为专有模拟服务的有力替代方案，降低调查研究中预测试和压力测试的门槛。

Abstract: We present Anamnesis, an interactive system for demographically controllable survey simulation using large language models. Open-source, and designed for non-technical users/researchers, Anamnesis enables the prototyping and stress-testing of survey instruments on virtual populations rather than real human subjects. The platform operationalizes the recently introduced Anthology and Alterity frameworks, which use structured narrative backstories to condition model responses, within a unified web interface. It supports open-ended generation, probabilistic demographic resampling, and multimodal (image and audio) surveys. We evaluate the system through two case studies: (1) replicating segments of Pew Research Center's American Trends Panel (ATP) on political typology and biomedical issues and (2) emulating human preference in the New Yorker Caption Contest. In both cases, Anamnesis produces opinion distributions that more closely match real-world survey data than standard persona-prompting baselines, offering a transparent, reproducible, and open-source alternative to proprietary simulation services.

</details>


### [101] [MafiaScope: Non-Invasive, Time-Resolved Belief Probing for LLM Agents in Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10645)
*Ilia Karpov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MafiaScope，一个将社交推理游戏《黑手党》转化为机器心理理论测量工具的开放测试平台，通过结构化探针问题追踪 LLM 智能体的信念轨迹，并揭示了其置信度校准差、高估被怀疑概率等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体的公开行为几乎无法反映其内在的社会推理过程——投票正确可能是猜测，善于撒谎则不留痕迹。因此需要一种能够测量机器心理理论（Machine Theory of Mind）的方法，绕过外显行为直接探查智能体的内部信念状态。

Method: 将社交推理游戏《黑手党》作为实验场景，在每次公开发言后，每个智能体私下回答一组可配置的结构化探针问题；答案不回流游戏中，由引擎根据已知真实状态自动评分。系统提供交互式可视化器，支持：(1) 化身模式（以某智能体的视角观看游戏）；(2) 时间线对齐的准确率和校准面板；(3) 反事实重放——从任意记录步骤分叉重演。

Result: 在 32 局 DeepSeek 案例研究中，解析了 13,815 条探针回答。主要发现：(1) 置信度校准差，期望校准误差（ECE）为 0.17；(2) 智能体高估自己被怀疑的程度，过度预测被怀疑的概率达 1.5 倍；(3) 通过 30 分叉重放实验完整演示了反事实重放工作流。引擎、查看器和 200+ 跨模型对局语料均开源发布。

Conclusion: MafiaScope 成功将社交推理游戏改造为机器心理理论的测量工具，揭示了 LLM 在社交推理场景中信念校准和元认知方面的系统偏差。该平台为研究智能体的内部信念动态、反事实推理和社交认知提供了可复现、可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: An LLM agent's public behaviour reveals little about its social reasoning: an agent that votes correctly may be guessing, and an agent that lies well leaves no trace of what it actually believes. We present MafiaScope, an open testbed that turns the social deduction game Mafia into a measurement instrument for machine Theory of Mind. After every public utterance, every agent privately answers a configurable set of structured probe questions; the answers never re-enter the game and are scored automatically against the ground truth the engine knows. An interactive visualizer renders the belief trajectories: impersonate mode shows the game as one agent sees it, panels chart timeline-aligned accuracy and calibration, and counterfactual replay forks any recorded step. In a 32-game DeepSeek case study with 13{,}815 parsed probe answers, stated confidence is poorly calibrated, with expected calibration error 0.17, agents over-predict being suspected 1.5 times, and a 30-fork replay experiment walks the counterfactual replay workflow end to end. Engine, viewer and a corpus of 200+ cross-model games are released under an open licence; live demo: https://karpovilia.github.io/mafiascope/; screencast: https://vimeo.com/1208920221.

</details>


### [102] [Knowledge Distillation for Automated AI Tutor Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10647)
*Tahmid Al Hannan,Diego Garcia,Alex Njoroge,Suha Al Juboori,Tarek Sakakini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FATE，一个基于8B参数语言模型的AI导师评估器，针对BEA 2025共享任务的四个评估维度（错误识别、错误定位、引导能力和可操作性），通过从大型模型进行知识蒸馏来缓解标注数据不足的问题，性能提升最高达22.63个百分点，并用其评估了主流商业模型的教学质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM快速融入K-12和高等教育，但缺乏可靠的方法来评估其作为AI导师的教学质量。教育评估领域标注数据稀缺，亟需专门的自动化评估工具。

Method: 提出FATE（FLC AI Tutor Evaluator），一个8B参数的语言模型，专门用于评估AI导师。模型对齐BEA 2025共享任务四个评估维度。为应对标注数据有限的问题，采用知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）从前沿LLM生成额外监督信号来训练模型。

Result: 知识蒸馏带来最高22.63个百分点的绝对性能提升。使用FATE对主流商业模型进行基准测试：Gemini 2.5 Flash表现最佳（82.88%），其次为ChatGPT 5.5 Instant（80.75%）、DeepSeek V4 Flash（80.13%）和Claude Sonnet 4.6（74.00%）。

Conclusion: FATE展示了专门化小型模型在自动化教育评估中的可行性和有效性，知识蒸馏是解决教育领域标注数据稀缺的有效策略，同时为商业模型的教学能力提供了基准参考。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into K-12 and higher education has outpaced the development of reliable methods for evaluating their pedagogical quality. As the research community starts to explore the space of automating evaluation of AI tutors, we introduce FATE (FLC AI Tutor Evaluator), a specialized 8B-parameter language model designed to evaluate AI tutors. Aligned with the four core evaluation tracks from the BEA 2025 Shared Task, our model assesses pedagogical ability across Mistake Identification, Mistake Location, Guidance, and Actionability. Because pedagogical evaluation is a specialized task with limited labeled data, we leverage knowledge distillation from a frontier LLM to generate additional supervision, yielding absolute performance gains up to 22.63 percentage points. Finally, we demonstrate FATE's utility as an automated evaluator by benchmarking instructional responses generated by popular commercial models, including ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, and DeepSeek. On average, we have found that Gemini 2.5 Flash perfomed best (82.88%), then ChatGPT 5.5 Instant (80.75%), followed by DeepSeek V4 Flash (80.13%) and Claude Sonnet 4.6 (74.00%).

</details>


### [103] [Unlocking Parallelism in Autoregressive Language Models via Speculative Decoding with Progressive Tree Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10661)
*Zipeng Gao,Zhi Zheng,Qingrong Xia,Junda Lin,Ziwei Zhao,Tong Xu,Zhefeng Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PTD 提出了一种无需训练、模型无关的渐进式树状并行推测解码策略，通过结构化引导和逐步剪枝，在单次前向传播中探索多条语义路径，实现了最高 2 倍的解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测解码依赖辅助草稿模块，带来训练和通信开销；而现有基于目标模型自身生成草稿的方法，由于缺乏结构协调，未能充分挖掘模型的并行能力。

Method: 提出 Progressive Tree Drafting (PTD)：将渐进式树结构与逐步剪枝机制耦合，在单次前向传播中引导 LLM 并行探索多条语义路径，保证草稿的多样性与连贯性。

Result: 在多个基准测试上实现最高 2 倍的解码加速，且无需训练、不依赖特定模型架构。

Conclusion: PTD 通过结构化的并行草稿策略有效释放了目标模型自身的并行潜力，在保持训练无关和模型无关的优点的同时，取得了显著的推理加速效果。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has significantly accelerated Large Language Model (LLM) inference by alleviating memory-bound bottlenecks. However, traditional speculative decoding typically relies on auxiliary draft modules, incurring significant training and communication overhead. Although recent methods attempt to generate drafts within the target model itself, they often fail to fully exploit its latent parallel capacity due to a lack of structural coordination. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Progressive Tree Drafting (PTD)}, which employs a structured, guided parallel drafting strategy to harness the model's parallel potential. By coupling a progressive tree structure with a stepwise pruning mechanism, PTD actively guides the LLM to explore multiple semantic paths in a single forward pass, ensuring both draft diversity and coherence. Experiments demonstrate that PTD achieves up to $2\times$ decoding speedup across various benchmarks while remaining training-free and model-agnostic. Our code is available at: https://github.com/MINE-USTC/PTD.

</details>


### [104] [A Corpus of Persuasion Techniques in Slavic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10715)
*Jakub Piskorski,Dimitar Iliyanov Dimitrov,Marina Ernst,Jacek Haneczok,Michał Marcińczuk,Arkadiusz Modzelewski,Roman Yangarber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向斯拉夫语系的劝说技术语料库，包含保加利亚语、波兰语和俄语文档，标注了25种细粒度劝说技术（分属6大类修辞策略），涵盖约7500个文本片段，并提供经典ML和生成式AI的基线检测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 劝说技术是影响舆论的重要修辞手段，但现有语料库多集中于英语等主流语言，针对斯拉夫语言的多语言劝说技术标注资源严重匮乏，制约了跨语言劝说技术检测研究的发展。

Method: 构建包含保加利亚语、波兰语和俄语三语种的劝说技术语料库，采用25种细粒度劝说技术分类体系（归为6大类修辞策略），在粗粒度文本片段级和细粒度句子级两个层次进行标注。共标注222篇文档（涵盖国内及国际热点争议话题），产生约7500个标注文本片段。同时使用经典机器学习模型和生成式AI模型建立基线，分别在文本片段级和句子级进行劝说技术的检测与分类。

Result: 成功构建了首个面向斯拉夫语系的多语言劝说技术语料库，提供了详细的语料库统计数据，分析了话题与劝说技术之间的相关性，并给出了基于经典ML和生成式AI的基线基准结果。

Conclusion: 该语料库填补了斯拉夫语言劝说技术标注资源的空白，为多语言劝说技术检测提供了标准化基准，有助于推动低资源语言在计算修辞学和舆情分析领域的研究。

Abstract: Persuasion techniques are powerful rhetorical devices used to sway public opinion in a wide range of media. We present a new corpus of persuasion techniques, focusing on Slavic languages. The corpus contains documents in Bulgarian, Polish, and Russian, annotated with persuasion techniques at the coarse-grained text-span level and fine-grained sentence level. The techniques are drawn from a taxonomy of 25 fine-grained persuasion techniques, grouped under six broad categories of rhetorical persuasion strategies. The corpus contains approximately 7500 text spans from 222 documents that cover topics hotly debated at the national and international levels. We describe the corpus creation process, provide detailed statistics, and examine correlations between topics and persuasion techniques. We use classic ML-based and generative AI-based models to provide baselines and benchmark results for the detection and classification of persuasion techniques at the text-span level and sentence level.

</details>


### [105] [The First ChineseBabyLM Challenge: training data-efficient and cognitively plausible language models for Chinese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10745)
*Siyuan Song,Zhiheng Qian,Yunhao Zhang,Linyang He,Xiaozhe Ji,Yingxin Lin,Hongao Zhu,Chongtian Shao,Chuhan Lang,Luan Li,Rui Wang,Renfen Hu,Shaonan Wang,Hai Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了将在2026年NLPCC会议上举办的首届ChineseBabyLM挑战赛，要求参赛者使用1亿中文Token从头训练语言模型，并在自然语言理解、认知对齐和汉字知识三个任务赛道进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该挑战赛旨在模拟人类儿童在有限语言暴露下的语言习得过程，推动在极低数据量（1亿中文Token）条件下训练语言模型的研究，探索模型在理解能力、认知发展和汉字知识方面的表现。

Method: 参赛者需使用1亿中文Token从头训练语言模型，不限制分词器、模型架构和训练轮数；模型将在三个赛道进行评估：自然语言理解（NLU）、认知对齐（cognitive alignment）和汉字知识（Hanzi knowledge）。

Result: 本文仅介绍了挑战赛的设计框架和规则，未报告具体的实验结果。
