Conclusion: ChineseBabyLM挑战赛为低资源中文语言模型训练提供了一个标准化评测平台，鼓励研究者在受限数据条件下探索语言建模与认知发展的交叉研究。

Abstract: This paper describes the first ChineseBabyLM challenge, which will be held in the 2026 NLPCC conference. The challenge calls for researchers to train language models from scratch with 100 million Chinese tokens and evaluates the models on 3 tracks of tasks: NLU, cognitive alignment and Hanzi knowledge. There is no restriction on tokenizer, model architecture and the number of training epochs. Details of the challenge can be found in https://chinese-babylm.github.io/.

</details>


### [106] [Trust Before Fusion: QIMG-7 and Source-Aware Resolution for Polluted Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10798)
*Saadeldine Eletter,Owais Aijaz,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出QIMG-7基准，系统评估多模态RAG在检索污染下的脆弱性，并设计SATR方法，通过源感知信任解析选择性地融合多模态信息，显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG评估多基于干净证据，但真实检索常返回不可靠内容（虚假文本、误导图像等），导致模型性能严重退化。缺乏系统性的污染场景基准和鲁棒应对策略。

Method: 1) 构建QIMG-7基准：覆盖4个数据集、7种图像攻击族、16种干净/污染配对设置，共1760行评估数据；2) 提出SATR（源感知信任解析）：训练无关方法，对比Parametric、Text-only、Full-MM三种候选答案，基于来源可靠性进行答案选择或回退，其中Field-Selector变体效果最佳。

Result: 在gpt-4o-mini主栈中，Full-MM支持率从干净文本的0.908骤降至污染文本的0.490；SATR Field-Selector达到0.816的最佳平衡分，比Full-MM提升11.7个百分点，比Cascaded Router提升2.7个百分点；消融表明显式文本可靠性建模是主要增益来源。

Conclusion: 在文本优先的事实型QA中面对多模态检索冲突时，选择性信任策略优于无条件的多模态融合。SATR为多模态RAG的鲁棒部署提供了有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is often evaluated with clean evidence, yet real retrieval can return topically relevant but unreliable content: false text and misleading images from corrupted metadata, entity swaps, typographic overlays, semantic edits, adversarial patches, blends, or style transfer. We introduce QIMG-7, a controlled benchmark for multimodal retrieval pollution in multi-sentence factual QA, spanning four datasets, seven image-attack families, and 16 paired clean/polluted regimes, for 1,760 evaluation rows per method. Across four generator/gate stacks, naive multimodal fusion is brittle: in the main gpt-4o-mini stack, Full-MM support drops from 0.908 with clean text to 0.490 with polluted text, often making Parametric fallback safer than retrieval. We propose source-aware trust resolution (SATR), a training-free approach that compares Parametric, Text-only, and Full-MM candidate answers and selects among candidate answers or falls back based on source reliability. The Field-Selector variant achieves the best balanced score, 0.816, improving over Full-MM by 11.7 points and over the Cascaded Router by 2.7 points. Ablations show that, in this text-first setting, explicit text-reliability modeling is the dominant driver of these gains. Overall, in text-first factual QA with multimodal retrieval conflict, our results support selective trust rather than unconditional fusion. Artifacts are available at https://github.com/SaadElDine/Trust_Before_Fusion.

</details>


### [107] [Diagnosing and Mitigating Thinking Collapse in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10805)
*Keqin Peng,Chen Li,Yuanxin Ouyang,Yancheng Yuan,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现OPSD在复杂推理任务中会引发"思考崩塌"现象——模型内在推理行为因教师梯度在关键决策点压制学生思维标记而骤降。为此提出AD-OPSD框架，通过非对称逐点散度门控锚定高风险标记到冻结基座模型的先验，在保留纠错能力的同时维持原生思考能力，在数学基准上最高提升+4.1%绝对精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) 是增强和对齐LLM的关键范式，但在复杂推理任务中，OPSD反而会导致下游性能下降。本文旨在系统性地探究这一反常现象的根本原因，揭示其背后的优化陷阱，并设计解决方案以在保持OPSD纠错能力的同时，避免对模型原生推理能力的破坏。

Method: 首先，定义"思考崩塌"（Thinking Collapse）指标——通过认知标记密度（ET per 1k）量化模型中间推理行为的衰退。其次，利用基于熵的梯度掩码和标记级目标分析，揭示崩塌机制：高学生熵决策分叉处，教师梯度将学生认知标记压制为非认知标记，且集中在高点向KL散度区域。最后，提出自适应双视角OPSD（AD-OPSD）：通过非对称逐点散度门控，将高抑制风险的沙盒标记动态锚定到冻结基座模型的参考先验，从而选择性保护原生思考能力。

Result: AD-OPSD在多个模型规模和数学基准数据集上，相较标准OPSD实现最高+4.1%的绝对平均精度提升。进一步分析表明，AD-OPSD有效缓解了思考崩塌，并且对不同后训练范式具有良好的泛化鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OPSD在复杂推理中的性能退化源于"思考崩塌"——教师梯度在高熵决策点系统性压制学生认知标记。AD-OPSD通过自适应锚定机制在保留纠错能力与维护原生推理之间取得平衡，为LLM自蒸馏训练提供了更鲁棒的优化框架。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for enhancing and aligning Large Language Models (LLMs). However, in complex reasoning tasks, OPSD paradoxically degrades downstream performance. In this paper, we systematically investigate this pathology and identify a severe optimization trap we define as \textbf{Thinking Collapse} -- a sharp decline in the model's native intermediate reasoning behavior, measured by epistemic-token density (ET per 1k). Through entropy-based gradient masking and token-level target analysis, we show that this collapse is triggered by aggressive teacher gradients at high-student-entropy decision forks, where student epistemic tokens are frequently suppressed into teacher non-epistemic targets and are highly concentrated in high pointwise student-teacher divergence regions. To resolve this optimization pathology, we propose \textbf{Adaptive Dual-Perspective OPSD (AD-OPSD)}, a robust control framework that dynamically moderates the self-distillation objective. AD-OPSD selectively anchors high-suppression-risk sandboxed tokens to a reference prior derived from the frozen base model via an asymmetrical pointwise divergence gate, preserving native thinking capacity while retaining OPSD's error-correcting power. Extensive experiments across competitive mathematical benchmarks show that AD-OPSD improves over standard OPSD by up to \textbf{+4.1\%} absolute average accuracy across diverse model scales and datasets. Further analysis demonstrates that AD-OPSD mitigates thinking collapse and generalizes robustly to different post-training paradigms.

</details>


### [108] [Abstractiveness Metrics for Evaluating Text Summarization: A Refined Formulation with Empirical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10806)
*Praveenkumar Katwe,Rakesh Chandra Balabantaray,Kali Prasad Vittala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一套基于启发式的抽象度量化指标（RA、SA、AR），通过文档长度调和均值与三次非重叠因子，以维度一致且有界的方式衡量摘要偏离原文抽取式复制的程度，并在XSUM数据集上验证了其区分抽取式与抽象式模型的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摘要评估主要依赖ROUGE等表层指标，缺乏对摘要"抽象程度"的量化手段。需要一套原则性的启发式指标来度量摘要偏离原文逐词复制的程度，从而更深入地评估模型生成摘要的抽象性，并辅助识别潜在的幻觉内容。

Method: 提出三个指标：(1) Reference Abstraction (RA)：度量参考摘要相对于源文档的抽象程度；(2) Summary Abstraction (SA)：度量模型生成摘要的抽象程度；(3) Abstraction Ratio (AR)：SA与RA的比值。核心公式为文档长度调和均值乘以三次非重叠因子（cubic non-overlap factor），该因子使输出维度一致、有界，并对抽取-抽象边界具有非线性敏感性。在XSUM数据集100篇文档上，使用BART-large-cnn、Pegasus-xsum、DistilBart、MT5-small四个模型进行验证。

Result: 指标成功区分了抽取式与抽象式模型：抽取式模型的SA值约为0.12–0.26，抽象式模型的SA值约为0.96–1.77。Abstraction Ratio能够有效识别出需要人工评估以判断是否存在幻觉的摘要。

Conclusion: 所提指标为摘要抽象度提供了原则性的量化工具，补充了ROUGE等传统指标的不足。AR指标尤其有助于筛选出高风险摘要进行人工审查，对摘要质量控制和幻觉检测具有实用价值。

Abstract: Quantifying abstractiveness in generated summaries is essential for evaluating summarization models beyond
  surface-level metrics like ROUGE. We introduce Reference Abstraction (RA), Summary Abstraction (SA), and Abstraction
  Ratio (AR) -- a set of principled heuristic metrics that measure how much a summary diverges from extractive copying
  of the source text. The formulation uses the harmonic mean of document lengths modulated by a cubic non-overlap
  factor, yielding dimensionally consistent, bounded output with non-linear sensitivity to the extractive-abstractive
  boundary. Evaluation on 100 XSUM documents across four summarization models (BART-large-cnn, Pegasus-xsum, DistilBart,
  MT5-small) demonstrates that the metrics successfully discriminate between extractive models (SA ~ 0.12-0.26) and
  abstractive models (SA ~ 0.96-1.77), and that the Abstraction Ratio identifies summaries requiring manual evaluation
  for potential hallucination. Code and results are available at https://github.com/katweNLP/AbstractionStudy.

</details>


### [109] [Large Language Models for Token-Efficient and Semantic-Preserving Opinion Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10825)
*Fabrizio Marozzo,Stefano Iannicelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向LLM的观点摘要框架，通过多维分类（情感、主题等）与分层抽样策略，在提示LLM之前筛选紧凑且具代表性的观点子集，从而在保持语义保真度的同时显著降低token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模观点文本（产品评论、酒店反馈、社交帖子）存在冗余、不平衡等问题，直接使用全部数据提示LLM做摘要会导致高昂的计算成本和token消耗，且易产生偏向多数观点的摘要，难以忠实反映观点的多样性。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 对观点进行多维分类（情感极性、主题类别等），构建分层结构；(2) 采用一系列分层抽样策略从各层中选取紧凑且具代表性的子集；(3) 使用定制化提示模板，引导LLM基于所选子集生成平衡摘要，突出各维度下的显著方面（如产品/酒店的优缺点）。

Result: 在Amazon产品评论、Tripadvisor酒店评论和X/Twitter帖子三个数据集上，该方法显著降低了token使用量与计算成本，在内容覆盖度、平衡性和语义保持方面一致优于传统AI摘要方法和标准LLM摘要基线。

Conclusion: 多维分类与分层抽样相结合是一种高效且语义保真的观点摘要策略，可在不牺牲摘要质量的前提下大幅压缩输入规模，为大规模观点分析提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Opinionated text - spanning product reviews, hotel feedback, and social posts - captures rich signals about user experiences, preferences, and concerns. However, the scale, redundancy, and imbalance of such corpora make it challenging to analyze opinions effectively, particularly when the goal is to generate summaries that remain faithful to the diversity of viewpoints expressed. This paper presents a framework that preserves semantics in LLM-based opinion summarization while minimizing token usage. We combine multidimensional classification (e.g., sentiment, topics) with a family of stratified sampling strategies to select compact yet representative subsets of opinions before prompting the LLM. Tailored prompts then produce balanced summaries that surface the salient aspects expressed in the opinions (e.g., strengths and weaknesses of products/hotels). Experiments on Amazon product reviews, Tripadvisor hotel reviews, and X/Twitter posts demonstrate that our method significantly reduces token usage and computational cost while consistently outperforming traditional AI-based and standard LLM summarization baselines in terms of content coverage, balance, and semantic preservation.

</details>


### [110] [Quantifying the Sources of Instability in LLM-Based Stance Analysis of Public Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10846)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文区分了计算社会科学预处理流程中的两类不稳定性来源——预处理管道本身（如说话人分割、分段规则）和下游测量工具（LLM标注 vs. 关键词词典），并通过对256段YouTube访谈的分析，发现管道敏感性集中于低样本量说话人，而跨测量方法的分歧更大且更系统性，呼吁研究者同时检验两类变异来源的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算社会科学越来越多地依赖自动化预处理管道将原始媒体转换为可分析文本，但同一输入经不同管道处理后可能产生不同输出。现有研究往往将预处理不稳定性和测量方法不稳定性混为一谈，缺乏一个清晰的诊断框架来区分两者各自的影响，导致研究者难以判断研究结论的稳健性究竟受哪一环节的威胁更大。

Method: 使用256段YouTube访谈（涵盖5个领域、41位公众人物），系统比较两种说话人分割管道和两种测量方法（LLM标注 vs. 关键词词典），均以情感效价（affective valence）与认知情态（epistemic modality）之间的耦合关系为目标变量。通过计算管道诱导的相关系数变化、跨方法耦合方向一致性、以及聚合效价比例稳定性等指标，分离并量化两类不稳定性来源。

Result: （1）预处理管道敏感性集中在视频样本量有限的说话人（N≤5），而对于四个样本量充足的说话人（N≥16），管道引起的平均绝对相关系数变化仅为0.13；（2）跨测量方法分歧更大且更系统——即使在同一预处理管道内，LLM和关键词词典方法对多位高样本量说话人给出了相反的耦合方向；（3）聚合层面的效价比例高度稳定（|Δp(neg)| < 6个百分点），无论管道或方法如何变化，这反而掩盖了上述两类不稳定性。

Conclusion: 论文贡献了一个诊断框架，将管道效应与测量效应分离。核心建议是：研究者在分析访谈数据中的跨维度关系时，必须验证其结论对预处理管道和测量方法选择两类变异来源均具有稳健性，尤其应关注测量方法的选择所带来的系统性偏差。

Abstract: Computational social science increasingly relies on automated preprocessing pipelines -- speaker diarization, ASR transcript cleaning, sentence segmentation -- to convert raw media into analyzable text. When these pipelines produce different outputs from the same input, two distinct sources of instability can arise: the preprocessing pipeline itself (diarization method, segmentation rules) and the downstream measurement instrument (LLM annotation vs.\ keyword lexicon). Using 256 YouTube interviews across 41 public figures from five domains, we compare two speaker-diarization pipelines and two measurement methods, all targeting the coupling between affective valence and epistemic modality. We find that (1) preprocessing pipeline sensitivity is concentrated in speakers with limited video samples (N $\leq 5$); for the four best-sampled speakers (N $\geq 16$), the mean absolute pipeline-induced change in $r(\text{neg}, \text{emph})$ is only $0.13$; (2) cross-method disagreement is larger and more systematic -- the LLM and keyword-lexicon methods assign opposite coupling directions to several well-sampled speakers, even within the same preprocessing pipeline; and (3) aggregate valence proportions are highly stable ($|Δp(\text{neg})| < 6$pp) regardless of pipeline or method, masking both sources of instability. The contribution is a diagnostic framework that separates pipeline effects from measurement effects: researchers studying cross-dimensional relationships in interview data should verify that their conclusions are robust to both sources of variation, with particular attention to measurement method choice.

</details>


### [111] [Capabilities of Claude Fable 5 on Biomedical Challenge Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10849)
*Dominic Okonkwo,Magnus Hodgson,Temitope I. David,Susan Adanna Ihejirika*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了Claude Fable 5在8个生物医学基准上的表现，核心发现是该模型在多个基准上出现大量拒绝回答（8.0%–99.4%），但排除拒绝项后，其准确率在所有基准上均达到或超越其他模型。研究识别出两种可区分的拒绝模式：基础科学/机制类拒绝和特定疾病域拒绝（如先天性代谢病）。结论是Fable 5的生物医学实用性主要受限于其回答意愿而非能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物医学基准评估存在两大问题：传统基准已近饱和（天花板效应），而开放式回答又依赖其他语言模型进行评分，引入评分偏差。因此需要采用固定答案键的确定性评分方式，对最新前沿模型进行严格评估。

Method: 将Claude Fable 5与两个Claude前代模型及GPT-5基线在8个生物医学基准（4个文本、4个多模态）上进行比较，全程使用固定答案键进行确定性评分。在每个结果表中单独追踪并报告"拒绝回答"（refusal）这一结果类别，将其与正确/错误回答区分开来，并进行排除拒绝项后的准确率重算。

Result: Fable 5在不同基准上的拒绝率从8.0%到99.4%不等，而其前代模型和GPT-5均未出现此模式。排除拒绝项后，Fable 5在所有基准上的准确率均达到或超越所有对比模型。识别出两种拒绝模式：(1) MedQA和MedXpertQA MM中集中在基础科学与机制类内容的拒绝，经两个基准各自的类别标签独立验证；(2) RareBench上先天性代谢病表现几乎被普遍拒绝，而成人起病的自身免疫性疾病则不然。

Conclusion: Claude Fable 5在生物医学领域的核心瓶颈并非能力不足，而是回答意愿受限。该模型的拒答行为具有系统性、可分类的模式，提示其安全对齐策略可能过度抑制了生物医学领域的正常问答，对临床和科研应用构成潜在可用性障碍。

Abstract: Frontier language models are increasingly evaluated on biomedical benchmarks, but two problems undermine most published evaluations: legacy benchmarks are near-saturated, and open-ended responses are graded by other language models. We evaluate Claude Fable 5, Anthropic's most capable publicly available model, across eight biomedical benchmarks, four text and four multimodal, using deterministic scoring against fixed answer keys throughout. We include two Claude predecessors and GPT-5 as baselines. Refusal is tracked as a distinct outcome in every result table. That decision produces the paper's central finding. Fable 5 refuses between 8.0% and 99.4% of questions depending on the benchmark, a pattern absent in both predecessors and in GPT-5. Once refused items are excluded from the denominator, Fable 5's accuracy exceeds or meets every other model on every benchmark in this study. We identify two distinguishable refusal patterns: one concentrating in basic-science and mechanism content across MedQA and MedXpertQA MM, confirmed independently on two benchmarks using each benchmark's own category labels; and a separate disease-domain pattern on RareBench, where inborn metabolic disease presentations are refused near-universally while adult-onset autoimmune presentations are not. The primary constraint on Fable 5's biomedical usefulness is willingness to engage, not capability once it does.

</details>


### [112] [The Nuts and Bolts of Natural Language to SQL Translation: A Systematic Analysis of Model Pipeline Optimisation Approaches and their Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10911)
*Filip Klubicka,Vasudevan Nedumpozhimana,Sneha Rautmare,Bora Caglayan,Mingxue Wang,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了NL2SQL流水线中多个扩展组件的交互影响，包括NatSQL中间表示、基于合成数据的预处理与微调、以及新型重排序模型，通过消融实验和Shapley分析揭示各组件的效果取决于与基线系统及彼此之间的相互作用，而非简单堆叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型时代，NL2SQL仍是一个具有广泛应用价值的开放问题。现有研究多关注单一改进，缺乏对多种流水线扩展组件之间交互效应的系统理解。作者旨在探索这些组件的组合规律，为开发更轻量化的模型提供指导。

Method: 将NatSQL中间表示（简化SQL结构）、基于合成数据生成的预处理步骤和微调步骤、以及一种新颖的beam级别重排序（reranker）模型集成到NL2SQL流水线中。以SmBoP和RASAT为骨干架构，通过消融实验与Shapley值分析量化各组件对整体性能的边际贡献及其交互效应。

Result: 简单组合所有扩展组件并不能取得最优结果；各组件的效果依赖于它们与基线系统以及彼此之间的相互作用，不同组件组合在不同骨干架构上表现出差异化的增益模式。

Conclusion: 在NL2SQL流水线设计中，不应盲目堆叠扩展组件，而需关注组件间的协同与冲突关系，根据不同基线系统进行针对性组合才能获得最佳性能，这为轻量化NL2SQL模型的开发提供了重要启示。

Abstract: In the age of large language models, Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) translation remains an open problem with many useful applications. We explore interactions between several NL2SQL pipeline extensions to inspire development of more lightweight models. Specifically, we integrate the NatSQL intermediate representation, include a preprocessing step and a fine-tuning step based on synthetic data, and develop a novel reranker model to improve SQL selection in the final beam. We perform an ablation study supplemented by a Shapley analysis of these different components integrated with two backbone architectures, SmBoP and RASAT. We find that simply combining all of them does not lead to best results, but that their impact depends on their interactions with the baseline system, as well as each other.

</details>


### [113] [EasyOPD: An Easy-to-use On-Policy Distillation Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11012)
*Jie Sun,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Qiyong Zhong,Gengsheng Li,Zhepei Hong,Chang Wu,Pengfei Liu,Junfeng Fang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EasyOPD 是一个基于 verl 分布式强化学习框架构建的在线策略蒸馏框架，通过解耦用户配置、方法特定的监督逻辑与后端执行，统一了跨分词器、自蒸馏和逐步蒸馏三种在线策略蒸馏范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型蒸馏依赖固定的教师生成数据，无法覆盖学生策略演化过程中遇到的状态；现有在线策略蒸馏方法在监督形式、分词器兼容性、教师访问方式和监督粒度上差异显著，导致实现碎片化、难以复现和扩展。

Method: 基于 verl 分布式强化学习框架构建 EasyOPD，将系统分为用户配置层、方法特定监督逻辑层和 verl 执行后端三个层次；通过损失构造、rollout 元数据、奖励处理、分词器对齐和教师端计算等扩展边界连接方法模块与共享后端。在此框架下实例化了三种代表性 OPD 设置：跨分词器 OPD、在线策略自蒸馏和逐步 OPD。

Result: 在推理、代码生成、科学知识和工具使用等基准测试上，这些方法可通过同一 verl 后端执行，同时保留各自的方法特定目标和任务相关的性能特征。

Conclusion: EasyOPD 通过统一的框架设计有效解决了 OPD 方法碎片化问题，提供了可复现、可扩展的在线策略蒸馏方案，并释出可运行的 YAML 配置、文档和演示包。

Abstract: Conventional language-model distillation often relies on fixed teacher-generated data, which may not cover the states encountered by an evolving student policy. On-policy distillation (OPD) instead collects teacher or evaluator supervision on student-generated rollouts. However, existing OPD methods differ substantially in supervision form, tokenizer compatibility, teacher access, and supervision granularity, leading to fragmented implementations that are difficult to reproduce and extend. We present \textsc{EasyOPD}, an on-policy distillation framework built on verl, a distributed reinforcement-learning framework for large language models. \textsc{EasyOPD} separates user-side configuration, method-specific supervision logic, and verl-based execution. Its method modules connect to the shared backend through extension boundaries for loss construction, rollout metadata, reward processing, tokenizer alignment, and teacher-side computation. We instantiate representative methods for three OPD settings -- cross-tokenizer OPD, on-policy self-distillation, and step-wise OPD. Experiments on reasoning, code-generation, scientific-knowledge, and tool-use benchmarks show that these implementations can be executed through the same verl-based backend while retaining their method-specific objectives and task-dependent performance profiles. We release \textsc{EasyOPD} with runnable YAML configurations, documentation, and an installable demonstration package and video.

</details>


### [114] [Can a Language Model Learn Facts Continually in Its Weights?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11020)
*Charles O'Neill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了持续学习中权重写入是否支持知识积累。通过在Qwen3模型上写入虚构事实并进行序列化写入实验，发现训练数据的广度决定了知识类型：裸陈述训练产生背诵，而多样化重述可大幅缩小背诵到使用的差距。后续写入会重定向问题而非擦除知识，遗忘的事实仍保留对数概率，且通过上下文提示可恢复。最终结论是，当事实需要组合或经历后续写入时，可靠通道是上下文而非权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习的核心承诺是语言模型能在训练后通过权重写入持续积累新知识，但权重写入能否真正支持知识积累尚未有定论。本文旨在系统探究这一问题。

Method: 作者在Qwen3模型上写入虚构事实，从创建到后续20至100次序列化写入进行追踪，使用五种类型的留出问题评估，并以原始模型在提示中给出事实作为参考基准。对比了裸陈述训练与多样化重述训练两种数据条件。

Result: 裸陈述训练产生背诵行为，多样化重述将背诵到使用的差距从27.4降至5.4个点。20次序列写入后，裸陈述事实仅保留1%准确率，而广泛学习数据写入的事实保留46%。遗忘的事实保留了写入时增加的大部分对数概率，且裸陈述训练下70%的错误答案包含最近写入的事实。相同写入几乎不损害模型对上下文事实的使用，遗忘的学习事实在提示中供应后可恢复到77-80%准确率。后续写入导致的干扰与事实存储方式无关，且KL散度可追踪无关能力损害。所有干预措施均无法保持早期事实可访问。

Conclusion: 广泛数据可创造可用知识，冻结参考可保留能力，但任何干预都无法在后续写入后保持早期事实可及。当事实需要组合或在后续写入后存活时，可靠通道是上下文而非权重。

Abstract: Continual learning promises a language model that keeps acquiring knowledge after training, with each new fact written into its weights. Whether weight writes can support accumulation remains undecided. We follow invented facts written into Qwen3 models from creation through sequences of twenty to one hundred later writes, using held-out questions of five types, with the original model given the fact in its prompt as the reference. Across these experiments, the breadth of the training data determines the kind of knowledge created. Bare-statement training produces recitation, while diverse restatements reduce the recitation-to-use gap from 27.4 to 5.4 points without showing the model a conclusion. This difference carries into later writes: after twenty sequential writes, bare-statement facts retain 1% accuracy while facts written from broad study data retain 46%. We also find that facts can be behaviourally forgotten without being erased. Forgotten facts keep most of the log-probability added by their write, and under bare-statement training 70% of wrong answers about them contain the most recently written fact. The same writes barely degrade the model's use of facts in context, and a forgotten study fact supplied in the prompt recovers to 77-80% on its questions. These results describe knowledge that is stored but question-keyed: later writes redirect the questions that reached it. Damage to unrelated abilities tracks KL divergence from the original model, and the later writes cause interference regardless of how the earlier fact was stored. Broad data can create usable knowledge, and a frozen reference can preserve capability, but no intervention we tested, including those built on accurate local measurements of each write, keeps earlier facts reachable. When facts must be composed or survive later writes, the reliable channel is context rather than the weights.

</details>


### [115] [Dimensionality in Satisfaction Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11026)
*Andrew Hong,Jason Potteiger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用GPT-4.1对约9000条客服对话进行多维度满意度标注（整体、客服、结果、产品、客户努力度），验证了LLM标注与客户自评之间高度一致，揭示了全量数据与调查样本之间的满意度偏差，并论证了分解式满意度的价值在于归因与覆盖而非增量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统客服满意度测量依赖客户主动返回的调查问卷，回复率低且存在选择性偏差，无法覆盖全部对话，也难以细粒度识别满意度驱动因素。研究者希望利用LLM实现对话级别的自动化多维度满意度标注，以解决覆盖面和归因粒度不足的问题。

Method: 使用GPT-4.1对一家全球消费品公司的约9000条客服对话文本进行五维满意度标注（整体、客服表现、结果、产品、客户努力度），将标注结果与客户自评满意度进行相关性验证，分析分歧案例的集中模式，检验各轴之间的共线性，并比较全量标注与调查样本的满意度均值差异。

Result: 四个轴与自评满意度高度相关：整体、客服、结果轴未经调整的相关性接近0.65，努力度为-0.54（负相关），产品轴与可用代理指标的相关性较弱。分歧集中在少数可识别的离散会话中，排除严重分歧后整体相关性升至0.811，排除全部分歧尾部后达0.914。各轴之间高度共线，加入各轴不能提升对客户评分的预测力，分解的价值在于归因与覆盖。全量标注的满意度均值（2.91/5）显著低于调查样本（3.62/5）。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的多维度满意度标注是可行的，与客户自评高度一致。分解式满意度的核心价值不在于提升预测精度，而在于实现对每一条对话的全面覆盖与多维度归因，从而揭示调查数据中被系统性高估的满意度真实水平，为从对话数据中挖掘更细致的客户体验驱动因素提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: We used a large language model (GPT-4.1) to annotate the text of about 9,000 support conversations at a global consumer-goods firm, decomposing customer-care satisfaction into component axes (overall, agent, outcome, product, and customer effort), and validated the LLM annotations against the satisfaction ratings customers gave themselves. Four of five axes track self-reported satisfaction closely (overall, agent, and outcome near an unadjusted 0.65; effort -0.54), while product satisfaction is weak against the available proxy. The unadjusted correlation also understates the alignment: the disagreements concentrate in a small, readable tail of divergent sessions rather than in general drift, and the overall correlation rises to 0.811 when only the severe divergences are excluded and to 0.914 when the full divergent tail is excluded. The axes are also highly collinear, and adding them to the overall score does not improve prediction of the customer's rating, the decomposition's value is not incremental prediction but attribution and coverage. And, with greater coverage the picture of the data changes. Read on every contact rather than the few that return a survey, satisfaction is markedly lower than the survey reports (a full-census 2.91 against the surveyed 3.62 on a five-point scale). The promise of decomposed satisfaction as a methodology is the ability to identify more nuanced drivers of customer experience in conversational data.

</details>


### [116] [Flout at Your Own Risk: LLMs Struggle with Pragmatic Cooperativity Under Epistemic Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11053)
*Hannah VanderHoeven,Abhijnan Nath,Nikhil Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在部分信息条件下的多智能体协作任务中的语用推理能力，形式化了"协作认知不对称"概念，将任务成功与Grice合作原则关联，并通过实验评估了LLM作为说话者和听者时的合作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在协作和智能体流程中应用日益广泛，但尚不清楚它们在无法获取与协作者相同信息时，能否展现出基于语境线索的语用推理能力。现有研究缺乏对LLM在部分信息条件下多方案协作中语用能力的系统考察。

Method: 形式化提出"协作认知不对称"概念，将客观任务成功与Grice合作原则显式关联；设计多方案协作任务，在部分信息条件下评估多种LLM（包括提示策略和训练后策略）分别作为说话者和听者时的协作表现；分析失败模式与Grice准则违反之间的关联。

Result: LLM在协作场景中确实展现出一定的语用能力，且可通过提示和训练后策略激发；但在不完全信息下的语用沟通仍面临挑战；某些失败模式与未被识别出的Grice准则违反存在相关性。

Conclusion: LLM具备初步的语用推理能力，但在认知不对称的协作场景中仍存在显著局限，尤其体现在对Grice准则违反的无意识层面，提示未来需要在模型训练和设计上进一步强化语用能力。

Abstract: Fruitful collaborations rely on cooperative communications, including of contextual cues to incorporate into reasoning. The increasing use of LLMs in collaborative and agentic pipelines raises questions about the extent to which they exhibit these pragmatic capabilities, especially in scenarios where they may not have access to the same information as their collaborators. In this paper, we perform a novel investigation into the pragmatic reasoning capabilities of LLMs in a multi-party collaborative task under partial information conditions. We formalize a notion of collaborative epistemic asymmetry that explicitly connects objective task success to Grice's cooperative principle and empirically assess various LLMs' abilities to act cooperatively as both speakers and listeners, including both prompting and post-training strategies. Our results show that while LLMs exhibit certain pragmatic capabilities in collaborative settings, and these can be elicited through prompting and post-training, they still face challenges in pragmatic communication with incomplete information, and that certain failure modes do correlate with floutings of Grice's maxims that go unrecognized.

</details>


### [117] [MJ: Multi-turn LLM Jailbreaking via Decomposed Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11070)
*Junyoung Park,Namgyu Park,Sechan Lee,Yoon-Chan Jhi,Jihoon Cho,Sangdon Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DC-GRPO框架，通过将对话级信用分配分解为轮次级信用分配，解决了多轮越狱攻击中不同轮次贡献难以区分的问题。该框架结合即时信用与未来信用，为每轮对话生成独立的组相对学习信号，在多个基准测试上大幅超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话场景下，LLM越狱攻击面临信用分配（credit assignment）的核心挑战：不同对话轮次对最终越狱成功与否的贡献不同，但现有的学习信号（如轨迹级评分）过于粗糙，无法区分各轮次的个体贡献，导致优化效率低下。

Method: 提出DC-GRPO（Decomposed Credit GRPO），一个统一的轮次级信用分配框架，用于GRPO在多轮越狱学习中的优化。其核心思想是：将即时信用（immediate credit）和未来信用（future credit）相结合，为每一轮对话分配独立的组相对学习信号，而非将单一的轨迹级评分广播到整个对话。该框架下实例化了静态加权和动态加权两种规则，二者共享轮次级结构，仅在两种信用来源的平衡方式上有所不同。

Result: 在多个受害LLM和基准测试上，动态加权和静态加权变体分别取得了98.26%和97.88%的平均ASR5@3分数，显著优于SEMA（86.58%）和TROJail（86.23%）等SOTA方法。

Conclusion: DC-GRPO的核心经验增益来源于轮次级组相对信用分配机制本身，而非特定的加权规则。两种加权变体表现一致强劲，验证了细粒度轮次级信用分配在多轮越狱攻击学习中的关键作用。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) operate in interactive multi-turn settings, making multi-turn jailbreaking a realistic threat model and an important setting for automated red teaming. A core challenge in learning multi-turn jailbreak attackers is credit assignment: different turns contribute differently to the final outcome, yet existing learning signals are often too coarse to identify their individual contributions. We propose decomposed credit GRPO (DC-GRPO), a unified turn-level credit assignment framework for Group Relative Policy Optimization in multi-turn jailbreak learning. DC-GRPO assigns a separate group-relative learning signal to each turn by combining immediate and future credit, avoiding the credit misassignment induced by broadcasting a single trajectory-level score across the dialogue. We instantiate this framework with static and dynamic weighting rules that differ in how the two credit sources are balanced while sharing the same turn-level structure. Across multiple victim LLMs and benchmarks, the dynamic- and static-weighted variants achieve average ASR5@3 scores of 98.26% and 97.88%, respectively, substantially outperforming the state-of-the-art methods, including SEMA (86.58%) and TROJail (86.23%). Their consistently strong performance indicates that the central empirical benefit comes from turn-level group-relative credit assignment rather than a particular weighting rule. Warning: This paper contains examples of harmful content.

</details>


### [118] [ResearchQA: Benchmarking Citation-Grounded Question-Answering on Scientific Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11074)
*Saba Imran,Debanjum Singh Solanky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ResearchQA是一个包含6,211个问答对的引用支撑评估基准，覆盖494篇开放获取论文和8个领域，通过引用匹配和LLM评估器评估模型在"带引用的论文对话"场景中的表现，发现开放权重模型在引用准确性上接近闭源模型且延迟低3-6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辅助科学阅读的评估方法无法有效检测回答是否由可验证的引用支撑。需要一种基于引用支撑的评估基准，既允许多条有效支撑段落，又能奖励模型在论文不支持时的合理拒答。

Method: 构建ResearchQA基准：从494篇开放获取论文中生成6,211个问答对，涵盖8个领域和4种问题类型（查找型、理解型、多跳型、对抗型）。评估采用双重机制：确定性引用匹配器检测答案是否被论文段落支撑，以及基于LLM的评分量规评估器。同时评估8个领先的闭源和开放权重模型。

Result: 基于引用的指标（段落覆盖率和引用准确性）比LLM评估器分数更能区分不同系统——各模型间引用准确性差异显著，而评估器分数高度压缩。开放权重模型在引用准确性上接近最佳闭源模型，且单样本延迟低3到6倍。

Conclusion: ResearchQA为科学阅读场景提供了更严格的引用支撑评估范式，揭示了单纯依赖LLM评估器可能导致模型区分度不足的问题。开放权重模型在效率与准确性之间展现出良好的平衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to assist scientific reading, but existing evaluation methods often fail to detect whether answers are supported by verifiable citations. We introduce ResearchQA, a benchmark of 6,211 single-paper question-answer pairs from 494 open-access papers spanning eight domains and four question types: lookup, comprehension, multi-hop, and adversarial. ResearchQA is designed for citation-grounded evaluation: it permits multiple valid supporting passages for a claim and rewards grounded refusal when the source paper does not support an answer. We evaluate eight leading closed- and open-weight models in a citation-grounded chat-with-paper setting using a deterministic citation matcher and an LLM-based rubric evaluator. Citation-based metrics separate systems more clearly than LLM-evaluator scores: section coverage and citation accuracy vary substantially across models, while evaluator scores remain tightly compressed. We further find that open-weight models approach the best closed-model citation accuracy while achieving 3 to 6 times lower per-example latency. We release the benchmark, evaluation harness, and evaluator prompt.

</details>


### [119] [Do LLMs Fabricate Legal Citations? A Bilingual Benchmark on Saudi Data Protection Law and the GDPR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11127)
*Noura Suliman Alrajeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究构建了一个包含120个问题的双语基准测试，评估免费LLM在GDPR和沙特PDPL两部数据保护法上的法规引用准确性，发现模型在GDPR上表现近乎完美，但在沙特PDPL上普遍虚构引用，且模型置信度无法甄别虚构内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织与监管机构越来越多地依赖LLM处理合规问题，但错误的法规引用可能悄无声息地渗透进法律建议、合规文件与政策决策中，带来系统性风险。目前缺乏对LLM在多语言、跨法域场景下法规引用虚构行为的系统性评估。

Method: 构建了一个双语（阿拉伯语/英语）基准，包含三类问题：(1) 直接引用检索问题；(2) 虚假前提验证探针；(3) 故意不可回答的"陷阱"问题（如涉及已废止条款、仅存在于实施细则中的期限等）。评估三款免费可用的LLM（Gemini 2.5 Flash、GPT-OSS-120B、Nemotron-3-Super-120B），采用全自动评分并与人工验证的金标准对比。

Result: 发现显著的"法域鸿沟"：GDPR的直接引用准确率高达94-100%，而沙特PDPL仅为60-77%（普遍虚构），且与查询语言无关。最高虚构率（67%）源于法律文本与实施细则的混淆；91%的虚构引用以置信度≥0.8的高自信状态输出。

Conclusion: 虚构行为取决于法律所属法域而非查询语言，模型自信度无法提供保护。机构依赖LLM进行合规筛查时，必须由逐字验证（verbatim-verification）等外部保障机制把关，而非依赖模型自我置信度。

Abstract: Organizations and regulators increasingly consult large language models (LLMs) for regulatory-compliance questions, yet a wrong statutory citation can silently propagate into legal advice, compliance documentation, and policy decisions. We introduce a bilingual benchmark of 120 questions probing whether freely accessible LLMs fabricate article citations for two data-protection instruments: the EU General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) and the Saudi Personal Data Protection Law (PDPL). The benchmark pairs direct citation retrieval questions with false premise verification probes and deliberately unanswerable "trap" questions -- including questions about a repealed article and about deadlines that exist only in implementing regulations, not in the law itself. Every question is posed in both Arabic and English, and all scoring is fully automatic against a manually verified gold reference. Evaluating three freely accessible models (Gemini 2.5 Flash, GPT-OSS-120B, Nemotron-3-Super-120B), we find a dramatic jurisdiction gap: near-ceiling citation accuracy on the GDPR (94-100% on direct retrieval) against majority fabrication on the Saudi PDPL (60-77%), invariant to query language; the highest fabrication rates (67%) arise from statute-vs-regulations confusion, and 91% of fabricated citations are asserted with confidence >= 0.8. Fabrication tracks the jurisdiction of the law, not the language of the query, and model confidence provides no protection -- indicating that verbatim-verification safeguards, rather than model self confidence, must gate any institutional reliance on LLMs for compliance screening.

</details>


### [120] [TIGER: Text-Conditioned Visual Gated Routing with Acceptance Alignment for Multimodal Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11131)
*Quynh Vo,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Ponhvoan Srey,Luu Anh Tuan,Thong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TIGER 提出一种文本条件视觉门控路由框架，通过动态选择与当前文本状态相关的稀疏视觉token、并采用基于验证接受前缀长度的强化学习训练策略，显著提升了多模态模型推测解码的接受前缀长度和加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码在纯文本LLM上效果显著，但在视觉语言模型（VLM）上收益有限，原因在于：(1) 草稿模型在视觉关键内容上容易发散；(2) 现有方法未直接处理无关视觉证据；(3) 训练目标未直接优化决定加速效果的关键指标——验证器接受的前缀长度。

Method: TIGER 包含两个核心设计：(1) 文本条件视觉门控路由机制，根据草稿模型当前的文本状态动态选择上下文相关的稀疏视觉token子集，而非暴露全部视觉token或固定压缩接口；(2) 接受对齐的分组策略训练，以蒸馏预热和KL锚定为基础，引入基于验证器接受前缀长度的奖励，鼓励草稿模型生成能通过更长验证的推测序列。

Result: 实验表明，TIGER 在精确验证端推测解码下，在接受前缀长度和推测加速比上均取得一致提升，同时在视觉路由分析中保持了可比的下游精度，实现了有利的质量-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: TIGER 通过动态视觉token路由和接受前缀感知的训练策略，有效解决了VLM推测解码中视觉信息冗余和草稿-目标对齐不足的问题，为多模态高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by letting a lightweight drafter propose multiple tokens that are verified by a larger target model. Although effective for text-only LLMs, speculative decoding yields limited gains in VLMs because drafters often diverge on vision-critical content, while existing multimodal acceleration methods do not directly address irrelevant visual evidence or optimize the verifier-accepted prefix length that governs speedup. We propose TIGER, a Text-conditioned vIsual GatEd Routing framework for multimodal speculative decoding. TIGER dynamically selects a sparse set of context-relevant visual tokens based on the drafter's current textual state, rather than expose the full visual token set or a fixed compressed interface. To better align training with inference-time efficiency, we optimize the drafter with acceptance-aligned group-based policy training using verifier-derived rewards based on accepted prefix length, built on top of distillation warm start with KL anchoring. This encourages the drafter not only to imitate the target model, but also to produce speculative continuations that survive verification for longer prefixes. Experiments show that TIGER yields consistent gains in accepted prefix length and speculative speedup under exact verifier-side speculative decoding, while achieving favorable quality-latency trade-offs with comparable downstream accuracy in visual-routing analyses.

</details>


### [121] [Unified Gradient Projection: Language-Balanced Continual Learning for Multilingual Low-Resource ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11163)
*Ziang Ren,Guodong Lin,Yuchen Ai,Kaize Tan,Wei-Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出统一梯度投影(UGP)方法，通过在统一投影空间中使用语言平衡回放的参考梯度来约束参数更新，有效缓解多语言ASR模型在低资源语言微调时的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练ASR模型（如Whisper）在低资源语言上微调时会出现灾难性遗忘，现有多语言持续学习方法难以调控跨任务干扰，主导语言会偏置优化过程。

Method: 提出统一梯度投影（Unified Gradient Projection, UGP）方法：利用语言平衡回放（language-balanced replay）生成参考梯度，在统一投影空间中约束参数更新方向；通过均衡投影中各语言的贡献，减少主导语言偏差；并将梯度级投影与数据级回放相结合，在稳定性和可塑性上获得互补增益。

Result: 在多种低资源语言组和不同模型规模上，UGP实现了有效适配并显著减轻遗忘。在Whisper-large-v3上，UGP实现了接近零的平均遗忘率。

Conclusion: UGP通过统一梯度投影有效解决了多语言ASR持续学习中的主导语言偏差和灾难性遗忘问题，梯度级约束与数据级回放的结合为多语言低资源适配提供了稳定且可塑的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained ASR models such as Whisper exhibit strong multilingual capabilities. However, fine-tuning on low-resource languages often causes catastrophic forgetting. Although continual learning mitigates this issue, existing methods struggle to regulate cross-task interference in multilingual settings, where dominant languages bias optimization. We propose Unified Gradient Projection (UGP), which constrains parameter updates using reference gradients from language-balanced replay in a unified projection space. By equalizing per-language contributions in the projection, UGP reduces dominant-language bias and improves cross-lingual stability. We further show that combining gradient-level projection with data-level replay yields complementary gains in stability and plasticity. Across diverse low-resource language groups and model scales, UGP enables effective adaptation while substantially mitigating forgetting. On Whisper-large-v3, it achieves near-zero average forgetting.

</details>


### [122] [Query-Focused Event Summarization: A Dataset and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11166)
*Chenyu Hu,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了面向查询的事件摘要（QFES）任务及QFESum数据集，并设计了结合自适应阈值检索与层次聚类摘要的两阶段框架，在事件导向的大规模语料摘要上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有查询聚焦摘要（QFS）数据集缺乏事件导向的摘要场景，且大多数QFS方法难以处理大规模语料（数百至数千篇文档），亟需专门针对主题事件的多维度查询摘要任务与数据集。

Method: 首先提出了QFES任务并构建了包含8个主题事件、16,684篇文档和104条查询的QFESum数据集；然后设计了两个阶段框架：（1）基于自适应阈值的查询聚焦检索（RAT），用于从大规模语料中筛选相关文档；（2）基于层次聚类的查询聚焦摘要（SHC），用于生成面向查询的摘要。

Result: 在QFESum数据集上的实验表明，RAT和SHC相比基线方法均取得一致且显著的性能提升，验证了该两阶段框架在QFES任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: QFESum数据集填补了事件导向查询摘要领域的空白，RAT+SHC的两阶段框架为大语料查询聚焦摘要提供了有效解决方案，相关数据和代码已公开。

Abstract: A thematic corpus is a collection of semantically coherent documents that collectively describe different aspects of a shared thematic event. Such a corpus typically contains hundreds or even thousands of documents. While users' interests in a thematic event often span multiple dimensions, Query-Focused Summarization (QFS) aims to generate summaries tailored to users' queries. However, existing QFS datasets lack event-oriented summarization, and most QFS methods struggle with large-scale corpora. To address these challenges, we propose the Query-Focused Event Summarization (QFES) task and construct the QFESum dataset, which contains 8 thematic events, 16,684 documents, and 104 queries. Furthermore, we introduce a two-stage QFES framework consisting of Query-Focused Retrieval with Adaptive Thresholding (RAT) and Query-Focused Summarization based on Hierarchical Clustering (SHC). Experimental results on QFESum show that RAT and SHC consistently outperform the baselines, demonstrating their effectiveness for QFES. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/sarcasm-hcy02/QFES-QFESum.

</details>


### [123] [Amplitude-Only FFN Intervention for Tool-Structured LLM Inference Method: Gated Evaluation Protocol, and Cross-Model Empirical Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11183)
*Sheng Xu,Boyuan Huang,Ke Jia,Jiadun Zhu,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型作为工具使用代理时的推理时FFN干预方法，提出振幅门控(Amplitude Gating)替代正交残差投影(ORP)，在不修改预训练权重方向的前提下调制激活幅度，以改善结构化输出质量。在多个Qwen模型上，该方法在工具/结构化任务上取得正向收益，Hermes函数调用任务提升最高达+11.36个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在作为工具使用代理时，微小的格式、参数或函数调用错误即可导致原本合理的响应失效。现有方案通常需要重新训练模型权重，成本高昂。作者希望探索推理时FFN干预方法，在不重新训练的情况下改善模型的结构化输出质量。

Method: 首先探索了正交残差投影(ORP)，通过方向修正修复输出，但发现其虽能定位敏感的SwiGLU FFN干预位点，却常常弊大于利。因此提出振幅门控(AG)，一种非破坏性替代方案：保留预训练FFN权重方向，仅在生成过程中调制激活幅度。设计了细粒度干预系统，覆盖P1/P2/P3及分支级P1s/P2a/P2b位点，并引入评估协议——分离组合预言机上限与固定配置和学习门控，强制样本级统计，采用任务感知指标处理二值和部分得分数据集。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-9B、Qwen3-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上，AG整体呈弱正向，但在工具结构化任务上表现最强。Qwen3.5-9B上，类别级学习门控将工具/结构化/代理性能从38.66%提升至42.92%(+4.27pp)，Hermes函数调用任务达约+7.6pp。Qwen3-8B上，Hermes JSON模式提升+11.36pp。Qwen2.5-7B保留预言机上限但当前学习门控未能捕获。熵AG与Newton-Schulz窗口化AG对比显示两者无统一优势。

Conclusion: 工具结构化推理是安全FFN级推理优化的最可信首要目标，但前瞻性在线验证和更广泛的跨模型评估仍属必要。部署需依赖模型和类别特定的路由策略。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate as tool-using agents, where small format, argument, or function-call errors can invalidate otherwise plausible responses. We study inference-time feed-forward network (FFN) intervention for improving structured outputs without retraining model weights. Our project began with Orthogonal Residual Projection (ORP), a direction-changing repair attempt that revealed sensitive SwiGLU FFN intervention sites but often caused more harm than fixes. We therefore propose Amplitude Gating (AG), a non-destructive alternative that preserves pretrained FFN weight directions and modulates only activation magnitudes during generation. We define a fine-grained intervention system spanning P1/P2/P3 and branch-specific P1s/P2a/P2b sites, and introduce an evaluation protocol that separates combination-oracle headroom from fixed configurations and learned gates, enforces sample-level accounting, and uses task-aware metrics for binary and partial-credit datasets. Across Qwen3.5-9B, Qwen3-8B, and Qwen2.5-7B, AG is weakly positive in aggregate but strongest on tool-structured tasks. On Qwen3.5-9B, a category-level learned gate improves tool/structured/agentic performance from 38.66% to 42.92% (+4.27 percentage points), with Hermes function-call tasks reaching about +7.6 points. On Qwen3-8B, Hermes JSON mode improves by +11.36 points. Qwen2.5-7B retains oracle headroom but current learned gates fail to capture it, showing that deployment requires model- and category-specific routing. Comparisons of entropy AG with Newton-Schulz-windowed AG show that neither family is uniformly dominant. These results identify tool-structured inference as the most credible first target for safe FFN-level inference optimization, while prospective online validation and broader cross-model evaluation remain necessary.

</details>


### [124] [ProgramTab: Boosting Table Reasoning of LLMs via Programmatic Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11207)
*Pei Guo,Enjie Liu,Yunzhi Tan,Mochi Gao,Jianxin Zhang,Ruichao Zhong,Juntao Li,Bo Hu,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProgramTab 框架通过 Python 代码预处理表格数据，并结合行列提取与 SQL 生成来高效抽取关键信息，解决了大表格下 LLM 推理性能下降的问题，在表格推理数据集上优于所有基于 LLM 的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理大表格时面临两个核心瓶颈：一是长文本建模困难，二是 LLM 输入长度受限，导致性能显著下降。Text-to-SQL 方法虽可提取子表，但表格数据（尤其是网络表格）往往缺乏 SQL 查询所需的结构和一致性，无法有效执行数学逻辑运算。因此需要一种新的方法来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出 ProgramTab 框架，核心思路是利用上下文学习（in-context learning）引导 LLM 完成两项关键操作：(1) 使用 Python 代码对表格数据进行预处理，处理非结构化或不一致的表格；(2) 通过行列提取与 SQL 生成来抽取关键内容，生成更小的子表供后续推理使用。

Result: 在表格推理数据集上的实验结果表明，ProgramTab 框架能够有效处理基于表格的推理任务，并且性能上超越了所有基于 LLM 的基线方法。

Conclusion: ProgramTab 通过引入 Python 代码预处理与结构化提取相结合的策略，克服了大表格推理中长文本建模和输入长度限制的挑战，为表格推理任务提供了一种更鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Table-based reasoning with large language models (LLMs), which requires reasoning based on natural language questions and structured tabular data, has gained widespread attention. However, a series of issues still constrain the application of this task. The previous approaches suffered from significant performance degradation when faced with large tables due to the difficulty of long text modeling and the limitation of input length for LLMs. The text-to-SQL approach is used to efficiently extract key information from tables and generate smaller sub-tables. However, tabular data, especially web tables, often lack the necessary structure and consistency, making them unsuitable for performing mathematical logic operations using SQL queries. We propose the ProgramTab framework, which guides LLMs employing in-context learning to perform tabular data preprocessing with Python code, as well as the momentous contents extraction with row and column extraction and SQL generation. The experiment results on table reasoning datasets demonstrate that the ProgramTab framework effectively deals with table-based reasoning tasks and outperforms all LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [125] [When the Target Domain Changes: AI-Mediated Construct Drift in High-Stakes English Language AssessmenW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11213)
*Yi Gui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从构念效度角度出发，将生成式AI在学术交流中的普及重新框定为高风险语言测试中的分数解释问题，提出“AI中介构念漂移”概念，并设计“有界AI中介”作为效度导向的测试设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高风险英语水平测试仍以无辅助的独立表现作为分数解释的依据，但目标语言使用域（学术场景）已越来越多地涉及生成式AI辅助交流，导致从无辅助测试表现推断学术交际能力的“外推推论”变得不再自明，亟需从构念效度层面重新审视这一问题。

Method: 本文采用概念性效度论证框架，分三个层次综述现有文献：多数研究将AI视为评估基础设施（如评分、反馈），少数研究探讨AI对构念效度和外推推论的深层影响。在此基础上，提出“AI中介构念漂移”的定义，并设计“有界AI中介”原则——一种标准化测试条件，所有考生使用同一机构控制的AI助手，设定预设辅助边界、记录交互日志，并设计能区分理解性支持与答案生成的任务。

Result: 论文指出，现行研究严重偏向将AI作为操作性工具，对其在构念效度层面的理论化探讨严重不足。提出分数解释在用于支持AI中介学术交流的主张时应当被收窄和补充，而非简单沿用无辅助测试模式的解读框架。

Conclusion: 语言测试领域需要将AI从操作性问题重新定位为分数解释与效度问题。“有界AI中介”设计可在保证标准化和可比性的前提下，使测试更贴近真实学术交流场景，但分数解释范围必须相应调整，以确保推论的有效性。

Abstract: High-stakes English proficiency tests treat standardized, unaided performance as evidence for score interpretations about academic English proficiency. This interpretation remains meaningful, but as target language use domains increasingly involve generative AI, the extrapolation from unaided test performance to academic communicative readiness becomes less self-evident. This conceptual validity argument reframes AI as a score-interpretation problem in high-stakes language testing, not only an operational issue of scoring, feedback, security, or misconduct. Synthesizing current literature in three uneven layers, the paper shows that most work treats AI as assessment infrastructure, while far less theorizes its implications for construct validity and extrapolation warrants. It defines AI-mediated construct drift as the misalignment that arises when communicative abilities required in the target domain change through AI mediation while test constructs remain anchored to an unaided-performance model. It proposes bounded AI mediation as a validity-oriented design principle: a standardized condition in which all test takers access the same institutionally controlled AI assistant, with predefined assistance boundaries, logged interactions, and tasks that distinguish comprehension support from answer generation. The paper argues that score interpretations should be narrowed and supplemented when used to support claims about AI-mediated academic communication.

</details>


### [126] [Q-BridgeNet: A Quantization Network for Cross-Lingual Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11215)
*Liqian Feng,Lintao Wang,Xiaochen Liu,Anusha Withana,Ken-Tye Yong,Dehui Kong,Zhiyong Wang,Kun Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Q-BridgeNet 提出了一种统一的 multilingual 手语翻译框架，通过在 sign language 侧使用离散 Q-units（共享基础码本 + 语言特定残差码本）来解耦跨语言语义，同时在 spoken language 侧微调多语言 LLM，有效缓解了跨语言冲突，在多个数据集上达到了 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 multilingual SLT 方法难以在统一模型中同时实现：(1) 最小化跨语言冲突；(2) 捕捉共享的跨语言语义；(3) 保留不同手语的语言特定变化。这三个目标的平衡是当前方法的瓶颈。

Method: 提出 Q-BridgeNet 框架。手语侧：通过自适应分割（adaptive segmentation）和残差向量量化（residual vector quantization, RVQ），学习离散 Q-units——共享 base codebook 提供语言无关的语义基元，语言特定的 residual codebooks 细化异构手语语义。口语侧：在多语言 LLM 上微调，使其在 Q-unit 空间上运行，利用跨语言先验实现统一 SLT。

Result: 在 PHOENIX14T、How2Sign 和 CSL-Daily 三个数据集上验证，Q-BridgeNet 在 native sign-spoken pairs 上达到 SOTA，同时在 non-native pairs 上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: Q-BridgeNet 通过双边的跨语言冲突缓解策略（手语侧的离散量化表示 + 口语侧的多语言 LLM），首次实现了统一且高效的多语言手语翻译模型，代码已开源。

Abstract: Most sign language translation (SLT) methods focus on isolated native sign-spoken pairs (e.g., American Sign Language - English). Extending language-specific SLT models to multilingual translation would improve accessibility by enabling communication across diverse sign and spoken language communities. However, existing multilingual SLT approaches still struggle to learn a unified model that minimizes cross-lingual conflicts while capturing shared cross-lingual semantics and preserving language-specific variations across different sign languages. Therefore, we propose Q-BridgeNet, a unified framework for multilingual SLT that jointly mitigates cross-lingual conflicts across both the sign language and spoken language sides. On the sign language side, Q-BridgeNet learns discrete Q-units via adaptive segmentation and residual vector quantization: a shared base codebook provides language-agnostic semantic primitives, while language-specific residual codebooks refine heterogeneous signing semantics. On the spoken language side, a multilingual LLM is fine-tuned to operate in the Q-unit space, leveraging cross-lingual priors to enable a unified SLT model. Experiments on PHOENIX14T, How2Sign, and CSL-Daily show that Q-BridgeNet effectively mitigates cross-lingual conflicts, achieving state-of-the-art performance on native sign-spoken pairs while also demonstrating strong generalization to non-native pairs. Our source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/FengLiQ/Q-BridgeNet

</details>


### [127] [TreeThink: A Modular Tree Search Library for Mathematical Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11258)
*Burak S. Akbudak,Zeynel A. Uluşan,Can S. Erer,Gözde Gül Şahin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TreeThink 是一个开源 Python 库，为神经定理证明提供模块化、全异步的树搜索框架，原生集成 Lean 4、Rocq、Isabelle/HOL 等形式化验证器的 REPL 服务器，支持跨语言形式证明搜索，异步执行可实现最高 6.3 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 树搜索库主要面向自然语言推理，缺乏与形式化验证器的原生集成；而定理证明系统通常依赖任务特定的搜索实现，缺乏通用、模块化的树搜索框架来桥接 LLM 推理与形式化验证。

Method: 提出 TreeThink 库：(1) 集成经典树搜索方法（如 MCTS 等）与基于 vLLM 的推理流水线；(2) 支持多种节点评估技术，从轻量启发式到神经评估器；(3) 直接连接各形式化语言的 REPL 服务器，实现实时验证与证明状态提取；(4) 采用全异步架构实现并行搜索与验证。

Result: 在 miniF2F 和 MATH500 上验证：展示了跨语言形式证明搜索能力、自然语言推理支持，以及异步执行带来最高 6.3 倍的 wall-clock 加速。

Conclusion: TreeThink 填补了 LLM 树搜索与形式化验证之间的工具链空白，为神经定理证明提供了统一、高效、可扩展的搜索基础设施，代码以 MIT 协议开源。

Abstract: Tree search algorithms enable systematic exploration of the proof space in neural theorem proving. Existing LLM tree search libraries primarily target natural language reasoning and do not provide native integration with formal verifiers, while theorem proving systems often rely on task-specific search implementations. We introduce TreeThink, an open-source Python library for modular, fully asynchronous tree search in neural theorem proving. It integrates established tree search methods with vLLM-based inference pipelines and diverse node evaluation techniques, ranging from lightweight heuristics to neural evaluators. We support Lean~4, Rocq, and Isabelle/HOL alongside natural language. It connects directly to each language's Read-Eval-Print Loop (REPL) server for real-time verification and proof state extraction. We evaluate TreeThink on miniF2F and MATH500, demonstrating cross-language formal proof search, natural language reasoning support, and up to 6.3$\times$ wall-clock speedup from asynchronous execution. Source code is released under the MIT license at https://github.com/GGLAB-KU/treethink , and the library is accessible as a downloadable package at https://pypi.org/project/treethink/ .

</details>


### [128] [Automated Textbook Auditing with Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11276)
*Ciprian Cristescu,Adrian-Marius Dumitran,Angela-Liliana Dumitran,Gabriel Stefan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AI Textbook Auditor 是一个模块化多智能体流水线系统，用于自动化教育材料的质量保证。它通过事实与技术轨道、语法轨道两条路径分析教科书PDF，并由裁判智能体过滤误报，最终生成结构化的人工可审查报告。在罗马尼亚两本高中教科书上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教科书质量保证需要同时核查事实准确性、领域技术正确性和语言质量，通用语法检查工具无法胜任。现有的人工审核耗时费力，亟需一种自动化辅助工具来降低人工定位问题的工作量。

Method: 提出模块化多智能体流水线（AI Textbook Auditor），接受PDF输入，通过两条轨道并行分析：（1）事实与技术轨道：由多个专业LLM智能体组成的集成系统检测事实错误、代码错误、定义错误和概念不一致，人文领域辅以网络搜索增强；（2）语法轨道：以PDF原生方式运行以保留变音符号编码。最后由裁判智能体（Judge Agent）利用领域特定规则过滤误报，生成结构化报告。支持视觉原生页面渲染和PyMuPDF文本提取两种摄入模式，并可通过自定义提示编码学科特定错误分类法实现领域适配。

Result: 在罗马尼亚两本高中教科书上演示：计算机科学教材发现56个技术问题（七类），专家验证精确率62.5%；历史与社会科学教材发现72个问题，涵盖事实错误、意识形态偏见和语法问题。

Conclusion: 该系统定位为分类筛选工具，旨在减少人工定位候选问题的工作量，但任何编辑操作前仍需人类专家验证。验证结果表明该系统在多个学科领域具有实用价值。

Abstract: Ensuring the quality of educational materials requires more than standard proofreading: textbooks must be audited for factual accuracy, domain-specific technical correctness, and linguistic quality simultaneously -- a task that general-purpose grammar checkers cannot address. We present \textbf{AI Textbook Auditor}, a modular multi-agent pipeline for automated quality assurance of educational materials across subject domains. The system accepts a textbook PDF and produces a structured, human-reviewable report via two analysis tracks: a \textbf{Factual and Technical Track} in which an ensemble of specialized LLM agents detects factual inaccuracies, code errors, incorrect definitions, and conceptual inconsistencies, augmented with web search for humanities domains; and a \textbf{Grammar Track} operating PDF-natively to preserve diacritical encoding. A \textbf{Judge Agent} filters false positives using domain-specific rules before presenting findings to a human reviewer. The pipeline supports two ingestion modes -- vision-native page rendering and PyMuPDF text extraction -- and is domain-adaptable via custom prompts encoding subject-specific error taxonomies. We demonstrate the system on two Romanian upper-secondary textbooks: a CS textbook (56 technical findings across seven categories, with an expert-validated precision of 62.5\%) and a history and social sciences textbook (72 findings spanning factual errors, ideological bias, and grammar). The system is designed as a triage tool that reduces the manual effort of locating candidate issues, with human expert validation required before any editorial action.

</details>


### [129] [FAD-SA-GRU: Enhancing Hate Speech Detection in Algerian Dialect Through Feature-Augmented Self-Attention GRU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11279)
*Sara Yakoubi,Ikram Khalfallah,Kenza Khelkhal,Dihia Lanasri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究阿尔及利亚阿拉伯方言(Darija)社交媒体中的仇恨言论自动检测，提出融合DZ FastText、DZ AraVec和DziriBERT三种嵌入的多嵌入融合架构FAD-SA-GRU，结合自注意力增强的GRU编码器，在二元仇恨言论分类任务上达到93.2%准确率和92.1% F1-score，优于所有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上仇恨言论泛滥对社会、心理和伦理构成重大挑战，而阿尔及利亚阿拉伯方言(Darija)因同时混杂阿拉伯语、法语和Arabizi(拉丁字母书写的阿拉伯语)，语言多样性极高，属于低资源方言，自动检测极具挑战性，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 系统比较四类文本分类方法：(1)基于TF-IDF特征的传统机器学习；(2)基于循环神经网络的深度学习模型；(3)基于Transformer的语言模型(DziriBERT、多语言BERT)；(4)提出全新的FAD-SA-GRU混合架构——通过多嵌入融合层整合DZ FastText、DZ AraVec和DziriBERT的语义表示，再输入自注意力增强的GRU编码器进行分类。

Result: 在阿尔及利亚Darija社交媒体标注数据集上，FAD-SA-GRU全面超越所有基线：准确率93.2%、精确率93.4%、召回率91.0%、F1-score 92.1%、ROC-AUC 97.0%。

Conclusion: 融合互补嵌入表示与注意力机制序列建模的方法对低资源方言阿拉伯语的仇恨言论检测具有显著效果，FAD-SA-GRU架构为该领域提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of social media platforms has transformed online communication by enabling users to exchange information and opinions instantly. However, these platforms have also facilitated the dissemination of abusive and hateful content, posing major social, psychological, and ethical challenges. Hate speech can incite discrimination, harassment, and violence against individuals or communities based on attributes such as ethnicity, religion, gender, nationality, or political affiliation. Consequently, automatic hate speech detection has become a major research topic in natural language processing (NLP) and an essential component of content moderation systems.
  This paper investigates automatic hate speech detection in the Algerian Arabic dialect (Darija) on social media. This task remains challenging because of the dialect's linguistic diversity, characterized by the coexistence of Arabic, French, and Arabizi (Arabic written using the Latin alphabet). We compare four categories of text classification approaches: (1) traditional machine learning models using TF-IDF features, (2) deep learning models based on recurrent neural networks, (3) Transformer-based language models, including DziriBERT and multilingual BERT, and (4) a novel hybrid architecture, FAD-SA-GRU, which combines semantic representations from DZ FastText, DZ AraVec, and DziriBERT through multi-embedding fusion, followed by a self-attention-enhanced GRU encoder.
  Experiments on an annotated dataset of Algerian Darija social media comments for binary hate speech classification show that FAD-SA-GRU outperforms all baselines, achieving 93.2% accuracy, 93.4% precision, 91.0% recall, 92.1% F1-score, and 97.0% ROC-AUC. Results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining complementary embedding representations with attention-based sequence modeling for robust hate speech detection in low-resource dialectal Arabic.

</details>


### [130] [The In-Car Sign Language Corpus (ICSL): A Multi-Modal Resource for Constrained-Space Sign Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11341)
*Raviteja Boddu,Guilherme Vieira Leite,Joed Lopes da Silva,Ângelo Benetti,Isabela Barbieri,Natália de Melo Afonso,Thyago Santos,Helio Pedrini,Felipe Venâncio Barbosa,José Mario De Martino,Munir Georges,Alessandro Zimmer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向车内共享出行场景的巴西手语（Libras）数据集ICSL，包含高精度动捕数据和真实车内多模态录制数据（2D相机+3D ToF传感器），总计超150万帧，旨在推动受限、遮挡、非正面环境下的手语识别研究，以改善听障人士的公共交通无障碍体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语识别（SLR）在真实受限环境（尤其是车辆内部）中的应用几乎未被探索。共享出行服务中，听障人士面临独特的交流障碍，而车内环境具有空间受限、遮挡严重、非正面视角等技术挑战，亟需专门的数据集以推动该方向的研究。

Method: 构建ICSL双轨数据集：(1) 实验室高精度运动捕捉（MoCap）数据，用于建立理想化的语言基线；(2) 真实车内多模态录制数据，通过2D摄像头与3D ToF传感器同步采集。数据集提供合成手语虚拟人动画与真实手语翻译视频之间的对比分析基础，并配有词汇手语和非词汇手语元素的gloss标注，支持深度神经网络的训练与评估。

Result: 收集了超过150万帧的多模态同步数据集，覆盖多种车内场景下的Libras使用者。数据集结构完整，包含详细的用例说明、采集方案、元数据结构及gloss标注，为受限空间手语识别和域自适应研究提供了基准。

Conclusion: 车内手语是研究受限、遮挡、非正面环境的典型技术案例。ICSL数据集为车载手语识别提供了首个系统化的基准，有助于推动车内无障碍技术研究和提升听障乘客的生活质量，同时承认聋人群体已有的多样化交流策略，将汽车特定限制的识别作为增强车内无障碍的重要切入点。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenges of using sign language within shared mobility services, such as taxis, carpools, or ride-sharing platforms. The use of sign language recognition (SLR) in real-world, confined environments, specifically vehicle interiors remains largely unexplored. To motivate research in this area, we present the In-Car Sign Language (ICSL) dataset for Brazilian Sign Language (Libras), with the long-term goal of improving public transport accessibility for the Deaf and Hard-of-Hearing community. The dataset consists of: (1) high-precision laboratory motion capture (MoCap) data to establish an idealized linguistic baseline and (2) real-world multi-modal in-car recordings captured using a 2D camera and 3D Time-of-Flight sensors. The dataset provides a basis for comparative analyses between synthesized signing avatar animations and recorded real signing interpreter videos, which enable future research into robust "in-the-wild" SLR models and domain adaptation. We describe in detail the use cases, the setup, the data collection protocol, and the metadata structure of the corpus. In total, we recorded a multimodal dataset exceeding 1.5 million frames, comprising the synchronized multimodal streams described above featuring Libras users across various in-car scenarios. The corpus is provided with gloss annotation of lexical signs and non-lexical sign language elements specially designed to support the training and evaluation of deep neural networks for constrained space recognition. In-vehicle signing offers a technically significant example of a constrained, occluded, and non-frontal environment. While recognizing the diverse communication strategies already employed by the Deaf community, identifying automotive-specific limitations provides a useful stepping stone for research into enhancing in-car accessibility and passenger quality of life.

</details>


### [131] [Characterising AI Models for Cataloguing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11353)
*Miguel Arana-Catania,Neil Jefferies*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了AI模型在数字化馆藏目录记录自动生成任务中的应用，对比了不同实现方式和模型，并提供了定性与定量评估以及通用性建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字馆藏建设不仅需要数字化内容本身，还需要创建目录记录，而这一关键环节长期依赖缓慢且昂贵的人工专家标注，因此探索AI模型自动化完成该任务具有重要的实践价值。

Method: 针对目录记录生成任务，采用多种AI模型及不同实现方案进行实验，并通过定性和定量两种方式进行评估对比。

Result: 实验给出了不同AI模型在目录记录生成任务上的定性与定量评估结果，并提出了超越该特定用例的AI模型使用建议。

Conclusion: AI模型可有效应用于数字馆藏的目录记录生成任务，不同的模型和实现方式各有优劣，研究给出的通用性建议可推广至更广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: The creation of digital collections involves not only the digitisation of content, but also the creation of catalogue records for it. This often-overlooked task requires slow and costly expert manual work. In this project, we have evaluated the application of AI models to this task, comparing different implementations and models. This work includes a qualitative and quantitative evaluation of the experiments carried out, as well as recommendations on the use of AI models that go beyond the specific use case.

</details>


### [132] [RefineEvo: Planning-Guided Heuristic Evolution with Bidirectional Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11358)
*Yang Wu,Junran Pan,Yifan Zhang,Ning Xu,Fanshuo Zeng,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RefineEvo 是一个将自动启发式设计从静态试错转变为规划引导、经验驱动的进化框架，通过 Planner 动态调度算子、Reflector 构建双向经验池，在组合优化问题上表现优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的自动启发式设计方法依赖固定的进化算子，缺乏有效积累和复用历史搜索经验的能力，导致搜索效率低下。

Method: 提出 RefineEvo 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Planner，根据当前搜索状态动态调度进化算子并触发细化；(2) Reflector，从搜索轨迹中提炼经验，构建包含正面洞察与负面陷阱的双向经验池（Bidirectional Experience Pool），实现轨迹感知、情境条件的搜索引导。

Result: 在多个经典组合优化基准上，RefineEvo 持续超越强基线方法，在提升解质量的同时提高了 token 效率。

Conclusion: RefineEvo 实现了更高效、更自主的启发式设计，证明了规划引导与经验驱动机制在 AHD 中的有效性。

Abstract: Automatic Heuristic Design (AHD) has emerged as a transformative approach for solving combinatorial optimization problems. While recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based methods have shown promise, they predominantly rely on fixed evolutionary operators and struggle to effectively accumulate and reuse historical search experience. This paper proposes RefineEvo, a novel evolutionary framework that transforms AHD from a static trial-and-error process into a planning-guided, experience-driven system. RefineEvo introduces a Planner to dynamically schedule evolutionary operators and trigger refinement based on the current search state, and a Reflector to distill valuable lessons into a Bidirectional Experience Pool containing both positive insights and negative pitfalls. This synergistic framework enables the system to adapt its search tools to the evolving complexity of the problem and leverage trajectory-aware, situation-conditioned insights to guide generation. Experiments on several classic combinatorial optimization benchmarks demonstrate that RefineEvo consistently outperforms strong baselines. In particular, RefineEvo delivers superior solution quality while improving token efficiency, enabling more efficient and autonomous heuristic design.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Sally-Anne: Evaluating Theory of Mind in LLMs using Epistemic Schelling Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11363)
*Roberta Rocca,Sami Boukortt,Geoff Keeling,Winnie Street*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“认知不对称谢林任务”(EAST)的双人对话游戏基准，用于评估大语言模型在动态、自然化情境下的功能性心理理论(ToM)能力。研究发现，即便在传统静态基准上表现出色，大多数模型仍存在显著的社会推理能力缺口，主要源于认知追踪错误（如混淆私有知识与共同知识）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的ToM评估（如Sally-Anne任务）存在两个核心缺陷：一是模型可能因预训练中接触过类似任务而“刷榜”，二是这些测试无法评估模型在自然交互情境中泛化的功能性ToM能力。因此需要一种更稳健、更具泛化性的评估范式。

Method: 提出EAST——一种双玩家对话游戏。在该任务中，LLM-LLM配对需要在不同的认知透明度条件下（即双方对彼此知识的知晓程度不同），独立收敛到语义上的谢林点（Schelling points）。通过操控认知状态（如私有知识 vs. 共同知识），评估模型能否稳健运用ToM达成协调。

Result: 实验揭示了功能性社会推理的显著能力缺口：仅前沿模型能成功应对不同认知透明度条件。推理轨迹分析表明，协调失败主要由认知追踪错误驱动，典型表现为将私有知识与共同知识混淆。即便模型在传统静态基准上表现优异，稳健的社会推理与认知追踪仍是关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 稳健的社会推理和认知追踪能力仍是当前LLM的关键瓶颈，EAST为未来的LLM评估与发展提供了明确的目标方向。

Abstract: Text-based evaluations of Theory of Mind (ToM) in Large Language Models (LLMs) often involve cognitive tests akin to the Sally-Anne task that can be gamed due to exposure to relevantly similar tasks in pre-training and do not obviously test models' functional ToM abilities in ways that generalize to naturalistic settings. To address these issues, we introduce the Epistemic Asymmetry Schelling Task (EAST), a two-player dialogue game designed to benchmark robust and generalizable ToM abilities. By requiring LLM-LLM dyads to independently converge on semantic Schelling points under varying states of epistemic transparency, we evaluate whether models can robustly apply ToM to achieve coordination. Our results reveal a significant capability gap in functional social reasoning, with only frontier models successfully navigating the varying epistemic demands of the tasks. Analysis of reasoning traces shows that coordination failures are primarily driven by epistemic tracking errors, such as conflating private knowledge with mutual knowledge. Despite high performance on traditional static benchmarks, our study shows that robust social reasoning and epistemic tracking remain a critical bottleneck, providing concrete targets for future LLM evaluation and development.

</details>


### [134] [Agentic Routing: The Harness-Native Data Flywheel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11399)
*Xinchen Liu,Hang Zhou,Yingjie Zong,Yuchuan Tian,Liuyang Song,Shuo Zhang,Yulong Li,Wei He,Mengyu Zheng,Runke Liu,Siyang Cheng,Xiang Kuang,Hailin Hu,Kai Han,Yunhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Harness-Native agentic routing范式，在Agent执行步骤级别根据完整harness状态进行模型路由（单模型成本优化或多模型集成精度提升），并将路由决策产生的结构化数据形成数据飞轮，用于训练更好的路由器与harness原生模型。在OpenSquilla中实现四层路由栈，在DRACO和PinchBench上验证了单模型与多模型路由效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由方法面向单轮chat场景优化成本-质量权衡，忽略了Agent执行中的状态、中间失败和反馈循环；同时前沿模型在代码编辑、长上下文、工具使用、数学推理等维度上日益结构化分化，使得Agent内部的模型选择成为一个核心系统问题。

Method: 提出Harness-Native agentic routing：在Agent执行的每一步，根据完整harness状态（查询、上下文、控制流、动作历史、验证结果等）进行路由决策——选择单一最优模型以降低成本，或选择多个互补模型以获得集成精度提升。路由决策自动生成结构化记录（查询、harness状态、模型选择、执行轨迹、结果、成本），标签由环境提供，形成"harness原生数据飞轮"：执行轨迹训练更好的路由器和harness原生模型，改善成本-质量权衡，进而在相同预算下生成更多轨迹。在OpenSquilla框架中实现四层路由栈，包含开源的LightGBM冷启动排序器和分阶段的路由器-模型训练路径。

Result: 在DRACO和PinchBench等Agent基准上实验，验证了单模型路由的成本效益和多模型路由的精度提升，并论证Agent路由不仅是成本控制手段，更是Agent原生训练的数据引擎。

Conclusion: Agentic routing将模型选择从单轮优化提升为步骤级、状态感知的系统决策，同时利用路由决策产生的数据形成自我强化的飞轮效应，为Agent原生模型训练提供数据引擎范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly executed not by a single model call, but by an execution harness that manages observation, context, control, action, state, and verification. At the same time, frontier and open models are becoming structurally specialized: a model that is strong at code editing, long-context recovery, tool use, mathematical reasoning, or low-latency response may not dominate on the other axes. This makes model selection inside an agent a core systems problem rather than a per-query serving trick. Existing routing methods mostly optimize single-turn cost-quality trade-offs and therefore miss the execution state, intermediate failures, and feedback loops that make agents different from chat completion. We propose Harness-Native agentic routing, a step-level routing paradigm that selects either a single best-fit model for cost-effective execution or multiple complementary models for ensemble-style accuracy improvement, conditioned on the full harness state. The key insight is that every routing decision naturally produces a structured data record -- consisting of the query, harness state, model choice or model set, execution trace, outcome, and cost -- whose labels are supplied by the environment rather than by the router itself. These records form a harness-native data flywheel: execution traces train better routers and harness-native models, which improve cost-quality trade-offs and generate more traces under the same budget. We instantiate this idea in OpenSquilla with a four-layer routing stack, an open LightGBM cold-start ranker, and a staged router-model path that turns logged arena records into progressively stronger routing policies. The report studies singleton and multi-model routing on agentic benchmarks including DRACO and PinchBench, and argues that agentic routing is not merely cost control, but a data engine for agent-native training.

</details>


### [135] [Cross-Architecture LLM Ensembles, Feature-Based Reranking and Retrieval-Augmented Prompting for Legal Information Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11400)
*Amal Saad Alshehri,Nelly Bencomo,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Team DU participated in all five tasks of COLIEE 2026, employing open-weight systems across legal case/statute retrieval, entailment, and judgment prediction. Key results include first place in statute entailment (96.3% accuracy via cross-architecture ensemble), top unofficial performance in tort prediction/rationale extraction, and significant post-competition gains through prompt engineering and model scaling.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Legal information processing tasks require text matching, reasoning, and robust generalization under limited supervision. The work aims to systematically evaluate diverse inductive biases — cross-architecture ensembling, feature-based reranking, and retrieval-augmented prompting — across heterogeneous legal NLP tasks to determine which strategies are most effective in each setting.

Method: The approach spans five tasks: (1) Task 1 (legal case retrieval): a learning-to-rank system with 34 features combining lexical/semantic retrieval, structural, citation authority, and temporal features; (2) Task 2 (case entailment): prompt engineering from single- to multi-selection prompting; (3) Tasks 3–4 (statute retrieval & entailment): a cross-architecture ensemble of nine models from three families; post-competition, Qwen3-235B with structured legal reasoning prompt replaces the entailment model; (4) Pilot Task (tort prediction & rationale extraction): a multi-view system combining five claim-level models, with verdict refinement using claim-derived features.

Result: Task 4: 96.3% accuracy, ranked 1st among 33 submissions from 11 teams. Pilot Task: 73.1% TP accuracy and 68.2% RE F1 (unofficial), exceeding all official TP entries and matching the best RE score. Task 2: Post-competition F1 improved from 0.343 to 0.555, surpassing the best official F1 of 0.490. Task 3: Post-competition accuracy improved from 79.3% to 91.5%. Task 1: F1 = 0.314, ranked 11th of 54 submissions from 22 teams.

Conclusion: Legal information processing benefits from different inductive biases depending on the task. Cross-architecture ensembling is most effective for statute entailment, feature-based reranking excels in case retrieval, and retrieval-augmented prompting proves most beneficial for case entailment. No single strategy dominates across all legal NLP tasks.

Abstract: Legal information processing spans retrieval, entailment and judgment prediction problems, requiring text matching, reasoning and robust generalisation with limited supervision. We report Team DU's participation in all five tasks of COLIEE 2026, using open-weight systems for legal case retrieval, case entailment, statute retrieval and entailment, and legal judgment prediction. For Tasks 3 and 4, all models predate the 15 July 2025 cutoff required by the rules. For Task 4 (statute entailment), a cross-architecture ensemble of nine models from three families achieves 96.3% accuracy, placing first among 33 submissions from 11 teams. For the Pilot Task (tort prediction and rationale extraction), a multi-view system combining five claim-level models and refining the verdict using features derived from the claim predictions achieves 73.1% TP accuracy and 68.2% RE F1 as an unofficial submission, scoring above all official entries on TP and matching the highest on RE. For Task 2 (legal case entailment), changing only the prompt from single- to multi-selection raises F1 from 0.343 to 0.555 in post-competition evaluation on released gold labels, exceeding the best official submission (F1 = 0.490). For Task 3 (statute retrieval and entailment), replacing the entailment model with Qwen3-235B and a structured legal reasoning prompt raises accuracy from 79.3% to 91.5% in post-competition analysis. For Task 1 (legal case retrieval), a learning-to-rank system combining lexical and semantic retrieval with structural, citation authority, and temporal features (34 in total) achieves F1 = 0.314 (rank 11 of 54 submissions from 22 teams). Overall, legal information processing benefits from different inductive biases across tasks, with cross-architecture ensembling, feature-based reranking and retrieval-augmented prompting each proving most effective in different settings.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Conditional copula graphic estimator for semi-competing risks data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09894)
*Shamsia Sobhan,Elif Fidan Acar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种条件copula-graphic估计量，可在半竞争风险数据中对非终末事件时间的生存函数进行协变量调整估计，通过Archimedean copula参数化条件依赖结构，采用迭代算法交替更新估计，并在模拟和真实数据中验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半竞争风险数据的非终末事件生存函数估计方法主要集中于无条件设定，而临床应用中常需纳入协变量以控制混杂并改善估计精度，但缺乏能同时在边缘生存函数和依赖结构中引入协变量的方法。

Method: 提出条件copula-graphic估计量，采用半参数框架：用Archimedean copula参数化条件依赖结构，依赖参数函数和边缘分布通过非参数方法估计。具体通过序贯迭代算法交替更新非终末事件生存函数与条件copula来实现估计。

Result: 通过模拟数据和真实数据的评估，条件copula-graphic估计量表现良好；与无条件copula-graphic估计量对比，揭示了忽略协变量效应会带来的不良后果。

Conclusion: 条件copula-graphic估计量能有效在半竞争风险场景中整合协变量信息，同时调整边缘生存函数及依赖结构，相比无条件方法具有明显优势。

Abstract: In semi-competing risks data, the interest lies in the estimation of the survival function of a non-terminal event time, which is subject to dependent censoring by a terminal event. This problem has been extensively studied in the literature, but mostly focusing on unconditional settings. However, in many clinical applications incorporating covariates is necessary to control for confounding and improve survival function estimation. In this paper, we propose a conditional copula-graphic estimator that allows for covariate adjustment in the marginal survival functions of the non-terminal and terminal event times as well as in their dependence structure. The proposed estimator is semiparametric in that the conditional copula is specified parametrically using an Archimedean copula, but its dependence parameter function and margins are estimated nonparametrically. The estimator is obtained via a sequential iterative algorithm with alternating updates of the survival function of the non-terminal event and the conditional copula. The performance of the conditional copula-graphic estimator is assessed using simulated and real data, and is compared to that of the unconditional copula-graphic estimator to investigate the consequences of failing to account for covariate effects.

</details>


### [137] [Runs and Bootstrap Tests For Signal Feature Significance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09913)
*Greg Kreider*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文利用马尔可夫链理论推导了最长游程分布的递归表达式，并基于此开发了针对数据特征高度的置换检验和自助检验方法，可同时评估峰值和谷值，最终应用于数据间距中的多模态检测与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游程检验作为一种经典的非参数方法，长期以来用于检测数据中是否存在非随机信号。然而，对最长游程的精确分布以及如何利用游程特征进行更细致的数据结构推断（如多模态检测）仍缺乏系统的方法。

Method: 1. 使用马尔可夫链理论推导最长游程分布的递归表达式；2. 基于游程特征构建置换检验（permutation test），获取特征高度的概率；3. 进一步利用原始连续数据构建自助检验（bootstrap test），评估峰值高度和谷值（flats）高度；4. 将上述检验应用于数据间距特征，实现多模态的检测与定位。

Result: 所提出的方法能够同时评估数据中的高峰（峰值）和低谷（平坦区域），并通过数据间距中的特征检验有效检测和定位多模态结构。

Conclusion: 基于马尔可夫链的递归分布推导为游程检验提供了更精确的理论基础，结合置换检验和自助检验，该方法为数据的多模态检测与定位提供了一种灵活且强大的非参数工具。

Abstract: Runs tests have long been used as a non-parametric check if data contains a non-random signal. We derive a recursive expression for the distribution of the longest run using Markov chain theory. Next we develop a permutation test on the runs comprising a feature to get the probability of its height. This leads finally to a bootstrap test on the height using the raw, continuous data. Such a test can evaluate not only the large heights of peaks but also the small heights of flats. We can apply these tests to features in the spacing of data to detect and locate multi-modality.

</details>


### [138] [Toward Joint Prediction of a Longitudinal Marker and a Terminal Event: A bivariate discrete-time framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09931)
*Stephanie Armbruster,Daniel Kramer,Rui Duan,Rajarshi Mukherjee,Sebastien Haneuse,Harrison Reeder*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对植入式心律转复除颤器(ICD)治疗决策中涉及死亡与生活质量的多重权衡问题，本文提出一个贝叶斯双变量离散时间联合建模框架，同时预测终末事件（死亡）和纵向标志物（生活质量），并通过离散化处理死亡截断问题，最终在SCD-HeFT临床试验数据上验证其临床适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测工具仅关注单变量结局，将其他临床终点作为输入而非联合输出，无法直接为ICD治疗中涉及死亡风险与生活质量的多重权衡决策提供信息支持。此外，纵向标志物在终末事件（死亡）后无法定义，存在截断问题，传统连续时间方法可能产生不合理外推。

Method: 提出一个离散时间双变量联合建模框架：(1)将研究时间离散化，联合建模终末事件与纵向标志物为双变量过程；(2)纳入全局时不变和局部时变依赖结构，以及共享脆弱项刻画潜在关联；(3)采用贝叶斯范式进行参数估计，生成患者特异性的联合后验预测分布（终末事件发生时间与未来标志物轨迹）；(4)提供离散化选择、模型构建与选择的指导。

Result: 该框架能灵活捕捉终末事件与纵向标志物之间的动态交互关系，避免死亡截断后的不合理外推。通过联合后验预测，可为ICD治疗决策提供死亡风险与生活质量轨迹的综合权衡信息。在SCD-HeFT数据上的应用展示了临床可行性和新颖洞见。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯双变量离散时间联合模型框架为以预测为目标的临床场景提供了一种灵活、可解释的工具，能够直接量化终末事件与纵向结局之间的复杂权衡，为个体化ICD治疗决策提供更全面的预测支持。

Abstract: Sudden cardiac death (SCD) is a leading cause of death in the U.S. Patients at elevated risk of SCD are primarily treated with an implantable cardioverter-defibrillator (ICD), which can prevent cardiovascular death but often causes severe side effects, such as reduced quality of life from shock-induced pain. Decisions about ICD treatment involve complex personal trade-offs across multiple health events, including mortality and quality of life. While prediction tools could help patients and providers weigh these trade-offs, they focus on univariate outcomes, treating other clinical endpoints as inputs, so trade-offs cannot be directly informed. To address this, we propose a novel general framework that jointly models a terminal event and a longitudinal marker as a bivariate process over discrete time, for settings where prediction is the primary goal. Discretizing study time lets the framework capture the dynamic interplay between outcomes while avoiding implicit extrapolation beyond truncation by a terminal event, such as death. The framework flexibly accommodates phenomena arising in applied contexts, including global time-invariant and local time-dependent dependence structures between the terminal event and marker, and latent association via a shared frailty term. Estimation proceeds via the Bayesian paradigm, facilitating uncertainty quantification and yielding patient-specific joint posterior predictions for time to terminal event and future marker trajectory. We introduce this bivariate discrete-time framework with a focus on its flexibility, provide guidance on choosing the discretization of study time and on Bayesian model construction and selection, and discuss insights gained from its novel output, joint posterior predictions. Finally, we demonstrate its clinical applicability using data from the Sudden Cardiac Death in Heart Failure Trial (SCD-HeFT) on SCD and ICD therapy.

</details>


### [139] [Geometric mean-based pairwise comparison method with the reference values -- statistical approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10038)
*Konrad Kułakowski,Jacek Szybowski*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从统计学视角重新审视了成对比较法（以AHP为背景），提出使用参考值和几何平均来计算备选方案权重，从而能够同时刻画不一致性现象与偏好距离，并定义了具有清晰直观解释的质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的AHP方法依赖主特征向量来计算权重，但对比较矩阵中的不一致性以及备选方案之间的偏好距离缺乏统一的、直观的统计学解释框架。作者希望用统计学视角统一这两个问题，并提供具有清晰解释力的权重质量评估指标。

Method: 采用统计学视角，引入参考值概念，使用几何平均（而非主特征向量）来计算备选方案的优先级权重。基于该统计框架，同时捕获成对比较中的不一致性现象和备选方案间的偏好距离，并定义相应的权重向量质量评估指标。

Result: 通过统计学方法，成功将不一致性和偏好距离纳入统一框架；所定义的质量指标具有清晰、直观的解释，能够有效衡量所得权重向量的可靠性。

Conclusion: 基于几何平均和参考值的统计方法为成对比较法提供了更直观的理论基础，不仅能同时处理不一致性与偏好距离，还产出可解释性强的权重质量指标，是对经典AHP方法的有益补充和拓展。

Abstract: For many years, the pairwise comparison method has been widely used for decision-making involving experts. The best-known example of this method is the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP). In this now classic approach, the weights of alternatives are calculated using the principal eigenvector of the comparison matrix.
