In this paper, we present a statistical view of the pairwise comparison method, using reference values and the geometric mean to calculate alternative priorities. Thanks to this approach, we can simultaneously capture both the phenomenon of inconsistency in pairwise comparisons and the preference distance between alternatives. In this paper, we define indicators that measure the quality of the obtained weight vector, which, thanks to the statistical approach, have a clear and intuitive interpretation.

</details>


### [140] [Scalable Bayesian structure learning of directed acyclic graphs via Laplace approximation, with an application to breast cancer gene expression networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10222)
*Samaneh Nazari,Mohammad Arashi,Abdolnasser Sadeghkhani*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于拉普拉斯近似的贝叶斯评分函数，用于非共轭Normal-Gamma先验下的有向无环图（DAG）结构学习，并将其嵌入Metropolis-Hastings采样器，同时扩展到二值临床结局的probit链接。方法在模拟和真实数据上均展现出优于现有基准的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯DAG结构学习面临两大障碍：(1) DAG空间超指数增长；(2) 非共轭先验下节点边际似然的不可解性。现有闭式解多局限于共轭的Normal-Inverse-Gamma先验，限制了灵活先验知识的融入。

Method: 在精度矩阵的修正Cholesky参数化下，采用Normal-Gamma非共轭先验，推导节点边际积分为广义逆高斯形式（精确值为第二类修正贝塞尔函数），并提出其大参数渐近的拉普拉斯近似评分函数。将该评分嵌入Metropolis-Hastings DAG采样器，并通过probit链接将潜高斯网络耦合至二值临床结局。

Result: 模拟实验中，所提先验在典型临床样本量下优于共轭基线及PC、GES、NOTEARS、DAGMA等基准。在Sachs蛋白信号网络数据上恢复了已知结构；在威斯康星乳腺癌数据上，DAG-probit扩展以稀疏可解释的直接预测因子实现了0.94的交叉验证ROC-AUC。

Conclusion: 所提的拉普拉斯近似贝叶斯评分方法有效解决了非共轭先验下的DAG结构学习难题，在有限样本下具有竞争力，且其probit扩展为临床预测提供了稀疏可解释的因果模型。

Abstract: Structure learning of directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) from observational data is a foundational task in causal discovery and is widely used to infer regulatory networks from medical and genomic measurements. The Bayesian formulation quantifies model uncertainty and admits prior biological knowledge, but its practical use has been hampered by the super-exponential growth of the DAG space and by the intractability of the node-marginal likelihood under flexible, non-conjugate priors. Existing closed-form solutions are largely confined to the conjugate Normal--Inverse-Gamma prior. We develop a Laplace-approximated Bayesian scoring function for the non-conjugate Normal--Gamma prior on the modified Cholesky parameterisation of the precision matrix, embed it in a Metropolis--Hastings sampler over DAGs, and couple the latent Gaussian network to a binary clinical outcome through a probit link. We show that the node-marginal integral is of generalised inverse-Gaussian form, so that its exact value is a modified Bessel function of the second kind and the proposed scoring function is its leading large-argument asymptotic; the posterior of each conditional variance is likewise generalised inverse-Gaussian and is sampled exactly. In simulation, the proposed prior improves on the conjugate baseline and on the PC, greedy-equivalence-search, NOTEARS, and DAGMA benchmarks at sample sizes typical of clinical cohorts. On two real datasets, the Sachs protein-signalling network, scored against its validated consensus graph, and the Wisconsin Diagnostic Breast Cancer data, the method recovers known structure and, through the DAG-probit extension, predicts malignancy from nuclear morphometry with a cross-validated ROC-AUC of $0.94$ using a sparse, interpretable set of direct predictors.

</details>


### [141] [Bayesian Robustness Values for Modern Causal Panel Estimators via Riesz Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10276)
*Makoto Nakakita,Takahiro Hoshino*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对因果面板估计量（包括合成双重差分、矩阵补全、固定效应插补和分组时间平均处理效应）的敏感性分析工作流程，结合Riesz表示遗漏变量偏差边界与部分R²稳健性值，并区分两条报告路径：路径A给出直接敏感性轮廓，路径B在诊断通过时使用观测协变量基准，否则仅作降级处理。作者还推导了估计量特定的Riesz诊断指标，并通过蒙特卡洛压力测试和两个实证应用（加州控烟面板和县级最低工资）进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果面板估计量在实际应用中面临未观测混杂的威胁，现有敏感性分析方法缺乏对各类现代面板估计量的统一覆盖框架，且研究者常机械地使用观测协变量作为基准而不检验其可信度。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供系统的敏感性分析工作流程和诊断工具。

Method: 作者将Riesz表示遗漏变量偏差边界与部分R²稳健性值相结合，构建双路径报告框架：路径A直接基于结果侧和Riesz侧的部分R²值刻画加性或投影混杂的敏感性轮廓；路径B对观测协变量基准施加计数、alpha侧对齐、模型检验、依赖性和主导性五项诊断，仅通过时作为辅助信息，否则降级。推导了各估计量特定的Riesz诊断，区分固定权重、目标水平和第一阶段条件等类型。蒙特卡洛压力测试评估校准基准设定、主导性失效、粗糙基准、基准依赖、噪声协变量和集中权重等情景。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟区分了校准基准与主导性失效等不同情景。在加州控烟面板中，SDID估计值为每人-15.60包；经修正的有限供体安慰剂推断给出标准误9.49和add-one p值0.051；重构权重有限差分审计将路径A的无效化稳健性值从0.054调整为0.045，低个位数灵敏度结论不变。县级最低工资应用展示了同一轮廓在多队列交叠面板中的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的敏感性分析工作流程为现代因果面板估计量提供了统一、透明且诊断驱动的敏感性评估框架，强调不应机械采用观测协变量基准，而需通过系统诊断检验其可信度；路径A可作为默认报告路径，路径B仅在诊断通过时提供补充信息。

Abstract: We develop a sensitivity-analysis workflow for causal panel estimators, covering synthetic difference-in-differences, matrix completion, fixed-effect imputation, and group-time average treatment effects. The workflow combines Riesz-representation omitted-variable-bias bounds with partial-$R^2$ robustness values and separates two reporting routes. Route A gives a direct sensitivity profile for additive or projected confounding summarized by outcome-side and Riesz-side partial $R^2$ values. Route B treats observed-covariate benchmarks as auxiliary data only when benchmark-count, alpha-side alignment, model-check, dependence, and dominance diagnostics are credible; otherwise its main role is demotion. We derive estimator-specific Riesz diagnostics and clarify which are fixed-weight, target-level, or first-stage-conditional rather than full derivatives of regularized training maps. Monte Carlo stress tests distinguish calibrated benchmark settings from dominance failure, coarse alpha-side benchmarks, benchmark dependence, noisy covariates, and concentrated SDID weights. In the California tobacco-control panel, the SDID estimate is $-15.60$ packs per capita; corrected finite-donor placebo inference gives standard error 9.49 and add-one $p=0.051$. A refit-weight finite-difference audit changes the Route A nullification robustness value from 0.054 to 0.045, leaving the low-single-digit conclusion unchanged. A county-level minimum-wage application applies the same profile to a multi-cohort staggered panel.

</details>


### [142] [Graph statistics: An emerging discipline in non-Euclidean data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10324)
*Rongling Wu,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文概述了图统计作为数据科学新兴学科的理论基础、方法创新与应用前景，强调其通过整合演化博弈论、生态位理论、拓扑数据分析及图论，以准动态非线性建模应对非欧几里得数据的分析挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据自然呈现为动态网络结构，传统统计学局限于欧几里得空间，无法有效处理此类复杂互联数据。因此，需要建立图统计这一新学科，以填补非欧数据统计分析的空白。

Method: 文章采用综述方法，整合演化博弈论、生态位理论、拓扑数据分析和图论，构建准动态非线性建模框架，以此形成面向非欧数据的图统计方法论体系。

Result: 研究表明，图统计通过上述理论整合已形成一种新的统计思维范式，能够有效分析非欧几里得数据，并有望在多个学科领域产生变革性影响。

Conclusion: 图统计作为一门新兴学科，为将大数据转化为实用知识提供了原则性框架，将在数量遗传学、系统生物学、材料科学和人工智能等领域引发革命性突破。

Abstract: The explosive growth of complex data has catalyzed the emergence of graph statistics as a fundamentally new discipline in data science. Unlike traditional statistics, which operates primarily within the comfortable confines of Euclidean spaces, graph statistics confronts the reality that modern data naturally organize themselves as dynamic networks composed of complex interconnections. In this article, we present an overview of graph statistics as an emerging discipline, tracing its theoretical foundations, methodological innovations, and transformative applications. We examine how the integration of evolutionary game theory, ecological niche theory, topological data analysis, and graph theory through quasi-dynamic nonlinear modeling has created a new norm of statistical thinking capable of analyzing non-Euclidean data. We show how graph statistics is poised to revolutionize fields ranging from quantitative genetics and systems biology to materials science and artificial intelligence, offering a principled framework for transforming big data into practical knowledge.

</details>


### [143] [Adaptable Regularized CCA Tests for Independence of High-Dimensional Random Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10500)
*Haoran Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种基于岭正则化和PCA降维的自适应典型相关分析检验方法，用于高维随机向量独立性检验，包含正则化似然比检验和正则化最大根检验两种形式，并给出数据驱动的正则化参数选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典典型相关分析（CCA）在高维设定下检验统计量不稳定，需要一种能适应高维场景的稳健独立性检验方法。

Method: 将岭正则化和主成分降维嵌入CCA框架，依据降维维度构建正则化似然比检验和正则化最大根检验两种统计量，并推导其在原假设和备择假设下的渐近性质，同时提出数据驱动的正则化参数选择策略。

Result: 建立了所提检验在原假设和代表性备择假设下的渐近分布理论；大量模拟研究表明该方法在多种场景下均具有优良的有限样本表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应检验框架有效解决了高维独立性检验中经典CCA统计量不稳定的问题，兼具理论保证和实用价值。

Abstract: We propose an adaptable testing procedure for independence between two high-dimensional random vectors. The method incorporates ridge regularization and principal component-based dimension reduction into the canonical correlation analysis (CCA) framework, thereby stabilizing classical test statistics in high-dimensional settings. Depending on the reduced dimension, we develop both a regularized likelihood ratio test and a regularized largest-root test to accommodate different testing scenarios. We establish the asymptotic behavior of the proposed procedures under both the null hypothesis and representative alternatives, and further develop a data-driven method for selecting the regularization parameter. Extensive simulation studies demonstrate favorable finite-sample performance across a broad range of settings.

</details>


### [144] [Proximal Identification and Estimation in Front-Door Causal Structures with Unobserved Confounding of the Mediator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10515)
*Helen Guo,Beatrix Yaxin Wen,Ilya Shpitser*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种前门准则的代理推广方法，在存在未观测混杂因素的情况下，通过引入可观测的代理变量，允许中介变量与结局之间存在未观测混杂，从而扩展了经典前门准则的适用范围，并给出了相应的识别策略和估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的前门准则虽能在存在未观测的"处理-结局"混杂时实现非参数识别，但要求中介变量完全无混杂（即不存在未观测的"中介-结局"混杂），这一严格条件极大限制了其实际应用。本文旨在放松这一限制，使前门类识别策略能适用于更广泛的因果推断场景。

Method: 作者提出了前门准则的代理推广：允许处理与结局之间存在任意未观测混杂，同时允许中介变量受到未观测混杂因素的影响，前提是能够观测到这些未观测混杂因素的"代理变量"（proxy）。基于此框架，推导出三种新的非参数识别策略，并给出了两种估计方法——基于plug-in的估计以及基于影响函数的估计，后者具有双重稳健性质。

Result: 在新的识别框架下，因果效应函数在三种识别策略下均可被表达为可观测数据的泛函。模拟实验评估了所提估计方法的有限样本性能，验证了plug-in估计和影响函数估计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入代理变量，前门准则的适用范围被显著拓宽，解决了经典前门准则中"中介变量必须无混杂"的严苛要求。所提出的影响函数估计方法具备双重稳健性，在实际应用中更具优势。

Abstract: Unobserved confounding is a fundamental obstacle in causal inference problems. In the graphical modeling literature, a general theory has been developed that allows identification in the presence of hidden variables, with some limitations. In particular, Pearl's celebrated front-door criterion allows nonparametric identification in the presence of unobserved common causes of the treatment and the outcome, however it requires the presence of an unconfounded variable that mediates all causal influence from the treatment to the outcome. This stringent requirement limits the applicability of the front-door criterion. We propose proximal generalizations of the front-door criterion, allowing both arbitrary treatment/outcome confounding, and unobserved confounders of the mediator, provided informative proxies for the latter type of confounders are observed. In addition to deriving three new identification strategies in this setting, we provide plug-in and influence function-based estimation strategies for the resulting functionals, and evaluate their performance through simulations.

</details>


### [145] [Observation-Level Watermarking and Detection for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10554)
*Dongyu Cui,Xuan Bi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: STAMP 是一种面向表格数据的水印框架，可处理数值、离散、分类及混合数据类型，支持低至单样本的可靠检测，并具有理论一致性和检测准确率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印技术主要针对图像和文本，表格数据水印研究不足，且现有方法多聚焦于数值型数据，对离散、分类和混合数据关注较少。

Method: 提出 STAMP 框架，能够适配并保留广泛的数据分布类型，同时配套设计了相应的检测机制，支持极小样本（甚至单样本）场景下的水印识别。

Result: 通过大量仿真研究和两个真实数据应用，验证了该方法在子集化等条件下仍保持有效性和鲁棒性，同时维持高数据保真度和高检测率。

Conclusion: STAMP 填补了表格数据水印的空白，兼具理论保障与实践效果，适用于多种数据类型和现实场景。

Abstract: With the development of generative AI, watermarking techniques have been widely used to detect the authenticity of AI-generated data and protect the rights of users and creators. While it is already well applied in data types including imaging and text data, watermarking tabular data is still under-explored. Existing methods primarily focus on numerical data, leaving discrete, categorical, and mixed data less studied. In this work, we propose STAMP (Single-observation Tabular Attribution and Marking Procedure), a novel framework for watermarking tabular data that can accommodate and preserve a wide range of distributions. We also develop a corresponding detection mechanism, which can reliably identify watermarks even when the sample size is as small as one. We establish theoretical guarantees for asymptotic consistency and detection accuracy. Finally, through extensive simulation studies and two real-data applications, we demonstrate that the proposed method is effective and robust to subsetting, while maintaining data fidelity and a high detection rate.

</details>


### [146] [When Does Survey-Aware Cross-Validation Matter? The ICC, Not the Design Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10634)
*M. Ehsan Karim,Md Belal Hossain*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种低成本诊断方法（结果变量和初步线性预测变量的簇内组内相关系数ICC），用于判断在复杂调查数据中是否有必要使用设计尊重型交叉验证。通过模拟和三项全国健康调查的实证表明，ICC能正确预判朴素CV与调查CV之间是否存在实际差异，并发现分层方案在公开数据中常不可行，权重处理错误的量级远大于折划分方案的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K折交叉验证假设观测独立可交换，但复杂抽样调查中的分层、聚类和不等权重违反该假设。虽然"调查CV"（设计尊重型交叉验证）已存在，但何时需要这种额外谨慎才能改变结论的问题一直悬而未决。研究者希望在实际建模前提供判断依据。

Method: 提出两个诊断指标：结果变量的簇内ICC和初步线性预测变量的簇内ICC。通过模拟正对照验证其敏感性，再在三项全国健康调查（慢性疼痛、糖尿病、青少年自杀倾向预测）中对比朴素CV与设计尊重型CV，使用惩罚模型、随机森林和无惩罚模型。还评估了分层方案的可行性，并给出备选层次方案。

Result: 两项诊断在所有三项实证中正确预判了结果：均未出现实际规模的方案差异，唯一排除零的区间显示的是悲观而非乐观（即未出现簇泄露导致的乐观偏差），即使设计效应很大也是如此。分层方案在公开使用设计中常不可行。权重处理错误的量级远超折划分方案的影响。

Conclusion: 簇内ICC而非设计效应才是判断是否需要调查CV的正确触发指标。在多数实际应用中朴素CV可能已足够，但权重处理错误更值得关注。论文附有可复现代码。

Abstract: K-fold cross-validation assumes exchangeable observations, violated by the stratification, clustering, and unequal weights of complex sample surveys. Design-respecting "survey CV" exists, but the question of when the extra care changes any conclusion has remained open. We answer it before validating any model, with two inexpensive diagnostics: the within-cluster intraclass correlation (ICC) of the outcome and of a preliminary linear predictor. A simulated positive control demonstrates their sensitivity, with naive cross-validation growing optimistic about new-cluster performance as the ICC rises while cluster-level folds stay honest. We then evaluate paired naive-versus-design-respecting cross-validation in three national health surveys (chronic-pain, diabetes, and adolescent-suicidality prediction; penalized and random-forest learners, plus an unpenalized comparator) - one reanalysis, one prospective application, and one prespecified screen. In all three the diagnostics correctly anticipated the outcome: no scheme difference of practical size, and the only interval excluding zero showed pessimism, not the optimism that cluster leakage produces - even where the design effect was large (which, unlike the ICC, is not the right trigger). We also show the stratified recipe is often infeasible in public-use designs and give a fallback hierarchy, and we document weight-handling errors whose order-of-magnitude artifacts dwarfed any fold-scheme effect. Reproducible code accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [147] [GNet: A scalable and flexible Gaussian process network with nonparametric neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10735)
*Mengyang Gu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: GNet 是一种可扩展的灵活高斯过程网络，其非参数激活函数由高斯过程建模。作者提出联合逆卡尔曼滤波器以加速训练和预测，避免构建协方差矩阵，在非线性函数预测、真实数据非参数回归及高维密度泛函理论问题中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯过程模型面临计算和存储成本高昂的问题，尤其是在大规模数据和高维输入场景下。作者旨在设计一种既保持高斯过程非参数灵活性，又能大幅降低计算与存储开销的可扩展模型。

Method: 提出 GNet（高斯过程网络），将高斯过程作为非参数激活函数嵌入网络结构。核心创新是联合逆卡尔曼滤波器（jointly inverse Kalman filter），该快速算法配合梯度闭式解，可在无需显式构造协方差矩阵的前提下完成模型训练和预测。

Result: 在统一优化框架下，GNet 在多个测试问题上表现出竞争力：非线性函数预测、真实数据非参数回归，以及经典密度泛函理论中高维输入的单体直接相关函数预测。

Conclusion: GNet 结合联合逆卡尔曼滤波器，在大规模预测建模中展现出广泛适用性，能够显著降低计算和存储成本，同时保持强劲性能。

Abstract: We develop GNet, a scalable and flexible Gaussian process network with nonparametric activation functions modeled by Gaussian processes. To reduce computational and storage costs, we introduce the jointly inverse Kalman filter, a fast algorithm together with closed-form expressions of gradients for accelerating model training and predictions without the need to form covariance matrices. Using a unified optimization setting, GNet shows competitive performance across a diverse range of test problems, including predicting nonlinear functions, nonparametric regression of real-world data, and predicting one-body direct correlation functions with high-dimensional inputs in classical density function theory. The strong performance of GNet, accelerated by the jointly inverse Kalman filter, suggests broad applicability to large-scale predictive modeling with substantially reduced computational and storage costs.

</details>


### [148] [When Screening Misleads: A Robust Mendelian Randomization Test for Reliable Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10755)
*Litao Jia,Bo Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文揭示了孟德尔随机化研究中先筛选关联再检验因果的做法会导致严重的I类错误膨胀，并提出了一种对筛选步骤稳健的新型MR检验方法，该方法还能利用既往关联研究的汇总统计量提升检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物医学研究中，当真实的暴露和结局变量未知时，研究者常先进行关联筛选，再对显著相关的暴露-结局对进行因果效应检验。这种常见做法下，经典的MR ratio estimator会产生严重的I类错误膨胀，亟需一种在此筛选程序下依然有效的稳健检验方法。

Method: 提出了一种新型稳健孟德尔随机化检验方法。该方法在无真实因果效应的情况下，无论是否经过关联筛选步骤，均能保持正确的I类错误率。此外，该方法可整合既往关联研究的汇总统计量来提升因果效应检验的统计功效。

Result: 所提方法在无真实因果效应时始终维持正确的I类错误率，且该性质独立于关联检验结果。广泛的模拟研究验证了该方法相较于经典MR方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 先筛选后检验的做法破坏了经典MR方法的统计推断有效性；而本文提出的稳健MR检验方法有效解决了筛选带来的I类错误膨胀问题，并能借助外部汇总统计量增强功效，具有重要的方法学价值和实践意义。

Abstract: Mendelian Randomization (MR) has been widely used as a standard approach for identifying causal effects in biomedical research. When the true exposure and outcome variables are unknown among many potential candidates, a common but problematic practice is to first screen for associations and then evaluate causal effects only among exposure-outcome pairs that show significant correlations. We demonstrate that the classical MR ratio estimator suffers from severe type I error inflation under this selection procedure. To address this issue, we propose a novel robust MR test that remains valid regardless of the prior association screening step when there is no true causal effect. We show that the proposed test consistently maintains the correct type I error rate, independent of the association test results. Furthermore, our method can incorporate summary statistics from previous association studies to improve the power of causal effect detection. Extensive simulation studies illustrate the advantages of the proposed method compared with the classical MR approach.

</details>


### [149] [Causal Estimation of Share-Induced Engagement with Flywheel Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10820)
*Weitao Cheng,Yilin Li,Yong Wang,Nian Si*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对在线平台分享功能的实验框架，用于测量分享功能通过社交网络多轮传播（飞轮效应）引发的总参与度因果效应。该方法基于流平衡恒等式，将分享诱导的参与解释为几何放大过程，利用常见的归因日志给出封闭形式的传播调整量，克服了传统A/B测试中无干扰假设的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台的可持续增长依赖分享功能来重新激活现有用户并形成"飞轮效应"——被激活用户通过社交网络多轮传播放大总参与度。然而，经典的A/B测试依赖无干扰假设（SUTVA），无法处理社交网络中用户相互影响的干扰问题，因此需要新的因果推断框架来测量分享功能的全局处理效应。

Method: 作者基于"流平衡恒等式"（flow-balance identity）构建估计量，将分享诱导的参与度建模为几何放大过程。该估计量利用日常可获取的归因日志（attribution logs），推导出考虑多轮传播的封闭形式传播调整量。同时，在温和条件下证明估计量的一致性，并提出用于管道验证的A/A测试程序。此外，通过泊松近似将框架扩展至用户级别的再激活指标。

Result: 模拟研究表明，相比均值差估计量和一阶调整方法，所提方法显著降低了偏差；A/A测试能维持名义第一类错误率。在真实大规模在线平台上的实证应用验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为在线平台评估分享功能设计提供了可靠的因果推断工具，能够捕捉社交网络中多轮传播的飞轮效应，解决了传统A/B测试因网络干扰而产生的偏差问题，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Sustainable user growth in online platforms depends not only on acquiring new users but also on reactivating and engaging existing ones through social sharing features. A well-designed sharing feature can trigger a self-reinforcing ``flywheel effect'': reactivated users become potential sharers whose engagement propagates through the network over multiple rounds, amplifying total engagement. Measuring the causal impact of such sharing features is challenging, as their effects unfold through complex social networks and temporal cascades, violating the no-interference assumption underlying classical A/B testing. We develop a framework for experiments on sharing features that accounts for interference caused by the flywheel effect and targets a global treatment effect on share-induced engagement. Our estimator is motivated by a flow-balance identity and interprets share-induced engagement as a geometric amplification process, yielding a closed-form propagation adjustment that accounts for multi-round diffusion using commonly available attribution logs. Under mild conditions, we establish consistency of the proposed estimator and develop a valid A/A testing procedure for pipeline validation. Simulation studies show that our method substantially reduces bias relative to the difference-in-means estimator and first-order adjustments, while the proposed A/A test maintains nominal Type I error. We also extend the framework to a user-level reactivation metric via a Poisson approximation. Finally, we demonstrate the approach on a real-world large-scale online platform and discuss empirical implications for evaluating sharing feature designs.

</details>


### [150] [A Beta-Based Heteroskedasticity-Consistent Covariance Matrix Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10905)
*Marina O. Cunha,Francisco Cribari-Neto,Pedro R. D. Marinho*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的异方差一致协方差矩阵估计量，用基于Beta分布拟合的数据驱动校正取代传统杠杆值调整，能自适应样本杠杆结构，在有限样本下推断更准确，并提供开源R包hcinfer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异方差一致估计量（如HC0-HC4）依赖杠杆值进行校正，但在高杠杆观测存在时调整因子可能过度膨胀，影响有限样本推断精度。需要一种能自动适应样本杠杆结构、避免过度校正的新方法。

Method: 提出用拟合Beta分布来刻画观测杠杆值的分布特征，基于Beta分布参数估计导出数据驱动的校正因子，替代传统HC类估计量中固定的杠杆调整方案。Beta参数从样本杠杆值中估计，使调整因子自动适应具体数据。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟表明，新估计量在有限样本下具有准确的推断性能和置信区间覆盖率，同时保持良好的渐近性质。实证应用进一步展示了其在存在影响观测时的实际优势。

Conclusion: 基于Beta分布拟合的异方差一致协方差矩阵估计量有效解决了传统方法在高杠杆情形下的过度校正问题，兼顾有限样本精度与渐近有效性，具有推广价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new heteroskedasticity-consistent covariance matrix estimator for ordinary least squares regression. The proposed estimator replaces the conventional leverage-based adjustment used in existing heteroskedasticity-consistent estimators with a data-driven correction derived from a fitted Beta distribution. The Beta parameters are estimated from the observed leverage values, allowing the adjustment factors to adapt automatically to the leverage structure of the sample. As a result, the proposed estimator accommodates heterogeneous leverage patterns while avoiding the excessive growth of adjustment factors that may arise with some existing methods. Monte Carlo simulations show that the proposed estimator yields accurate finite-sample inference and confidence interval coverage while retaining the desired asymptotic properties. Empirical applications further illustrate its practical advantages in the presence of influential observations. To facilitate its adoption, an open-source \textsf{R} package, \textsl{hcinfer}, has been developed and made publicly available.

</details>


### [151] [New Equivalence Tests for Approximate Independence in Contingency Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11130)
*Vladimir Ostrovski*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于二维列联表中近似独立性检验的新的等价性检验方法，利用渐近理论计算临界值，并通过Bootstrap提升有限样本表现，同时开发了边界点估计器以提高Bootstrap检验的统计效率和计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的独立性检验只能判断两个分类变量是否完全独立，但在实际应用中，研究者往往需要判断两个变量是否"近似独立"，即偏离独立性的程度是否在可接受的范围内。现有的等价性检验方法在列联表场景中较为有限，且有限样本下的表现有待改善，这促使作者开发新的等价性检验框架。

Method: 作者基于渐近理论推导了等价性检验的临界值；采用Bootstrap重抽样方法改善有限样本下的检验表现；提出了边界点估计器（estimator of boundary points），使Bootstrap检验在统计上更高效且在计算上可行；通过模拟研究比较不同表格大小下各检验方法的性能，并将方法应用于真实数据集。

Result: 模拟结果表明，所提出的等价性检验在不同表格大小下均具有良好表现，Bootstrap校正有效提升了有限样本性能。边界点估计器的引入使检验在保持统计有效性的同时具备计算可行性。真实数据分析进一步验证了方法的实用性。方法已实现为R包并开源发布。

Conclusion: 本文提出的等价性检验框架为二维列联表中的近似独立性判断提供了有效工具。Bootstrap与边界点估计的结合使检验既具有理论保证，又在实际应用中高效可用。该方法的R实现便于研究者直接使用。

Abstract: We introduce new equivalence tests for approximate independence in two-way contingency tables. The critical values are calculated asymptotically. The finite sample performance of the tests is improved by means of the bootstrap. An estimator of boundary points is developed to make the bootstrap based tests statistically efficient and computationally feasible. We compare the performance of the proposed tests for different table sizes by simulation. Then we apply the tests to real data sets. The tests are implemented in R and available online, see [https://github.com/TestingEquivalence/EquivalenceTestIndependenceR].

</details>


### [152] [A General U-Statistic Framework for High-Dimensional Multiple Change-Point Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11256)
*Bin Liu,Yufeng Liu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于U统计量的统一框架，用于高维数据中多变点的检验、估计与推断。该方法通过滑动窗口内的两样本U统计量，结合L∞范数乘子自助法进行检验，并利用U统计量投影精炼算法(U-PRA)实现变点位置的极小极大最优估计，同时可构建置信区间，在重尾分布等复杂场景下表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高维变点分析方法存在三个主要局限：一是通常仅针对特定参数（如均值或方差）或特定任务（检验或估计）设计，缺乏通用性；二是依赖严格的分布假设，对重尾数据鲁棒性差；三是检验与估计方法相互割裂，缺少统一框架。

Method: 提出统一框架：(1) 在滑动窗口内构造两样本U统计量，通过灵活选择核函数适应方差变化或稳健统计量等多种结构变化；(2) 检验方面，构建基于L∞范数的检验统计量，并采用高维乘子自助法校准，在稀疏备择下达到极小极大最优功效；(3) 估计方面，先给出变点数量与位置的初始估计，再通过U统计量投影精炼算法(U-PRA)进行精炼，达到极小极大最优定位速率，并推导精炼估计量的渐近分布以构建置信区间。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，该方法在包括重尾分布在内的多种场景下均优于现有方法。在基因组拷贝数变异数据上的应用验证了其实用价值。配套R包U-PRA已公开发布。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于U统计量的统一框架有效解决了高维多变点分析中检验与估计割裂、分布假设严格、任务单一化等问题，兼具理论最优性与实际应用的灵活性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: High-dimensional change-point analysis is essential in modern statistical inference. However, existing methods are often designed either for specific parameters (e.g., mean or variance) or for particular tasks (e.g., testing or estimation), making them difficult to generalize. Moreover, they typically rely on restrictive distributional assumptions, limiting their robustness to heavy-tailed data. We propose a unified framework for testing, estimating, and inferring multiple change points in high-dimensional data. Our approach leverages a two-sample U-statistic within a moving window, allowing flexible kernel function selection to accommodate structural changes in general parameters such as variance changes or robust statistics. For testing, we develop an L-infinity norm-based statistic with a high-dimensional multiplier bootstrap procedure, achieving minimax-optimal power under sparse alternatives. For estimation, we construct an initial estimator for the change-point number and locations and refine it using the U-statistic Projection Refinement Algorithm (U-PRA), attaining minimax-optimal localization rates. We further derive the asymptotic distribution of refined estimators, enabling valid confidence interval construction. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the better performance of our method across various settings, including heavy-tailed distributions. Applications to genomic copy number variation data highlight its practical utility. An R package implementing the proposed method, U-PRA, is publicly available at https://github.com/liubin0145/R-codes-UPRA/.

</details>


### [153] [A Correlation-Free Test for High-Dimensional Elliptical Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11304)
*Wenrui Wu,Bingye Yang,Minghua Deng,Xu Guo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对高维椭圆分布的无相关检验方法，通过高斯近似和高斯乘数自助法，在维度与样本量相当甚至更大的情况下实现拟合优度检验，并证明其理论有效性和实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆分布是多元正态分布的重要推广，在高维数据分析中应用广泛。然而，当维度与样本量可比或更大时，现有的椭圆分布拟合优度检验面临巨大挑战，尤其是样本协方差矩阵的逆在高维下不可用或不稳定。因此，亟需一种在高维设定下仍然有效的检验方法。

Method: 作者提出了一种"无相关"（correlation-free）检验统计量，其构造不依赖于样本协方差矩阵的逆。理论分析方面，在有限矩条件下且维度满足 $\log p = o(n^{1/14})$ 时，建立了该统计量在一般相关结构下的高维高斯近似。进一步，提出了高斯乘数自助法（Gaussian multiplier bootstrap）检验程序，并严格证明了其理论有效性。

Result: 数值模拟表明，该方法在有限样本下表现稳定，对多种备择假设具有优良的检验功效。在真实数据集上的应用进一步验证了所提检验方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的无相关检验方法有效解决了高维椭圆分布拟合优度检验的难题，具有良好的理论基础和实际应用前景，为高维统计推断提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Elliptical distributions provide a flexible and widely used extension of multivariate normal distribution. They play a critical role in many statistical procedures when dealing with high-dimensional data. However, goodness-of-fit testing for elliptical distributions remains challenging when the dimension is comparable to or larger than the sample size. In this work, we propose a correlation-free test for high-dimensional elliptical distributions. We establish high-dimensional Gaussian approximation for the test statistic under general correlation structures, allowing the dimension to grow as $\log p=o(n^{1/14})$ under finite moment conditions, without using the inverse sample covariance matrix. We further develop Gaussian multiplier bootstrap test procedure and prove its theoretical validity. Numerical studies demonstrate stable finite-sample behavior and favorable power against a range of alternatives. Applications to real datasets illustrate practical utility of the proposed test.

</details>


### [154] [Towards a Geometric Characterization of Multiverse Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11345)
*Giovanni Saraceno,Antonio Calcagnì*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种分布-几何框架，将多宇宙分析中的每个可接受规范表示为目标输出空间上的概率分布，通过分布间距离诱导几何结构，利用邻域、直径、Fréchet重心和离散度等工具，同时保留效应变异和推断不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多宇宙分析采用阶段性方法（先生成多宇宙，再用决策表、规范曲线、模型权重或标量摘要），这种视角虽有用但掩盖了推断不确定性在规范间的排列结构。需要一种能同时捕捉效应变异和不确定性变异的补充方法。

Method: 将每个可接受的规范映射为目标输出空间上的一个概率分布；定义分布间的合适距离（如Wasserstein距离）以诱导几何结构；在此几何框架下，通过局部邻域分析、多宇宙直径、Fréchet重心（分布均值）以及全局离散度测度来研究分析规范的集合。

Result: 数值示例和真实案例表明，该分布-几何方法能有效补充现有多宇宙摘要工具，同时保留效应方向的变异和推断不确定性，揭示传统标量摘要所隐藏的规范间不确定性结构。

Conclusion: 分布-几何视角为多宇宙分析提供了一种新的互补框架，克服了传统阶段性方法的信息损失，使研究者能更全面地理解分析选择如何驱动结论变化。

Abstract: Multiverse analysis makes explicit how empirical conclusions depend on alternative, defensible analytical specifications. Standard approaches usually generate the multiverse first and then summarize it through decision tables, specification curves, model weights, or scalar outputs such as estimates and \textit{p}-values. This stagewise view is useful, but it can hide how inferential uncertainty is arranged across specifications. We propose a distributional-geometric framework in which each admissible specification is represented by a probability distribution on a common target-output space. After defining a suitable distance between these distributions, the induced geometry allows the multiverse of analyses to be studied through local neighbourhoods, diameters, Fréchet barycentres, and dispersion measures. Numerical examples alongside a real case study illustrate how the approach complements existing multiverse summaries by retaining both effect variation and uncertainty variation.

</details>


### [155] [Robust Subgroup Analysis for Heterogeneous Censored Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11389)
*Zhaohui Xu,Daoji Li,Zemin Zheng*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对异质性删失数据的子群分析方法，结合逆概率加权、M估计和凹式成对融合惩罚，在加速失效时间模型下同时识别子群并估计协变量效应，无需先验子群成员信息，并通过RISA-ADMM算法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据通常来自异质性总体，不同子群间的模式差异显著。正确识别子群能提升预测精度、避免偏差、支持精准决策。然而，现有子群分析方法大多针对完整数据设计，缺乏适用于删失数据（尤其是异质性加速失效时间模型下）的稳健方法。

Method: 研究者提出一种新方法：首先采用逆概率加权处理删失机制；其次利用M估计构建稳健损失函数；然后引入凹式成对融合惩罚（concave pairwise fusion penalization）对个体间系数差异进行正则化，实现自动子群识别与协变量效应估计的同步进行。为求解该优化问题，开发了RISA-ADMM算法并证明其收敛性。此外，在温和正则条件下推导了估计量的理论性质。

Result: 通过大量模拟实验和在德国信用数据集上的应用，验证了所提方法的稳健性和有效性。方法能够在无需子群标签先验信息的情况下，准确识别异质性删失数据中的子群结构并估计模型参数。

Conclusion: 该方法为异质性删失数据的子群分析提供了一种新颖且稳健的解决方案，兼具理论保证与计算可行性，具有重要的实践应用价值。

Abstract: Subgroup analysis is important in practice because real-world data typically come from heterogeneous populations, where meaningful patterns can differ substantially across subpopulations. Correctly identifying these subgroups can improve prediction accuracy, prevent biased or misleading conclusions, and support more effective, targeted decision-making. While most existing subgroup analysis methods are developed for complete data, in this paper we propose a novel and robust approach for censored data under heterogeneous accelerated failure time (AFT) models. Specifically, we combine inverse probability weighting, M-estimation, and concave pairwise fusion penalization to simultaneously identify subgroups and estimate covariate effects for heterogeneous censored data, without requiring prior knowledge of individual subgroup memberships. We further develop an efficient RISA-ADMM algorithm to implement the method and establish its convergence. Furthermore, we derive the theoretical properties of the proposed estimators under mild regularity conditions. Extensive simulations and an application to the German credit dataset demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [156] [Modeling Time-course Gene Expression Data through Bayesian Partition Functional Principal Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11558)
*Marion Kerioui,Daniel Temko,Shahin Tavakoli,Hélène Ruffieux*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出分区函数型主成分分析（PFPCA），一种联合学习共享时间模式并对变量按潜在动态进行聚类的贝叶斯模型，在纵向基因表达数据中展现出优于两步基线的恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物标志物（如基因表达）的纵向测量日益普及，但现有方法无法同时解决三大核心挑战：降维、量化个体间变异以及揭示跨生物标志物共享的时间结构。需要一种能联合进行聚类和函数型主成分分析的方法。

Method: 提出PFPCA贝叶斯模型，将混合模型与组内多元函数型主成分分析相结合；开发可扩展的均值场变分推断算法，用于联合推断函数型主成分载荷、个体水平得分、分组归属及分区大小。

Result: 模拟实验中，PFPCA在恢复分区和潜在函数结构方面均显著优于两步基线；在最具挑战性的设置下，PFPCA恢复真实分区的比例为27%，而两步基线仅为1%。在H3N2流感感染个体的纵向基因表达数据中，识别出具有协调激活模式的基因组，并揭示了与免疫应答动态和症状状态相关的时间特征。

Conclusion: 联合推断策略在同时进行变量聚类与函数型降维方面具有显著优势，PFPCA为纵向高维生物标志物数据的时间模式发现提供了有效工具。

Abstract: High-dimensional biomarkers such as gene expression levels are now routinely measured over time, allowing biological processes to be studied dynamically rather than through cross-sectional snapshots. However, existing methods do not adequately address the central applied challenges posed by such data: simultaneously reducing dimensionality, quantifying inter-individual variability and uncovering temporal structure shared across biomarkers. We introduce Partition Functional Principal Component Analysis (PFPCA), a Bayesian model that jointly learns shared temporal patterns and clusters variables according to their latent dynamics. PFPCA combines a mixture model with multivariate functional principal component analysis performed within each group. We develop a scalable mean-field variational algorithm for joint inference of functional principal component loadings, individual-level scores, group assignments and partition sizes. Simulations show clear gains from joint inference: PFPCA recovers both the partition and the latent functional structure more accurately than a two-step baseline. In the most challenging settings, PFPCA retrieves the true partition in 27% of replicates compared with 1% for the two-step baseline. Applied to longitudinal gene-expression data from individuals experimentally infected with H3N2 influenza virus, PFPCA identifies groups of genes with coordinated activation patterns and reveals temporal signatures associated with immune-response dynamics and symptom status.

</details>


### [157] [Calibrated Horizon-Weighted Local Projection Designs for Markov Switchbacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11694)
*Makoto Nakakita,Teruo Nakatsuma*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫轮换实验的时间分配设计问题，针对动态局部投影目标，提出了一种校准选择器，用于选择最小化协方差、HAC、残差自助法或已实现调度风险的可行持久性参数。文中建立了平衡同方差马尔可夫基准模型，并给出闭式解。通过半合成的低碳伦敦评估验证了方法在现实负荷自协方差下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在马尔可夫轮换实验（switchback experiments）中，当报告对象为动态局部投影（local-projection）目标时，如何设计时间分配方案以最小化估计风险是一个核心问题。现有方法缺乏对序列依赖性和持久性选择的系统校准框架，亟需一种能够根据实验前设定的估计量和报告对象来优化分配持久性的方法。

Method: 作者提出了一种**校准选择器（calibrated selector）**，在预先设定估计量和报告对象的前提下，从预先指定的马尔可夫类中选择使风险最小化的可行持久性参数。首先建立**平衡同方差马尔可夫基准模型**，利用滞后分配信息矩阵的AR(1)-Toeplitz结构及其三对角逆矩阵得出闭式解，将局部投影报告权重映射为持久性建议。在此基础上，用残差化、序列依赖、试点校准或随机化风险替代基准协方差，形成实际推荐。通过**半合成低碳伦敦（Low Carbon London）评估**，利用观测到的半小时基线动态和已知注入响应评估设计风险。

Result: 基准模型给出了闭式解，清晰展示了局部投影权重与一阶马尔可夫持久性选择之间的映射关系。半合成评估表明，在现实负荷自协方差下，协方差计算能够有效评估设计风险，并识别出何时应使用校准协方差选择替代同方差马尔可夫公式。在近边界设计中，当多时段正态近似不可靠时，需采用随机化优先的推断方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的校准选择器框架能够有效指导马尔可夫轮换实验的时间分配设计，基准闭式解提供了直观的理论指导，而基于实际数据的风险校准方法增强了现实适用性。该方法在低碳伦敦用电数据的半合成评估中得到验证，为动态局部投影目标下的实验设计提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: We study temporal assignment design for Markov switchback experiments when the reported object is a dynamic local-projection target. We develop a calibrated selector that chooses the feasible persistence minimizing the covariance, HAC, residual-bootstrap, or realized-schedule risk of the estimator and reporting object specified before the experiment. A balanced homoskedastic Markov benchmark yields a closed form because the lagged-assignment information matrix is AR(1)-Toeplitz with a tridiagonal inverse. The benchmark maps local-projection reporting weights into persistence recommendations within a prespecified first-order Markov class. Field recommendations replace the benchmark covariance with residualized, serially dependent, pilot-calibrated, or randomization-based risk. A semi-synthetic Low Carbon London evaluation uses observed half-hourly baseline dynamics and known injected responses to assess design risk. It evaluates the covariance calculations under realistic load autocovariance and identifies when calibrated covariance selection should replace the homoskedastic Markov formula. Near-boundary designs use randomization-first inference when many-spell normal approximations are unsupported.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Knowledge Graphs Meet Graph Neural Networks: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09666)
*Chengcheng Sun,Jiayun Tian,Cheng Zhai,Zhixiao Wang,Yajie Song,Xiaobin Rui,Jian Zhang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的二级分类框架，系统性地回顾了基于图神经网络（GNN）的知识图谱技术全景，涵盖知识图谱构建、嵌入、推理与应用四大环节，并按GNN模型类型（GCN、GAT、HGNN等）进行归类，分析了各类方法的优势与局限，最后展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GNN在知识图谱领域已被广泛应用，但目前缺乏对GNN方法贯穿知识图谱全技术管线的系统性综述。为填补这一空白，作者希望提供一个统一的分类视角，帮助研究者全面理解GNN在知识图谱各任务中的应用现状。

Method: 作者提出了一个二级分类框架：第一级按知识图谱技术管线划分（知识图谱构建、知识图谱嵌入、知识推理、知识图谱应用）；第二级按GNN模型视角划分（如GCN、GAT、HGNN等）。基于该框架，对不同任务中GNN技术的优势进行了分析，并对各类GNN模型进行了详细回顾与总结。

Result: 通过系统性梳理，总结了各类GNN模型在不同知识图谱任务中的优势与局限性，揭示了GNN在知识图谱全生命周期中的适用性和表现差异。

Conclusion: GNN在知识图谱技术全管线中展现出强大潜力，但仍存在若干未解决的挑战。文章指出了未来有前景的研究方向，为该领域后续发展提供了参考。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm in Knowledge Graphs (KGs) due to their intrinsic ability to model graph-structured data. However, there remains a lack of a systematic review about GNN-based methodologies across the entire knowledge graph technologies pipeline. To address this gap, we first propose a novel two-level taxonomy framework for GNN-based knowledge graph technologies: the KG technologies pipeline and GNN-based perspective. Specifically, the knowledge graph technologies pipeline covers knowledge graph construction, knowledge graph embedding, knowledge reasoning and knowledge graph applications. Meanwhile, the GNN-based perspective provides a new categorization of knowledge graph technologies with GNN models, such as GCN, GAT, and HGNN. Then, we analyze the advantages of GNN technology based on the characteristics of different tasks in the knowledge graph lifecycle. Furthermore, we detailed review various GNN-based models for knowledge graph following the proposed taxonomy, and summarize strengths and limitations. Finally, we discuss unresolved challenges and outline promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [159] [Position: Every Ground Truth is a Human Construction, not an Objective Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09668)
*Charlotte Högberg,Ericka Johnson,Kiri L. Wagstaff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了机器学习中的"真实标注"并非客观中立的天然测量值，而是由人类与技术共同建构的产物，呼吁学界关注其情境依赖性，并通过"情境化可靠性"提升模型透明度与问责机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习社区普遍将真实标注数据集视为客观、中立的参考标准，忽视了其背后的人为建构过程。这种忽视可能导致模型在实际部署中出现可靠性问题，缺乏对数据适用边界和局限性的清晰认知。

Method: 本文采用立场论文的形式，通过理论论证和概念分析，引入"情境化可靠性"这一概念框架，对真实标注数据的建构性、偶然性和情境依赖性进行系统阐述。

Result: 提出真实标注数据集是偶然性的而非普遍性的；明确阐述数据集构建中不可见的选择与安排；论证关注情境依赖性可提升可靠性认知；提出"情境化可靠性"概念，要求明确表达模型的局限与优势。

Conclusion: 机器学习社区应正视真实标注的建构性本质，通过阐明数据集构建中的隐性选择，提升情境化可靠性，从而增强模型使用的透明性、问责性，并促进跨学科协作。

Abstract: Ground truth datasets play a fundamental role as reference values in the training and evaluation of machine learning models. This position paper argues that ground truths are not neutral objective measurements that are naturally given, but instead that they are constructed by arrangements of humans and technologies. We argue that the ML community will benefit from articulating and discussing these often invisible or unreported choices and acknowledging that reference data sets are contingent, not universal. Focusing on the situated and context-dependent nature of ground truths can improve reliability by enabling a better informed perspective on where, when, and how the datasets, and the models they have shaped, can best be used. We argue for increasing `situated reliability' which includes articulating the limits and strengths of models and their truth claims. Finally, paying more attention to the construction of ground truths can support transparency, accountability, and interdisciplinary work.

</details>


### [160] [AuditWeave: A Tamper-Evident, Auditor-Navigable Evidence Layer for AI-Assisted and Data-Transformation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09682)
*Vimal Nakrani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuditWeave 是一个轻量级 Python 库，通过仅追加的哈希链账本记录 AI 辅助决策和数据转换工作流的每一步，确保事后可追溯且任何篡改均可通过链验证检测，开销仅为每事件数十微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在审计、金融、医疗等受监管领域，AI 系统越来越多地参与决策，组织需要在事后重建某个结论所依据的证据，并证明该推理记录未被篡改。现有工具面向的是机器学习工程师的操作需求（如模型可观测性、漂移监控、治理报告），而非面向审查者追溯单个结论的需求。

Method: 设计了一个系统无关的轻量事件词汇表，覆盖检索增强生成（RAG）流水线和表格/数据湖转换；将所有事件写入仅追加的哈希链账本，任何修改、重排序、插入或删除均可通过链验证检测；以无运行时依赖的 Python 库形式实现。

Result: 在参考实现上评估了记录开销、可扩展性和篡改检测正确性：完整性保证的开销为每事件数十微秒；在 2000 次随机试验中，链验证成功标记了四类篡改（修改、重排序、插入、删除）中的所有注入变异。

Conclusion: AuditWeave 以极低的性能代价提供了端到端的可审计追溯能力，填补了现有工具在面向审查者的决策追溯方面的空白。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly used to assist consequential decisions in regulated domains such as auditing, finance, and healthcare. This creates a recurring obligation: an organization must be able to reconstruct, after the fact, which evidence informed a given conclusion, and to show that the record of that reasoning was not altered. Existing tools address related but distinct problems - model observability, drift monitoring, governance reporting - and are built for the machine-learning engineer operating a system, not the reviewer who must trace one specific conclusion back to its supporting evidence. We present AuditWeave, a lightweight Python library, with no runtime dependencies, that records the steps of AI-assisted and data-transformation workflows into a single append-only, hash-chained ledger. A small, system-agnostic event vocabulary spans both retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines and tabular/lakehouse transformations, so a conclusion that draws on both can be traced end-to-end through one record. Within a sealed ledger, any modification, reordering, insertion, or deletion of events is detectable through chain verification. We describe the design and evaluate recording overhead, scalability, and tamper-detection correctness on the reference implementation. The integrity guarantees cost tens of microseconds per event, and, as the hash-chain construction implies, verification flagged every injected mutation across four mutation classes over 2,000 randomized trials.

</details>


### [161] [Ablation, Statistical Inference, and Validation for KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09683)
*Paolo D'Alberto,Ashish Siarasao,Elliott Delaye,Rajeev Patwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统比较Turbo-Quant与SpectralQuant的KV-cache压缩方案，评估包含WHT旋转+Beta Lloyd-Max和QJL在内的非支配方案，通过统计验证方法区分系统性编解码差异与实现方差。核心发现：特征基方法在厚尾数据上因协方差不稳定性而失效，但在结构化场景中表现优异，且有效语义维度适应校准预算而非真实数据秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV-cache压缩领域缺乏对不同方案的系统性统计比较，尤其缺少将编解码器系统差异与随机实现波动分离开来的严格验证框架。此外，特征基方法在不同数据分布（厚尾vs结构化）下的行为差异尚未被充分揭示。

Method: 采用统计验证方法论，系统比较Turbo-Quant和SpectralQuant两种KV-cache压缩框架下的多种非支配方案，包括WHT旋转配合Beta Lloyd-Max量化以及QJL（Quasi-Johnson-Lindenstrauss）投影方法，控制实现方差以分离系统性编解码差异。

Result: (1) 特征基方法在厚尾数据上因协方差矩阵估计不稳定而表现不佳；(2) 在结构化数据场景中，特征基方法表现优异；(3) 有效语义维度$d_{eff}$并非反映真实数据秩，而是随校准预算自适应调整。

Conclusion: KV-cache压缩方案的选择需依据数据分布特性：特征基方法适用于结构化数据，但对厚尾分布鲁棒性不足；有效语义维度与校准预算的耦合关系提示我们，压缩效率的提升可能更多依赖于校准策略的优化，而非单纯追求对数据内在秩的精确估计。

Abstract: This study systematically compares Turbo-Quant and SpectralQuant KV-cache compression, evaluating non-dominated schemes, including WHT rotation with Beta Lloyd-Max and QJL, through a statistical validation methodology that separates systematic codec differences from implementation variance. Key findings reveal that while eigenbasis-based methods fail on heavy-tailed data due to covariance instability, they excel in structured regimes, with the effective semantic dimension ($d_{eff}$) adapting to calibration budgets rather than true data rank. (this is an abstract of the abstract thank you )

</details>


### [162] [SciML in the Wild: A Diagnostic Study of When Structural Priors Help and When They Hurt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09684)
*Vrishank Sai Anand,Prathamesh Dinesh Joshi,Raj Abhijit Dandekar,Rajat Dandekar,Sreedath Panat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在宏观经济预测这一压力测试场景下，评估了ARIMA、LSTM、NODE、PINN和UDE五类模型。结果表明无一模型表现持续优异，但约束较少的模型（ARIMA、NODE）系统性地优于依赖启发式先验的模型（PINN、UDE）。核心结论：当结构先验与数据生成过程不匹配时，结构先验可能成为"误正则化器"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习（SciML）方法（如NODE、PINN、UDE）的有效性依赖于结构先验能反映真实支配动力学的假设。本文旨在挑战这一假设，探究当该假设不成立时会发生什么，以宏观经济预测作为压力测试领域来检验结构先验在实际应用中的稳健性。

Method: 选取23个国家的稀疏年度宏观经济数据，在多个时间划分和5个随机种子下，系统对比五类模型：ARIMA（纯统计基线）、LSTM（纯深度学习基线）、NODE（弱结构先验）、PINN和UDE（强结构先验）。通过预测性能的相对排序，诊断结构先验在不同数据条件下的作用。

Result: （1）所有模型在低频宏观经济预测中均未实现持续强预测性能；（2）呈现出清晰的相对层次：ARIMA和NODE等约束较少的模型持续优于PINN和UDE等约束较强的启发式先验模型；（3）识别出先验错配、体制转换、结构断裂和优化不稳定等典型失效模式。

Conclusion: 这并非对SciML的否定，而是一项诊断性结果：当结构先验与数据生成过程不匹配时，结构先验会退化为"误正则化器"。作者呼吁SciML实践者应先检验结构是否真正有益，而非默认"更多结构即更好"。

Abstract: Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) methods such as Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (NODEs), Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), and Universal Differential Equations (UDEs) are most effective when structural priors reflect reliable governing dynamics. We ask what happens when this assumption is violated. Using macroeconomic forecasting as a stress-test domain, we evaluate five model families, ARIMA, LSTM, NODE, PINN, and UDE, across 23 countries using sparse annual data, multiple temporal splits, and five random seeds. Our results show that none of the evaluated models achieve consistently strong forecasting performance, highlighting the difficulty of low-frequency macroeconomic prediction. However, a clear relative hierarchy emerges: less-constrained models, particularly ARIMA and NODE, consistently outperform more-constrained heuristic-prior models such as PINN and UDE. Rather than treating this as a rejection of SciML, we interpret it as a diagnostic result: structural priors can act as misregularizers when they do not match the data-generating process. We identify failure modes including prior misalignment, regime shifts, structural breaks, and optimization instability, and argue that SciML practitioners should test whether structure helps before assuming that more structure is beneficial.

</details>


### [163] [MawForge: Memory-Bounded Expert Materialization for Local Mixture-of-Experts Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09686)
*Craig Opie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MawForge 探索在统一内存受限设备上进行本地 MoE 推理的可行性：将完整模型存于磁盘，按需将路由到的专家张量加载到有界执行缓存中。结果表明其作为有界执行机制和测量基底有效，但缓存最大化策略本身并非关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏 MoE 语言模型虽降低了每 token 的活跃计算量，但本地推理仍需将完整模型、KV 缓存、运行时缓冲区等全部装入快速内存，这对内存受限的统一内存机器构成严峻挑战。作者希望探索一种不同的系统假设：是否可以通过磁盘存储全模型、常驻公共张量、按需加载专家张量的方式，使本地 MoE 推理在受限设备上实用化。

Method: 提出 MawForge 系统，将完整 MoE 模型存储在磁盘上，常驻共享张量（如注意力层、路由器）保留在内存中，路由到的专家张量按需从磁盘物化到一个有界执行缓存中。系统作为有界执行机制和测量基底，用于评估专家重用、常驻足迹、KV 缓存大小、量化、路由局部性及 macOS 内存压力等多维度因素对性能的影响。

Result: MawForge 作为有界执行机制和测量基底是有效的，能够支持本地 MoE 推理。但其缓存最大化策略本身并非性能关键——真正决定性能的是专家重用率与常驻内存占用之间的权衡，以及 KV 缓存大小、量化方案、路由局部性和 macOS 内存压力等多种因素的共同作用。

Conclusion: 本地 MoE 推理在受限统一内存机器上的实用化，核心不在于精妙的缓存最大化策略，而在于对专家重用、内存占用、KV 缓存、量化和操作系统内存压力等多维因素的系统性权衡与调优。MawForge 为此类研究提供了有效的测量与执行基底。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models separate total parameter count from per-token active computation, but local inference systems often still require the full model, key-value cache, runtime buffers, and operatingsystem headroom to fit in fast memory. MawForge tests a different systems hypothesis: local MoE serving can be made practical on constrained unified-memory machines by storing the full model on disk, keeping common tensors resident, and materializing routed expert tensors into a bounded execution cache on demand. The central finding is that MawForge is effective as a bounded execution mechanism and measurement substrate for local MoE inference, but not as a cache-maximization policy. Performance depends on balancing expert reuse against resident footprint, KV-cache size, quantization, route locality, and macOS memory pressure.

</details>


### [164] [Prioritizing Search Space Regions in the Low Autocorrelation Binary Sequences Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09688)
*Blaž Pšeničnik,Borko Bošković,Jan Popić,Janez Brest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合Thompson采样与并行自回避行走的混合搜索框架，将LABS搜索空间划分为多个限制类并以多臂老虎机方式动态分配计算资源，在450≤L≤527及L=573共35个序列长度上刷新了已知最优结果，其中L=451实现了超过8.0的merit factor。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低自相关二元序列问题（LABS）是一个具有通信、信号处理和卫星导航等重要应用的难解组合优化问题。现有方法在长序列上难以有效探索巨大的搜索空间，亟需一种能自适应分配计算资源、高效利用并行计算能力的优化框架。

Method: 提出混合搜索框架：(1) 将搜索空间按限制类（restriction classes）划分，建模为多臂老虎机问题，利用Thompson采样在线自适应分配计算资源；(2) 采用并行自回避行走进行搜索，并通过GPU加速、共享后验更新、高效邻域评估及Bloom过滤器防止循环；(3) 设计两阶段优化策略：先在约束的偏斜对称（skew-symmetric）空间中搜索，再在无限制空间中精炼最优候选解。

Result: 实验覆盖长二元序列，在450≤L≤527范围内及L=573共35个序列长度上刷新了已知最优merit factor记录。特别地，在L=451处获得了merit factor超过8.0的最长序列。结果验证了Thompson采样能有效优先分配资源给表现更好的分区。

Conclusion: 该框架为高merit factor最大化提供了一种可扩展且高效的策略，证实了在线数据驱动资源分配在LABS优化中的价值。

Abstract: Low autocorrelation binary sequences problem (LABS) is a hard combinatorial optimization challenge with important applications in communications, signal processing, and satellite navigation. This paper proposes a hybrid search framework that combines Thompson sampling with parallel self-avoiding walks to adaptively allocate computational effort across restriction classes of the LABS search space. By modeling partitions as arms in a multi-armed bandit setting, the proposed method dynamically shifts search resources toward partitions that empirically produce higher merit factors while maintaining exploration of less-sampled regions. The approach is further accelerated through GPU-parallel execution, shared posterior updates, efficient neighborhood evaluation, and a Bloom filter for cycle prevention. In addition, we use a two-stage optimization strategy that first searches constrained partitioned skew-symmetric spaces and then refines the best candidates in the unrestricted space. Experiments on long binary sequences show that the proposed method improves the previously best-known results for 35 sequence lengths in the range $450 \le L \le 527$ and for $L=573$. In particular, we report a new longest sequence with merit factor exceeding $8.0$, obtained for $L=451$. The results also show that Thompson sampling effectively prioritizes partitions with better observed performance, confirming the value of online, data-driven resource allocation in LABS optimization. Overall, the proposed framework provides a scalable and effective strategy for high-performance merit factor maximization.

</details>


### [165] [What Context Does a Coding Agent Actually Need to Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09691)
*Brian Sam-Bodden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了编码智能体在代码编辑任务中实际需要多少上下文信息。通过将定位与执行分离，并在SWE-bench Verified上进行实验，发现需要的信息极其精简：源代码本身携带的信号远超自然语言摘要；文件剩余部分的上下文几乎无关紧要；压缩后的上下文仅需完整文件三分之一的token即可达到同等效果。同时也揭示了temperature-0推理存在约9%的结果翻转噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码智能体虽然能容纳整个代码仓库作为上下文窗口，但大量阅读是浪费的。核心问题不是智能体"能使用"多少上下文，而是它实际"需要"多少上下文——尤其是在编辑代码这一最关键的时刻。作者旨在通过严格的实验设计，量化代码编辑任务中真正有效的上下文信号，并区分"定位"与"执行"两个阶段的贡献。

Method: 在SWE-bench Verified基准上，作者将代码编辑任务分解为"定位工作点"和"执行编辑"两个阶段，通过oracle固定定位环节，仅改变代码的表示方式，比较不同上下文条件下真实问题的解决情况。具体包括：(1)用自然语言摘要替代源代码，对比行为问题的回答能力；(2)将多文件实例中非编辑文件的剩余部分替换为UML骨架和签名，对比与直接删除的效果；(3)测试压缩上下文与完整文件的性能差异。同时记录了temperature-0下API推理的结果翻转作为噪声基线。还发布了经过金标准验证的环境、每个实例的可表达性证明、确定性补丁构造及预注册假设。

Result: (1)源代码在45个held-out仓库的行为问题上回答正确27个，而自然语言摘要仅答对4个，且前沿模型与3B模型的摘要表现同样差；(2)在多文件实例中，将文件剩余部分渲染为UML骨架和签名与直接删除相比，解决的问题数无显著差异（N=70，精确McNemar检验p=0.75）；(3)压缩上下文在仅使用全文件三分之一token的情况下匹配了完整文件的性能，解决一个issue平均消耗19K上下文token而非94K；(4)temperature-0 API推理中约9%的逐实例结果在字节完全相同的运行间发生翻转，构成该基准上所有小效应的噪声底线。

Conclusion: 代码编辑任务所需的上下文极其精简。核心信号存在于待编辑的代码本身中，自然语言摘要无法替代；周围文件的上下文几乎不影响结果；压缩上下文能以极低成本匹配完整文件的性能。此外，temperature-0推理的不可复现性（约9%翻转率）为该领域所有报告的小效应设定了一个噪声底线。作者发布了完整的实验工具包以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: A modern coding agent can hold an entire repository in its context window. Most of its reading is wasted -- and the interesting question is not how much context an agent can use, but what it actually \emph{needs}. We study that question at the moment it matters most: when the agent must \emph{edit} code. Separating \emph{finding} the work site from \emph{acting} on it, we hold localization fixed with an oracle, vary only how the code is represented, and score context against real issue resolution on SWE-bench Verified. The answer is starkly minimal. The signal lives in the code being edited itself: natural-language summaries of it answer almost none of the behavioral questions that the source answers ($4/45$ vs.\ $27/45$, held-out repositories, independent judge), and the gap belongs to the representation, not the summarizer -- a frontier model's summaries score exactly as poorly as a 3B model's. The surrounding context hardly matters either: across every multi-file instance in Verified, under a protocol frozen before any data, rendering a file's remainder as UML skeletons and signatures resolves no more issues than deleting that remainder outright ($N{=}70$, exact McNemar $p{=}0.75$). That was our registered hypothesis, and it failed. Compressed context, meanwhile, matches whole files at a third of the tokens: a resolved issue costs $19$K context tokens, not $94$K. The instrument also yielded a finding the field should keep: temperature-0 API inference flips ${\sim}9\%$ of per-instance outcomes between byte-identical runs. That is a noise floor under every small effect reported on this benchmark, including ours. We release the instrument -- gold-validated environments, per-instance proof that every reference edit is expressible from every arm's context, deterministic patch construction, and pre-registered hypotheses whose nulls we publish.

</details>


### [166] [Reference-Based Distillation Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09692)
*Rajat Rawat,Sizhe Chen,Akshay Anand,Michael Duan,Bob Rotsted,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于参考的成员推理方法，通过比较学生模型与候选教师模型输出之间的偏好对齐程度，来检测模型蒸馏行为并识别教师模型。该方法在单教师蒸馏场景下准确率接近完美，并成功应用于揭示QwQ、DeepSeek-R1和GPT-OSS等当代模型之间的潜在蒸馏关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型蒸馏（使用第三方强模型输出进行训练）虽然广泛用于提升性能，但引发了不公平优势和策略违规的担忧。核心问题是：能否检测一个模型是否从另一个模型蒸馏而来？单独识别教师模型极具挑战性，但引入参考检查点后变得可行。

Method: 提出基于参考的成员推理（reference-based membership inference）的蒸馏检测方法。核心思路是比较学生模型相对于参考检查点，对不同候选教师模型输出的优先对齐程度，从而识别最可能的教师并检测蒸馏证据。为处理未知蒸馏流水线（如隐藏提示），方法直接从模型输出中推断代理提示模板。此外，还识别出o1/o3模型特有的字形级别（glyph-level）信号。评估方面，设计了混合评估方案，结合受控蒸馏实验和真实世界模型。

Result: 在单教师蒸馏场景中，即使底层蒸馏流水线大部分未知，该方法仍以接近完美的准确率恢复了真实教师模型。进一步引入了教师归因和蒸馏检测的统计检验，并将框架扩展到开放世界设定（不保证候选者中存在教师）。应用于当代模型后，获得了关于QwQ、DeepSeek-R1和GPT-OSS之间潜在蒸馏关系的新证据。

Conclusion: 基于参考的成员推理方法能够有效检测模型蒸馏并识别教师模型，在受控和真实世界场景中均表现优异。该方法为模型溯源和知识产权保护提供了实用的技术手段，并已揭示多个知名模型间的潜在蒸馏关系。

Abstract: Model distillation -- training on outputs from stronger third-party models -- is widely used to boost performance, but raises concerns about unfair advantages and policy violations. This motivates a fundamental question: can we detect whether a model was distilled from another? We show that, while identifying a teacher model from a student in isolation is highly challenging, it becomes tractable in a reference-based setting: given a model and an earlier-generation checkpoint from the same lineage, we can identify the teacher model used to train the later checkpoint. We introduce a distillation detection method based on reference-based membership inference. By comparing how strongly a student model preferentially aligns with outputs from different candidate teachers relative to a reference checkpoint, our method identifies the most likely teacher and detects evidence of distillation. To handle unknown distillation pipelines such as hidden prompts, we infer proxy prompt templates directly from model outputs. We additionally identify a distinctive glyph-level signal specific to o1/o3 models. Evaluating distillation detection is challenging because modern model lineages are already heavily entangled. To address this, we develop a hybrid evaluation spanning both controlled distillation experiments and real-world models. Across both settings, our approach recovers the true teacher with near-perfect accuracy in single-teacher distillation scenarios, even when the underlying distillation pipeline is largely unknown. We further introduce statistical tests for both teacher attribution and distillation detection, and extend our framework to open-world settings where no teacher is guaranteed to be present among the candidates. Applying our method to contemporary models yields new evidence regarding potential distillation relationships involving QwQ, DeepSeek-R1, and GPT-OSS.

</details>


### [167] [Depth-Entropy Guided Sampling for Training-Free LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09693)
*Zibin Meng,Peng Xie,Kani Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DEGS，一种无需训练、仅在测试时通过层间熵坍缩深度作为质量信号进行采样的方法，结合MCMC框架在推理基准上达到SOTA无训练精度，并在分布外任务上超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时采样方法仅利用输出层似然，忽略了Transformer内部前向传播中的层间动态信息。观察到强推理模型（包括RL后训练变体）表现出独特的"晚期熵坍缩"现象——logit-lens解码熵在较深层才急剧收敛，这一内在信号可被利用来提升采样质量。

Method: 提出DEGS方法：(1) 定义逐序列的坍缩深度D(x)，即logit-lens熵降至阈值时的层索引；(2) 构建联合目标 π(x) ∝ p(x)^α · exp(βD(x))，融合序列似然与深度-熵结构；(3) 将该目标嵌入MCMC幂采样框架（DEGS-MCMC），通过采样轨迹使近似随机信号复合累积。

Result: 在三个开源模型和四个推理基准上，DEGS达到无训练方法中SOTA精度；在分布外任务和困难子集上增益最大（即似然单独失效的场景）；计算开销仅为个位数百分比；在数学任务上略逊于域内训练的GRPO，但在GPQA分布外任务上全面超越GRPO。

Conclusion: 层间熵坍缩深度是一种有效的内在质量信号，可显著提升测试时采样的推理性能，尤其在分布外泛化场景中展现优势，为无训练推理增强提供了新方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become the dominant paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but it requires expensive training, curated data, and reward signals. Recent work shows that sampling from sharpened base-model distributions at test time recovers much of the RL gain, yet existing methods rely solely on output-layer likelihoods and ignore the transformer's internal forward-pass dynamics. We introduce Depth-Entropy Guided Sampling (DEGS), a training-free, test-time method that exploits layer-wise entropy collapse as an intrinsic quality signal. We observe that stronger reasoners -- including RL-posttrained variants -- exhibit a distinctive "late collapse": logit-lens decoded entropy stays elevated until deeper layers before converging. We define a per-sequence collapse depth $D(\mathbf{x})$ and a joint objective $π(\mathbf{x}) \propto p(\mathbf{x})^α\exp(βD(\mathbf{x}))$ that combines sequence likelihood with this depth-entropy structure, instantiated inside an MCMC power-sampling framework (DEGS-MCMC). Across three open-weight models and four reasoning benchmarks, this near-chance per-candidate signal compounds over the sampling trajectory into state-of-the-art training-free accuracy, with gains largest out of domain and on the harder splits -- exactly where likelihood alone falls short -- at single-digit-percent wall-clock overhead. DEGS narrowly trails an in-house GRPO reference on the math splits GRPO was trained for, yet surpasses it out of domain on GPQA for all three models, without any training, reward model, or labeled data.

</details>


### [168] [Manifold Constrained Tabular Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09710)
*Tian Li,Lucy Robinson,Varun Ojha,Huizhi Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HDE-Net 将表格数据建模迁移到双曲空间（Poincaré球），通过潜在决策节点和软决策路由机制，使DNN能更好地捕捉表格分类中的局部规则分割结构，在30个数据集上取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格分类通常由局部的、条件触发的规则主导，而非平滑的全局模式。然而，现有表格深度神经网络基于欧几里得表示，偏好平滑变化和语义局部性，这种几何不匹配使得DNN难以高效表示表格分类底层的离散规则分区结构。

Method: (1) 将异构特征抽象为统一的潜在决策节点（LDN），嵌入Poincaré球形成树状推理的连续表示；(2) 针对数值特征提出软决策路由机制，以可微分方式逼近基于区间的局部规则，使其LDN语义接近类别特征；(3) 引入熵感知容量分配算法，自适应调整每个数值特征的LDN数量以平衡表达力与复杂度。

Result: 在TALENT-tiny-core分类基准（30个数据集）上，HDE-Net取得最佳平均排名，超越工业级GBDT和近期表格DNN，同时保持高效率。

Conclusion: 通过在双曲空间中构建层次化决策建模，HDE-Net有效弥合了表格数据规则分区结构与DNN连续表示之间的几何鸿沟，为表格分类提供了一种兼具表达力与效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular classification is often governed by local, condition-triggered rules rather than smooth global patterns. However, tabular deep neural networks (DNNs) are typically built upon Euclidean representations that favor smooth variations and semantic locality. This potential geometric mismatch can make it challenging for tabular DNNs to efficiently represent the discrete, rule-partitioned structures often underlying tabular classification. To address this issue, we propose HDE-Net, a manifold-constrained DNN that enables hierarchical decision modeling in hyperbolic space. We first abstract heterogeneous features into unified Latent Decision Nodes (LDNs) and embed them in the Poincaré ball, forming a continuous representation that resembles tree-structured reasoning. For numerical features, we introduce a Soft Decision Routing mechanism that approximates range-based local rules in a differentiable manner, bringing their LDN semantics closer to those of categorical features. An entropy-aware capacity allocation algorithm further adapts the number of LDNs per numerical feature to balance expressiveness and complexity. On the TALENT-tiny-core classification benchmark (30 datasets), HDE-Net achieves the \textit{best average rank}, outperforming both industrial GBDTs and recent tabular DNNs while maintaining high efficiency.

</details>


### [169] [Low-Rank Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09694)
*Jonathan Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出低秩注意力残差（LR-AttnRes），通过将路由键维度从全维 d 降至低秩 r（r≪d），解耦路由与表征，在保持全维残差值的同时间降低计算开销并提升验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 Attention Residuals 将每个子层输出同时作为全维 key 和 value，导致路由与表征耦合，且深度路由分数随隐藏宽度 d 缩放，带来不必要的计算开销和表征约束。

Method: 提出两种 LR-AttnRes 变体：(1) Projected LR-AttnRes——从现有输出投影中发射可学习的低秩 key（r 维）用于路由，保持全维 value；(2) Sliced LR-AttnRes——直接使用 value 的最后 r 维作为路由 key，去除额外 key 投影路径，进一步减少残差侧 FLOPs。

Result: Projected LR-AttnRes 在测试变体中取得最优验证损失；Sliced LR-AttnRes 在去除辅助投影路径、降低计算量的同时仍提升性能。全面消融实验表明，深度路由在远小于模型宽度的维度下即可有效工作。

Conclusion: 低秩注意力残差成功解耦路由与表征，以极低维度实现有效深度路由，在降低计算开销的同时保持或提升模型性能，并开源代码与模型以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Attention Residuals replace the fixed residual sum with depthwise attention over previous sub-layer outputs in large language models (LLMs), but use each output as both a full-dimensional key and value. This couples routing with representation and makes depth-routing scores scale with the hidden width $d$. We propose Low-Rank Attention Residuals (LR-AttnRes), which keep full-dimensional residual values while using $r$-dimensional keys, with $r \ll d$, for routing. Projected LR-AttnRes emits learned low-rank keys from existing output projections, decoupling routing from residual content and achieving the best validation loss among the variants tested. Sliced LR-AttnRes uses the last $r$ dimensions of each value as the routing key, removing the auxiliary key-projection path and reducing residual-side FLOPs while still improving performance. Comprehensive sweeps show that depthwise routing can be effective with far fewer dimensions than the model width. We release code and models to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [170] [Estimation, Prediction, and Assortment Optimization for Markov Chain Choice Models with Panel Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09817)
*Yalcin Akcay,Gerardo Berbeglia,Young-San Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于马尔可夫链选择模型的框架，利用面板数据中的偏序偏好信息进行参数估计、个性化预测和品类优化，并通过新颖的EM算法在合成数据和真实数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统选择模型假设同一客户的多笔交易相互独立，忽略了历史交易间的依赖关系。本文旨在利用面板数据中蕴含的偏序偏好信息，在马尔可夫链模型下首次系统研究这种依赖关系，以提升参数估计、个性化选择预测和品类优化的效果。

Method: 提出基于马尔可夫链（MC）的选择模型框架，核心方法是新颖的期望最大化（EM）算法，将基于偏序关系的客户偏好信息融入参数估计过程。同时分析了条件选择预测和个性化品类优化问题的计算复杂性与求解结果。

Result: 在合成数据集和sushi数据集上，所提出的EM算法优于Simsek和Topaloglu（2018）的传统EM算法，以及Jagabathula和Vulcano（2018）的基于多项Logit的偏序基准方法。此外，给出了条件选择预测和品类优化问题的硬度与计算结论。

Conclusion: 该框架首次将面板数据下的选择建模引入马尔可夫链模型，提出的EM算法在估计精度上具有显著优势，且条件选择预测与品类优化的计算复杂性分析具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: We propose a framework for the Markov chain (MC) choice model with panel data, including parameter estimation, personalized choice prediction, and personalized assortment optimization. In contrast to the traditional setting, which assumes that each transaction is independently drawn from a random utility model, our framework accounts for dependencies among transactions for the same customer in historical data, captured by partial-ordering preference information. To the best of our knowledge, our framework initiates the study of choice modeling with panel data under MC. As our primary result, we propose novel expectation-maximization (EM) algorithms for MC parameter estimation by incorporating partial-ordering-based customer preference information. On synthetic datasets and the sushi dataset, our EM algorithms outperform the traditional EM algorithm of Simsek and Topaloglu (Operations Research, 66, 2018) and multinomial-logit-based partial-order benchmarks adapted from Jagabathula and Vulcano (Management Science, 64, 2018). As our secondary contribution, we present hardness and computational results for conditional choice prediction and assortment optimization problems. These results complement our estimation framework and clarify the computational landscape of conditional choice and assortment optimization, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [171] [FedCausal-Dyn: A Causal-Dynamic Paradigm for Federated Learning under Dynamic Feature Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09695)
*Kaijie Chen,Alex Johnson,Maria Garcia,Wei Zhang,Daniel Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCausal-Dyn框架，通过因果-动态范式解决联邦学习中的动态特征漂移问题，核心包括因果域特征分离、可靠性驱动的动态原型聚合以及因果特征引导的协同正则化，在三个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据分布随客户端和时间动态演变的特征漂移问题在金融科技等实际应用中普遍存在，但现有方法多假设静态漂移，在非平稳环境中效果受限，亟需能够应对动态特征漂移的鲁棒联邦学习框架。

Method: 提出FedCausal-Dyn框架，包含三大创新：(1) 因果域特征分离：通过专用投影头和对抗训练，将域不变因果特征与虚假的域特异性变化解耦；(2) 可靠性驱动的动态原型聚合：在全局聚合前，根据估计的可靠性对各局部类别原型进行加权；(3) 因果特征引导的协同正则化：将原型对比对齐与域不变性统一为联合优化目标。

Result: 在三个联邦域泛化基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，FedCausal-Dyn持续取得最先进性能，平均准确率最高且结果最稳定。消融实验证实了每个组件的关键贡献。

Conclusion: FedCausal-Dyn为动态特征漂移下的联邦学习提供了一种原则性强且鲁棒的解决方案，因果域分离与动态原型聚合的结合有效应对了非平稳环境中的分布偏移。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenging problem of dynamic feature drift in federated learning, where data distributions evolve across clients and over time -- a common scenario in real-world applications like financial technology. Existing approaches often assume static drift, limiting their effectiveness in non-stationary environments. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{FedCausal-Dyn}, a novel federated learning framework built on a causal-dynamic paradigm. Its key innovation is \textit{causal-domain feature separation}, which disentangles domain-invariant causal features from spurious, domain-specific variations via specialized projection heads and adversarial training. This enables \textit{reliable and dynamic prototype aggregation}, weighting local class prototypes by estimated reliability before global aggregation. We further introduce \textit{causal-feature guided collaborative regularization}, unifying prototype contrastive alignment and domain invariance into a cohesive objective. Extensive experiments on three federated domain generalization benchmarks demonstrate that FedCausal-Dyn consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, with the highest average accuracy and the most stable results. Ablation studies confirm each component's critical contribution. Our work provides a robust and principled solution for federated learning under dynamic feature drift.

</details>


### [172] [Conservation Laws for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10067)
*Ziv Aharoni,Henry D. Pfister*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了基于广义外部信息传输（GEXIT）函数的守恒定律，将扩散模型的数据-模型交叉熵统一表征为沿噪声路径的局部信息论导数的积分，揭示训练等价于学习边际后验分布，并验证了不同噪声类型对有限容量去噪器性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型通过链式法则精确优化数据似然，而扩散模型通常使用去噪目标训练，两类模型的似然表征方式存在根本差异。作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟，建立统一的信息论框架来刻画离散和连续扩散模型的似然。

Method: 基于广义外部信息传递（GEXIT）函数，针对一类广泛的無记忆噪声过程建立守恒定律：将数据-模型交叉熵精确表达为沿噪声路径的局部信息论导数积分。高斯情形退化为经典的I-MMSE关系。核心结论是局部性性质——信息论导数仅需边际后验即可计算，从而训练归结为通过最小化负对数似然来学习边际后验。

Result: 守恒定律表明熵不依赖于噪声路径，但有限容量的去噪器在不同噪声类型下对后验的近似精度不同，导致性能差异。在合成马尔可夫源、text8和CIFAR-10等标准基准上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该守恒定律为理解离散和连续扩散模型的似然提供了统一视角，揭示了训练的本质是学习边际后验，并解释了不同噪声过程导致性能差异的根源在于有限容量去噪器的近似精度差异。

Abstract: While autoregressive models optimize the exact data likelihood via the chain rule, diffusion models are typically trained with denoising objectives. We develop conservation laws based on generalized extrinsic information transfer (GEXIT) functions for a broad class of memoryless noise processes, showing that the data--model cross-entropy (CE) can be characterized exactly as an integral of local information-theoretic derivatives along the noise path. This yields a unified characterization of the likelihood for discrete and continuous diffusion, with the Gaussian case reducing to the well-known mutual information--minimum mean-square error (I-MMSE) relationship. An immediate implication is a locality property: one can compute the information-theoretic derivatives using only the marginal posteriors along the noise path. As a result, training reduces to learning the marginal posteriors by minimizing the negative log-likelihood. While the conservation law implies that the entropy does not depend on the noise path, finite-capacity denoisers approximate the posteriors with varying accuracy across noise types, leading to differences in performance. We validate these predictions on synthetic Markov sources and standard benchmarks, including text8 and CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [173] [Mitigating Early Training Collapse in CTR Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09696)
*Ergun Biçici,Erkan Çetinyamaç*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度CTR预测模型存在首轮训练后验证性能骤降的不稳定问题；降低学习率收效甚微，而控制特征稀疏性（移除高稀疏特征、聚合低频特征值）可显著稳定训练、提升离线与在线表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度CTR模型普遍存在一个反直觉现象：训练损失持续下降，但验证性能在首个epoch后急剧恶化。这种训练不稳定性制约了模型的有效学习与最终性能，现有工业实践中缺乏对其成因的系统性分析与可操作的缓解方案。

Method: 基于大规模工业数据集，作者首先对训练不稳定现象进行诊断分析。随后对比两类缓解策略：（1）降低学习率——仅带来边际收益；（2）控制特征稀疏性——具体包括移除高度稀疏特征、对低频特征值进行聚合（如将长尾类别合并为"其他"桶），以减少噪声梯度与过拟合风险。

Result: 降低学习率仅能获得有限的增量改善；而控制特征稀疏性效果显著：训练更加稳定，有效学习可延续至多个epoch，离线评估指标（如AUC、LogLoss）和在线业务指标均获得实质提升。

Conclusion: CTR深度模型训练不稳定的根源更在于特征稀疏性带来的噪声与过拟合，而非单纯的学习率设置问题。工业系统中应优先关注特征工程层面的稀疏性控制，而非仅依赖优化器超参数调整。

Abstract: Deep neural models for click-through rate prediction often exhibit a sharp decline in validation performance immediately after the first training epoch despite continued improvement in training loss. This instability restricts effective learning and limits model performance. In this study, we analyze this behavior using large-scale industrial datasets and evaluate practical mitigation strategies. While reducing the learning rate provides only incremental gains, controlling feature sparsity yields substantial improvements. Removing highly sparse features and aggregating infrequent feature values stabilizes training, extends useful learning beyond a single epoch, and improves both offline evaluation metrics and online system performance.

</details>


### [174] [Energy-guided Recursive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10128)
*Yifei Zhao,Ying Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ERM 将显式 Hopfield 能量函数引入递归推理模型，为测试时缩放提供了基于能量的内在选择原则（取代 q-head 或启发式投票），并结合 parallel tempering 提升采样效率，在 Sudoku、PPBench、Maze 上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归推理模型在测试时通过增加深度或随机广度生成多条轨迹，但缺乏原则性的轨迹选择机制，主要依赖额外的 q-head 或启发式投票，缺乏内在的评判标准。

Method: 提出 Energy-guided Recursive Model (ERM)，利用 Hopfield 型记忆为有效局部或全局结构定义显式能量函数，该能量函数作为候选轨迹的内在选择器（selector），并与 parallel tempering 等基于能量的采样技术无缝集成以提升采样效率和排序质量。

Result: 在 D=64 递归步、K=128 候选数设置下，ERM 在 Sudoku（98.97%）、PPBench（88.04%）和 Maze（99.30%）上均达到最优解，超越了 Probabilistic Tiny Recursive Model 和 Equilibrium Reasoners 等近期方法。

Conclusion: 将显式能量函数融入递归推理为实现更有效的推理提供了一条原则性路径，表明基于能量的内在选择可作为测试时缩放中轨迹筛选的可靠方案。

Abstract: Recursive reasoning models address structured problems by repeatedly updating latent states of small neural networks. However, their test-time scaling lacks a principled inference mechanism: increasing depth or stochastic breadth generates more trajectories without a clear criterion for selection, and existing methods predominantly rely on additional q-heads or heuristic voting. Here, we develop the Energy-guided Recursive Model (ERM), which introduces an intrinsic selection principle based on explicit Hopfield energies. ERM leverages Hopfield-type memories of valid local or global structures to define the selector over candidate trajectories. The resulting energy seamlessly integrates with energy-based techniques such as parallel tempering to enhance sampling efficiency and ranking. With $D=64$ recurrent steps and $K=128$ candidates, ERM reaches optimal solutions on Sudoku ($98.97\%$), Pencil Puzzle Bench (PPBench, $88.04\%$) and Maze ($99.30\%$), improving upon recent Probabilistic Tiny Recursive Model and Equilibrium Reasoners. These results suggest that incorporating explicit energy functions into recursive reasoning offers a principled path toward more effective inference.

</details>


### [175] [Safe responses matter: Output-aware safety guardrail mitigate over-refusal in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09697)
*Jiayi Li,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向输出的安全护栏方法（OutGuard），通过在MLLM的隐藏状态空间中训练轻量级分类器来预测即将生成的回复是否不安全，从而在保持安全性的同时大幅减少过度拒绝，解决了现有输入侧安全护栏的过度拒绝问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型的安全机制面临安全性与实用性的根本权衡：微调方法安全性强但损害通用能力，而输入侧安全护栏虽轻量但存在严重的过度拒绝问题。作者识别出过度拒绝的根源在于"输入感知"范式——安全护栏在不考虑模型自身能否生成安全回复的情况下就做出安全决策。实际上，MLLM本身具备将有害输入转化为无害输出的内在安全机制，但输入侧护栏覆盖了这一能力。

Method: 提出从"输入感知"范式转向"输出感知"范式。具体方法为：在模型的隐藏状态空间中操作，通过多实例对比学习（multi-instance contrastive learning）在隐藏状态表示上训练一个轻量级分类器，用于在回复完全生成之前预测其是否不安全。该分类器能够区分哪些输入会导致不安全输出、哪些不会，即使输入本身包含风险元素。仅在模型实际回复会是有害的情况下才进行精确干预。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的输出感知安全护栏在匹配现有方法安全性能的同时，大幅减少了过度拒绝，保留了模型的实用性和内置安全能力。

Conclusion: 输出感知范式是解决MLLM安全护栏过度拒绝问题的有效方案，通过关注模型实际生成内容而非仅凭输入判断，实现了安全性与实用性的更好平衡。代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing safety mechanisms for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) face a fundamental trade-off between safety and utility. Model fine-tuning achieves robust safety but compromises general utility. Input-side safety guardrails offer a lightweight alternative, yet they suffer from severe over-refusal, indiscriminately blocking benign queries or those the model could have safely answered through refusal or advisory responses. We identify that the root cause of over-refusal lies in the input-aware paradigm: safety guardrails make safety decisions without considering whether the model itself is capable of generating safe responses. Usually, MLLMs already possess intrinsic safety mechanisms that can transform harmful inputs into harmless outputs, but input-side safety guardrails override this capability, degrading user experience. Motivated by this insight, we propose a paradigm shift toward output-aware safety guardrails. Our method operates within the model's hidden state space to predict whether the forthcoming generation will be unsafe before it is fully produced. By training a lightweight classifier via multi-instance contrastive learning on hidden state representations, our approach distinguishes between inputs that will lead to unsafe outputs and those that will not, even when the inputs themselves contain risky elements. This enables precise intervention only when the model's actual response would be harmful. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our output-aware safety guardrail matches the safety performance of existing methods while drastically reducing over-refusal, preserving the model's utility and built-in safety capabilities. Code is available at: https://github.com/kunzhan/OutGuard

</details>


### [176] [The Differential Neural Tangent Kernel and Its Positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10200)
*Bangti Jin,Longjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出微分神经正切核（DNTK）框架，将NTK理论扩展至物理信息神经网络（PINNs），并证明了对于广泛的激活函数（包括RePU和光滑非多项式激活）及所有线性微分算子，无限宽DNTK的正定性，为PINNs梯度训练算法分析奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NTK是分析过参数化神经网络训练动态的有力工具，已被扩展到用于求解线性PDE的PINNs。NTK的正定性在该分析中起基础性作用，但由于PINNs涉及多个微分算子，建立其NTK正定性极为困难。现有理论框架在此处存在明显缺口。

Method: 作者提出微分神经正切核（Differential Neural Tangent Kernel, DNTK）这一新理论框架，从NTK视角分析PINNs。针对浅层和深层神经网络，在包括RePU和光滑但非多项式的激活函数等广泛类别下，面向所有线性微分算子，严格证明了无限宽DNTK的正定性。

Result: 成功证明了在无限宽极限下，对于浅层和深层网络、广泛的激活函数类（RePU、光滑非多项式激活）以及所有线性微分算子，DNTK是正定的。

Conclusion: DNTK框架成功解决了PINNs中NTK正定性建立的挑战，为后续使用梯度类算法训练PINNs的理论分析提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: The Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) is one powerful tool for analyzing the training dynamics of neural networks in the over-parameterized regime. Recently, the theoretical framework has been extended to physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for solving linear PDEs, one highly popular class of neural PDE solvers. In the analysis, the positivity of the associated NTK plays a fundamental role. However, establishing the positivity of the NTK for PINNs is highly challenging, due to the presence of multiple differential operators. In this work, we propose a new theoretical framework, called Differential Neural Tangent Kernel (DNTK), for analyzing PINNs through the lens of the NTK, and establish the positivity of the infinite width DNTK for both shallow and deep neural networks for a wide class of activation functions, including RePU and smooth but non-polynomial activations, for all linear differential operators. These theoretical results lay the foundation for the analysis of gradient type algorithms for training PINNs.

</details>


### [177] [Quantum-Inspired Contextual Learning for Sparse-Ring Fraud Detection in Dynamic Transaction Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09704)
*Behnam Tonekaboni,Hiroshi Yamauchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于量子启发的动态金融交易图欺诈检测基准与原型，重点研究跨多日的稀疏环欺诈模式。研究表明，将保留身份信息的图特征与拓扑摘要相结合的混合表示效果最佳，拓扑特征作为图特征的上下文层最为有效，量子启发的CML模型在该任务上展现出潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调性欺诈（如洗钱闭环）在单笔交易中不可见，而是以跨时间、跨关系的多期模式呈现。现有方法难以同时整合时间序列和关系图结构信息，作者聚焦于"稀疏环欺诈"——即完整的定向交易环分散在数天内完成，要求模型在时序和图结构两个维度上联合推理。

Method: (1) 使用合成交易模拟器生成数据，注入完整稀疏环（正样本）和断环诱饵（负样本）；(2) 将日度有向交易图聚合为滚动窗口；(3) 提取三类特征：原始图特征、持续同调摘要、以及二者融合的混合特征向量；(4) 以GRU作为序列级分类基线，对比量子启发的上下文机器学习（CML）模型。

Result: 仅使用拓扑摘要（持续同调）因丢失账户对身份和边方向信息而压缩过度，不足以独立完成监督环完成分类任务。将保留身份的图特征与拓扑摘要结合的混合表示取得最佳结果，表明拓扑信息作为动态图特征的上下文层最为有效。CML在该任务上表现优于GRU基线。

Conclusion: 在分布式时序-关系欺诈检测中，拓扑特征不宜单独使用，而应作为图结构特征的上下文增强层。量子启发的CML模型能有效整合跨时间和跨关系的证据，是处理此类欺诈模式的有前景的方法。该结论在合成数据和序列级标签的探索性设定下成立。

Abstract: We present an exploratory benchmark and quantum-inspired modeling prototype for fraud screening in dynamic financial transaction graphs. Coordinated fraud may not be visible from individual transactions alone, but may emerge as a multi-period relational pattern. We focus on sparse-ring fraud, a stylized pattern in which a completed directed cycle is distributed across several days, requiring models to integrate evidence across both time and graph structure. We study this problem using a synthetic transaction simulator with completed sparse-ring injections and broken-ring decoys.
