### [244] [Integrating Background Knowledge for Scalable Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10456)
*Mátyás Schubert,Theofanis Aslanidis,Tom Claassen,Sara Magliacane*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个在因果发现过程中（而非后处理阶段）有效利用专家背景知识的框架，专注于可扩展的因果发现方法，能够在降低计算需求的同时提升所学因果图结构质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际因果发现应用中常可获得专家背景知识（即对真实因果图的约束条件），这类约束有助于提升因果效应的可识别性和结构学习的准确性，并能缩减候选因果图空间。尤其在变量数量较大时，因果发现计算开销高，因此亟需在发现过程中有效利用背景知识。然而现有方法大多仅在因果发现后处理阶段使用背景知识来精炼所学图结构，未能充分发挥其降本增效的潜力。

Method: 作者开发了一个在因果发现过程中融入背景知识的通用框架，特别聚焦于仅恢复部分图结构的可扩展因果发现方法。该框架将背景知识约束整合进因果发现算法核心流程，而非仅用作后处理步骤，并在多个算法上进行了框架实现。

Result: 在多个算法上的实证结果表明，在因果发现过程中利用背景知识能够显著降低计算开销，同时提升所学因果图结构的质量。

Conclusion: 将背景知识融入因果发现过程（而非后处理）是一种有效的策略，尤其适用于大规模变量场景下的可扩展因果发现，可同时实现计算效率提升和结构质量改善。

Abstract: Expert background knowledge is often available in practical applications of causal discovery. Such constraints on the true causal graph can help causal discovery in terms of identifiability of causal effects and accuracy of the learned structure, but also in reducing the space of candidate causal graphs. As causal discovery can become computationally expensive for large number of variables, it is crucial to utilize background knowledge effectively during the causal discovery process. However, most current methods only use background knowledge in a postprocessing step after causal discovery to refine the learned graph. In this work, we develop a framework for utilizing background knowledge during the causal discovery process, focusing especially on scalable causal discovery methods that recover only a subset of the whole graph. We implement our framework for multiple algorithms and empirically show that utilizing background knowledge can both reduce computational requirements and increase the quality of the learned structures.

</details>


### [245] [Representation Learning for Semiparametric Causal Mediation Analysis under No Essential Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10540)
*Roberto Faleh,Sofia Morelli,Holger Brandt*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出UNIT方法，结合深度表征学习(TARNet)与G-estimation，在无本质异质性假设下估计结构中介参数。第一阶段的CATE估计作为第二阶段的插件权重，提升了中介效应估计精度，模拟中标准误降低约1.5倍，且无偏性和覆盖率不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在未测量中介-结局混杂的情况下，传统结构中介分析中G-estimation的权重函数依赖于难以准确估计的异质性处理效应。作者希望利用深度表征学习改善第一阶段异质性效应的估计精度，从而获得更优的插件权重，提升结构参数估计的效率。

Method: 提出两阶段估计器UNIT：第一阶段使用TARNet (Treatment-Agnostic Representation Network)，通过学习跨处理组的共享协变量表征来估计随机化处理对中介变量的异质性效应(CATE)；第二阶段将该CATE估计作为Zheng & Zhou (2015) G-estimation方程中权重函数的插件近似，识别并估计结构中介参数。整个过程依赖于无本质异质性(NEH)假设。

Result: 在非高斯协变量和非线性中介效应的模拟中，与经典方法相比，TARNet权重将第二阶段中介系数的标准误降低了1.45至1.51倍(各重复实验的中位数，n≥2000)，且未引入偏倚，覆盖率保持名义水平。

Conclusion: 更精确的第一阶段表征学习能产生更具信息量的插件权重，从而显著提升结构中介参数估计的精度。UNIT方法在保持有效推断的同时实现了效率增益，为存在未测量中介-结局混杂场景下的中介分析提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a two-stage estimator for structural mediation parameters that combines deep representation learning with G-estimation under the "no essential heterogeneity" (NEH) assumption. We call the method UNIT. In the first stage,TARNet estimates the heterogeneous effect of a randomized treatment on a mediator by learning a shared covariate representation across treatment arms.The resulting conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimate provides a plug-in approximation to the heterogeneity-dependent component of the weight function entering the G-estimating equation of Zheng and Zhou (2015), which identifies the structural parameters even in the presence of unmeasured mediator-outcome confounding. We show that more accurate first-stage representation learning can yield a more informative plug-in weight and thereby improve the precision of the structural parameter estimator. In simulations with non-Gaussian covariates and nonlinear mediator effects, TARNet weights reduce the Stage-2 standard error of the mediation coefficient by a factor of $1.45$ to $1.51$ (median across replications, $n \ge 2000$) relative to the classical approach, at no cost to bias or coverage.

</details>


### [246] [Beyond Looking Up, Try Looking Around: Harmonizing Global Structure and Local Consistency in Optimal Transport for Short Text Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10548)
*Zhihao Yao,Yuxuan Gu,Jixuan Yin,Bo Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的短文本聚类框架，通过在最优传输（OT）中引入实例级注意力机制来捕获样本间语义一致性，生成更可靠的伪标签，从而显著提升聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于最优传输的伪标签方法忽略了样本间的语义一致性，导致语义相似的样本可能被分配不同的伪标签，这些错误的伪标签会引导模型产生劣质聚类结果。

Method: 提出CAOT-STC框架，核心包括：(1) 设计实例级注意力机制捕获样本间语义关系；(2) 将语义关系融入最优传输公式，赋予传输过程邻域语义感知能力；(3) 求解改进后的OT公式，获得同时考虑样本间语义一致性和样本-簇全局结构信息的可靠伪标签；(4) 以此伪标签作为监督信号指导模型实现精确聚类。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法在短文本聚类任务上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将语义一致性显式建模融入最优传输框架，可有效缓解伪标签错误分配问题。该方法不仅在理论上弥补了现有OT方法的不足，在实验中也验证了其有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Pseudo-labeling based on Optimal Transport (OT) has become an effective mechanism for enhancing short text clustering. Existing OT methods are short in modeling semantic consistencies between samples, which may assign different pseudo-labels to semantically similar samples. These erroneous pseudo-labels can cause the model to produce inferior clusters. This paper proposes a novel short text clustering framework, which remedies the neglect of semantic consistency in existing OT methods, generating reliable pseudo-labels to facilitate clustering. Specifically, the proposed approach first designs an instance-level attention mechanism to capture semantic relationships between samples, which are then integrated into the OT formulation to endow the transport process with neighborhood semantic awareness. By solving the proposed OT formulation, reliable pseudo-labels are obtained that simultaneously account for sample-to-sample semantic consistency and sample-to-cluster global structure information. These pseudo-labels are then used as supervisory signals to guide the model to achieve accurate clustering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at: \href{https://github.com/YZH0905/CAOT-STC}{https://github.com/YZH0905/CAOT-STC}.

</details>


### [247] [Approximation of Analytic Functions by ReLU Neural Networks with Adjustable Depth and Width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10589)
*Yanming Lai,Defeng Sun,Yang Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在神经网络逼近理论的(N,L)-表征框架下，建立了ReLU网络逼近解析函数的上界，得出逼近速率为O(N^{-C L^τ})，揭示深度比宽度在解析函数逼近中更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于(N,L)-表征的工作主要关注有限光滑性函数类，其逼近速率中宽度N和深度L是对称的（均为O(N^{-2s/d}L^{-2s/d})）。本文旨在探索具有无限光滑性的解析函数在该框架下的逼近行为，以揭示深度和宽度角色的根本差异。

Method: 作者采用(N,L)-联合表征框架，通过精巧构造多个ReLU网络组件（包括幂函数逼近网络、多元乘法网络和多项式逼近网络），克服光滑性参数与逼近精度之间的权衡这一核心技术难点，最终推导出解析函数逼近的上界。

Result: 证明了ReLU网络逼近解析函数的上界为O(N^{-C L^τ})，其中C>0为常数，τ>0受L与N关系影响。特别地，当N大致按L^d缩放时τ=1。该结果表明逼近速率在N上呈多项式衰减，在L上呈指数级提升。

Conclusion: 与有限光滑性函数类中深度和宽度角色对称不同，在解析函数逼近中深度扮演了更为关键的角色，这一发现为网络架构设计提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: In contrast to most studies on neural network approximation theory that characterize results through a single parameter, such as the total number of network parameters, \cite{shen2020deep} pioneered the characterization of approximation rates as a joint function of the width parameter $N$ and the depth parameter $L$, thereby granting greater architectural flexibility. Existing works using the $(N,L)$-characterization focus on function classes with finite smoothness $s$, establishing a typical approximation rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(N^{-2s/d}L^{-2s/d}\right)$ with $d$ denoting the input dimension, which indicates that network depth and width play symmetric roles for these classes. In contrast, this paper establishes upper bounds for the approximation of analytic functions, which possess infinite smoothness, via ReLU networks under the $(N,L)$-characterization. Specifically, we derive approximation rates of $\mathcal{O}\left(N^{-C L^τ}\right)$, where $C>0$ is some constant and $τ>0$ is a parameter influenced by the relation between $L$ and $N$. In particular, $τ=1$ if $N$ scales roughly as $L^d$. Our findings reveal that depth plays a more critical role than width in the context of analytic function approximation. The main technical difficulty of obtaining such upper bounds lies in the trade-off between the smoothness parameters and the approximation accuracy. To overcome this difficulty, we employ refined constructions of several ReLU networks to approximate power functions, multivariate multiplication, and polynomials, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [248] [Demixing Sparse Signals from Nonlinear Observations using Generalized Non-convex Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10618)
*Raziyeh Takbiri*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究从少量非线性观测中恢复一对稀疏向量的问题，提出结合Huber损失与折叠凹惩罚的正则化框架，设计了NLD-PALM算法，证明了全局收敛性与局部线性收敛率，并建立了稀疏估计的统计误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信号处理中，常需从非线性、重尾或受污染噪声的少量观测中恢复两个稀疏向量的叠加。传统方法依赖凸惩罚或精确平方损失，对异常值敏感且需要已知稀疏度，无法在非凸非线性条件下提供统计保证。

Method: 提出Huberized数据保真度与广义折叠凹惩罚（SCAD、MCP）结合的正则化框架；设计带有回溯的两块近端交替算法（NLD-PALM），利用Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质证明迭代序列收敛到临界点并具有局部线性收敛率；通过精确符号确定分解建立Huber化非线性损失的受限强凸性，推导估计误差界；对未知单调链接函数，采用线性替代和截断Plan-Vershynin解耦得到协同恢复定理。

Result: 1) 估计误差界为 σ√(s log n / m)，适用于所有局部平稳点；2) 在beta-min条件下获得无log n和收缩偏差的oracle速率 σ√(s/m)；3) n=512实验中，比凸ℓ₁分离和贪婪硬阈值基线更早出现相变，在5%粗差异常值下比平方损失估计精度提高35倍；4) 成功实现经饱和放大器观测的尖峰加背景信号分离。

Conclusion: 所提框架在非凸非线性条件下实现了对异常值和重尾噪声的鲁棒性，无需已知稀疏度，统计保证适用于仅具有有限方差的对称噪声，且在实验中展现出显著的性能优势。

Abstract: We consider the recovery of a pair of sparse vectors from a limited number of nonlinear observations of their superposition: $y_i=g(\inner{\ba_i}{\bPhi\bw^\ast+\bPsi\bz^\ast})+e_i$, $i=1,\dots,m$, with $m\ll n$, incoherent orthonormal bases $\bPhi,\bPsi$, a scalar link $g$, and noise $e_i$ that may be heavy-tailed or contaminated. We propose a regularization-based framework combining a Huberized data fidelity with generalized folded-concave penalties (SCAD, MCP), and a two-block proximal alternating algorithm with backtracking (NLD-PALM) whose whole iterate sequence provably converges to critical points under the Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz property, with local linear rates. On the statistical side we establish restricted strong convexity of the Huberized nonlinear loss through an exact sign-definite decomposition, and derive estimation error bounds of order $σ\sqrt{s\log(n)/m}$ that hold at \emph{every} localized stationary point, an oracle rate $σ\sqrt{s/m}$ free of $\log n$ and shrinkage bias under a beta-min condition, and a co-equal recovery theorem for \emph{unknown} monotone links via a linear surrogate and a clipped Plan--Vershynin decoupling. The estimator requires no knowledge of the sparsity levels, and its guarantees hold under symmetric noise with only finite variance. Experiments at $n=512$ under a frozen data-driven regularization rule show an earlier phase transition than convex $\ell_1$ demixing and greedy hard-thresholding baselines, a $35\times$ accuracy advantage over squared-loss estimation under $5\%$ gross outliers, and successful demixing of spike-plus-background signals observed through a saturating amplifier.

</details>


### [249] [Edge Cluster Expansion with Radial Rotary Attention for Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10664)
*Zemin Xu,Wenbo Xie,P. Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了SO(2)等变理论在机器学习原子间势中的应用，指出传统SO(2)线性架构相对于SO(3) CGTP的局限，提出了两种Wigner D矩阵构造方法及两个新型交互构建模块（Edge Complex Product Basis和Radial Rotary Complex Attention），最终训练的TECE-OAM-RRA-1.0模型在Matbench Discovery上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示SO(2)等变架构在MLIPs中的局限性，尤其是传统SO(2)线性架构相对于SO(3) Clebsch-Gordan张量积方法的不足，从而为设计更强表达能力与更好外推性能的等变交互模块提供理论依据。

Method: 提出两种Wigner D矩阵构造方式：直接笛卡尔构造与递归Clebsch-Gordan构造；设计Edge Complex Product Basis（基于广义非对称缩并，通过复值等变乘法在边上直接构建高阶多体相互作用）；提出Radial Rotary Complex Attention以增强外推能力；并对Atomic Cluster Expansion模块进行多项改进。

Result: 在OMat24、sAlex和MPTrj数据集上训练模型，最终模型TECE-OAM-RRA-1.0在Matbench Discovery基准上取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: SO(2)等变架构通过本文提出的新型构建模块（Edge Complex Product Basis与RRA）以及Wigner D矩阵构造方法，可显著弥补传统SO(2)线性架构的不足，实现与SO(3) CGTP相媲美甚至更优的性能，为MLIPs的等变建模提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a systematic investigation of SO(2) theory to machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) and identify the limitations of conventional SO(2) Linear architectures relative to SO(3) Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Products (CGTP). Building on these insights, we propose direct Cartesian construction and recursive Clebsch-Gordan construction of Wigner D-matrices and introduce two novel interaction building blocks. First, we propose the Edge Complex Product Basis based on Generalized Asymmetric Contraction, a new formulation for many-body expansion that directly constructs higher-order interactions on edges through complex-valued equivariant multiplications. Second, we introduce Radial Rotary Complex Attention(RRA), which enhances extrapolation performance and surpasses existing attention vector formulations. We also introduce several improvements to the Atomic Cluster Expansion module. Building on these advances, we train our models on OMat24, sAlex, and MPTrj, and introduce TECE-OAM-RRA-1.0, which achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on the Matbench Discovery.

</details>


### [250] [Long-Memory Reservoir Computing for Data-Scarce Dengue Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11272)
*Rahul Goswami,Shinjini Paul,Palash Ghosh,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对登革热预测中序列短、噪声多、非线性及长记忆等挑战，提出两种长记忆储备池计算框架——分数阶回声状态网络（fESN）和小波回声状态网络（wESN），通过将长记忆机制融入ESN储备池，在多个登革热数据集上优于统计和深度学习基线，并结合保形预测提供校准的不确定性区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 登革热发病序列通常短、含噪声、非平稳、非线性且具有长程时间依赖性。ARFIMA可建模长记忆但受限于线性结构；深度神经网络能捕捉非线性但需大样本且不显式编码统计长记忆；标准ESN适合小样本但缺乏长记忆。因此需要一种能同时处理非线性与小样本场景的长记忆时间序列预测方法。

Method: 提出长记忆储备池计算框架，将专用长记忆和短记忆ESN储备池与岭回归读出层集成。两个变体：(1) fESN：在储备池动力学中嵌入分数差分以直接编码长程依赖性；(2) wESN：通过小波平滑提取稳定低频分量，再用记忆感知储备池建模。同时理论证明标准ESN在温和条件下产生短记忆过程，而所提储备池生成与统计长记忆一致的多项式衰减依赖性。结合保形预测实现无分布的校准不确定性区间。

Result: 在多个登革热数据集和不同预测时限上，fESN和wESN均优于统计基线和深度学习基线。结合保形预测后，能够提供无分布的校准不确定性区间。

Conclusion: fESN和wESN通过显式编码长记忆机制，有效弥补了标准ESN在长程依赖性建模上的不足，在小样本非线性时间序列预测任务（如登革热预测）中展现出显著优势，且与保形预测的集成进一步增强了其实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate dengue forecasting is crucial for public health planning, but remains challenging because incidence series are often short, noisy, non-stationary, nonlinear, and often affected by long-range temporal dependence. Fractional differencing in Autoregressive Fractionally Integrated Moving Average (ARFIMA) helps balance non-stationarity and persistence, but its linear structure limits its ability to capture nonlinear dynamics. Deep neural networks can model nonlinear patterns, but usually require large training samples and do not explicitly encode statistical long memory. Echo State Networks (ESNs), a widely used reservoir computing framework, are attractive in this setting because they retain nonlinear recurrent dynamics while training only a simple readout, making them suitable for data-scarce scenarios. However, standard ESNs lack long-term memory from a time-series perspective. This study proposes a long-memory reservoir computing framework that integrates dedicated long-memory and short-memory ESN reservoirs with a ridge-regression readout. We introduce two variants: Fractional ESN (fESN), which incorporates fractional-differencing dynamics into the reservoir to encode long-range dependence directly, and Wavelet ESN (wESN), which extracts stable low-frequency components through wavelet smoothing before modeling them with a memory-aware reservoir. We establish theoretical guarantees for closed-loop reservoir dynamics, showing that standard ESNs induce short-memory processes under mild conditions, whereas the proposed long-memory reservoirs generate polynomially decaying dependence consistent with statistical long memory. Across multiple dengue datasets and forecasting horizons, fESN and wESN outperform statistical and deep learning baselines. Combining conformal prediction with fESN and wESN provides distribution-free calibrated uncertainty intervals.

</details>


### [251] [Diversified Multinomial Logit Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11684)
*Heesang Ann,Taehyun Hwang,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出多样化多项Logit（DMNL）上下文赌博机模型，通过引入一般次模多样性函数来增强MNL选择概率，形式化了相关性-多样性权衡。作者设计了基于UCB的白盒算法OFU-DMNL，通过逐项最大化乐观边际增益构造商品组合，实现了至少(1-1/(e+1))近似遗憾界，并在实验中验证了其统计与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文MNL赌博机仅建模相关性驱动的选择，忽略了商品组合内部多样性的潜在收益；而次模/组合赌博机虽在奖励中编码多样性，却缺乏结构化的选择概率。两者之间存在空白，需要统一的模型来同时刻画相关性-多样性权衡。

Method: 作者提出DMNL模型，将MNL选择概率与一般次模多样性函数相结合。针对多样性引入后精确优化不可行的问题，设计OFU-DMNL算法：采用白盒UCB框架，通过逐项最大化乐观边际增益构造商品组合，避免黑盒优化预言机；利用上下文维度d、最大组合规模K等参数进行遗憾分析。

Result: OFU-DMNL实现了至少(1-1/(e+1))近似遗憾界 Õ(d√(T/K))，在近似因子方面优于标准次模基线。实验表明，与穷举枚举相比，该方法在保持可比遗憾的同时显著降低了运行时间。

Conclusion: DMNL赌博机为不确定性下的多样性感知商品组合优化提供了实用基础，OFU-DMNL则提供了统计与计算上均高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing contextual multinomial logit (MNL) bandits model relevance-driven choice but ignore the potential benefits of within-assortment diversity, while submodular/combinatorial bandits encode diversity in rewards but lack structured choice probabilities. We bridge this gap with the $\textit{diversified multinomial logit}$ (DMNL) contextual bandit, which augments MNL choice probabilities with a generally submodular diversity function, thereby formalizing the relevance--diversity trade-off within a single model. Incorporating diversity renders exact MNL assortment optimization intractable. We propose a $\textit{white-box}$ UCB-based algorithm, $\texttt{OFU-DMNL}$, that constructs assortments item-wise by maximizing optimistic marginal gains, avoids black-box optimization oracles. We show that $\texttt{OFU-DMNL}$ achieves at least a $(1-\frac{1}{e+1})$-$\textit{approximate}$ regret bound $\tilde{O}\left(d \sqrt{T/K}\right)$, where $d$ is the context dimension, $K$ the maximum assortment size, and $T$ the horizon, and attains an improved approximation factor over standard submodular baselines. Experiments demonstrate consistent gains and, relative to exhaustive enumeration, comparable regret with substantially lower runtime. Overall, DMNL bandits provide a practical foundation for diversity-aware assortment optimization under uncertainty, and $\texttt{OFU-DMNL}$ offers a statistically and computationally efficient solution.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [From ML Predictions to Informed Diagnostic Assistance Using the Toulmin Model of Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09664)
*Anca Marginean,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图尔敏论证模型的框架，将视网膜图像诊断分解为声明、依据、保证、限定词和反驳等组件，结合生物标志物提取、医学知识代理(MedGemma)和图像相似度(MedSigLip)，为人类专家提供结构化、可解释的ML诊断评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML模型生成的医学诊断往往以黑箱形式呈现，临床专家难以对其进行批判性审视。单纯依赖XAI方法缺乏结构化的论证逻辑，因此需要一种将诊断过程形式化为论证结构的框架，以支持更审慎的临床决策。

Method: 采用图尔敏论证模型将诊断分解为六个组件：(1)声明(claim)——ML模型生成的诊断结论；(2)依据(grounds)——由专门的生物标志物提取模型从图像中获取；(3)保证(warrant)——由配备医学知识的MedGemma代理分析依据与声明之间的推理桥梁；(4)限定词(qualifier)——基于保证模型和依据模型的整体定量评估确定；(5)反驳(rebuttal)——利用MedSigLip计算的图像相似度度量构建；(6)所有组件统一呈现给人类专家进行最终评估。

Result: 该框架能够将ML诊断过程转化为结构化的论证呈现，使人类专家可以分别审视每个论证组件的可信度，从而做出更知情和更具批判性的判断。

Conclusion: 将论证理论引入医学影像诊断的可解释性研究，比传统XAI方法更具结构性和说服力，为人机协同诊断提供了新的范式。

Abstract: To provide a structured and interpretable assessment, we decompose the image-based diagnosis into components following the Toulmin model of argumentation. This model consists of a claim, grounds, warrant, qualifier, rebuttal, and backing. Consider a claim generated by a machine learning (ML) model for retinal diagnosis. Rather than accepting this claim at face value, one could either apply explainable AI (XAI) methods or adopt an argumentation-based approach. In our framework, a model specialized in biomarker extraction from images provides the grounds. The warrant-linking the grounds to the claim - is analyzed by an agent equipped with medical knowledge; in our architecture, this role is fulfilled by a MedGemma agent. The qualifier is determined based on the overall quantitative evaluation of both the warrant and grounds models. Finally, a rebuttal is constructed using image similarity measures computed with MedSigLip. All these components are presented to the human expert, enabling a more informed and critical assessment of the ML-generated diagnosis.

</details>


### [253] [Format Sensitivity Index: Token-Controlled Prompt Wrapper Robustness and Schema Compliance in LLM Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09665)
*Deep Pankajbhai Mehta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了提示包装格式对模型评分的影响，提出格式敏感性指数(FSI)和可解析性敏感性指数(PSI)两个指标，基于14万次生成实验，发现不同模型间FSI差异超30倍，主要由合规失败驱动，可解析性是准确率的强预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示包装(prompt wrappers)仅在格式上存在差异，却能显著改变模型评分，甚至翻转排行榜结论。现有的基准测试评估往往忽略了这种由包装格式引入的方差，导致排行榜结论在统计上不可靠。因此，需要系统量化这种敏感性，并揭示其背后的驱动机制。

Method: 作者采用token控制协议(taken-controlled protocol)，在OpenRouter平台上进行大规模实验，覆盖7个QA任务、5种包装家族、4个7B到72B参数的指令模型，共生成约140,000条数据。提出两个核心指标：格式敏感性指数(FSI)，衡量包装选择导致的准确率波动范围；可解析性敏感性指数(PSI)，衡量答案可解析性的相应波动范围。使用固定效应回归分析，在控制任务、模型和包装因素后，检验可解析性对准确率的预测力。

Result: 不同模型间平均FSI差异超过30倍，该差异主要由合规失败(compliance failures)解释。固定效应回归表明，即使控制了任务、模型和包装变量，可解析性仍然是准确率的强预测因子。说明忽略包装方差和合规性的准确率报告在统计上是脆弱的。

Conclusion: 仅报告准确率而不考虑包装方差和合规性是统计上不可靠的做法。作者为基准测试评估和结构化输出部署提供了实用建议，呼吁将格式敏感性纳入标准评估流程。

Abstract: Prompt wrappers often differ only in formatting, yet they can change model scores enough to flip leaderboard conclusions. We study this variance under a token-controlled protocol and introduce two complementary metrics: the Format Sensitivity Index (FSI), the accuracy range induced by wrapper choice, and the Parseability Sensitivity Index (PSI), the corresponding range in answer parseability. Across 140,000 OpenRouter generations spanning 7 QA tasks, 5 wrapper families, and 4 instruct models from 7B to 72B parameters, we find that mean FSI varies by over 30x across models and is largely explained by compliance failures. A fixed-effects regression shows that parseability remains a strong predictor of accuracy even after controlling for task, model, and wrapper. We argue that reporting accuracy without wrapper variance and compliance is statistically fragile, and we give practical recommendations for both benchmarking and structured-output deployments.

</details>


### [254] [Faithful, Not Corrective: Message-Format Effects in Multi-Hop Agent Relays Are Tier-Dependent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09678)
*Zayx Shawn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了LLM智能体间信息传递中消息格式的影响：强中继下格式影响微小（近乎无损），弱中继下不同格式的保真度差异被放大8.7倍；结构化格式（尤其是固定键JSON）在跳跃传递中提供了忠实且错误局部化的通道，但并非纠错码。格式选择应依据链路中最弱的中继能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两派文献存在分歧：格式优化研究认为结构化消息可降本增效，格式限制研究则认为强制结构会损害生成质量。且现有研究均未考察多跳传递场景下的复制保真度问题。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统研究消息格式在多跳中继中的影响。

Method: 构建受控中继测试平台：将12条程序化生成的原子事实摘要，在6跳链路上以5种格式（自由自然语言、精确指令自然语言、JSON、三元组、键值对）逐跳重新编码，由固定强评分器对照程序化真值评分。实验覆盖两个中继能力层级、认知负载条件及配对分叉错误注入。

Result: (1) 强中继在忠实中继指令下近乎无损，传统"传话游戏"式崩溃未出现；加入认知负载后格式层面保真度变化在±1.8点以内，但生成成本增加24-53%。(2) 弱中继(1.5B)下六跳召回率的跨格式差异扩大8.7倍（从2.3到20.5点），由刚性格式的编码代价和固定键JSON特有的漂移抵抗性两个对立机制驱动，导致传递过程中格式排名反转。(3) 错误注入实验中，一旦注入错误值，83-100%的链路中该错误会持续到最终跳，与各格式对真值的保留率紧密匹配，且未对邻近事实造成可检测的附带损害。

Conclusion: 结构化格式提供的是忠实且错误局部化的通道，而非纠错码；在多智能体中继链路中，格式选择应依据链路中最弱的中继节点能力来决定。

Abstract: When LLM agents hand off information to one another, does the message format matter? Two literatures disagree: format-optimization work reports that structured messages cut cost without hurting accuracy, while format-restriction work finds that imposing structure degrades generation -- and neither measures what happens when a message traverses multiple hops, where copy fidelity, not one-shot generation, dominates. We introduce a controlled relay testbed: briefs of twelve programmatically generated atomic facts are re-encoded hop-by-hop in five formats (free NL, precision-instructed NL, JSON, triples, key-value) over six hops, scored by a fixed strong grader against programmatic ground truth, across two relay-capability tiers, a cognitive-load condition, and a paired-fork error injection. We find that message-format effects are tier-dependent. (i) Under faithful-relay instructions a strong relay is nearly lossless -- the documented "telephone-game" collapse does not occur -- and adding per-hop cognitive load leaves format-level fidelity unchanged (within +/-1.8 points) while raising generation cost by 24-53%. (ii) Under a weak (1.5B) relay the across-format spread of six-hop recall grows by a factor of 8.7 (from 2.3 to 20.5 points), driven by two opposing mechanisms -- an encoding toll paid by the rigid formats and drift resistance specific to the fixed-key JSON schema -- that flip the format ranking in transit. (iii) In a paired-fork injection, an injected wrong value, once present, persists to the final hop in 83-100% of chains in every format, closely matching each format's retention of the true value, with no detectable collateral damage to neighboring facts. Structure buys a faithful, error-localizing channel -- not an error-correcting code -- and format choice should follow the weakest relay in the pipeline.

</details>


### [255] [Boltzmann MapReduce: A Partition-Function Reduce for Forkable Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09689)
*Yossi Eliaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在局部渐近正态（LAN）框架下，工作者对大小为n的数据块发出的置信密度可表为Gibbs-Boltzmann测度，其逆温度β等于样本量n。由此得到三个推论：独立块携带独立的Boltzmann因子，MapReduce的reduce步骤本质上是一个配分函数，其众数对应于精度加权池化；频率学派一致性可理解为零温度极限（T=1/n→0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将分布式计算（MapReduce）中的统计推断问题与统计力学的Boltzmann-Gibbs框架建立形式对应关系，从而借助物理直觉理解大规模数据分布式推断的数学结构，特别是揭示置信密度聚合、精度加权池化与频率学派一致性之间的内在联系。

Method: 基于局部渐近正态（LAN）理论，导出一阶近似下每个数据块对应的置信密度为Gibbs-Boltzmann形式 exp{-βE(θ)}，β=n。利用指数族因式分解性质证明独立块的Boltzmann因子乘积结构，进而将MapReduce的reduce操作解释为配分函数积分 Z=∫∏ₖ hₖ dθ，并分析其众数等价于精度加权池化；将n→∞时的频率学派一致性解释为零温极限。

Result: 三个核心结果：(1) 不相交数据块携带独立的Boltzmann因子；(2) MapReduce的reduce步骤严格等价于计算配分函数，其众数给出精度加权（逆方差）池化估计；(3) 频率学派一致性对应于温度T=1/n趋于零的极限。这些结果在高斯/线性情形下精确成立，在其他情形下为一阶近似。

Conclusion: LAN理论在分布式推断与统计力学之间建立了深刻的形式对应：置信密度即Boltzmann测度，样本量即逆温度，MapReduce的reduce即配分函数，精度加权池化即众数估计，频率学派一致性即零温极限。这一对应为分布式统计计算提供了物理直觉和统一框架。

Abstract: To leading order under local asymptotic normality (LAN), the confidence density a worker emits over a chunk of size $n$ is a Gibbs--Boltzmann measure $\exp\{-βE(θ)\}$ whose inverse temperature is the sample size, $β=n$. Three consequences are exact in the Gaussian/linear case and first-order otherwise: disjoint chunks carry independent Boltzmann factors, so the MapReduce \emph{reduce}, read literally, is a partition function $Z=\int\prod_k h_k\,dθ$ whose mode is precision-weighted (inverse-variance) pooling; frequentist consistency is the zero-temperature limit $T=1/n\to0$

</details>


### [256] [Interpreting Latent CoT Reasoning as Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09698)
*Sabari Iyyappan Duraipandian,Shreya Sanjay Boyane,Manju Nagesh,Jerome Francis,Archana Vaidheeswaran,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将潜在推理过程建模为表征空间中的轨迹，运用动力系统分析（步间变化、方向一致性、Lyapunov灵敏度及UMAP/DMD/PHATE投影）揭示了两类不同的稳定性模式：CODI表现为稳定吸引子，COCONUT表现为不稳定扩张系统，SIM-CoT监督可收紧两者行为但不改变底层动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法（如CODI、COCONUT）在每步隐藏空间中叠加多个候选轨迹，缺乏可解释性。而已有的机械论方法仅揭示了压缩、捷径和叠加等现象，无法解释推理如何在潜在步骤间演化。因此需要一种能够刻画潜在推理动力学演化的分析框架。

Method: 将潜在token序列建模为表征空间中的轨迹，采用动力系统分析方法：定量指标包括步间变化量、方向一致性和Lyapunov灵敏度；定性可视化包括UMAP投影和DMD/PHATE分析。通过该方法对比CODI和COCONUT的动力学行为，并检验SIM-CoT监督的影响。

Result: 潜在CoT呈现结构化、非随机的动力学特征，可分为两种稳定性类别：(1) CODI表现为稳定吸引子行为；(2) COCONUT表现为不稳定扩张系统。SIM-CoT监督能够收紧两者的行为模式，但不改变其底层动力学结构。

Conclusion: 该框架推进了对潜在CoT推理动力学的可解释性理解，并为提升潜在推理性能提供了可操作的指导。代码和项目页面已开源。

Abstract: Recent latent reasoning methods, such as CODI and COCONUT, face a fundamental interpretability problem: they maintain multiple superimposed candidate traces in the hidden space at each step, unlike explicit- CoT, which follows a single transparent reasoning trace. Existing mechanistic methods show compression, shortcuts, and superposition without explaining how reasoning evolves across latent steps. To address this gap, we model latent token sequences as trajectories in representation space and apply dynamical systems analysis to characterize the evolution of reasoning. Using quantitative measures, such as step-to-step change, direction consistency, and Lyapunov sensitivity, alongside qualitative projections, such as UMAP and DMD/PHATE, we show that latent CoT exhibits structured, non-random dynamics with two distinct stability classes. CODI behaves as a stable attractor, while COCONUT behaves as an unstable expanding system, and SIM-CoT supervision tightens both behaviors without changing the underlying dynamics. This framework advances the interpretability of latent CoT reasoning dynamics and provides actionable insights for improving latent reasoning performance. Code1 and Project page2 available online.

</details>


### [257] [YUKTI: From Natural-Language Situations to Robust, Verifiable Decisions An Uncertainty-Typed Proposition IR, Assumption-Robust Pareto Frontiers, and a Regret Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09706)
*Suyash Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: YUKTI 提出了一种鲁棒的自动运筹规划方法，用类型化命题图替代点值系数，引入假设鲁棒帕累托前沿（ARPF），通过重采样评估决策的生存率ρ，并在误设、合规商业决策和真实数据集上验证了其在降低遗憾方面的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流自动规划管线（NL4Opt、OptiMUS等）将语言描述的决策问题转化为单一目标、点值系数的确定性优化问题，这种"脆弱的计算模仿"在真实资源配置场景中极易因假设偏差导致灾难性失败——系数猜测稍有偏差，所谓的"最优方案"即告瓦解。

Method: YUKTI 构建类型化命题图作为中间表示，图中关系携带形状先验、系数不确定性和溯源信息；各阶段分别路由至精确、非线性或进化求解器，并通过分布化帕累托交接（distributional Pareto hand-off）耦合多阶段决策；核心创新是假设鲁棒帕累托前沿（ARPF），通过对假设（包括结构性ε污染）进行重采样，计算每个动作的生存分数ρ，并证明ρ构成决策遗憾的精确因子。

Result: 在受控误设实验中，鲁棒折衷方案相比朴素点值方案将均值和尾部遗憾降低超90%；在受监管的商业决策中，能够在合法行动空间内优化并以欧元量化下行风险；在41,188条真实决策数据的样本外回测中，比记录状态 quo 提升34%，比朴素点值规则提升4%，同时缓解了优化者诅咒。

Conclusion: LLM 是规划表述者（formulator）而非求解器（solver）——给定正确数值和单目标优化的 LLM 与 YUKTI 对比，前者产生的保留遗憾约为 YUKTI 的47倍。此外，在长距离因果耦合场景下，前向交接变得不可靠，需转变为反向归纳的因果策略。

Abstract: Language models turn a worded situation into a numeric plan, and the dominant pipelines (NL4Opt, OptiMUS, ORLM, OR-LLM-Agent) commit to a single objective and point-valued coefficients, then solve once. For decisions that allocate real budget, effort, or clinical attention, that confidence is the failure mode: every objectified number is an assumption, and a plan optimal only if the guesses are exactly right is fragile -- mimicry of computation. YUKTI changes the target of autoformulation. Its representation is a typed-proposition graph whose relationships carry shape priors, coefficient uncertainty, and provenance. YUKTI routes each stage to an exact, nonlinear, or evolutionary solver; couples stages by a distributional Pareto hand-off; and introduces Assumption-Robust Pareto Frontiers (ARPF), resampling assumptions (including structural epsilon-contamination) to score how often each action survives (rho). We prove a bound making rho an exact factor of decision regret, add auditable traceability, and synthesize a benchmark-faithful data foundation when none exists (SRJANA). We validate three ways: under controlled misspecification the robust compromise cuts mean and tail regret by over 90% versus a naive point plan; on a regulated commercial decision we optimize inside a lawful action space and price the downside in euros; and on a real public dataset of 41,188 decisions an out-of-sample backtest beats the logged status quo by 34% and a naive point rule by 4% while reducing the optimizer's curse. The solvers are standard; we claim no benchmark-SOTA win. A head-to-head shows an LLM given the correct numbers, and single-objective optimization, both incur about 47x the held-out regret of YUKTI -- an LLM is a formulator, not a solver. Under long-range causal coupling, the forward hand-off becomes unsound, locating where it must become a backward-induction causal policy.

</details>


### [258] [GES-TSP: Graph Edge Sparsification for TSP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09708)
*Tianfeng Chen,Xianyue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习的图边稀疏化方法(GES)，用于加速大规模欧几里得旅行商问题(TSP)的精确求解。该方法自适应地利用实例几何结构信息进行剪枝，在MATILDA数据集上可剪除95%的边且解间隙保持在1%以内，在TSPLIB上泛化表现优异，部分大规模实例剪枝率超99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模TSP精确求解计算成本高昂，传统图稀疏化方法依赖固定启发式规则，无法充分利用实例特定的结构信息，导致稀疏化效果有限。因此需要一种能自适应捕捉实例几何结构特征的稀疏化方法，在显著压缩图规模的同时保持解质量。

Method: 提出Graph Edge Sparsification (GES)，一种基于学习的稀疏化方法。该方法融合几何结构信息与组合优化技术，针对不同TSP实例自适应生成稀疏化图：通过学习实例的几何特征，预测每条边属于最优解的概率，据此剪除低概率边，从而大幅缩减图规模并加速求解过程。

Result: 在MATILDA数据集上，GES可剪除高达95%的边，解间隙控制在最优值的1%以内。在TSPLIB基准上展现出强泛化能力，部分大规模实例剪枝率超过99%而最优间隙仍低于1%。

Conclusion: GES方法能够有效利用实例几何结构信息进行自适应图稀疏化，在显著降低计算复杂度的同时保持近似最优解质量，对大规模TSP的高效精确求解具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: Solving large-scale instances of the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) exactly is computationally expensive. Researchers often employ graph sparsification methods to improve computational efficiency. Traditional sparsification methods typically rely on fixed heuristics and fail to fully exploit instance-specific structural information. In this paper, we propose Graph Edge Sparsification (GES), a learning-based sparsification approach for Euclidean TSP. By incorporating geometric structural information and combinatorial optimization technology, our proposed method adaptively generates a sparsification graph for different instances, significantly reducing the graph size and accelerating the solving process. Experimental results demonstrate that our sparsification method can prune up to 95% of edges on the MATILDA dataset, while keeping the solution gap within 1% of the optimal value. Moreover, our approach exhibits strong generalization capability on the TSPLIB benchmark.In some large-scale instances, the pruning rate exceeds 99%, while the optimality gap remains below 1%.

</details>


### [259] [The Verifier is the Curriculum: Execution-Gated Self-Distillation for Cross-Family Game Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09709)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用确定性、不可博弈的严格启动过滤器（strict-launch）进行拒绝采样自蒸馏，在GameCraft-Bench上将14B模型的干净代码生成率从8.8%提升至42.2%，证明验证器决定了模型学习的课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习评判器的代码生成后训练方法存在奖励黑客问题——模型会优化代理特征以提高评分，而非真正改善生成质量。本文旨在探索一种确定性、免评判、不可博弈的验证信号，以引导模型真正提升代码功能正确性。

Method: 采用strict-launch（无头引擎下项目是否干净启动）作为确定性、免评判的二元过滤器，在GameCraft-Bench上对Qwen3-14B+LoRA进行拒绝采样自蒸馏。设置对照实验：金标准复制对照组、计数匹配分解实验（分离质量与数量通道）、以及将过滤器替换为宽松的BUILD检查（通过率99.9%）的消融实验。同时使用无头执行接地作为第二个不可博弈信号验证功能增益。

Result: 三轮蒸馏后，四个未见游戏家族的干净生成率从8.8%升至42.2%（p=0.0019），Best-of-K覆盖率从18/25提升至25/25（p<1e-4）。金标准复制组反而退化至5.6%（p=0.019）。分解实验显示质量贡献+8.8pp，数量贡献+8.5pp。替换为BUILD过滤器后增益完全消失（回到基线，p=1e-3）。无头执行接地随轮次单调上升，且接地候选数远超金标准复制组（16 vs. 5）。

Conclusion: 验证器即课程——模型学到的是验证器所认证的内容。严格且不可博弈的验证信号通过拒绝采样自蒸馏可实现显著的外分布泛化增益，效果远超同等规模的金标准数据训练。游戏生成是验证这一原理的可靠测试平台。

Abstract: Post-training a code generator against a learned judge can optimize proxy features that raise the score without improving the artifact. We study the opposite signal: a deterministic, judge-free, ungameable filter -- whether a generated project launches cleanly under a headless engine (strict-launch). Under this gate, rejection-sampling self-distillation compounds out-of-family generalization. On GameCraft-Bench (mapping a natural-language brief to a complete Godot project), a 14B model (Qwen3-14B+LoRA) distilled under strict-launch raises clean generation on four unseen game families from 8.8% to 42.2% per-candidate and best-of-K coverage from 18/25 to 25/25 (the gold ceiling) over three rounds, each a significant gain (p=0.0019, p<1e-4, p<1e-4). The gain is not from merely adding data: an exactly-matched gold-duplication control regresses below the base model (5.6% vs. 8.8%, p=0.019), while a count-matched decomposition splits the round-1-to-2 jump into comparable quality (+8.8pp) and quantity (+8.5pp) channels. Most directly, rerunning the loop with only the filter swapped -- the lenient BUILD check, which passes 99.9% of generations, in place of the launch gate -- erases the gain entirely (back to base, p=1e-3 vs. the launch-gated round), isolating verifier precision rather than the optimizer. A second ungameable signal, headless execution grounding, rises monotonically across rounds and yields far more grounded candidates than gold-duplication at a matched budget (16 vs. 5), confirming the gains are functional, not launch-but-empty. Game generation is a verifiable testbed for one lesson: the verifier is the curriculum -- what it certifies is what the model learns.

</details>


### [260] [Closed-Loop Control with Rule-Aligned Small Language Models and Multi-Agent Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09713)
*Yuchen Wang,Javal Vyas,Tong Liu,Mehmet Mercangoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究将紧凑型小语言模型（SLM）用于工业控制推理，结合数字孪生验证器与迭代重提示代理，在边缘端实现基于自然语言需求的可重构自主控制，实验显示91.5%的动作对齐准确率和95%的物理调节合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业自主化的关键瓶颈在于：大规模云端模型因推理延迟高、不透明及数据敏感性，无法满足边缘闭环控制的实时性与安全性要求。因此需要探索一种紧凑、可嵌入边缘设备、并能在验证器引导下进行闭环纠正的SLM方案。

Method: 采用Qwen2.5-1.5B作为基座模型，通过分组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行对齐微调。系统架构包含三个组件：(1) 动作代理，根据自然语言需求生成控制动作；(2) 符号化/数字孪生验证层，对候选动作进行合规检查；(3) 重提示代理，在动作未通过验证时迭代修正输出，直至产生有效动作。

Result: 在30次随机热控仿真实验（每次500步）中，框架达到91.5%的平均动作对齐准确率（各案例范围86.3%–100%），平均推理延迟3.84秒。在符号重映射机制下，物理量在目标范围内的比率达95%，表明即使token级一致率下降，系统仍能维持鲁棒的物理调节。

Conclusion: SLM+验证器的闭环架构为边缘端可重构自主控制提供了一条可行路径，兼顾了推理效率、物理安全性与自然语言交互的灵活性。

Abstract: A key step toward autonomous industrial operation is the ability to create and reconfigure control policies from natural-language requirement specifications, with minimal or no manual redesign. In this setting, policy generation by AI agents can be a credible path when paired with a plant-aware validator (e.g., a digital twin) that can check generated candidate actions before execution. However, practical deployment is constrained by inference latency and compute footprint: large cloud-based models are often too slow, opaque, or data-sensitive for edge closed-loop use. This work investigates whether a compact Small Language Model (SLM) can be retrained for control reasoning and embedded in a validator-guided correction loop. We use a Qwen2.5-1.5B model aligned via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), combined with (i) an action agent, (ii) a symbolic/digital-twin-style validation layer, and (iii) a reprompting agent that iteratively steers outputs toward valid actions. In randomized thermal-control simulations (30 experiments with 500 steps each), the framework achieves 91.5% average action-alignment accuracy (86.3%--100% across cases) at 3.84\,s mean inference latency. Under symbolic re-mapping, it maintains a 95% in-range rate, indicating robust physical regulation despite reduced token-level agreement. These results support SLM+validator architectures as a practical path toward reconfigurable autonomous control at the edge.

</details>


### [261] [Feedback-Coupled Memory Systems in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09714)
*Stefano Grassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对反馈耦合记忆系统（FCMS）架构中两个未被公理化定义的算子进行了具体实例化：用基于机制智能（MBI）定义代理更新算子，用耦合记忆图过程（CMGP）定义环境更新算子，从而构建了连续时间FCMS实例。推导出的Lyapunov全局耗散性条件$4\beta^2 < 2\eta\mu\gamma^2$统一并推广了离散FCMS和CMGP的稳定性判据，并通过数值仿真验证了稳定性阈值及违反时的自增强协调级联现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始FCMS框架中的代理更新算子$f_i$和环境更新算子$\Psi$仅被公理化假定而未给出具体定义，这限制了该框架在实际系统中的可操作性和理论完备性。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过引入经济原理和物理记忆图过程为两个算子赋予具体形式，从而构建可计算、可验证的闭环协调系统。

Method: （1）代理更新算子$f_i$通过基于机制智能（MBI）定义，采用去中心化价格机制和经济原理实现局部更新；（2）环境更新算子$\Psi$通过耦合记忆图过程（CMGP）定义，将环境视为记录并相干响应轨迹历史的物理基底，构成非马尔可夫框架；（3）推导连续时间FCMS的Lyapunov全局耗散性条件；（4）通过$N=2$代理的数值仿真和$N=10^6$的平均场验证来检验理论阈值。

Result: 推导出连续时间FCMS的Lyapunov全局耗散性阈值$4\beta^2 < 2\eta\mu\gamma^2$，该条件成功推广了离散FCMS稳定性条件$4\eta\beta^2 < \gamma$和CMGP物理分岔阈值$\alpha_c = 1/K$，揭示了"记忆耗散必须超过反馈增益"这一普适的组织原则。数值仿真和平均场验证均确认了该稳定性阈值，并观察到阈值违反时出现的自增强协调级联现象。

Conclusion: 记忆耗散超越反馈增益是跨离散与连续、跨代理与环境尺度的普适组织原则。通过MBI和CMGP对FCMS的实例化，使得原本公理化的框架具备了可计算性，为理解复杂系统中的自组织协调提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: The Feedback-Coupled Memory Systems (FCMS) architecture formalizes closed-loop coordination through four abstract operators, two of which - the agent update operator $f_i$ and the environmental update operator $Ψ$ - are left axiomatically undefined in the original framework. To address this, $f_i$ is defined by Mechanism-Based Intelligence (MBI), where agents update locally through a decentralized price mechanism and economic principles, and $Ψ$ is defined by the Coupled Memory Graph Process (CMGP), a non-Markovian framework where the environment is treated as a physical substrate that records and responds to trajectory history coherently without external forcing. The resulting continuous-time FCMS instantiation achieves Lyapunov global dissipativity governed by the computable threshold $4β^2 < 2ημγ^2$. This generalizes both the discrete FCMS stability condition $4ηβ^2 < γ$ and CMGP's physical bifurcation threshold $α_c = 1/K$, confirming that memory dissipation must outpace feedback gain as a universal organizing principle. Numerical simulation with $N=2$ agents and mean-field validation at $N=10^6$ confirm the stability threshold and the self-reinforcing coordination cascade that emerges when it is violated.

</details>


### [262] [AGM-like Paraconsistent Partial Meet Abductive Expansion Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09729)
*Ulisses Franceschi Eliano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在Pagnucco的AGM式溯因扩张操作和Aliseda的溯因推理分类学基础上，借助新近提出的弗协调逻辑RCbr（一种自外延的逻辑），首次提出了一个弗协调的AGM式溯因扩张操作，能够在吸纳矛盾解释假设时不导致平凡化，并给出了其公设和传递关系型部分交构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典AGM信念修正框架在处理溯因推理时，若遇到矛盾的解释假设，会导致认知状态的平凡化（trivialization），这是一个严重的局限。作者旨在克服这一缺陷，构建一个能够容纳矛盾解释假设而不导致系统崩溃的弗协调溯因扩张操作，填补AGM文献中该方向的空白。

Method: 以Pagnucco(1996)的AGM式溯因扩张操作为基础，结合Aliseda对溯因推理主要成分的分类学框架，利用新近提出的弗协调逻辑RCbr（一种形式不一致性逻辑LFI，具备自外延性即满足替换性质），构建了一个新的弗协调溯因扩张操作，给出了完整的公设体系以及传递关系型部分交（transitively relational partial meet）构造。

Result: 成功构建了首个弗协调的AGM式溯因扩张操作（属于新系统AGMpabd），该操作能在不导致平凡化的前提下吸纳矛盾解释假设。然而，该操作尚未将弗协调的否定算子和一致性算子赋予相关认知角色——这一增强留待后续第二个系统AGMcircabd来完成。

Conclusion: 本文提出的弗协调溯因扩张操作是AGM文献中同类首创，其形式化得以实现的关键在于RCbr逻辑的自外延性。该工作为弗协调信念修正与溯因推理的交叉研究开辟了新路径，但弗协调算子的认知角色整合仍有待进一步工作。

Abstract: In his 1996 doctoral thesis, Maurice Pagnucco created the first AGM-like abductive expansion operation. Taking his operation as a basis, as well as a taxonomy -- inspired by Atocha Aliseda -- responsible for highlighting and formalizing the main components of abductive reasoning, the main aim of this paper is to present a new paraconsistent AGM-like abductive expansion operation -- capable of assimilating contradictory explanatory hypotheses without trivialization and the consequent absurd epistemic state -- with its postulates and its transitively relational partial meet construction. To a large extent, the formal development presented in this paper was only made possible by the recent creation of the paraconsistent logic RCbr, an LFI (Logics of Formal Inconsistencies) that establishes properties especially relevant to belief revision contexts, in particular, the ability to be self-extensional -- i.e., to satisfy the replacement property. This is the first of two papers: the paraconsistent abductive expansion operation announced here -- which is part of a new system called AGMpabd -- despite bringing many interesting features, does not assign any relevant epistemic role to the paraconsistent operators of negation and consistency. Only in a second paper will an analogous paraconsistent abductive expansion operation -- which is part of another new system, AGMcircabd -- be enhanced in this direction. Nevertheless, to the best of my knowledge, the operation developed in this paper is the first of its kind in the AGM literature.

</details>


### [263] [Coresets Before Score Sets: Evaluation-Unsupervised Prompt Subset Selection for LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09739)
*Jihan Yao,Gantavya Bhatt,Arnav Das,Peter Jin,Ke Bao,Qiaolin Yu,Khushi Bhardwaj,Chang Su,Jialei Wang,Yikai Zhu,Sugam Devare,Damon Mosk-Aoyama,Zhen Dong,Venkat Krishna Srinivasan,Yineng Zhang,Oleksii Kuchaiev,Jiantao Jiao,Banghua Zhu,Jeff Bilmes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM基准测试核心集选择问题：从多个基准中选出少量提示子集，使其产生的模型得分和排名逼近全量基准的结果。作者提出评估无关的设置，利用次模函数（如设施选址函数）仅基于语义嵌入进行选择，在35个基准、18个大模型、61K+提示的大规模实验中，设施选址函数优于12种基线方法，且在有监督场景下也能匹敌或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估通常需要在大规模、异构的基准测试套件上运行，计算成本极高。现有基准压缩方法要么依赖大量模型评估结果（有监督），要么仅在单个基准内进行，缺乏跨基准的细粒度提示级选择方法。本文旨在提出一种评估无关的、细粒度的基准核心集选择方法，大幅降低评估成本的同时保持排名保真度。

Method: 提出基于次模函数的基准核心集选择框架，在评估无关（evaluation-unsupervised）设置下，仅使用提示的语义嵌入进行子集选择，无需任何模型评估结果。具体探索了多种次模函数：行列式点过程（DPP）、次模互信息函数、设施选址（Facility Location）函数等。核心发现是设施选址函数配合廉价语义嵌入效果最佳。方法支持两种粒度：跨基准提示级选择（细粒度）和整基准选择（粗粒度，有监督场景）。

Result: 在包含35个异构基准（覆盖5种能力类别）、18个前沿LLM、超61K提示的大规模实验套件上，基于语义嵌入的设施选址函数在多种核心集预算下，对LLM得分的保留能力优于12种基于分数和多样性的基线方法。在有监督的整基准选择场景下，同一目标在MMLU和MTEB排行榜上匹配或超越当前最优基线，且计算成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 次模性是基准压缩的一个强有力且可靠的工具。设施选址函数配合语义嵌入在评估无关场景下表现优异，且该方法可泛化至有监督的整基准选择场景。这为高效、低成本的LLM评估提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: We study LLM benchmark coreset selection: selecting a small subset of prompts over multiple benchmarks whose induced model scores and rankings approximate those obtained from the full benchmark suite. In evaluation-unsupervised benchmark coreset selection (our approach), the selection algorithm uses no model evaluation outcomes, and operates on a fine granularity by producing subsets of prompts over multiple benchmarks rather than producing a sub-collection of entire benchmarks. We use submodular subset selection, and we develop and evaluate many different submodular functions for this purpose, including determinantal point process (DPP) based approaches, submodular mutual information functions, and facility location-based functions. On a new large-scale suite of 35 heterogeneous benchmarks spanning five different capability categories, 18 frontier LLMs, and over 61K prompts, we find that the facility location (FL) function operating exclusively on inexpensive semantic prompt embeddings preserves LLM scores better than twelve separate score-based and diversity-based baselines, across a range of coreset budgets. Moreover, we show our proposed objective is not limited to the evaluation-unsupervised regime: in the setting where only a handful of whole benchmarks must be selected and a large amount of model scores are available, the same objective matches or outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on the MMLU and MTEB leaderboards, while being substantially cheaper to compute. Together, our results suggest that submodularity, in general, is a strong and reliable tool for benchmark compression.

</details>


### [264] [A Dynamic Scene Interaction Reasoning Framework for Scene-level Lane-Change Intention and Trajectory Prediction of Multiple Interacting Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09740)
*Joshua Kofi Asamoah,Blessing Agyei Kyem,Eugene Denteh,Armstrong Aboah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出动态场景图注意力框架（DSiGAT），通过时序图注意力消息传递同时预测交通场景中所有车辆的换道意图和未来轨迹，并在NGSIM和highD数据集上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有换道预测方法多聚焦于单一目标车辆，而多智能体预测方法仅输出未来位置，缺乏显式的机动意图信息。需要一种能对整个局部交通场景中每辆车的换道意图与轨迹进行联合预测的方法。

Method: 将场景建模为时变交互图，车辆为节点，时空与运动学关系为显式边特征。通过时序图注意力消息传递捕捉演化中的车辆间依赖与预机动线索。采用意图引导解码器将预测的机动动作与对应未来运动关联，并引入场景级一致性目标函数以鼓励多车未来轨迹的协调性。

Result: 在NGSIM I-80和US-101上换道意图预测准确率分别达90.12%和90.97%；轨迹RMSE相比最强基线降低最多52.94%；同时具有更低的智能体间碰撞率和联合位移误差，表明场景级预测更一致。

Conclusion: DSiGAT通过动态图注意力与意图引导解码，有效实现了多车换道意图与轨迹的联合预测，场景级一致性约束进一步提升了预测的协调性与准确性，消融和鲁棒性分析验证了各组件的贡献。

Abstract: Safe motion planning in advanced driver-assistance systems and autonomous vehicles requires an accurate understanding of how the surrounding traffic scene is likely to evolve. However, many existing lane-change prediction methods remain centered on a single target vehicle, while multi-agent forecasting approaches often describe scene evolution only through future positions and provide limited explicit information about the maneuver associated with each vehicle. This study proposes a dynamic scene graph attention framework that predicts the lane-change intention and future trajectory of every relevant vehicle within a local traffic scene. The scene is represented as a time-varying interaction graph in which vehicles are modeled as nodes and their spatial and kinematic relationships are encoded through explicit edge features. Temporal graph-attention message passing captures evolving inter-vehicle dependencies and pre-maneuver cues, while an intention-guided decoder links each predicted maneuver to its corresponding future motion. A scene-level consistency objective further encourages compatible multi-vehicle futures. Experiments on the NGSIM I-80, NGSIM US-101, and highD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines. DSiGAT achieves intention prediction accuracies of 90.12% and 90.97% on NGSIM I-80 and US-101, respectively, and reduces trajectory RMSE by up to 52.94% relative to the strongest baseline. It also produces lower inter-agent collision rates and joint displacement errors, indicating more coherent scene-level predictions. Ablation, sensitivity, robustness, and qualitative analyses further validate the contribution of the proposed components and the effectiveness of the scene-focused formulation.

</details>


### [265] [Scaffolding the Strategist: Architecture-Dependent Reasoning Interventions in Hotelling Spatial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09743)
*Pratyush Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过Hotelling线性城市模型，检验了结构化推理干预对大语言模型战略经济推理的影响，发现干预效果与模型架构存在显著的交叉交互：承诺式脚手架提升标准模型却损害推理模型，而原则性分离则呈现相反模式。此外，对抗性压力测试对推理模型的损害更大，且损害程度与基线难度负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在战略经济推理方面的能力尚不明确，尤其缺乏关于结构化推理干预（脚手架）是否有效、以及其效果如何依赖模型架构的系统性研究。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探讨不同推理干预对不同类型模型的影响差异。

Method: 以Hotelling线性城市模型为诊断工具，比较GPT-4.1-mini（标准指令遵循模型）与GPT-5-mini（推理优化模型）在五种条件下的表现：无脚手架基线及四种推理干预。实验涵盖8个问题（跨演绎与溯因推理）、3种提示框架、每种条件3次重复，共产生720个独立评分响应。统计方法采用t检验、效应量计算及回归分析。

Result: (1) 脚手架类型与模型架构之间存在显著交叉交互（t(7)=4.79, p=0.002, d=1.69）：承诺式脚手架提升标准模型（+0.21）但损害推理模型（-0.63）；原则性分离则相反（-0.40 vs. +0.31）。两个交叉点均显著（承诺：p=0.040；分离：p=0.002），且在所有8个问题中7/8方向一致。(2) 对抗性压力测试对两者均有损害，但推理模型受损程度是标准模型的2.6倍（-1.47 vs. -0.57; p=0.038），损害程度与基线难度负相关（R²=0.36, p=0.014）。(3) 存在持续的陈述性-程序性知识缺口：两模型识别正确策略的比率远超其执行能力；原则性分离可完全弥合推理模型的这一缺口，但没有干预措施能帮助标准模型弥合。

Conclusion: 结构化推理干预的效果高度依赖于模型架构，不存在一刀切的脚手架策略：对标准模型有益的方法可能损害推理优化模型，反之亦然。对抗性压力测试对推理模型尤其有害，且简单问题更易被干扰。陈述性-程序性缺口在标准模型中更为顽固，提示架构差异深刻影响模型将知识转化为行动的能力。这对LLM在经济学等战略推理领域的应用部署具有重要启示。

Abstract: We investigate whether structured reasoning interventions improve the strategic economic reasoning of large language models, and whether their effects depend on model architecture. Using Hotelling's linear city model as a diagnostic vehicle, we evaluate GPT-4.1-mini (a standard instruction-following model) and GPT-5-mini (a reasoning-optimized model) under five conditions - an unscaffolded baseline and four reasoning interventions - across eight questions spanning deductive and abductive reasoning, three prompt framings, and three repetitions per condition, yielding 720 individually judged responses. We find a statistically significant crossover interaction between scaffolding type and model architecture ($t(7) = 4.79$, $p = 0.002$, $d = 1.69$): commitment scaffolding improves the standard model ($+0.21$) while degrading the reasoning model ($-0.63$), and principled separation shows the opposite pattern ($-0.40$ vs. $+0.31$). Both crossovers are individually significant (commitment: $p = 0.040$; separation: $p = 0.002$) and hold across all eight questions with 7/8 directional consistency. Adversarial stress-testing harms both models, with $2.6\times$ greater degradation for the reasoning model ($-1.47$ vs. $-0.57$; $p = 0.038$), and the damage correlates negatively with baseline difficulty ($R^2 = 0.36$, $p = 0.014$). We further document a persistent declarative-procedural gap in which both models identify correct strategies at rates far exceeding their ability to execute them; separation fully closes this gap for the reasoning model while no intervention helps the standard model.

</details>


### [266] [A Theory of Least Autonomy in AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09744)
*Christophe Parisel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出最小权限原则不足以应对智能体AI系统，提出了"最小自治"的泛化概念，并构建了基于组合爆炸半径、有向智能体影响图以及合谋谓词的形式化理论，以量化和约束AI智能体在跨工作流和系统边界的权限组合与放大行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最小权限原则假设身份仅持有权限，但智能体AI系统可以主动组合、审批和放大权限，跨越工作流和系统边界，因此需要一种超越静态权限控制的新的安全原则和形式化框架。

Method: 1. 定义组合爆炸半径 d(a,b)，结合超度量树与格值机密性、完整性和控制上下文标签，度量企业层级结构中动作之间的结构分离程度；2. 构建有向智能体影响图 G(θ)，弧的存在需满足有向共享资源写-读会合或保守无向智能体间通信会合，且会合条件下的影响潜力不低于外部策略阈值 θ，并通过目录-半径概要支持 θ 的校准与审计；3. 基于图可达性定义合谋谓词，用于检测授权组合、决策操纵和跨域能力组合。

Result: 论文提出了一个完整的形式化理论框架，包括组合爆炸半径、有向影响图和合谋检测谓词，并通过目录-半径概要支持策略阈值的校准与审计，从理论上实现了对智能体AI系统权限组合与放大的量化分析与约束。

Conclusion: 最小权限原则不足以约束智能体AI系统；最小自治作为其泛化原则，配合本文的形式化理论（组合爆炸半径、影响图、合谋谓词），能够有效量化和检测智能体系统中的权限组合、决策操纵和跨域能力组合，为智能体AI的访问控制提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Least privilege, the principle that an identity should hold only the permissions strictly required for its task, has been a foundational primitive of access control for decades. We argue that this principle is insufficient for agentic AI systems, which do not merely hold permissions but can combine, approve, and amplify them across workflows and system boundaries.
  We propose least autonomy as an appropriate generalization and develop a formal theory. First, we define a compositional blast radius d(a,b) that measures structural separation between actions in an enterprise hierarchy, combining an ultrametric tree with lattice-valued confidentiality, integrity, and control-context labels. Second, we define a directed agent influence graph G(theta). An arc from U to V requires a directed shared-resource write-to-read meeting or a conservative undirected agent-to-agent (A2A) communication meeting, and a meeting-conditioned influence potential at or above an externally selected policy threshold theta. A catalogue-radius profile supports calibration and audit of theta. Finally, we define a collusion predicate over graph reachability that detects authorization composition, decision manipulation, and cross-domain capability composition.

</details>


### [267] [SupplyNetPy: An Open-Source Python Library for High-Fidelity Modeling and Simulation of Arbitrary Supply Chain and Inventory Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09745)
*Tushar Lone,Neha Karanjkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SupplyNetPy 是一个开源的 Python 库，用于任意多级供应链网络的建模与离散事件仿真，支持多种补货策略、易腐库存、节点中断及随机需求/提前期，并通过图结构描述供应链，提供日志和性能报告，旨在支持设计空间探索、假设分析、训练数据生成及数字孪生等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有供应链仿真工具在可编程性、可扩展性和开源生态方面存在不足，难以支持复杂模型的程序化生成与大规模仿真。SupplyNetPy 的核心动机是提供一个可编程生成和仿真复杂供应链模型的框架，从而赋能设计空间探索、假设分析、训练数据生成以及供应链数字孪生的构建。

Method: SupplyNetPy 采用基于图的供应链网络建模方法，用户通过定义节点和边的属性来描述任意多级结构。库内部实现离散事件仿真引擎，支持多种补货策略、易腐库存建模、节点中断事件、随机需求与随机提前期。所有组件通过继承机制实现可扩展性。仿真输出包括详细日志以及节点级和网络级的性能报告。

Result: 该库通过了三类验证：(1) 与解析基准的对比验证；(2) 与商业仿真工具的对比验证；(3) 已发表案例研究的复现验证，结果表明 SupplyNetPy 的仿真精度和可靠性得到充分证实。

Conclusion: SupplyNetPy 作为一个开源、文档完善且高度可扩展的供应链仿真库，填补了程序化供应链建模与仿真领域的空白，为研究人员和从业者提供了强大的设计空间探索和数字孪生构建能力。

Abstract: This paper introduces SupplyNetPy, an open-source, well-documented Python library for modeling and discrete-event simulation of supply chain networks with arbitrary multi-echelon structures. It supports multiple replenishment policies, perishable inventory, node disruptions, and stochastic demand and lead times. All components are extensible via inheritance. Users describe a supply chain as a graph with node and link attributes, while the library handles simulation, providing logs and extensive node and network level performance reports. This paper presents the motivation, design, key features, and architecture of SupplyNetPy, along with detailed validation results (against analytical benchmarks, a commercial tool, and a published case study). A key motivation behind SupplyNetPy's development is programmatic generation and simulation of complex models, enabling design-space exploration, what-if analysis, training data generation, and supply chain digital twins.

</details>


### [268] [Replicating Belief, Not Bits: Epistemic State Replication for Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09748)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 传统的状态机复制要求副本在比特级别完全一致，但随机性、模型驱动的智能体系统即便推理路径不同也能达成语义等价的正确决策。本文提出认知状态复制（ESR），将复制边界从"比特一致"转向"信念一致"，形式化地定义了认知节点状态、语义线性一致性、有界最终语义相干等概念，并给出了协议与初步仿真结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典状态机复制（SMR）依赖确定性执行实现比特级状态一致。但在以自主、随机和模型驱动的智能体分布式系统中，副本采用生成式模型时可能产生发散的推理路径、摘要和令牌边界，却仍能达到语义等价的正确操作决策。强制比特级一致反而会降低执行灵活性、引发上下文遗忘并限制性能，因此需要一种新的复制范式来适应这种语义等价但比特不一致的场景。

Method: 提出认知状态复制（ESR），核心理念是副本间应就"信念"而非"比特"达成一致。具体包括：1）将认知节点状态形式化为二元组 K=(L, B)，其中 L 为确定性、不可变的证据日志，B 为随机、演化的信念谱系；2）定义语义线性一致性，要求操作在验证器界定的语义兼容度量范围内反映最新已提交的操作语义；3）定义有界最终语义相干，在公平投递、单调证据、有界验证器扰动及收缩嫁接算子条件下，为预期语义发散提供上界；4）设计基于结构化认知增量（epistemic deltas）的洞察传播协议；5）形式化可验证语义回滚机制，在不引发上下文遗忘的前提下从信念谱系中剪除错误前提。

Result: 在所述假设下，原型的初步仿真结果表明 ESR 具备可行性，并能在二次认知故障方面展现出显著减少。

Conclusion: 在智能体分布式系统中，以"信念一致"替代"比特一致"的 ESR 范式是可行且有前景的方向，能够在保持正确性与语义相干性的同时，避免因强制比特一致带来的灵活性损失与上下文遗忘问题。

Abstract: In distributed systems, the classical State Machine Replication (SMR) model assumes that correct replicas execute deterministic transitions to yield identical bitwise states. However, the rise of agentic distributed systems -- where autonomous, stochastic, and model-driven agents orchestrate infrastructure -- presents scenarios where deterministic, bitwise replication is insufficient. Replicas operating with generative models may exhibit divergent reasoning paths, summaries, and token boundaries, yet reach semantically equivalent and correct operational decisions. Forcing bitwise agreement across these stochastic participants degrades execution flexibility, induces context amnesia, and limits performance.
  We argue that in such settings replicas should agree on belief, not bits. We propose Epistemic State Replication (ESR), a belief-replication layer for agentic distributed systems that shifts the replication boundary from data visibility to knowledge visibility. We formalize the epistemic node state as a pair K = (L, B) separating the deterministic, immutable evidence log (L) from the stochastic, evolving belief lineage (B). To govern execution safety, we define Semantic Linearizability, which requires operations to reflect the latest committed operational meaning within a verifier-bounded semantic compatibility metric, and Bounded Eventual Coherence, which bounds expected semantic divergence under fair delivery, monotonic evidence, bounded verifier disturbance, and a contractive graft operator. We outline protocols for propagating derived insights using structured epistemic deltas, and formalize Verifiable Semantic Rollbacks to prune faulty premises from belief lineages without inducing context amnesia. We prototype ESR and report preliminary simulation results that show feasibility under the stated assumptions and illustrate reductions in secondary cognitive faults.

</details>


### [269] [Incremental Transformer for Surrogate-Based Inverse Design of Geopolymer Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10896)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione,Filippo Grassia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对小数据、异质混合变量且受物理关系约束的工程逆向设计难题，提出了一种拓扑感知的增量Transformer（INCRT）代理框架，用于物理约束下的地质聚合物配合比逆向设计。该框架结合了本征维度分析、混合变量设计空间表示、表格代理预测、INCRT流形合理化及约束逆向优化，在公开的地聚合物混凝土数据集上验证了其有效性——能在满足强度目标的同时兼顾碳减排、物理可行性和数据流形支持度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程信息学中，当观测数据量小、变量异质混合且受物理关系约束时，传统逆向设计面临严峻挑战：现有的纯数据驱动方法可能生成物理上不可行或偏离数据支撑的候选方案，而仅依赖物理约束又难以保证解的合理性。因此需要一种能同时利用数据流形结构与物理约束的代理框架。

Method: 提出INCRT拓扑感知代理框架，包含五个核心模块：(1) 本征维度分析，揭示高维设计空间的冗余性；(2) 混合变量设计空间表示；(3) 表格代理预测（非线性模型预测抗压强度，正则化线性模型预测碳排放）；(4) INCRT流形合理化层，提供原型配合比方案（prototype regimes）和流形支持度评分；(5) 对比三种逆向优化策略——无约束代理优化、物理约束优化、拓扑感知物理约束优化。

Result: 在粉煤灰-矿渣基地质聚合物混凝土公开数据集上：(1) 高维设计空间存在强冗余，可约化为少数有效配合比机制；(2) 抗压强度需非线性表格代理模型，碳排放主要由组分决定、可用正则化线性模型良好恢复；(3) INCRT作为合理化层（而非替代表格预测器）有效；(4) 无约束优化可匹配目标强度但可能产生物理无效或脱离流形的候选；(5) 仅物理约束不能保证数据支撑；(6) 拓扑感知策略在目标达标、碳减排、物理可接受性和流形邻近度之间实现最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 所提框架并非替代实验验证，而是为从小规模、混合型、物理受限的工程数据集中筛选可信候选配合比提供有力支撑。INCRT作为流形合理化层与表格代理预测器协同工作，拓扑感知物理约束优化在三类策略中综合表现最优。

Abstract: Small-data inverse design is challenging in engineering informatics when observations are heterogeneous, mixed-type, and constrained by physical relations among design variables. This work proposes a topology-aware surrogate framework guided by an Incremental Transformer (INCRT) for physics-constrained inverse design, applied to geopolymer mixture design. The method integrates intrinsic-dimensionality analysis, mixed-variable design-space representation, tabular surrogate prediction, INCRT-based manifold rationalisation, and constrained inverse optimisation. Using a public benchmark of fly-ash and slag-based geopolymer concrete mixtures with compressive-strength and carbon-emission targets, the high-dimensional design space proves strongly redundant, organising around fewer effective mixture regimes. Compressive strength requires nonlinear tabular surrogates, while carbon emission is largely determined by composition and well recovered by regularised linear models. INCRT thus acts not as a replacement for tabular predictors but as a rationalisation layer providing prototype regimes and a manifold-support score for inverse design. Three strategies are compared: unconstrained surrogate optimisation, physics-constrained optimisation, and topology-aware physics-constrained optimisation. Unconstrained optimisation can match target strength but may yield physically invalid or off-manifold candidates; physics-only constraints do not always ensure data support. The topology-aware strategy yields candidates balancing target compliance, carbon reduction, physical admissibility, and proximity to the learned feasible manifold. The framework aims not to replace experimental validation but to support screening of credible candidate mixtures from small, mixed, physically constrained engineering datasets.

</details>


### [270] [Task-Conditioned Synthetic Data Generation for Improving Machine Learning Performance in Agricultural Prediction Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09751)
*Hamid Ebrahimy,Moritz Lucas,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种任务条件合成数据生成算法TCSDG，结合贝叶斯网络与表格基础模型TabICL，在作物产量预测和作物类型分类任务中，于89%和74%的实验里提升了机器学习性能，显著优于六种基准SDG算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在农业变量估算中的表现受限于参考数据的数量与质量，而现有合成数据生成方法在表格数据上的效果有限，需要一种更有效的合成数据方法来解决数据稀缺问题。

Method: 提出TCSDG算法，将贝叶斯网络生成器与基于Transformer的表格基础模型TabICL配对，采用教师-学生知识迁移和上下文学习范式，针对下游预测任务进行任务条件化的合成数据生成。在12个研究站点、两种训练数据比例、四种倍增比和三种预测ML算法下进行系统评估。

Result: TCSDG在89%的作物类型分类实验和74%的作物产量预测实验中提升了ML性能；在聚合层面是唯一在两个任务上均一致改善性能的方法，且显著优于六种基准SDG算法。

Conclusion: 精心设计和处理的合成数据能够提升精准农业中ML预测的性能，TCSDG提供了一个实用且可扩展的合成数据生成框架，代码已开源。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) algorithms have been widely used to estimate agricultural variables across diverse contexts. However, because the quantity and quality of training data strongly influence performance of ML algorithms, their use can be constrained by limited or incomplete reference data. Synthetic Data Generation (SDG) offers a practical approach to address this issue by producing artificial but realistic samples that preserve key characteristics of the original data. Building on teacher-student knowledge transfer and in-context learning for tabular data, this study proposes a Task-Conditioned SDG (TCSDG) algorithm that pairs a Bayesian Network generator with a transformer-based tabular foundation model (TabICL). The proposed algorithm was evaluated on two agricultural prediction tasks: crop yield prediction and crop type classification. Six benchmark SDG algorithms were also utilized to compare their performance with that of TCSDG. Across twelve study sites, two training-data fractions, four multiplication ratios, and three predictive ML algorithms, augmenting the original data with TCSDG-generated synthetic data improved ML performance in 89% of the crop type classification experiments and 74% of the crop yield prediction experiments. TCSDG also substantially outperformed benchmark SDG algorithms and was the only method to consistently improve ML performance across both tasks at the aggregate level. The study demonstrates that carefully designed and processed synthetic data can improve ML performance in precision-agriculture applications. TCSDG offers a practical and extensible framework for generating synthetic data that supports downstream ML agricultural prediction. The full implementation of TCSDG is publicly available as open source at https://github.com/HamidEbrahimy/TCSDG.

</details>


### [271] [LegalFarePlan: A Label-Setting Framework for Fare-Transparent Urban Rail Route Planning under Non-Additive Fare Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09755)
*Tanghui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 LegalFarePlan，一个票价透明的路径规划框架，通过将合法的中途出站再进站操作建模为显式可审计的约束，在给定的额外时间预算内计算可解释的分段付费行程方案，以实现非累加式票价系统下的费用优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市轨道交通票价系统可能存在非累加性：单次付费行程的票价可能不等于多段合法分段行程的票价之和。现有的路径规划器通常忽略这种合法的出站再进站（exit-and-reentry）操作，导致无法为乘客提供票价最优的行程方案。因此，需要一种能够显式建模并审计此类操作的路径规划框架。

Method: 论文提出 LegalFarePlan 框架，将合法出站再进站操作建模为显式可审计约束。输入包括公交网络、票价函数、换乘规则、站点级出站/再进站成本、额外时间预算和分段限制。实现了多种求解方法：Dijkstra 最短时间和直接路径规划基线、贪心分段启发式算法、有界精确标签设定算法（bounded exact label-setting）以及帕累托前沿搜索。

Result: 在57个站点、360对OD的半合成基准测试中，有界精确搜索在45分钟额外时间预算下，对71.11%的OD对识别出正面的建模票价节省，平均节省3.78个合成票价单位，最大节省9.0个合成票价单位。

Conclusion: 结果展示了方法的行为特征和可复现性，但并非针对港铁或任何实际运营商的实证结论。该框架为票价透明的路径规划提供了一种可审计的解决方案。

Abstract: Urban rail fare systems may be non-additive: the fare of a single paid journey from an origin to a destination can differ from the sum of fares over multiple legally separated journey legs. This paper presents LegalFarePlan, a fare-transparent route-planning framework that models legal exit-and-reentry operations as explicit, auditable constraints. Given a transit network, fare function, transfer rules, station-level exit/re-entry costs, an extra-time budget, and a split limit, the planner computes explainable route plans over paid journey segments. The artifact implements Dijkstra shortest-time and direct route-planner baselines, a greedy split heuristic, bounded exact label-setting, and Pareto-frontier search. Evaluation uses controlled synthetic data and a 57-station semi-synthetic benchmark with 360 OD pairs. On the semi-synthetic benchmark, bounded exact search identifies positive modeled fare reductions for 71.11% of OD pairs, with mean reduction 3.78 and maximum reduction 9.0 synthetic fare units under a 45-minute extra-time budget. These results demonstrate method behavior and reproducibility; they are not empirical conclusions about MTR or any transit operator.

</details>


### [272] [BatteryLake: Agentic, Physics-Grounded Curation of Heterogeneous Battery Aging Data and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09762)
*Tianwen Zhu,Hao Wang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BatteryLake 是一个受管控的数据湖仓平台，通过基于物理基础的智能体策展框架，将原始公共电池老化数据集转化为基准就绪的资产，并发布了涵盖 41 个数据集的开放基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前公共电池老化数据集格式不一致、模式不清晰、元数据分散，策展工作依赖人工且难以复现，而通用数据集成工具又缺乏对电化学时间序列数据领域语义的理解。

Method: 提出一个三部分的框架：(1) 使用 LLM 智能体提取元数据并合成数据集专用转换器，每个输出均以原文证据为基础，无证据时主动弃权；(2) 引入人在回路机制，将验证建模为选择性预测，并通过 26 条模式、统计和物理合理性规则把关数据准入；(3) 发布涵盖 25 个以上机构的 41 个数据集的开放基准。

Result: 构建了标准化的 SOH 与 RUL 任务基准，包含三种数据划分协议和八个基线模型家族，平台、基准和策展协议均已公开发布。

Conclusion: BatteryLake 通过智能体驱动的领域感知策展，实现了电池老化数据的可扩展治理，为电池健康管理研究提供了可复现的标准化数据基础设施。

Abstract: Public battery aging datasets are a critical asset for advanced health management, but their practical use is often limited by inconsistent formats, unclear schemas, and metadata scattered across repositories and publications. Current curation remains largely manual and hard to reproduce, while general-purpose data integration tools miss the domain-specific semantics of electrochemical time-series data. We present BatteryLake, a governed data lakehouse that turns raw public battery data into benchmark-ready assets through an agentic, physics-grounded curation framework, with three contributions. First, LLM agents extract metadata and synthesize dataset-specific converters, grounding every output in verbatim evidence and abstaining when none supports a value. Second, a human-in-the-loop mechanism frames verification as selective prediction and gates admitted data through 26 schema, statistical, and physical-plausibility rules. Third, we release an open benchmark of 41 datasets from over 25 institutions, with standardized SOH and RUL tasks, three split protocols, and eight baseline model families. The platform, benchmark, and curation protocol are publicly available at https://tianwen1209.github.io/batterylake/.

</details>


### [273] [How Much Does Correctness Cost? Budgeted Placement of Strong Correctors in a Weak Multi-Agent Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09765)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在不可靠智能体群中，如何通过少量昂贵的"预言机"校正器以最小成本达成正确共识。将问题建模为图上共识，每个预言机以成本耦合的凹强度将节点拉向真相，以相干性 H(R)=tr M(R)^{-1} 衡量质量。证明了 H 保持子模性，贪心算法可达 1-1/e 近似最优；导出了预算-正确性前沿 B*(ε)；发现成本-质量曲率决定策略：数学验证任务呈凹性（分散部署），代码追踪任务呈凸性（集中部署），具有任务依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式共识系统中，存在大量廉价但不可靠的智能体，需要引入少量昂贵且强力的"预言机"校正器来引导正确共识。核心问题是如何在有限预算下最优地分配这些预言机（数量、强度、位置），以最大化共识质量，这一问题此前缺乏系统的理论分析。

Method: 将问题形式化为图上的共识模型：每个预言机将图中一个节点"钉"向真相方向，其强度与成本呈凹函数关系。共识质量用相干性度量 H(R) = tr(M(R)^{-1})，其中 M(R) 为信息矩阵。分析 H 的子模性质，利用贪心算法进行预算约束下的最优放置；通过反转预算约束导出预算-正确性前沿 B*(ε)，在完全图上给出闭式解；在 Qwen3 (0.6-32B) 模型系列上实测数学验证和代码追踪任务，观察成本-质量曲率的凹凸性。

Result: 1）H 函数即使在预言机强度不同的情况下仍保持子模性，贪心算法实现 (1-1/e) 近似比；2）导出预算-正确性前沿 B*(ε)，完全图上有闭式解，同成本预言机时存在最小数量 k*；3）成本-质量律的曲率决定部署策略：凹性（回报递减）适合分散部署多个中等预言机，凸性（回报递增）适合集中部署少数强预言机；4）Qwen3 实测显示数学验证任务呈凹性，代码追踪任务呈凸性，判决具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: 预算约束下的预言机最优放置问题可通过子模优化的贪心算法高效近似求解。部署策略（集中 vs 分散）取决于具体任务的成本-质量曲率，不存在普适最优方案，需根据任务类型选择。该框架为实际分布式系统中校正资源的配置提供了理论指导。

Abstract: A cheap swarm of unreliable agents can be steered to a correct consensus by a few strong, expensive "oracle" correctors. We ask how much one must spend, and where to place the oracles. We model the swarm as a consensus on a graph in which each oracle pins one node toward the truth at a cost-coupled, concave strength, and measure quality by the coherence H(R)=tr M(R)^{-1}. Our first result is that H stays submodular (each added oracle helps less than the last) even when the oracles differ in strength, so a cost-benefit greedy comes within 1-1/e of the best placement at any budget. Inverting the budget gives the budget-correctness frontier B*(eps), the least spend that guarantees an eps-correct consensus: closed-form on the complete graph, and a minimal oracle count k* when oracles cost the same. Whether a budget then buys a few strong oracles or many medium onese curvature of the cost-quality law: diminishing returns favour spreadsharply increasion. Measured onthe Qwen3 ladder (0.6-32B), the law is concave for math verificatio convex foremergent code tracing, so the verdict is genuinely task-dependent.https://github.com/YehudaItkin/budgeted-oracle-placemen

</details>


### [274] [Norm Enforcement for AI Agents: Robustly Shaping Behavior in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09766)
*Yaowen Ye,Jacob Steinhardt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究语言模型智能体的规范执行机制，发现简单机制易被恶意利用，提出基于可靠性估计和累进惩罚的鲁棒机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体竞争环境中，智能体可能为个体利益损害集体福祉（如为争夺社交媒体参与度发布误导内容）。人类社会通过规范和执行机制来解决此类问题，但现有的简单执行机制用于AI智能体时，可能被未对齐智能体利用获取竞争优势，因此需要设计更鲁棒的规范执行机制。

Method: 在三个模拟环境中，针对多种智能体群体，研究规范执行机制。发现简单机制易被利用后，提出两个关键设计原则：(1) 随时间推移估计每个智能体的可靠性；(2) 对重复不当行为施加累进惩罚，以此更新可靠性估计。将基于这些原则的机制与基线方法进行对比实验。

Result: 简单执行机制即使在没有明确训练或提示的情况下，也会被未对齐智能体利用以获取竞争优势。基于可靠性估计和累进惩罚原则设计的机制能够抵御利用，同时在惩罚规范违反方面，成本与基线相当或更低。

Conclusion: 规范执行机制可以作为塑造智能体行为的可扩展杠杆，但前提是设计时必须预见到这些机制本身会成为其所治理系统的一部分，即需要具备反身性设计思维。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed in shared environments where they pursue diverse goals and compete for rewards. This multi-agent competition can lead to behaviors that serve individual gains at collective cost -- for instance, marketing agents may post misleading content as a result of competing for engagement on social media. Human societies address such problems through norms that constrain acceptable behavior, supported by enforcement mechanisms that detect and penalize violations. Motivated by this, we study norm enforcement mechanisms for language model agents. We find that simple enforcement mechanisms are exploited by misaligned agents for competitive advantage, even when they are not explicitly trained or prompted to do so. We thus turn our attention to designing more robust mechanisms, and identify two key ingredients: estimating each agent's reliability over time, and updating this estimate with escalating penalties for repeated misbehavior. Across three simulated environments and a variety of agent populations, mechanisms built on these principles resist exploitation, while still penalizing norm violations at comparable or lower cost than baselines. Our results position norm enforcement mechanisms as scalable levers for shaping agents' behavior, but only when designed to anticipate becoming part of the system they govern. Our code and data are available at https://yaowenye.com/norm-enforcement.

</details>


### [275] [Verification of Adaptive Agentic Controllers through Finite Rule Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09770)
*Roberto Garrone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对工业自适应代理AI系统从原型到生产的验证鸿沟，提出一种有界验证协议：将控制器表示为有限符号规则、诊断谓词、解释日志和留出重评估，在不依赖无限制人工干预的条件下，检测、局部修复或拒绝特定控制器故障。在库存控制基准上验证了三种结果，贡献在于提供了一套仿真兼容的验证方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业代理AI系统在原型阶段表现良好，但生产部署时面临严重验证困难。自适应代理可能产生看似合理的输出，却因非确定性、保密约束、有限上下文和弱可观测性而难以验证，亟需一种不依赖无限制人工判断的控制器故障检测与修复机制。

Method: 提出有界验证协议，将自适应代理控制器建模为有限可修订对象。具体包括：(1)用有限符号规则表示控制器；(2)显式诊断谓词映射故障；(3)解释日志记录推理过程；(4)留出重评估验证修复效果。诊断故障被映射到预定义规则级编辑操作——规则添加、规则删除和优先级修订，修复后的控制器在留出仿真种子或克隆初始状态上进行评估。

Result: 在受财务约束的库存控制基准实验中观察到三类结果：(1)资源诱导型故障：单次规则编辑无法修复；(2)部分修复：因违反阈值或护栏而被拒绝；(3)局部一步修复：通过移除平滑规则成功修复了订单波动故障。

Conclusion: 该方法论贡献在于提供了一套仿真兼容的程序，用于测试特定控制器级故障能否在受控条件下变得可观测、可解释、可局部修订并经受实证重检验。

Abstract: Industrial agentic AI systems increasingly exhibit a gap between prototype capability and production deployment. In particular, adaptive agents may generate plausible outputs while remaining difficult to verify under non-determinism, confidentiality constraints, limited context, and weak observability. This paper formulates a bounded verification protocol for adaptive agentic controllers represented by finite symbolic rules, explicit diagnostic predicates, explanation logs, and held-out re-evaluation. The central research question is: when an adaptive agentic controller is represented through finite rules, explicit diagnostic predicates, explanation logs, and held-out re-evaluation, which classes of controller failure can be detected, locally repaired, or rejected without relying on unrestricted human-in-the-loop judgment? The proposed framework treats the controller as a finite revisable object. Diagnostic failures are mapped to predefined rule-level edits, including rule addition, rule deletion, and priority revision. Repaired controllers are then evaluated on held-out simulation seeds or cloned initial states. Experiments in a stylized financially constrained inventory-control benchmark show three outcomes: resource-induced failures that remain non-repairable by one rule edit, partial repairs that are rejected because they violate thresholds or guardrails, and a local one-step repair of an order-volatility failure induced by removing a smoothing rule. The contribution is methodological and provides a simulation-compatible procedure for testing whether specific controller-level failures can be made observable, explainable, locally revisable, and empirically re-tested under controlled conditions.

</details>


### [276] [EvoCUA-1.5: Online Reinforcement Learning for Multi-turn Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09773)
*Mianqiu Huang,Taofeng Xue,Chong Peng,Jinrui Ding,Sicheng Fan,Jiale Hong,Yufei Gao,Xiaocheng Zhang,Linsen Guo,Xin Yang,Dengchang Zhao,Yuchen Xie,Peng Pei,Xunliang Xie,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoCUA-1.5 将计算机使用智能体从离线学习扩展到在线强化学习，提出 STEPO 策略优化、策略感知过滤与校准、DTAC 动态课程及异步 RL 基础设施，在 OSWorld-Verified 上达到 63.2% 成功率，超越同规模开源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习和离线轨迹精炼方法无法覆盖真实计算机使用中的因果反馈循环——每个动作都会改变屏幕状态、未来动作空间和恢复选项。在线 RL 能弥补这一缺陷，但多轮交互带来了上下文管理、稀疏终端奖励、变长轨迹和缓慢环境反馈等独特挑战，不能直接套用单轮语言 RL 的方法。

Method: 提出四个核心组件：(1) STEPO（Step-Level Policy Optimization）：将轨迹级优势分解为步骤级样本后仍保持优势平衡；(2) 策略感知过滤与通过率校准：基于可验证合成任务进行数据筛选和难度校准；(3) DTAC（Dynamic Tri-Adaptive Curriculum）：融合可学习任务、困难正样本回放和受控不可行任务曝光的三重自适应课程；(4) 全异步 RL 基础设施：配备过期控制与 mini-group batching。整体框架将自我进化智能体从离线经验学习升级为在线 RL 训练。

Result: 在 OSWorld-Verified 基准上达到 63.2% 成功率，优于 32B/35B 参数规模的可比开源基线模型，甚至接近参数量显著更大的模型。消融实验表明各组件均对训练稳定性和下游性能有正向贡献。

Conclusion: EvoCUA-1.5 为多轮计算机使用智能体的在线 RL 规模化训练提供了实用框架，证明了精心设计的步骤级优化、自适应课程和异步基础设施能够有效克服多轮交互 RL 的固有挑战。

Abstract: Computer-use agents must solve long-horizon tasks through repeated interaction with partially observable, multimodal desktop environments. Although imitation learning and offline trajectory refinement provide strong priors, static traces cannot cover the causal feedback loop of real computer use: each action changes the screen state, future action space, and recovery options. EvoCUA-1.5 extends self-evolving computer-use agents from offline experience learning to online reinforcement learning, where policies interact with executable sandbox environments and improve from verifiable task outcomes. Online RL in this setting requires more than directly reusing single-turn language-RL recipes. Multi-turn interaction introduces context-managed observations, sparse terminal rewards, variable-length trajectories, and slow environment feedback. EvoCUA-1.5 addresses these challenges with Step-Level Policy Optimization (STEPO), which preserves trajectory-level advantage balance after decomposition into step-level samples; policy-aware filtering and pass-rate calibration over verifiable synthesized tasks; Dynamic Tri-Adaptive Curriculum (DTAC), which combines learnable tasks, difficult positive replay, and controlled infeasible-task exposure; and a fully asynchronous RL infrastructure with staleness control and mini-group batching. Experiments show that these components improve training stability and downstream performance. EvoCUA-1.5 achieves 63.2\% success on OSWorld-Verified, outperforming comparable 32B/35B-scale open-weight baselines and even approaching models with significantly larger parameter counts. Overall, EvoCUA-1.5 provides a practical framework for scaling online RL in multi-turn computer-use agents.

</details>


### [277] [From Patterns to Maze Structures: SMT-Based Path Synthesis and 2D/3D Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09781)
*Shengyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从文本或形状等输入模式自动构建迷宫结构的流程，其核心路径合成问题被编码为SMT（可满足性模理论）中的全局约束，单次调用求解器即可生成满足邻接性、连续性和模式覆盖约束的路径，该路径可作为平面迷宫和三维编织迷宫的构建支架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将迷宫设计中的路径合成问题形式化为可自动求解的约束问题，使任意指定的输入模式（文字、形状等）都能自动生成符合该模式的迷宫结构，减少人工设计负担，并统一处理平面迷宫与三维编织迷宫的生成。

Method: 将路径合成问题编码为SMT（Satisfiability Modulo Theories）中的全局约束，包括邻接性约束（确保路径连续）、连续性约束（保证路径不自交）、以及模式约束覆盖（路径覆盖指定的文字或形状区域）。对于平面迷宫生成自回避的平面路径，对于编织迷宫则生成具有指定上下穿层关系的分层遍历路径。最终路径作为支架，在平面中构造传统迷宫，在三维中实现编织迷宫。

Result: 该流程能够在单次SMT求解调用中为每个固定边界实例生成满足所有约束的路径，并成功将合成路径转化为具体的平面迷宫和三维编织迷宫构造。文章扩展了Bridges 2026会议论文，提供了更多SMT-LIB示例和路径到迷宫构造的完整说明。

Conclusion: 通过将迷宫路径合成归约为SMT约束求解，该方法实现了从任意输入模式到迷宫结构（平面或三维编织）的自动化构造，展示了约束求解在程序化几何设计中的有效性，并扩展了生成式迷宫设计的表达能力。

Abstract: We present a pipeline for constructing maze structures from input patterns such as text or shapes. The central path-synthesis problem is encoded in Satisfiability Modulo Theories as global constraints on adjacency, continuity, and pattern-constrained coverage, allowing each fixed-bound instance to be solved in one call. The resulting path is either a planar, self-avoiding route or a layered traversal with prescribed over--under crossings, and it serves as a scaffold for constructing planar mazes and three-dimensional realizations of woven mazes. This report extends the published Bridges 2026 conference paper with more representative SMT-LIB examples and a fuller account of how synthesized paths become concrete maze constructions in planar and three-dimensional form.

</details>


### [278] [Length Penalties Make Chain-of-Thought Less Monitorable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09786)
